### [1] [Knowledge-guided Disentanglement with Atomic Actions for Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26097)
*Tianci Wu,Siqi Cao,Guangming Zhu,Jiang Lu,Siyuan Wang,Longfei Zhang,Jincai Huang,Jun Sheng,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出知识引导的原子动作解耦方法(KDA)，利用大语言模型将动作标签分解为原子动作，通过知识注入模块(KIM)和知识解耦模块(KDM)增强视频特征表示与解耦能力，在多标签动作识别任务上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 复杂场景中多动作并发且细致，现有方法依赖整体表示难以捕捉细粒度语义；基于提示的方法缺乏显式语义引导，仅依赖视觉或结构化线索的方法仍停留于粗粒度层面。因此需要引入细粒度语义知识来增强动作表示并实现更精确的解耦。

Method: 1) 利用大语言模型(LLMs)将动作标签分解为具有显式时空语义的原子动作；2) 提出知识注入模块(KIM)，将原子动作知识集成到视频特征中；3) 提出知识解耦模块(KDM)，基于增强后的表示进一步解耦原子动作知识，提供精确语义引导；4) 设计知识解耦损失(KD Loss)，促进KDM中知识组件的清晰解耦。

Result: KDA在多标签动作识别基准上取得最先进性能，提升了特征判别性；KIM和KDM可无缝集成到其他方法中，展现出良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度语义知识引导的动作解耦方法可有效提升复杂场景下的多标签动作识别性能，且所提模块具有即插即用的通用性。

Original Abstract: Action recognition in complex scenes often involves multiple concurrent fine-grained actions, making it challenging to model internal action structures. Most existing methods rely on holistic representations, which are insufficient for capturing subtle interactions and fine-grained semantics. While recent prompt-based approaches introduce disentanglement, they lack explicit semantic guidance, and methods based solely on visual or structured cues remain coarse-grained. In this paper, we propose Knowledge-guided Disentanglement with Atomic Actions (KDA), which leverages fine-grained semantic knowledge to enhance action representations and enable more precise disentanglement. Specifically, we use Large Language Models (LLMs) to decompose action labels into atomic actions, providing explicit spatial-temporal semantics. A Knowledge Injection Module (KIM) first integrates atomic action knowledge into video features. Based on this enhanced representation, a Knowledge Disentanglement Module (KDM) further disentangles atomic action knowledge to produce more precise semantic guidance for action disentanglement. A Knowledge Disentanglement Loss (KD Loss) is introduced to encourage clearer disentanglement of knowledge components within KDM. Extensive experiments demonstrate that KDA improves feature discriminability and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multi-label action recognition benchmarks. Moreover, KIM and KDM can be readily integrated into other methods, demonstrating strong generality.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂场景中的动作识别通常涉及多个并发细粒度动作，这使得对内部动作结构的建模极具挑战性。现有方法大多依赖整体表示，难以捕捉细微的交互与细粒度语义。近年基于提示的方法引入了解耦思想，但缺乏显式的语义引导，而仅依靠视觉线索或结构化线索的方法仍停留在粗粒度层面。本文提出知识引导的原子动作解耦方法（KDA），利用细粒度语义知识增强动作表示，实现更精确的解耦。具体而言，我们使用大语言模型（LLMs）将动作标签分解为原子动作，提供显式的时空语义。知识注入模块（KIM）首先将原子动作知识集成到视频特征中；在此基础上，知识解耦模块（KDM）进一步对原子动作知识进行解耦，为动作解耦提供更精确的语义引导。知识解耦损失（KD Loss）用于促进KDM内部知识组件的清晰解耦。大量实验表明，KDA提升了特征判别性，并在多标签动作识别基准上达到了最先进性能。此外，KIM和KDM可便捷地集成到其他方法中，展现出强大的通用性。

</details>


### [2] [Weight and Height Estimation from a Single Human Image Captured in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26104)
*Hira Yaseen,Arif Mahmood,Waqas Sultani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度神经网络的多任务学习方法，利用RGB、深度图、姿态亲和图和边缘图等多种模态，从日常社交网络图像中自动估计BMI、体重和身高，并构建了一个包含6105张野外全身体图像的新数据集。实验表明，全身体图像相比半身和面部图像能取得更优的BMI估计效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: BMI作为反映人体身心健康、日常生活和财务状况的重要指标，具有长期健康预测价值（如疾病风险和寿命估计）。然而，现有方法在野外单张人体图像上进行自动BMI估计面临人体姿态、相机几何、外观和背景多样性的巨大挑战，且缺乏公开的全身体图像数据集。

Method: 采用深度神经网络，结合单任务与多任务学习框架，利用RGB图像、深度图、姿态亲和图（Pose-affinity maps）和边缘图等多种模态作为输入，使用VGG、DenseNet和ResNet等不同CNN主干网络，在全身体、半身和面部图像上分别进行BMI、体重和身高的回归预测。同时构建了一个包含6105张图像、带有身高/体重/BMI真实标签的野外数据集。

Result: 全身体图像在BMI估计上的表现优于半身图像和面部图像；实验对比了VGG、DenseNet和ResNet等多种CNN主干网络的效果。

Conclusion: 基于全身体图像的深度多任务学习能有效从野外日常图像中估计BMI、体重和身高，且全身体模态优于半身和面部模态。

Original Abstract: A person's physical characteristics such as weight and height are important indicators of his physical and mental health, daily life routines and finances. Body Mass Index (BMI) is a well known measure that encodes the characteristics of both the weight and the height. BMI has been used as a self-monitoring tool, and it has long-term implications on one's life. For example, it may help predicting the risk of various diseases and estimating longevity. Automatic BMI estimation using a single person image in the wild is a challenging task due to wide variations in human pose, camera geometry, personal appearance and distracting backgrounds. In this paper, we explore the performance of deep neural networks using single and multi-task learning by employing different modalities including RGB, depth-maps, pose-affinity maps, and edge-maps to predict BMI, weight, and height from daily life images available on social networking websites. Currently, no full body image dataset for BMI estimation is publicly available, therefore we propose a new dataset consisting of 6105 images with ground truth labels of height, weight and BMI. Our proposed dataset is collected in the wild containing images from various ethnicity and distributed over varying age groups and gender. It consists of frontal, back, full and half body, side poses, mirror selfies with varying backgrounds and scale variations and may contain artifacts hiding partial or full face. Extensive experimentation is performed using full body, half body and face images only using different CNN backbones including VGG, Densenet and ResNet. Our experimental results demonstrate that full body images have produced better results than the other half body and facial images in the wild.

中文摘要翻译: 一个人的身体特征（如体重和身高）是其身心健康、日常生活习惯和财务状况的重要指标。身体质量指数（BMI）是一种众所周知的度量指标，它编码了体重和身高两者的特征。BMI已被用作自我监测工具，并且对一个人的生活具有长期影响。例如，它可能有助于预测多种疾病的风险并估算寿命。利用野外单人图像自动估计BMI是一项具有挑战性的任务，原因在于人体姿态、相机几何、个人外观以及干扰背景的巨大变化。在本文中，我们探索了使用单任务和多任务学习的深度神经网络性能，采用包括RGB图像、深度图、姿态亲和图和边缘图在内的不同模态，从社交网站上可获取的日常生活图像中预测BMI、体重和身高。目前，尚无公开的全身体图像数据集用于BMI估计，因此我们提出了一个新数据集，包含6105张图像，并带有身高、体重和BMI的真实标签。我们提出的数据集是在野外收集的，包含来自不同种族的图像，并覆盖不同年龄组和性别。它包含正面、背面、全身和半身、侧身姿势、镜子自拍等，具有不同的背景和尺度变化，并且可能包含遮挡部分或全部面部的伪影。我们使用不同的CNN骨干网络（包括VGG、DenseNet和ResNet），对全身图像、半身图像和仅面部图像进行了广泛的实验。我们的实验结果表明，在野外条件下，全身体图像的估计效果优于半身图像和面部图像。

</details>


### [3] [TraceCLIP: Recovering Local Semantics from Patch-to-CLS Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26107)
*Xinran Liu,Shouqian Shi,Yutong Chen,Ge Wang,Xin-Wei Yao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceCLIP是一种无需额外训练的框架，通过从CLS注意力输出中分离出局部语义证据，并利用语义-测质地貌门控校准密集特征重建，在零样本语义分割任务上显著优于此前最强的无训练方法，揭示了全局对齐表征内部仍可获取空间局部化语义信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CLIP的图像级对比学习目标只对齐文本与全局表征，对局部视觉-语言对应关系的约束是间接的。现有方法需要额外监督、外部模型或任务特定适配，而无训练方法主要从现有补丁特征恢复密集响应，未探究局部语义在CLIP内部何处最易获取。因此迫切需要一种无需额外训练即可有效提取CLIP中局部语义信息的方法。

Method: TraceCLIP首先通过追溯CLS注意力输出中的补丁特定项，恢复潜在的补丁级语义证据（贡献特征）；然后将贡献特征转化为语义-测地线拓扑门控，用于校准最后一层补丁亲和度，从而进行密集特征重建。整个过程无需额外训练、外部视觉基础模型或区域级监督。

Result: 在8个零样本语义分割基准上，TraceCLIP在平均mIoU上比此前最强的无训练方法提升1.3至4.5个百分点，且在两个骨干网络和背景设置下均表现一致。诊断实验表明贡献特征具有强局部语义判别能力和文本条件空间对齐能力。

Conclusion: 全局对齐的表示（如CLIP）内部仍保留着可访问的空间局部化语义信息，TraceCLIP通过追溯贡献项和拓扑门控机制有效释放了这些信息，为密集视觉-语言理解提供了一种高效的无训练范式。

Original Abstract: Dense vision-language understanding, including object localization, region recognition, and open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, requires associating language concepts with spatially grounded visual regions. CLIP provides a strong foundation for these tasks by learning a shared image-text embedding space from large-scale contrastive pre-training. However, its image-level objective aligns text with a CLS-derived global representation, leaving local vision-language correspondence only indirectly constrained. Existing methods either introduce additional supervision, external models, or task-specific adaptation, while training-free approaches mainly recover dense responses from existing patch features without examining where local semantics become most accessible within CLIP. We introduce TraceCLIP, a training-free framework that recovers latent patch-level semantic evidence by isolating the patch-specific terms written into the CLS attention output. TraceCLIP further converts contribution-derived semantic responses into a semantic-geodesic topology gate that calibrates final-layer patch affinity for dense feature reconstruction. Diagnostic experiments show that these contribution features exhibit strong local semantic discrimination and text-conditioned spatial alignment. On eight zero-shot semantic segmentation benchmarks, TraceCLIP achieves gains of 1.3 to 4.5 points in average mIoU over the strongest prior training-free methods across both backbones and background settings, without additional training, external vision foundation models, or region-level supervision. More broadly, these findings suggest that spatially localized semantics may remain accessible within the internal construction of globally aligned representations.

中文摘要翻译: 密集视觉-语言理解，包括目标定位、区域识别和开放词汇语义分割，需要将语言概念与空间定位的视觉区域相关联。CLIP通过大规模对比预训练学习共享的图像-文本嵌入空间，为这些任务提供了坚实基础。然而，其图像级目标将文本与CLS导出的全局表征对齐，使得局部视觉-语言对应关系仅受到间接约束。现有方法要么引入额外监督、外部模型或任务特定适配，而无训练方法主要从现有补丁特征恢复密集响应，并未探究局部语义在CLIP内部何处最易获取。我们提出TraceCLIP，一种无训练框架，通过分离写入CLS注意力输出的补丁特定项来恢复潜在的补丁级语义证据。TraceCLIP进一步将贡献导出的语义响应转化为语义-测地线拓扑门控，用于校准最终层补丁亲和度以进行密集特征重建。诊断实验表明，这些贡献特征展现出强大的局部语义判别能力和文本条件空间对齐能力。在八个零样本语义分割基准上，TraceCLIP在两个骨干网络和背景设置下的平均mIoU相比此前最强的无训练方法提升了1.3至4.5个百分点，且无需额外训练、外部视觉基础模型或区域级监督。更广泛而言，这些发现表明空间局部化语义信息可能仍然可以在全局对齐表征的内部构造中被获取。

</details>


### [4] [DVPSFormer: Efficient Online Depth-aware Video Panoptic Segmentation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26165)
*Yung-Hsu Yang,Luigi Piccinelli,Siyuan Li,Mattia Segu,Lei Ke,Martin Danelljan,Yuqian Fu,Zuria Bauer,Fisher Yu,Hermann Blum,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DVPSFormer，一种统一的在线架构，通过显式场景离散化（ESD）和在线多数投票（OMV）机制，实现高效的4D场景理解（深度感知视频全景分割），在Cityscapes-DVPS和SemKITTI-DVPS基准上达到新SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度感知视频全景分割（DVPS）方法依赖计算昂贵的多阶段流水线或离线跟踪，无法满足实时决策需求，亟需一种统一、高效的在线架构。

Method: 提出DVPSFormer统一在线架构，核心包括：(1) 显式场景离散化（ESD），利用分割查询表示前景和背景区域，配合离散到连续（D2C）深度头单次解码度量深度；(2) 在线多数投票（OMV）机制，利用时间一致性优化实例跟踪中的分类。

Result: 在Cityscapes-DVPS和SemKITTI-DVPS基准上达到新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: DVPSFormer通过ESD和OMV机制实现了语义与几何学习的紧密耦合，显著降低了延迟，为在线机器人感知提供了精简高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation requires a holistic understanding of dynamic environments, necessitating the simultaneous estimation of metric depth, semantic segmentation, and instance trajectories. While depth-aware video panoptic segmentation (DVPS) unifies these tasks, existing approaches often rely on computationally expensive, multi-stage pipelines or offline tracking, rendering them unsuitable for real-time decision-making. To address this, we propose DVPSFormer, a unified online architecture designed for efficient 4D scene understanding. Central to our approach is explicit scene discretization (ESD), a novel mechanism that leverages segmentation queries to represent foreground and background regions, enabling a discrete-to-continuous (D2C) depth head to decode metric depth in a single pass. This tightly couples semantic and geometric learning while significantly reducing latency. Furthermore, we propose an online majority voting (OMV) mechanism that exploits temporal consistency to refine classification during instance tracking. DVPSFormer establishes a new state-of-the-art on the Cityscapes-DVPS and SemKITTI-DVPS benchmarks, offering a streamlined solution for online robotic perception. Code and models are available at https://royyang0714.github.io/DVPSFormer.

中文摘要翻译: 安全自主导航需要对动态环境进行全面理解，需要同时估计度量深度、语义分割和实例轨迹。虽然深度感知视频全景分割（DVPS）统一了这些任务，但现有方法通常依赖计算昂贵的多阶段流水线或离线跟踪，使其不适合实时决策。为解决这一问题，我们提出了DVPSFormer，一种专为高效4D场景理解设计的统一在线架构。我们方法的核心是显式场景离散化（ESD），一种利用分割查询表示前景和背景区域的新机制，使离散到连续（D2C）深度头能够在单次前向传播中解码度量深度。这紧密耦合了语义与几何学习，同时显著降低了延迟。此外，我们提出了一种在线多数投票（OMV）机制，利用时间一致性来优化实例跟踪过程中的分类。DVPSFormer在Cityscapes-DVPS和SemKITTI-DVPS基准上建立了新的最先进水平，为在线机器人感知提供了一种精简的解决方案。代码和模型可在https://royyang0714.github.io/DVPSFormer获取。

</details>


### [5] [A Picture Says Thousands of Words - Harnessing Dermal Exposure Data from Images through Hybrid Deep Learning for Enhanced Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26170)
*Hua Qian,Manisha Kotha,Tuan Tran,Jennifer Shin,Haining Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种混合计算机视觉方法，结合Mask R-CNN和颜色分割算法，从图像中量化暴露皮肤面积用于皮肤暴露评估。在170幅室内涂漆图像上，暴露皮肤与身体像素比与人工估计的一致性达约80%。该方法为从图像中提取半定量暴露信息提供了可扩展方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有皮肤暴露评估依赖人工估计或测量，效率低且难以规模化。亟需一种自动化的计算机视觉方法，从图像中快速、一致地量化暴露皮肤面积，以支持职业健康和环境暴露研究。

Method: 采用混合计算机视觉方法：首先使用Mask R-CNN识别图像中的人体目标并去除背景干扰；然后利用基于颜色的分割算法从人体区域中分割出暴露皮肤区域；最终计算暴露皮肤与身体的像素比。

Result: 在170幅室内涂漆图像上测试，所提方法得到的暴露皮肤与身体像素比与人工估计的一致性约为80%。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了一种可从图像中提取半定量暴露信息的可扩展方案，未来可扩展至身体部位识别、个人防护装备检测以及基于视频的暴露分析。

Original Abstract: This study developed a hybrid computer vision method to quantify exposed skin from images for dermal exposure assessment. Using 170 indoor-painting images, Mask R-CNN first identified human subjects and removed background interference; a color-based algorithm then segmented exposed skin. The resulting exposed-skin-to-body pixel ratios showed approximately 80% agreement with human estimates. The approach demonstrates a scalable way to extract semi-quantitative exposure information from images, with future extensions to body-part recognition, PPE detection, and video-based exposure analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 本研究开发了一种混合计算机视觉方法，用于从图像中量化暴露皮肤面积，以支持皮肤暴露评估。基于170幅室内涂漆图像，首先利用Mask R-CNN识别人体目标并去除背景干扰；随后通过基于颜色的算法分割暴露皮肤区域。所得暴露皮肤与身体像素比与人工估计的一致性约为80%。该方法展示了一种从图像中提取半定量暴露信息的可扩展途径，未来可扩展至身体部位识别、个人防护装备检测以及基于视频的暴露分析。

</details>


### [6] [Rad-JEPA 3D: Radiology Joint-Embedding Predictive Model for 3D Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26196)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh,Minh-Van Nguyen,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Rad-JEPA 3D是一个基于联合嵌入预测框架的3D医学CT预训练模型，通过混合H-Mamba编码器和隐藏状态正交正则化（HSOR），在约12万CT扫描上预训练，以4.0B参数达到SOTA性能，尤其在空间推理任务上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D医学图像编码器在预训练时难以保留CT体数据的粗粒度空间和几何结构，导致在下游任务（如器官分离、异常检测、空间理解）中性能受限，尤其在结合语言模型进行视觉问答时表现不佳。

Method: 提出Rad-JEPA 3D框架：1）采用联合嵌入预测架构，从遮罩视角预测完整扫描的潜在特征；2）设计混合H-Mamba编码器，融合Mamba状态空间分支（建模切片间连续性）和分组查询注意力分支（捕获跨平面空间上下文），通过轻量级逐token路由器组合；3）提出隐藏状态正交正则化（HSOR），对齐师生隐藏状态并减少特征冗余。

Result: 在约12万CT扫描上预训练，Rad-JEPA 3D以4.0B总参数量在封闭式VQA上达到与SOTA竞争的结果，在Spatial-Med基准上取得最佳平均空间推理分数。消融实验证实混合模块和HSOR贡献互补增益，诱导的空间结构可替代大语言模型规模在体推理任务上的作用。

Conclusion: Rad-JEPA 3D通过联合嵌入预测和混合编码器设计，有效学习了保留空间结构的CT体表示，以小参数量实现了优异的空间推理和器官识别性能，验证了空间结构可替代语言模型规模的观点。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is central to 3D medical image analysis, where unlabeled CT volumes are abundant but expert annotations are scarce. Yet existing volumetric encoders often fail to preserve the coarse spatial and geometric structure that downstream reasoning depends on, limiting their performance on organ disentanglement, abnormality detection, and spatial understanding when paired with language models. We introduce Rad-JEPA 3D, a joint-embedding predictive framework that learns volumetric CT representations by predicting the latent features of a complete scan from a masked view. At its core is a hybrid H-Mamba encoder that fuses a Mamba state-space branch, which models inter-slice continuity through sequential scanning, with a grouped-query attention branch, which captures cross-plane spatial context, combined through a lightweight per-token router. To improve the quality of intermediate representations, we further propose Hidden States Orthogonal Regularization (HSOR), which aligns student-teacher hidden states and reduces feature redundancy throughout the encoder. This layer-wise regularization produces more consistent and discriminative volumetric representations, leading to improved performance on organ recognition and spatial reasoning tasks. Pretrained on approximately 120,000 CT scans, Rad-JEPA 3D attains state-of-the-art results despite its compact size: with only 4.0B total parameters, it achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art on closed-ended VQA and the best average spatial-reasoning score on the Spatial-Med benchmark. Ablation studies confirm that the hybrid block and HSOR contribute complementary gains, and that the induced spatial structure can substitute for raw language-model scale on volumetric reasoning tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督预训练是3D医学图像分析的核心，其中未标记的CT体数据丰富但专家标注稀缺。然而，现有的体素编码器常常未能保留下游推理所依赖的粗粒度空间和几何结构，限制了它们在器官分离、异常检测以及与语言模型配合时的空间理解方面的表现。我们提出了Rad-JEPA 3D，一个联合嵌入预测框架，通过从遮罩视角预测完整扫描的潜在特征来学习体素CT表示。其核心是一个混合H-Mamba编码器，它融合了一个Mamba状态空间分支（通过顺序扫描建模切片间连续性）和一个分组查询注意力分支（捕获跨平面空间上下文），两者通过一个轻量级的逐token路由器相结合。为了改进中间表示的质量，我们进一步提出了隐藏状态正交正则化（HSOR），它对齐了师生网络的隐藏状态并减少了整个编码器中的特征冗余。这种逐层正则化产生了更一致且更具判别性的体素表示，从而在器官识别和空间推理任务上提升了性能。在约12万次CT扫描上预训练后，Rad-JEPA 3D尽管模型紧凑，仍取得了最先进的结果：总参数量仅为4.0B，它在封闭式VQA上达到了与最先进方法竞争的结果，并在Spatial-Med基准上取得了最佳平均空间推理分数。消融研究证实，混合模块和HSOR贡献了互补的增益，并且所诱导的空间结构可以在体素推理任务上替代原始的语言模型规模。

</details>


### [7] [WildShadowRemover: In-the-Wild Video Shadow Removal via Detail-Preserving Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26203)
*Jiamin Xu,Cong Wang,Zheng Dong,Chi Wang,Renshu Gu,Weiwei Xu,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WildShadowRemover框架，通过LoRA微调预训练视频扩散模型，结合细节注入模块和阴影掩码引导的频率分解调制模块，实现鲁棒的野外视频阴影去除，并构建了大规模配对数据集WildShadow。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 野外视频阴影去除在非约束真实场景中尚未被充分探索，现有方法受限于复杂光照、多样化阴影外观和有限训练数据，亟需一个能在无约束真实场景中有效工作的鲁棒框架。

Method: 1) 基于预训练视频扩散模型，通过LoRA微调适配阴影去除任务；2) 在冻结的VAE解码器中引入细节注入模块以保留细粒度图像细节；3) 提出阴影掩码引导的频率分解调制模块，选择性恢复高频纹理并抑制阴影伪影；4) 利用Depth Anything 3的单目深度先验提供几何感知引导；5) 构建大规模配对视频阴影去除数据集WildShadow。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在阴影去除质量与时间一致性上优于现有方法，能生成时间连贯的无阴影视频，具有优越的视觉质量和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的WildShadowRemover框架有效解决了野外视频阴影去除难题，通过扩散模型适配与多模块协同设计，在合成与真实场景中均展现出优异性能。

Original Abstract: Video shadow removal in the wild remains challenging due to complex illumination, diverse shadow appearances, and limited training data. Despite its importance to numerous vision and graphics applications, it remains largely unexplored in unconstrained real-world scenarios. To address this gap, we present WildShadowRemover, a framework that adapts a pretrained video diffusion model for robust video shadow removal via LoRA fine-tuning. To preserve fine image details while retaining the model's powerful generative prior, we augment the frozen VAE decoder with a detail injection module and introduce a shadow-mask-guided frequency-decomposed modulation module to selectively restore high-frequency textures while suppressing shadow artifacts. Monocular depth priors from Depth Anything 3 further provide geometry-aware guidance under challenging lighting conditions. We also construct WildShadow, a large-scale paired video shadow removal dataset and benchmark, covering diverse synthetic scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in shadow removal quality and temporal consistency, producing temporally coherent shadow-free videos with superior visual quality and strong generalization across challenging in-the-wild scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 野外视频阴影去除由于复杂光照、多样的阴影外观以及有限的训练数据而仍然具有挑战性。尽管该任务对众多视觉与图形应用至关重要，但在无约束的真实场景中仍未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出WildShadowRemover，一个通过LoRA微调适配预训练视频扩散模型以实现鲁棒视频阴影去除的框架。为在保留模型强大生成先验的同时保持细粒度图像细节，我们使用细节注入模块增强冻结的VAE解码器，并引入阴影掩码引导的频率分解调制模块，以选择性恢复高频纹理同时抑制阴影伪影。来自Depth Anything 3的单目深度先验在复杂光照条件下提供几何感知引导。我们还构建了WildShadow，一个大规模配对视频阴影去除数据集与基准，覆盖多样化的合成场景。大量实验表明，我们的方法在阴影去除质量和时间一致性上优于现有方法，能生成时间连贯的无阴影视频，具有优越的视觉质量，并在具有挑战性的野外场景中展现出强泛化能力。

</details>


### [8] [Lightweight Image Classification of Raptor Species for Edge Devices: Rare-Species Dataset Expansion via Video Frame Extraction, Knowledge Distillation, and TensorRT Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26238)
*Takeshi Nishikawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究面向风轮机碰撞缓解场景中的轻量级猛禽物种分类，采用DINOv2-L蒸馏多种轻量学生模型，通过数据集扩展和分组划分有效缓解物种混淆，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上实现313 images/s的边缘实时部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在风轮机碰撞缓解场景中，需要边缘端实时、轻量级的猛禽物种分类，以区分受保护物种（如虎头海雕）与其他物种，从而触发差异化的驱离或停机策略，降低碰撞风险。

Method: 使用DINOv2-L（304M参数）作为教师模型，蒸馏三个轻量学生模型（MobileNetV4、ViT-Small、EfficientNet-B0）；将数据集扩展至12,519张图像（通过视频帧提取将虎头海雕图像从463张增至2,050张）；采用视频级和源图像级分组划分以避免数据泄漏；最终集成三个学生模型进行预测。

Result: 三学生集成在分组划分下宏平均召回率0.935±0.004（常规图像级划分下0.955），参数约为教师模型的1/8；白尾海雕召回率提升最高38.6个百分点，误分类为虎头海雕的比例从61%降至15%；EfficientNet-B0在Jetson Orin Nano上TensorRT FP16推理达到3.19 ms/image（313 images/s），FP16与FP32的argmax一致性为99.95%。

Conclusion: 主要性能提升来源于数据集扩展和教师模型重新微调，而非蒸馏方法本身或教师模型的选择（DINOv2-L vs DINOv3-L）；所提轻量级方案可在边缘端实现高精度、实时的猛禽物种分类。

Original Abstract: We investigate lightweight raptor-species classification for real-time edge deployment in wind-turbine collision mitigation. Using DINOv2-L (304M parameters) as a teacher, we distilled three lightweight students (MobileNetV4, ViT-Small, and EfficientNet-B0). To reduce confusion between closely related species, we expanded the dataset to 12,519 images, including an increase in Steller's Sea Eagle images from 463 to 2,050 via video-frame extraction. Under a group split that separates samples at the video- and source-image level to mitigate source leakage at that granularity, the three-student ensemble achieved a macro recall of 0.935 +/- 0.004 over five distillation seeds (0.955 on a conventional image-level split, retaining 97.5% of the teacher's macro recall) with roughly one-eighth as many parameters. On a subset of 1,258 images disjoint from the former training images, White-tailed Eagle recall improved by up to 38.6 percentage points, while the rate at which it was misclassified as the Steller's Sea Eagle decreased from 61% to 15% of errors. TensorRT FP16 deployment of EfficientNet-B0 on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano achieved 3.19 ms/image including host-device transfer (313 images/s), with 99.95% argmax agreement with FP32. In five-seed controlled comparisons, neither distillation (versus CE-only) nor the change from a DINOv2-L to a DINOv3-L teacher yielded a clear ensemble-level improvement; the primary gains stem from the dataset expansion and teacher re-fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了面向风轮机碰撞缓解场景中实时边缘部署的轻量级猛禽物种分类。使用DINOv2-L（304M参数）作为教师模型，蒸馏了三个轻量学生模型（MobileNetV4、ViT-Small和EfficientNet-B0）。为减少近缘物种间的混淆，我们将数据集扩展至12,519张图像，其中通过视频帧提取将虎头海雕的图像从463张增加到2,050张。在采用视频级和源图像级分组划分（以缓解该粒度下的数据泄漏）的条件下，三学生集成在五个蒸馏种子上取得了0.935±0.004的宏平均召回率（常规图像级划分下为0.955，保留了教师模型97.5%的宏平均召回率），参数数量约为教师模型的八分之一。在与先前训练图像互斥的1,258张图像子集上，白尾海雕召回率最高提升了38.6个百分点，同时其被误分类为虎头海雕的错误比例从61%降至15%。在NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上，EfficientNet-B0的TensorRT FP16部署达到每张图像3.19 ms（含主机-设备传输，即313 images/s），与FP32的argmax一致性为99.95%。在五种子控制的对比实验中，蒸馏（相较于仅使用交叉熵损失）以及从DINOv2-L更换为DINOv3-L教师模型均未带来明显的集成级性能提升；主要增益来源于数据集扩展和教师模型的重新微调。

</details>


### [9] [Where Physics Meets Privacy: Federated PINNs for Privacy-Preserving Brain Tumor Biomechanical Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26207)
*Mahmuda Akter Sristy,Md Al-Mahfuz Chowdhury,Momota Ahsana Meem,Sajid Ahamed,Kazi Irfan Subhan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种联邦物理信息神经网络（FPINN），将联邦学习与基于线性弹性力学的物理信息损失相结合，在保护患者隐私的前提下，利用多中心MRI数据准确预测脑肿瘤引起的组织位移和刚度变化，准确率达91.4%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统脑肿瘤诊断依赖静态成像，无法捕捉肿瘤生长、组织位移和刚度变化；而深度学习模型通常需要集中患者数据，违反GDPR和HIPAA等隐私法规，且泛化能力受限。为解决这一矛盾，需要在保护数据隐私的同时实现对脑组织力学行为的有效建模。

Method: 提出联邦物理信息神经网络（FPINN），结合联邦学习与物理信息损失函数。三个模拟临床站点各自在本地患者MRI数据上训练基于线性弹性力学方程构建的物理信息神经网络，仅通过FedAvg协议在100轮通信中与中央服务器共享模型权重，原始数据始终留在本地。

Result: 联邦模型总体准确率91.4%（非联邦基线为90.0%），三类肿瘤平均AUC达0.985，其中垂体瘤准确率从85.6%提升至94.5%。训练产生了光滑、无散的位移场，与预期组织变形一致。

Conclusion: 联邦训练可以与基于物理的约束相结合，在保护数据隐私的同时不造成显著的性能损失，为神经肿瘤学中的多中心协作诊断提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Brain tumors such as glioma, meningioma, and pituitary adenoma alter the mechanical behavior of soft brain tissue, yet common diagnostic methods rely on static imaging that cannot capture tumor growth, tissue displacement, or changes in stiffness over time. Deep learning models for this task typically require pooling patient data at one site, which conflicts with privacy rules such as GDPR and HIPAA and limits generalization across institutions, a challenge that is pronounced in neuro oncology given patient diversity. This study presents a federated physics informed neural network combining federated learning with a physics informed loss built on the equations of linear elasticity. Three simulated clinical sites each train a local network on patient specific MRI data using a physics informed loss, and only model weights are shared with a central server through the FedAvg protocol over one hundred rounds, keeping raw data at its site of origin. The federated model reached an overall accuracy of 91.4%, against 90.0% for a non federated baseline trained on pooled data, an average AUC of 0.985 across tumor classes, and a rise in pituitary tumor accuracy from 85.6 to 94.5%. Training produced smooth, divergence free displacement fields consistent with expected tissue deformation, showing that federated training can be paired with physics based constraints without a meaningful loss in performance.

中文摘要翻译: 脑肿瘤（如胶质瘤、脑膜瘤和垂体腺瘤）会改变脑软组织的力学行为，然而常见的诊断方法依赖于静态成像，无法捕捉肿瘤生长、组织位移或随时间变化的刚度变化。针对此任务的深度学习模型通常需要在一个中心汇集患者数据，这与GDPR和HIPAA等隐私规则相冲突，并限制了跨机构的泛化能力——这在神经肿瘤学中因患者多样性而尤为突出。本研究提出了一种联邦物理信息神经网络，将联邦学习与基于线性弹性力学方程的物理信息损失相结合。三个模拟临床站点各自使用物理信息损失在患者特定的MRI数据上训练本地网络，仅通过FedAvg协议在100轮训练中与中央服务器共享模型权重，原始数据始终保留在其产生站点。联邦模型达到了91.4%的总体准确率，而基于汇集数据训练的非联邦基线为90.0%；三类肿瘤的平均AUC为0.985，垂体瘤准确率从85.6%提升至94.5%。训练产生了光滑、无散的位移场，与预期的组织变形一致，表明联邦训练可以与基于物理的约束相结合，且不会造成显著的性能损失。

</details>


### [10] [Lag-aware cross-hand alignment for dual-hand action segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26215)
*Fatemeh Ziaeetabar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lag-Aware Cross-Hand Alignment (LACA)，一个轻量级模块，显式估计左右手特征流之间的方向性时间偏移分布，改善双手动作分割中的跨手时间对齐问题，无需额外标注即可提升分割和边界定位性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有双手动作分割方法在同时间索引处融合左右手表征，但双手协调过渡可能存在非零且时变的时间延迟，导致对齐不准确。

Method: 提出LACA模块，显式估计方向性时间偏移分布以检索跨手信息，引入可学习的空状态抑制不兼容的跨手转移，使用兼容性感知目标进行监督（从帧级标注自动导出），并推出未来不可见变体LACA-C支持实时感知。

Result: HA-ViD和ATTACH数据集中分别有44.7%和48.9%的过渡锚点存在非零跨手匹配；LACA在HA-ViD上将双手mF1@50从40.4提升至42.5，边界F1从56.5提升至59.6；在ATTACH上从19.9提升至21.8和从44.7提升至47.9；仅增加约0.0086M参数。LACA-C实现83.6%过渡线索召回率、233ms中位可用延迟、0.72次/分钟误报。

Conclusion: 显式的跨手时间对齐能够同时提升动作分割和边界定位性能，并支持及时的、无需未来信息的感知。

Original Abstract: Dual-hand action segmentation commonly fuses left- and right-hand representations at identical temporal indices, although coordinated hand transitions may occur with nonzero and time-varying delays. We introduce Lag-Aware Cross-Hand Alignment (LACA), a lightweight module that explicitly estimates directional temporal-offset distributions between hand-specific feature streams. LACA retrieves cross-hand information from the estimated offsets and incorporates a learned null state to suppress transfer when no compatible cross-hand transition is supported. Alignment is supervised using compatibility-aware targets derived automatically from frame-level training annotations, without requiring additional labels. Analysis of the HA-ViD and ATTACH training annotations reveals robust nonzero cross-hand matches for 44.7% and 48.9% of transition anchors, respectively, compared with 18.6% and 21.3% under temporally shifted controls. When integrated into Polyphony, LACA improves the two-hand mean F1@50 from 40.4 to 42.5 and boundary F1 from 56.5 to 59.6 on HA-ViD, and from 19.9 to 21.8 and 44.7 to 47.9, respectively, on ATTACH, relative to our reproduced Polyphony baseline. These gains require only approximately 0.0086 million additional trainable parameters. We further introduce LACA-C, a future-free variant that restricts alignment and the complete inference pipeline to current and past observations. On ATTACH, LACA-C achieves 83.6% transition-cue recall, a seed-averaged median availability delay of 233~ms, 0.72 false cues per minute, and segmentation-stage throughput of 224.9 current-position predictions per second. These results demonstrate that explicit cross-hand temporal alignment improves both action segmentation and boundary localization while supporting timely future-free perception.

中文摘要翻译: 双手动作分割通常在相同时间索引处融合左右手表征，尽管协调的手部过渡可能伴随非零且时变的时间延迟。本文提出Lag-Aware Cross-Hand Alignment (LACA)——一个轻量级模块，显式估计手部特异性特征流之间的方向性时间偏移分布。LACA从估计的偏移中检索跨手信息，并引入一个可学习的空状态，以在不存在兼容的跨手过渡时抑制信息传递。对齐过程使用从帧级训练标注中自动导出的兼容性感知目标进行监督，无需额外标签。对HA-ViD和ATTACH训练标注的分析表明，分别有44.7%和48.9%的过渡锚点存在鲁棒的非零跨手匹配，而在时间移位控制条件下仅有18.6%和21.3%。将LACA集成到Polyphony中后，在HA-ViD数据集上，双手平均F1@50从40.4提升至42.5，边界F1从56.5提升至59.6；在ATTACH数据集上，分别从19.9提升至21.8和从44.7提升至47.9（相对于我们复现的Polyphony基线）。这些提升仅需增加约0.0086百万可训练参数。我们进一步引入LACA-C，一种无需未来的变体，将对齐及完整推理流程限制在当前和过去观测上。在ATTACH上，LACA-C实现了83.6%的过渡线索召回率、233毫秒的种子平均中位可用延迟、每分钟0.72次误报，以及分割阶段每秒224.9个当前位置预测的吞吐量。这些结果表明，显式的跨手时间对齐能够同时提升动作分割和边界定位性能，并支持及时的无需未来信息的感知。

</details>


### [11] [BG-REAL: A Public Real-Data Anchored Benchmark for Background Manipulation Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26232)
*Bugra Alperen Uluirmak,Rifat Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BG-REAL是一个面向背景篡改检测与定位的公开基准包，基于Open Images V7构建，包含7000个样本、六种编辑类型和三个基线模型。实验发现，基线模型普遍将重编码伪影误判为篡改，虚假阳性率高达0.57-1.00，揭示了重编码伪影作为共享捷径风险的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像取证评估多聚焦于显著前景目标的篡改（如复制-移动、拼接），而背景篡改这一实际但研究不足的设定缺乏专门基准。BG-REAL旨在填补这一空白，提供一个以背景篡改检测为中心的标准化评估平台。

Method: 从Open Images V7实例分割数据构建BG-REAL基准包：涵盖6000个公开数据锚定样本和1000个合成控制样本，共7000个处理样本，覆盖六种编辑家族；配备匹配的真实对照、源组分块、掩码与泄漏质量控制、599条人工辅助质检行；提供三条外部基线（TruFor、MVSS-Net、HiFi-Net）和五种子模型评估。

Result: 通过匹配真实对照诊断发现，基线模型在固定阈值下将重编码真实图像误分类为篡改的虚假阳性率范围为0.57（TruFor最低）到1.00（若干弱基线或掩码相关基线），表明重编码伪影是跨基线的共享捷径风险，而非单个模型特有的问题。

Conclusion: BG-REAL作为通用图像篡改定位基准的背景篡改聚焦补充，而非一个完全真实或通用的基准。其价值在于提供标准化的背景篡改评估框架，并揭示了重编码伪影作为基线模型共同捷径风险的关键发现。

Original Abstract: Background manipulation is a practical but under-specified image-forensics setting: the manipulated evidence can sit outside the salient foreground object, while many evaluations emphasize object-centric copy-move, splicing, or generic synthetic edits. We introduce BG-REAL, a public real-data anchored benchmark package for background manipulation detection and localization. The current release is built from Open Images V7 instance-segmentation sources and contains 7,000 processed samples over 1,200 source groups, including 6,000 public-data anchored samples and 1,000 synthetic control samples. BG-REAL covers six edit families, matched authentic controls, source-group splits, mask and leakage QA, 599 human-assisted quality-control rows, three completed external baselines (TruFor, MVSS-Net, and HiFi-Net), and five-seed model evaluation. Beyond aggregate accuracy, we use matched-authentic-control diagnostics to measure how often baselines misclassify re-encoded authentic images as manipulated at a threshold fixed on held-out validation data; false-positive rates range from 0.57 (TruFor, the lowest) to 1.00 (several weak or mask-informed baselines), indicating that re-encoding artifacts are a shared shortcut risk across baselines rather than a problem specific to any one model. The release provides the construction pipeline, evaluation protocol, paper-ready figures, and reproduction documentation. We frame BG-REAL as a background-manipulation-focused complement to general image-manipulation-localization benchmarks, not as a fully real-only or general-purpose benchmark.

中文摘要翻译: 背景篡改是一个实际但研究不足的图像取证设定：篡改证据可能位于显著前景目标之外，而许多评估侧重于以目标为中心的复制-移动、拼接或通用合成编辑。我们提出BG-REAL，一个公开的真实数据锚定基准包，用于背景篡改检测与定位。当前版本基于Open Images V7实例分割源构建，包含来自1200个源组的7000个处理样本，其中6000个公开数据锚定样本和1000个合成控制样本。BG-REAL覆盖六种编辑家族、匹配的真实对照、源组分块、掩码与泄漏质量控制、599条人工辅助质检行、三个已完成的外部基线（TruFor、MVSS-Net和HiFi-Net）以及五种子模型评估。除总体精度外，我们使用匹配真实对照诊断来衡量基线模型在固定阈值下（基于留出验证数据设定）将重编码真实图像误分类为篡改的频率；虚假阳性率范围为0.57（TruFor，最低）到1.00（若干弱基线或掩码相关基线），表明重编码伪影是跨基线的共享捷径风险，而非单个模型特有的问题。本发布提供了构建流程、评估协议、论文就绪图表和复现文档。我们将BG-REAL定位为通用图像篡改定位基准的背景篡改聚焦补充，而非一个完全真实或通用的基准。

</details>


### [12] [Spline-Based Boundary Representations for Sparse View Reconstruction and Simulation Using Isogeometric Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26234)
*Davor Dobrota,Vsevolod Skorokhodov,Chenghao Xu,Olga Fink,Malcolm Mielle*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FORGE-SIM方法，从稀疏带位姿的RGB图像直接重建多片B样条边界表示，生成兼容CAD和仿真的水密光滑几何体，并支持将观测场（如热状态、语义信息）投影到同一B样条基上，实现图像重建与仿真就绪建模的统一优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于图像的三维重建虽能恢复视觉细节模型，但其表面表示不具备数值仿真所需的水密性、光滑性等特性，无法直接用于仿真框架。因此需要一种方法能从图像直接重建仿真就绪的几何表示。

Method: 直接在优化框架中重建多片B样条边界表示（B-rep），通过优化样条表示本身来生成紧凑、光滑、水密的几何体；并设计策略将观测场（如热状态、语义信息）投影到同一B样条基上，使其可直接用于仿真。

Result: 重建模型质量足够高，能够支持热仿真和模态分析；生成的几何体原生兼容CAD和仿真工作流。

Conclusion: 通过将图像重建与仿真就绪建模统一在单一优化框架中，消除了计算机视觉与数值分析之间长期存在的壁垒，有望推动仿真驱动的设计、检测和数字孪生应用的新工作流。

Original Abstract: Image-based reconstruction aims to recover three-dimensional geometry from images. Recent advances have enabled the recovery of visually detailed models, yet their representations are not well-suited for numerical simulation. Simulation frameworks typically require explicit, watertight, and smooth geometries to ensure numerical robustness and accuracy, properties that surfaces extracted from image-based reconstructions lack. We propose FORGE-SIM, a method to directly reconstruct a multi-patch B-spline boundary representation from sparse posed RGB images without manual intervention. By optimizing the spline representation itself, our approach produces compact, smooth, and watertight geometries that are natively compatible with both Computer Aided Design and simulation workflows. Additionally, we introduce a strategy to project observation-derived fields, such as a thermal state and semantic information, onto the reconstructed models in the same spline basis, enabling immediate use in simulation. We demonstrate that the obtained models are of sufficiently high quality to enable thermal simulation and modal analysis. By unifying image-based reconstruction and simulation-ready modeling within a single optimization framework, this work removes a long-standing barrier between computer vision and numerical analysis. We anticipate that it will enable new workflows for simulation-driven design, inspection, and digital twin applications.

中文摘要翻译: 基于图像的三维重建旨在从图像中恢复三维几何结构。近期进展使得恢复视觉细节丰富的模型成为可能，但这些表示并不适合数值仿真。仿真框架通常需要显式、水密且光滑的几何体以确保数值鲁棒性和精度，而基于图像重建提取的表面缺乏这些特性。本文提出FORGE-SIM方法，能够从稀疏的带位姿RGB图像中直接重建多片B样条边界表示，无需人工干预。通过优化样条表示本身，该方法生成紧凑、光滑且水密的几何体，原生兼容计算机辅助设计与仿真工作流。此外，我们还引入一种策略，将观测派生的场（如热状态和语义信息）投影到同一B样条基的重建模型上，使其能够立即用于仿真。实验表明，所获得的模型质量足够高，可支持热仿真和模态分析。通过将基于图像的重建与仿真就绪建模统一于单一优化框架，这项工作消除了计算机视觉与数值分析之间长期存在的障碍。我们预计它将为仿真驱动的设计、检测和数字孪生应用开辟新的工作流。

</details>


### [13] [LumaGuide: Distribution Shaping for Training-Free HDR Generation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26237)
*Bowen Chen,Shreshth Saini,Balu Adsumilli,Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LumaGuide是一种无需训练即可在扩散模型采样过程中直接塑造输出分布（如亮度分布）的框架，通过可微能量引导实现高动态范围（HDR）图像生成，并能扩展到视频生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预训练扩散模型受限于训练数据的统计偏差，难以生成高动态范围（HDR）内容，而现有方法通常需要重新训练或修改模型参数。

Method: 提出LumaGuide框架，通过可微能量基引导（differentiable energy-based guidance）在采样过程中将生成过程导向目标特征分布，无需修改模型参数。在HDR生成中，于感知均匀的PQ空间中控制亮度直方图分布。

Result: 对齐亮度直方图足以产生HDR一致的行为，包括连贯的高光与保留的阴影细节，同时保持语义保真度。该方法还能通过数据驱动预设、参考图像或文本驱动预测器灵活指定目标分布，并自然扩展到具有时间一致性约束的视频生成。

Conclusion: 可控生成可以通过在采样时直接塑造输出分布来实现，无需重新训练扩散模型。

Original Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models generate realistic images but are constrained by the statistical biases of their training data, limiting their ability to produce high dynamic range (HDR) content. In this work, we introduce LumaGuide, a training-free framework for distribution shaping in diffusion models. Instead of modifying model parameters, LumaGuide steers the sampling process to match target feature distributions via differentiable energy-based guidance. We instantiate this framework for HDR generation by controlling luminance distributions in perceptually uniform PQ space. Our results show that aligning luminance histograms is sufficient to induce HDR-consistent behavior, including coherent highlights and preserved shadow detail, while maintaining semantic fidelity. Beyond HDR, LumaGuide enables flexible specification of target distributions through data-driven presets, reference images, or text-driven predictors, and extends naturally to video generation with temporal consistency constraints. More broadly, our work demonstrates that controllable generation can be achieved by directly shaping output distributions at sampling time, without retraining diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练的扩散模型能够生成逼真的图像，但受限于其训练数据的统计偏差，难以生成高动态范围（HDR）内容。本文提出LumaGuide，一种无需训练的扩散模型分布塑形框架。不同于修改模型参数，LumaGuide通过可微能量基引导将采样过程导向目标特征分布。我们将其实例化为HDR生成任务，在感知均匀的PQ空间中控制亮度分布。结果表明，对齐亮度直方图足以诱导出HDR一致的行为，包括连贯的高光与保留的阴影细节，同时保持语义保真度。除HDR外，LumaGuide可通过数据驱动预设、参考图像或文本驱动预测器灵活指定目标分布，并自然扩展到具有时间一致性约束的视频生成。更广泛地，本文工作表明，无需重新训练扩散模型，通过直接在采样时塑造输出分布即可实现可控生成。

</details>


### [14] [Comparing the Performance of Foundation Model Derived Embeddings with Traditional Approaches for Distant Metastasis Prediction in Head and Neck Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26276)
*Erich Schmitz,Meixu Chen,Bowen Jing,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了利用CT影像基础模型作为特征提取器预测头颈癌远处转移风险的效果。在2327名患者的术前CT数据上，基于CT Foundation嵌入的模型AUC达0.791，优于传统影像组学（0.772）和深度学习模型（0.753），接近三者组合模型（0.794），表明基础模型可减少领域专家依赖和标注需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有预测头颈癌远处转移风险的机器学习方法大多依赖肿瘤分割等感兴趣区域先验知识，这类方法需要专家知识、耗时且存在用户间变异性。近期发展的医学影像基础模型可提取模态相关特征以简化下游预测任务，但其在HNC DM风险预测中的有效性尚未被充分验证。本文旨在评估基础模型作为特征提取器的效果。

Method: 从RADCURE数据集中收集2327名HNC患者的术前CT图像，构建三组特征集：传统影像组学特征、基于深度学习的特征、以及CT Foundation模型导出的特征。分别使用这三组特征训练多层感知器（MLP）预测DM风险，并通过ROC AUC等指标对比性能。

Result: 基于CT Foundation嵌入的模型AUC为0.791，优于影像组学模型（0.772）和深度学习模型（0.753），与三者联合模型（0.794）性能接近。

Conclusion: 基于基础模型的特征提取为传统影像组学提供了有前景的替代方案，能够减少对领域专家知识和大量标注数据集的依赖。其极低的预处理需求使其更具可访问性和可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Background: Early prediction of distant metastasis (DM) risk in head and neck cancer (HNC) can enable timely interventions that may improve treatment outcomes. Many current machine learning methods rely on prior knowledge of the region of interest such as tumor segmentations, which require expert knowledge, is time-consuming and introduces user-dependent variability. Medical image-based foundation models have recently been developed for specific imaging modalities to streamline down-stream prediction tasks by extracting modality-relevant features. Purpose: In this study, we evaluate the effectiveness of using a foundation model as the feature extractor to predict DM risk in HNC patients and compare its performance with traditional approaches that require prior knowledge on the regions of interest. Methods: Preoperative CT images of 2327 patients from the RADCURE dataset were used. Three features-sets were created including radiomics, deep-learning based features, and CT Foundation derived features. The feature-sets were used individually in a multi-layer perceptron (MLP) to predict DM risk. Results: The model using CT Foundation embeddings outperformed the radiomics and deep learning-based models, achieving a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (AUC) of 0.791, compared to AUC values of 0.772 and 0.753 for the radiomics and deep learning-based models, respectively. The CT Foundation based model had similar performance to a model that combined the use of radiomics and deep learning-based features that achieved an AUC of 0.794. Conclusions: Features based on foundation models offer a promising alternative to traditional radiomics while reducing the need for domain expertise and extensively annotated datasets. Their minimal preprocessing requirements also make them a more accessible and scalable option.

中文摘要翻译: 背景：早期预测头颈癌（HNC）远处转移（DM）风险可以及时干预，从而改善治疗结果。许多现有的机器学习方法依赖肿瘤分割等感兴趣区域的先验知识，这需要专家知识、耗时且引入用户依赖变异性。基于医学影像的基础模型近期已被开发用于特定成像模态，通过提取模态相关特征来简化下游预测任务。目的：本研究评估了使用基础模型作为特征提取器预测HNC患者DM风险的有效性，并将其性能与需要感兴趣区域先验知识的传统方法进行比较。方法：使用来自RADCURE数据集的2327名患者的术前CT图像。构建了三组特征集，包括影像组学特征、基于深度学习的特征和CT Foundation导出的特征。这些特征集分别用于多层感知器（MLP）中预测DM风险。结果：使用CT Foundation嵌入的模型优于影像组学和基于深度学习的模型，实现了0.791的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUC），而影像组学和基于深度学习的模型的AUC分别为0.772和0.753。基于CT Foundation的模型与结合使用影像组学和深度学习特征的模型（AUC为0.794）性能相近。结论：基于基础模型的特征为传统影像组学提供了一种有前景的替代方案，同时减少了对领域专业知识和大量标注数据集的需求。其极低的预处理要求也使其成为更易获取和更具可扩展性的选择。

</details>


### [15] [HeteroPROPMT: A Real-time and Privacy-Preserving Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26283)
*Armin Maleki,Hayder Radha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HeteroPROMPT，一种实时且保护隐私的异构协同感知框架，通过轻量级视觉提示模块对齐异构车辆的特征空间，无需重新训练或暴露私有元数据，在OPV2V-H和V2XSet数据集上以极少可训练参数实现了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有协同感知方法在处理异构车辆（不同传感器、模型、数据集、训练域）时存在特征空间偏移问题，而现有方案需重新训练融合检测组件或引入特定模态的解释器，扩展性差且涉及隐私风险。

Method: 提出HeteroPROMPT框架：1) 设计模块化视觉提示（prompt），通过轻量级学习微调对BEV特征进行通道和空间位置的调制，将异构代理特征快速对齐到以自我为中心的统一特征空间；2) 保持代理编码器、协同融合和检测堆栈冻结；3) 引入自编码器学习紧凑统一表示，从共享特征中提取模态线索，实现实时模态分类和路由，无需暴露私有元数据。

Result: 在OPV2V-H和V2XSet数据集上，HeteroPROMPT的平均精度（AP）超过最先进的异构CP方法，且可训练参数数量少几个数量级；模态分类器在部署时对加入代理的模态预测准确率超过99.99%。

Conclusion: HeteroPROMPT提供了一种可扩展、实用且保护隐私的异构协同感知解决方案，通过轻量级提示调优有效解决了特征空间对齐问题。

Original Abstract: Collaborative Perception (CP) improves autonomous systems' awareness of their surroundings by sharing sensor data, intermediate features, and detection results. In real-world deployments, however, collaborating vehicles often use heterogeneous sensors, perception models, datasets, and training domains, creating feature-space shifts that degrade downstream fusion and detection. Existing approaches typically retrain fusion and detection components or introduce modality-specific feature interpreters. These methods scale poorly to newly joining agents and often require access to proprietary metadata, raising privacy concerns. We propose HeteroPROMPT, a real-time and privacy-preserving framework for heterogeneous collaborative perception. HeteroPROMPT rapidly aligns each heterogeneous agent's features with an ego-centric unified feature space through modular prompts and lightweight learning-based tuning, while keeping agent encoders and the collaborative fusion and detection stacks frozen. Its visual prompt-based training and inference modulate Bird's Eye View (BEV) features across channels and spatial locations with low computational overhead. For metadata-free deployment, an autoencoder learns a compact unified representation and extracts modality cues from shared features, enabling real-time modality classification and routing to the appropriate HeteroPROMPT modules without exposing proprietary agent information. Experiments on the OPV2V-H and V2XSet datasets show that HeteroPROMPT improves Average Precision over state-of-the-art heterogeneous CP methods while using orders of magnitude fewer trainable parameters. This offers a scalable and practical CP solution. The proposed modality classifier also predicts the joining agent's modality from compact features with greater than 99.99 percent accuracy during deployment. Code will be available at https://github.com/arminmaleki007/HeteroPROMPT.

中文摘要翻译: 协同感知（CP）通过共享传感器数据、中间特征和检测结果，提升了自主系统对周围环境的感知能力。然而，在实际部署中，协作车辆通常使用异构传感器、感知模型、数据集和训练域，导致特征空间偏移，从而降低了下游融合和检测性能。现有方法通常需要重新训练融合和检测组件，或引入特定模态的特征解释器。这些方法对后续加入的代理扩展性差，且往往需要访问私有元数据，引发隐私问题。我们提出HeteroPROMPT，一种实时且保护隐私的异构协同感知框架。HeteroPROMPT通过模块化提示（prompt）和轻量级基于学习的微调，快速将每个异构代理的特征与以自我为中心的统一特征空间对齐，同时保持代理编码器以及协同融合和检测堆栈冻结。其基于视觉提示的训练和推理以较低的计算开销对鸟瞰图（BEV）特征进行跨通道和空间位置的调制。为实现无需元数据的部署，自编码器学习紧凑的统一表示，并从共享特征中提取模态线索，从而在无需暴露代理私有信息的情况下实现实时模态分类和路由。在OPV2V-H和V2XSet数据集上的实验表明，HeteroPROMPT在平均精度（AP）上超越了最先进的异构CP方法，同时使用的可训练参数数量少了几个数量级。这提供了一种可扩展且实用的CP解决方案。提出的模态分类器还能在部署期间从紧凑特征中以超过99.99%的准确率预测加入代理的模态。代码将在 https://github.com/arminmaleki007/HeteroPROMPT 提供。

</details>


### [16] [Eddeep: a deep-learning framework for fast eddy-current distortion correction in diffusion MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26292)
*Antoine Legouhy,Ross Callaghan,Yuchuan Qiao,Whitney Stee,Philippe Peigneux,Hojjat Azadbakht,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Eddeep，一种用于弥散MRI中涡流畸变校正的深度学习框架，将问题分解为监督图像翻译和无监督配准两个阶段，在保证校正质量与FSL Eddy相当的同时大幅缩短推理时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 弥散MRI易受涡流引起的几何畸变影响，现有最优校正方法（如FSL Eddy）虽效果好但计算成本高，亟需一种高效且准确的校正方法以支持大规模研究和临床部署。

Method: Eddeep采用两阶段策略：第一阶段使用监督图像翻译网络标准化弥散加权图像与b=0图像的外观，消除对比度差异对配准的干扰；第二阶段使用无监督配准网络，在物理约束的二次畸变模型下同时估计涡流畸变和帧间头动参数，仅需一次前向传播即可完成校正。

Result: 在UK Biobank（域内）和Memodyn（域外）数据集上，使用帧间抖动、弥散峰度成像残差、信号不规则性和互信息等指标评估，Eddeep实现了与FSL Eddy相当的校正质量，同时显著降低了推理时间。

Conclusion: 深度学习无需迭代优化即可实现准确高效的涡流畸变校正，为大规模研究和临床中更快速的弥散MRI处理流程提供了支持。

Original Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) relies on diffusion-weighted echo-planar imaging, which is highly susceptible to eddy-current-induced geometric distortions. These distortions vary across diffusion volumes according to gradient strength and direction, causing between-volume misalignment that can bias downstream microstructural analyses. Current state-of-the-art correction methods, such as FSL Eddy, achieve high-quality correction through iterative prediction-correction schemes but are computationally expensive. We propose Eddeep, a deep-learning framework for fast eddy-current distortion correction in dMRI. Eddeep decomposes the problem into two stages. First, a supervised image translation network standardises the appearance of diffusion-weighted and b=0 images, removing contrast differences that hinder reliable registration. Second, an unsupervised registration network estimates both eddy-current distortion and between-volume head motion parameters under a physics-constrained quadratic distortion model, enabling correction in a single forward pass. The method was trained on UK Biobank data and evaluated on both in-domain (UK Biobank) and out-of-domain (Memodyn) datasets. Across a range of complementary metrics, including between-volume jitter, diffusion kurtosis imaging residuals, signal irregularity, and mutual information, Eddeep achieved correction quality comparable to that of FSL Eddy while substantially reducing inference time. These results demonstrate that deep learning can provide accurate and efficient eddy-current distortion correction without relying on iterative optimisation, supporting the development of faster diffusion MRI processing pipelines for large-scale studies and clinical deployment. The code is available at: https://github.com/CIG-UCL/eddeep.

中文摘要翻译: 弥散MRI依赖于弥散加权回波平面成像，该技术极易受涡流引起的几何畸变影响。这些畸变根据梯度强度和方向在不同弥散体积间变化，导致体积间错位，进而影响下游微结构分析。当前最先进的校正方法（如FSL Eddy）通过迭代预测-校正方案实现高质量校正，但计算成本高昂。我们提出Eddeep，一种用于弥散MRI中快速涡流畸变校正的深度学习框架。Eddeep将问题分解为两个阶段：首先，一个监督图像翻译网络标准化弥散加权图像与b=0图像的外观，消除阻碍可靠配准的对比度差异；其次，一个无监督配准网络在物理约束的二次畸变模型下同时估计涡流畸变和体积间头动参数，仅需单次前向传播即可完成校正。该方法在UK Biobank数据上训练，并在域内（UK Biobank）和域外（Memodyn）数据集上评估。在包括帧间抖动、弥散峰度成像残差、信号不规则性和互信息在内的一系列互补指标上，Eddeep实现了与FSL Eddy相当的校正质量，同时大幅减少了推理时间。这些结果表明，深度学习无需依赖迭代优化即可提供准确高效的涡流畸变校正，为大规模研究和临床部署中更快速的弥散MRI处理流程提供了支持。代码地址：https://github.com/CIG-UCL/eddeep。

</details>


### [17] [MoSAIC: Aligned Intervention Supervision for Part-Local Motion Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26304)
*Nazanin Amini,Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoSAIC是一个用于局部参考条件运动风格迁移的潜在扩散框架，通过对齐干预监督训练实现选择性运动编辑，在保留非选定区域的同时有效转移指定身体部位的风格。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有运动数据集缺乏任意局部内容-参考组合的配对目标，且自重建训练可能导致扩散模型仅复制内容运动而弱化参考信息，难以实现精确的局部运动风格迁移。

Method: MoSAIC框架通过解剖区域分解内容与参考特征，分离根轨迹条件路径，将用户选定的参考路由到特定身体部位；核心贡献是对齐干预监督（aligned intervention supervision），通过受控局部变换构建同步参考和反事实目标，使训练中可直接观测请求的区域响应和需保留的运动。

Result: 在128个运动与896个路由条件的冻结评估中，部分掩蔽路由相比全身路由将保留区域误差从70.64降至66.45mm，匹配噪声的离目标泄漏从18.08降至9.88mm；匹配预算延续研究表明对齐干预监督使选定目标响应相对提升8.8%，请求路由影响集中度提高2.0个百分点。

Conclusion: MoSAIC有效改善了选择性局部运动编辑中响应与保留之间的权衡，实现了可控的部分局部运动编辑。

Original Abstract: Editing character motion often requires transferring a gesture or gait from one or more reference motions while preserving the source action, timing, root trajectory, and unselected body regions. Existing motion datasets, however, rarely provide paired targets for arbitrary part-local content--reference combinations, and self-reconstruction training may allow a diffusion model to reproduce the content motion while underusing the routed reference. We present MoSAIC, a latent diffusion framework for part-local reference-conditioned motion style transfer. MoSAIC factorizes content and reference features by anatomical region, preserves the root trajectory through a separate conditioning pathway, and routes user-selected references to individual body parts. Its central contribution is aligned intervention supervision, which constructs synchronized references and counterfactual targets through controlled local transformations, making both the requested regional response and the motion to be preserved directly observable during training. In a frozen evaluation comprising 128 motions and 896 routed conditions, part-masked routing reduces preserved-region error from 70.64 to 66.45~mm and matched-noise off-target leakage from 18.08 to 9.88~mm relative to whole-body routing, while retaining a positive selected-region response. A matched-budget continuation study further shows that retaining aligned intervention supervision produces an 8.8\% relative increase in selected-target response and a 2.0-percentage-point increase in requested-route influence concentration. These results demonstrate that MoSAIC improves the response--preservation trade-off required for selective and controllable part-local motion editing.

中文摘要翻译: 编辑角色运动通常需要从一个或多个参考运动中迁移手势或步态，同时保留源动作、时序、根轨迹和未选中的身体区域。然而，现有的运动数据集很少为任意局部内容-参考组合提供配对目标，而自重建训练可能使扩散模型复制内容运动而未能充分利用路由的参考。我们提出MoSAIC，一个用于局部参考条件运动风格迁移的潜在扩散框架。MoSAIC通过解剖区域分解内容和参考特征，通过单独的条件路径保留根轨迹，并将用户选定的参考路由到各个身体部位。其核心贡献是对齐干预监督（aligned intervention supervision），该方法通过受控局部变换构建同步参考和反事实目标，使得训练过程中可直接观测到请求的区域响应和需保留的运动。在包含128个运动和896个路由条件的冻结评估中，与全身路由相比，部分掩蔽路由将保留区域误差从70.64降至66.45毫米，匹配噪声的离目标泄漏从18.08降至9.88毫米，同时保持了正向的选定区域响应。匹配预算的延续研究进一步表明，保留对齐干预监督可使选定目标响应相对提升8.8%，请求路由影响集中度提高2.0个百分点。这些结果表明，MoSAIC改善了选择性可控局部运动编辑所需的响应-保留权衡。

</details>


### [18] [Seeing or Knowing? Visual Context Sensitivity in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26326)
*Jiaang Li,Chengzu Li,Zhaochong An,Yifei Yuan,Xi Liu,Serge Belongie,Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型（MLLMs）在视觉证据与语言先验冲突时表现不佳，其瓶颈不在于视觉编码阶段，而在于无法可靠地控制对视觉证据的依赖。作者通过图像重建探针和WhatIfVis基准测试，揭示了模型虽能编码视觉信息，但缺乏稳定的视觉上下文敏感性，并通过监督微调和激活修补实现了对“视觉-先验”权衡的控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究多模态大模型在视觉证据与预训练语言先验冲突时失败的根本原因——是视觉编码阶段的信息丢失，还是后感知阶段的利用/控制问题。

Method: (1) 设计两个诊断范式：图像重建探针（检测视觉信息是否被编码）和多模态上下文敏感性测量。(2) 构建WhatIfVis基准，涵盖时空、颜色、计数、大小、重量五个粗粒度维度，每个问题可基于图像或先验回答。(3) 使用监督微调（SFT）和激活修补（activation patching）分析可控性，并通过学习到的引导向量（steering vector）实现无需指令的视觉-先验权衡控制。

Result: (i) 粗粒度视觉证据被保留，可从冻结MLLM的最终层图像token中重建，失败源于后感知利用而非感知编码。(ii) 未经SFT的模型视觉上下文敏感性不稳定，SFT可提高可控性并跨域泛化，激活修补定位到特定架构深度。(iii) 沿学习到的向量可控制视觉-先验权衡，无需指令即可提高可控性。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的粗粒度属性，MLLMs能够编码视觉证据，但无法可靠地控制对其的依赖——瓶颈在于控制能力而非感知能力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance by integrating visual inputs with the rich priors of pretrained language models. However, they often fail on vision-centric tasks, especially when visual evidence conflicts with pretrained knowledge. We explore these failures separately using two diagnostic paradigms: (1) probing whether visual information is available, via image reconstruction, and (2) measuring multimodal context sensitivity, the extent to which the model follows visual context versus the language prior. To support the second, we introduce the WhatIfVis, a benchmark spanning five coarse-grained dimensions (spatial-temporal, color, count, size, and weight) whose questions admit answers from either the image or the prior. Our analysis yields three findings: (i) Coarse-grained visual evidence is preserved, as these attributes can be reconstructed from the final-layer image tokens of frozen MLLMs. Failures on questions about these attributes therefore point to post-perceptual utilization, rather than to degraded visual encoding during perception. (ii) Even when explicitly instructed to use or ignore visual evidence, vanilla models (without supervised fine-tuning on the WhatIfVis) show unstable visual context sensitivity. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves this controllability and generalizes across domains, and activation patching further localizes the vision-versus-prior trade-off at architecture-specific depths across all six models. (iii) The vision-versus-prior trade-off is controllable along a learned vector. Applying this steering vector, even without any intent instruction, improves controllability over the vanilla model. Together, these results relocate the bottleneck, indicating that for the coarse attributes we study, MLLMs encode the visual evidence but cannot reliably control their reliance on it.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型通过将视觉输入与预训练语言模型的丰富先验知识相结合，取得了强大的性能。然而，它们在视觉中心任务上经常失败，尤其是当视觉证据与预训练知识发生冲突时。我们通过两种诊断范式分别探究这些失败：(1) 通过图像重建探针检测视觉信息是否可用；(2) 测量多模态上下文敏感性，即模型遵循视觉上下文与依赖语言先验的程度。为支持后者，我们引入了WhatIfVis基准，涵盖五个粗粒度维度（时空、颜色、计数、大小、重量），其问题既可从图像也可从先验知识中得出答案。我们的分析得到三项发现：(i) 粗粒度视觉证据被保留——这些属性可从冻结MLLMs最终层图像token中重建。因此，关于这些属性的失败指向后感知阶段的利用问题，而非感知阶段视觉编码的退化。(ii) 即使被明确指示使用或忽略视觉证据，未经监督微调的原始模型仍表现出不稳定的视觉上下文敏感性。监督微调提高了这种可控性并跨领域泛化，激活修补进一步将视觉-先验权衡定位在所有六个模型的特定架构深度。(iii) 视觉-先验权衡可沿一个学习到的向量进行控制。即使没有任何意图指令，应用该引导向量也能提高相对于原始模型的可控性。综合这些结果，瓶颈被重新定位：对于我们研究的粗粒度属性，MLLMs编码了视觉证据，但无法可靠地控制对其的依赖。

</details>


### [19] [Zero-Fi: Zero-Shot Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition via Contrastive Signal-Language Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26381)
*Yitong Shen,Cheng Guo,Peiliang Wang,Jingzhe Zhang,Yi Sheng,Haopeng Zhang,Hongfei Xue,Yili Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Zero-Fi框架，利用对比信号-语言对齐实现零样本Wi-Fi人体活动识别，无需为未见过的活动类别提供标注Wi-Fi样本或模型适配，在公开基准数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Wi-Fi人体活动识别方法假设活动类别是封闭的，需要为每个目标类别提供标注的Wi-Fi样本，无法识别未见过的活动类别。

Method: 构建对比信号-语言对齐框架Zero-Fi，从互补的Wi-Fi信号特征中学习统一表征，并将其与自然语言活动描述的语义表征在共享嵌入空间中对齐，实现跨模态对齐。

Result: 在大型公开基准数据集上，Zero-Fi能够有效识别未见过的活动类别，实现了零样本识别。

Conclusion: 信号-语言对齐能够将Wi-Fi感知扩展到预定义活动类别之外，为开放场景下的Wi-Fi人体活动识别提供了新途径。

Original Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition has advanced substantially, but most existing methods assume a closed set of activities and require labeled Wi-Fi samples for every target class, limiting their ability to recognize unseen activities. We present Zero-Fi, a contrastive signal-language alignment framework for zero-shot Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition. Zero-Fi learns unified representations from complementary Wi-Fi signal features and aligns them with the semantic representations of natural-language activity descriptions in a shared embedding space. This cross-modal alignment enables Zero-Fi to recognize new activity classes without requiring labeled Wi-Fi samples or model adaptation for those classes. Experiments on large-scale public benchmark datasets demonstrate effective zero-shot recognition of held-out activity classes, highlighting the potential of signal-language alignment to extend Wi-Fi sensing beyond predefined activity classes.

中文摘要翻译: 基于Wi-Fi的人体活动识别已取得显著进展，但现有方法大多假设活动类别是封闭的，且需要为每个目标类别提供标注的Wi-Fi样本，限制了其识别未见活动的能力。本文提出Zero-Fi，一种面向零样本Wi-Fi人体活动识别的对比信号-语言对齐框架。Zero-Fi从互补的Wi-Fi信号特征中学习统一表征，并将其与自然语言活动描述的语义表征在共享嵌入空间中对齐。这种跨模态对齐使得Zero-Fi能够识别新的活动类别，而无需这些类别的标注Wi-Fi样本或模型适配。在大型公开基准数据集上的实验表明，Zero-Fi能够有效识别未见过的活动类别，凸显了信号-语言对齐将Wi-Fi感知扩展到预定义活动类别之外的潜力。

</details>


### [20] [Registration-Grounded Spectral Fusion for Unregistered WLI/NBI Endoscopic Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26395)
*Pengyu Jie,Wanquan Liu,Rui He,Pengcheng Li,Weiping Wen,Deyu Meng,Junwei Han,Chenqiang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对白光成像与窄带成像在配准状态下因空间未对齐导致融合效果差的问题，提出了一种基于可靠性感知的复数域融合框架，通过拓扑正则化特征对应和可靠性引导的跨模态交互，在复数域中分别建模WLI的幅度响应和NBI的相位响应，有效提升了内镜病变分割性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: WLI和NBI提供互补的内镜病变视图，但由于视角变化、组织变形和手持顺序采集导致空间未对齐，直接融合会混合非对应区域，甚至降低病灶边界分割性能。现有方法缺乏对跨模态匹配可靠性的建模，也未能区分两种模态的不同作用。

Method: 提出可靠性感知的复数域融合框架：(1) 建立拓扑正则化的特征对应，估计跨模态对应是否可靠；(2) 基于可靠性引导，在可学习的复数表示中选择性融合WLI和NBI特征，其中WLI贡献外观相关的幅度响应，NBI贡献结构敏感的相位响应；(3) 显式建模两种模态的不同角色，抑制局部不匹配区域的不可靠跨模态交互。

Result: 在配对的WLI/NBI内镜数据集上，所提方法一致提升了病变分割性能。角色互换实验和模块消融实验进一步验证了模态角色设计和可靠性引导的跨模态交互的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过可靠性感知的配准校准和复数域融合，可以有效解决WLI/NBI未对齐融合问题，且显式区分幅度-相位角色对提升分割性能至关重要。

Original Abstract: White-light imaging (WLI) and narrow-band imaging (NBI) provide complementary views of endoscopic lesions, but their paired observations are often spatially misaligned due to viewpoint changes, tissue deformation, and sequential handheld acquisition. This makes direct WLI/NBI fusion prone to mixing non-corresponding regions and may even degrade segmentation around lesion boundaries. To address this problem, we propose a reliability-aware complex-domain fusion framework for paired-but-unregistered WLI/NBI lesion segmentation. The framework first establishes topology-regularized feature correspondence and further estimates where the cross-modal correspondence is reliable. Guided by this reliability, the model selectively fuses WLI and NBI features in a learnable complex representation. In this representation, WLI-derived cues mainly provide appearance-related magnitude responses, while NBI-derived cues provide structure-sensitive phase responses. Unlike conventional real-valued or symmetric multimodal fusion, the proposed method explicitly models the different roles of WLI and NBI and suppresses unreliable cross-modal interaction in locally mismatched regions. Experiments on paired WLI/NBI endoscopic datasets show that the proposed reliability-aware registration grounding and complex-domain fusion consistently improve lesion segmentation performance. Role-reversal and module ablation studies further validate the necessity of both the modality-role design and reliability-guided cross-modal interaction.

中文摘要翻译: 白光成像（WLI）和窄带成像（NBI）为内镜病变提供了互补的视图，但由于视角变化、组织变形以及手持式顺序采集，它们的配对观测往往存在空间未对齐的问题。这使得直接的WLI/NBI融合容易混合非对应区域，甚至可能降低病灶边界处的分割性能。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种面向配对但未配准的WLI/NBI病变分割的可靠性感知复数域融合框架。该框架首先建立拓扑正则化的特征对应，并进一步估计跨模态对应在哪些区域是可靠的。在该可靠性的引导下，模型在可学习的复数表示中选择性地融合WLI和NBI特征。在该表示中，WLI衍生的线索主要提供外观相关的幅度响应，而NBI衍生的线索则提供结构敏感的相位响应。与传统的实值或对称多模态融合不同，该方法显式地建模WLI和NBI的不同角色，并在局部不匹配区域抑制不可靠的跨模态交互。在配对的WLI/NBI内镜数据集上的实验表明，所提出的可靠性感知配准校准和复数域融合一致地提升了病变分割性能。角色互换实验和模块消融研究进一步验证了模态角色设计和可靠性引导的跨模态交互的必要性。

</details>


### [21] [Do Unified Multimodal Models Think in One Space? A Lens Through Cross-Branch Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26411)
*Yu Wang,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了统一多模态模型（UMMs）中理解与生成能力是否共享统一的语义空间，提出了跨分支语义引导（cross-branch semantic steering）框架，发现理解分支的语义向量可迁移至生成分支实现可控图像合成，而反向迁移效果有限，揭示了架构统一并不保证语义对齐。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 统一多模态模型将理解与生成集成在同一架构中，但这两类能力是否共享统一的、可迁移的语义空间尚不明确。由于两者在异构表征（文本token vs. 视觉潜在变量）和不同训练目标上运作，直接比较非常困难，因此需要一种干预性框架来探测其语义对齐程度。

Method: 提出跨分支语义引导（cross-branch semantic steering）框架，从理解分支中提取语义方向向量并应用于生成分支，反之亦然，通过干预性实验来探测两分支间语义表征的可迁移性。

Result: （1）从理解分支学到的引导向量可迁移至生成分支，实现可控图像合成并提升语义忠实度；（2）反向（生成→理解）迁移效果持续受限；（3）这种不对称性源于表征不匹配：理解分支的向量捕获可迁移的、以对象为中心的语义，而生成分支的向量主要编码低层外观特征。

Conclusion: 架构统一并不保证语义对齐；跨分支语义引导可作为一种实用的工具来探测多模态表征的对齐程度。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to integrate understanding and generation within a single architecture, yet it remains unclear whether these capabilities share a unified and transferable semantic space. This question is fundamentally challenging, as the two branches operate over heterogeneous representations (text tokens vs.\ visual latents) and distinct training objectives, making direct comparison difficult. To address this, we introduce \emph{cross-branch semantic steering}, an intervention-based framework that extracts semantic directions from one branch and applies them to the other. We show that steering vectors learned from the understanding branch can transfer to generation, enabling controllable image synthesis and improved semantic faithfulness. In contrast, the reverse direction consistently shows limited effectiveness. Our analysis suggests that this asymmetry may be related to a practical representational mismatch: understanding-derived vectors capture transferable, object-centric semantics, while generation-derived vectors primarily encode low-level appearance features. Our results reveal that architectural unification does not guarantee semantic alignment, and establish cross-branch steering as a practical tool for probing multimodal representations.

中文摘要翻译: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）旨在将理解与生成能力集成在单一架构中，然而这两类能力是否共享统一且可迁移的语义空间仍不清楚。这一问题本质上具有挑战性，因为两个分支在异构表征（文本token vs. 视觉潜在变量）和不同的训练目标上运作，使得直接比较十分困难。为解决此问题，我们提出了跨分支语义引导（cross-branch semantic steering），一种基于干预的框架，从一个分支提取语义方向并将其应用于另一个分支。我们发现，从理解分支学到的引导向量可以迁移到生成分支，实现可控的图像合成并提升语义忠实度。相反，反向迁移的效果持续有限。我们的分析表明，这种不对称性可能与实际表征不匹配有关：理解分支的向量捕获可迁移的、以对象为中心的语义，而生成分支的向量主要编码低层外观特征。我们的结果揭示，架构统一并不保证语义对齐，并将跨分支语义引导确立为探测多模态表征的一种实用工具。

</details>


### [22] [When Fish Look Alike: Tracking Identities with Dual-branch Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26412)
*Vran Lee,Xin Liu,Yijie Wei,Yeqiang Liu,Hwa Liang Leo,Zhenbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对密集同质目标（如鱼群）多目标跟踪中外观特征退化、计算开销大的问题，提出TIDE（双分支弹性身份追踪），利用自适应几何对应IoU替代昂贵的外观特征，并引入系统级部署弹性，实现轻量级（20.47G FLOPs，HOTA 28.43）与可扩展（HOTA 29.98）双分支，相比SU-T降低38.7倍计算量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于检测和重识别（Re-ID）的跟踪器（如SU-T）虽精度高，但计算开销大，无法部署到边缘设备；且在严重遮挡下外观特征退化，导致模块失效。需一种轻量、鲁棒的跟踪方案。

Method: 提出TIDE框架，核心包括：（1）自适应几何对应IoU（Adaptive Geometric Correspondence IoU），利用空间和结构一致性进行关联，无需昂贵的外观特征；（2）系统级部署弹性，解耦算法流水线与硬件约束；（3）双分支设计：轻量L分支用于边缘部署，可扩展S分支用于高精度云分析。

Result: 在MFT-Edge基准上，轻量L分支以20.47G FLOPs实现HOTA 28.43，相比SU-T降低38.7倍计算量；可扩展S分支达到HOTA 29.98，弥合了高精度云分析与高效边缘跟踪之间的差距。

Conclusion: TIDE通过空间-结构一致性关联机制和弹性部署设计，在极低计算开销下实现了竞争力的跟踪精度，为密集同质目标的边缘端实时跟踪提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Tracking dense, homogeneous targets like schooling fish remains a major challenge for multiple object tracking due to extreme inter-individual homogeneity, severe physical clustering, and rapid non-rigid deformations. While heavy-backbone separated detection and embedding trackers like SU-T push accuracy boundaries using complex Re-Identification networks, their computational overhead prohibits edge deployment. Furthermore, these modules often fail when appearance features degrade under severe occlusions. To overcome this, we propose Tracking Identities with Dual-branch Elasticity (TIDE). Bypassing expensive appearance cues, TIDE utilizes the Adaptive Geometric Correspondence IoU, an association mechanism leveraging spatial and structural consistency to robustly handle complex morphological variations. Crucially, TIDE introduces system-level deployment elasticity, decoupling the algorithmic pipeline from strict hardware constraints. Evaluations on the MFT-Edge benchmark demonstrate that our Lightweight L-branch achieves a competitive HOTA of 28.43 using merely 20.47G FLOPs. This represents a 38.7-fold computational reduction compared to upper bounds like SU-T, directly facilitating real-time edge deployment. Concurrently, our Scalable S-branch establishes a 29.98 HOTA, successfully bridging the gap between high-precision cloud analysis and efficient edge tracking. The dataset and codes are released at https://vranlee.github.io/TIDE/.

中文摘要翻译: 跟踪密集、同质的目标（如鱼群）仍然是多目标跟踪中的重大挑战，原因在于个体间极端同质性、严重的物理聚集以及快速的非刚性形变。尽管像SU-T这类采用重型骨干网络分离检测与嵌入的跟踪器通过复杂的重识别网络提升了精度边界，但其计算开销阻碍了边缘部署。此外，当外观特征在严重遮挡下退化时，这些模块常常失效。为解决此问题，我们提出了双分支弹性身份追踪（TIDE）。TIDE绕过昂贵的外观线索，利用自适应几何对应IoU——一种利用空间和结构一致性来稳健处理复杂形态变化的关联机制。关键的是，TIDE引入了系统级部署弹性，将算法流水线与严格的硬件约束解耦。在MFT-Edge基准上的评估表明，我们的轻量L分支仅用20.47G FLOPs就达到了具有竞争力的HOTA 28.43。与SU-T等上限方法相比，这实现了38.7倍的计算量降低，直接促进了实时边缘部署。同时，我们的可扩展S分支建立了29.98的HOTA，成功弥合了高精度云分析与高效边缘跟踪之间的差距。数据集和代码已发布在https://vranlee.github.io/TIDE/。

</details>


### [23] [FAS-R1: A Unified Multi-Task MLLM for Reasoning Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26432)
*Hongyang Wang,Yichen Shi,Hongrui Li,Yiru Huo,Jun Feng,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FAS-R1，一种两阶段推理导向的MLLM框架，用于人脸防伪检测。通过高质量长思维链数据集冷启动微调，再结合退化模拟增强和难度感知GRPO后训练，在真实性分类、攻击类型识别和伪冒区域定位上均取得优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有判别式FAS模型大多以标签为中心，而基于MLLM的方法虽能输出结构化结果，但仍主要依赖监督微调，往往产生模板化的推理过程且对困难攻击优化不足。因此亟需一种能提供丰富语义解释并优化困难样本的推理导向FAS框架。

Method: FAS-R1采用两阶段训练：1）使用FAS-R1-23K高质量长思维链数据集进行冷启动监督微调；2）进行FAS专用的GRPO后训练，其中包含退化模拟增强（DSA）以提升视觉质量变化下的稳定推理能力，以及难度感知GRPO（DA-GRPO）以缓解易样本主导问题，尤其针对化妆、口罩等细微或模糊攻击进行优化。

Result: 3B参数FAS-R1模型在域内任务上达到98.75%真实性准确率、93.33%攻击类型准确率、96.30/94.73% AP@40/AP@50；在跨域真实性泛化和推理质量上均优于对比系统；不同基模型实验展现了良好的扩展性。

Conclusion: FAS-R1通过两阶段推理导向训练范式，有效提升了人脸防伪检测的语义解释能力和困难攻击优化效果，在域内和跨域场景下均表现出色，具有良好的可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Face anti-spoofing (FAS) is increasingly expected to provide not only bona fide/spoof decisions, but also attack semantics and image-grounded evidence for human inspection. Existing discriminative FAS models remain largely label-centric, while recent MLLM-based methods offer structured outputs but still rely mainly on supervised fine-tuning, often producing template-like rationales and weak optimization for difficult attacks. We propose FAS-R1, a two-stage reasoning-oriented MLLM framework for unified FAS prediction, covering authenticity classification, attack-type recognition and spoof-region localization. FAS-R1 first uses FAS-R1-23K, a high-quality long-CoT dataset, for cold-start supervised fine-tuning, and then performs FAS-specific GRPO post-training. Degradation-Simulated Augmentation (DSA) encourages stable spoof-cue reasoning across visual-quality shifts, while Difficulty-Aware GRPO (DA-GRPO) mitigates easy-sample dominance that may leave difficult task--attack groups under-optimized, especially for subtle or ambiguous attacks such as makeup and mask attacks. The main 3B FAS-R1 model achieves 98.75\% authenticity accuracy, 93.33\% attack-type accuracy, and 96.30/94.73\% AP@40/AP@50 in-domain. It also outperforms the compared systems in cross-domain authenticity generalization and answer-and-rationale quality. Experiments with different base models further show favorable scaling behavior. The code will be released soon.

中文摘要翻译: 人脸防伪检测（FAS）日益要求不仅提供真实/伪造的二分类判决，还需提供攻击语义和基于图像的证据以供人工审查。现有的判别式FAS模型主要仍以标签为中心，而近期基于MLLM的方法虽能输出结构化结果，但仍主要依赖监督微调，往往产生模板化的推理过程，且对困难攻击的优化不足。本文提出FAS-R1，一种两阶段推理导向的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）框架，用于统一的FAS预测，涵盖真实性分类、攻击类型识别和伪冒区域定位。FAS-R1首先利用FAS-R1-23K——一个高质量的长思维链数据集——进行冷启动监督微调，然后进行FAS专用的GRPO后训练。退化模拟增强（DSA）有助于在视觉质量变化下保持稳定的伪冒线索推理，而难度感知GRPO（DA-GRPO）则缓解了易样本主导问题，避免了困难任务-攻击组（尤其是化妆、口罩等细微或模糊攻击）欠优化。FAS-R1主模型（3B参数）在域内任务上达到98.75%的真实性准确率、93.33%的攻击类型准确率以及96.30/94.73%的AP@40/AP@50。在跨域真实性泛化和回答与推理质量方面，该模型也优于对比系统。基于不同基模型的实验进一步展示了良好的扩展性。代码将很快开源。

</details>


### [24] [Interpretable Image-Level Acne Severity Grading via EfficientNet-B0 Transfer Learning and Grad-CAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26461)
*Sophie Zeng,Sean Kalaycioglu,Collin Hong,Haipeng Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文基于ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet-B0模型，通过迁移学习在ACNE04数据集上开发了一个四类痤疮严重程度分类器（基于Hayashi标准），在15%测试集上达到93.5%准确率和94.4%宏F1分数，并提供完整的开源实现（PyTorch和MATLAB）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有痤疮严重程度分级依赖人工评估（如IGA或Hayashi标准），存在评估者间变异大和成像条件不一致的问题，亟需一个准确、可重复的自动化分级方法。

Method: 使用ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet-B0作为骨干网络进行迁移学习；在公开ACNEE04数据集（2983张标注图像）上微调；采用AdamW优化器、几何与光度数据增强、基于验证集宏F1的检查点选择策略；提供Grad-CAM可视化解释；同时提供PyTorch/timm和MATLAB R2026a的开源实现。

Result: 在分层15%测试集上：准确率93.5%，宏F1分数94.4%，各类别F1分数0.92-0.97；83%的误差发生在相邻等级之间；二次加权Cohen's kappa为0.956（95% CI: 0.935-0.973）；Bootstrap置信区间表明性能稳定；Grad-CAM热图聚焦于前额、脸颊和下巴等临床相关区域。

Conclusion: 轻量级迁移学习能够实现准确、均衡且可解释的痤疮严重程度自动分级，且提供可复现的跨平台开源参考实现，为未来的前瞻性和设备分层临床验证奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Acne vulgaris affects most adolescents and many adults. Accurate severity grading guides treatment, monitoring, and clinical trial endpoints, but manual assessment using the Investigator's Global Assessment or Hayashi criteria is limited by inter-rater variability and inconsistent imaging conditions. We developed a four-class acne severity classifier based on the Hayashi criteria using transfer learning with an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B0 model. The model was fine-tuned on the public ACNE04 dataset of 2,983 labeled images using AdamW optimization, geometric and photometric augmentation, and checkpoint selection based on validation macro-F1. On a held-out stratified 15 percent test set, the classifier achieved 93.5 percent accuracy and 94.4 percent macro-F1, with per-class F1 scores from 0.92 to 0.97. Eighty-three percent of errors occurred between adjacent grades. Quadratic-weighted Cohen's kappa was 0.956, with a 95 percent confidence interval of 0.935 to 0.973. Bootstrap confidence intervals indicated stable performance. Grad-CAM visualizations from the final convolutional block focused on clinically relevant facial regions, including the forehead, cheeks, and chin. The complete pipeline is provided as functionally equivalent open-source implementations in Python using PyTorch and timm, and in MATLAB R2026a. The software includes a clinician-facing inference interface and a fallback backbone option that supports operation without specialized pretrained-weight packages. These results show that lightweight transfer learning can provide accurate, balanced, and interpretable acne severity grading while offering a reproducible cross-platform reference for future prospective and device-stratified clinical validation.

中文摘要翻译: 寻常痤疮影响大多数青少年和许多成年人。准确的严重程度分级指导治疗、监测和临床试验终点，但使用研究者全球评估或Hayashi标准的手工评估受限于评估者间变异和不一致的成像条件。我们基于Hayashi标准，利用ImageNet预训练的EfficientNet-B0模型进行迁移学习，开发了一个四类痤疮严重程度分类器。该模型在包含2983张标注图像的公开ACNEE04数据集上，采用AdamW优化、几何与光度数据增强以及基于验证集宏F1的检查点选择策略进行微调。在分层保留的15%测试集上，分类器达到了93.5%的准确率和94.4%的宏F1分数，各类别F1分数在0.92到0.97之间。83%的误差发生在相邻等级之间。二次加权Cohen's kappa为0.956，95%置信区间为0.935至0.973。Bootstrap置信区间表明性能稳定。来自最后一个卷积块的Grad-CAM可视化聚焦于临床相关的面部区域，包括前额、脸颊和下巴。完整的流程以功能等效的开源实现形式提供，包括基于PyTorch和timm的Python版本以及MATLAB R2026a版本。该软件包含面向临床医生的推理界面和一个后备骨干网络选项，支持无需专用预训练权重包即可运行。这些结果表明，轻量级迁移学习可以提供准确、均衡且可解释的痤疮严重程度分级，同时为未来的前瞻性和设备分层临床验证提供可复现的跨平台参考。

</details>


### [25] [HERMES: A Hybrid Ensemble for Head-and-Neck Tumor Segmentation, TN Staging, and Recurrence-Free Survival on PET/CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26498)
*Kai Wang,Meixu Chen,Elie Nasr,Ryan Lanning,Moyed Miften*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HERMES是一个容器化算法，针对HECKTOR 2026三项子任务（GTVp/GTVn肿瘤分割、T/N分期、无复发生存预测），采用STU-Net Small集成实现分割，从预测掩膜提取紧凑几何特征用于分期（N分期平衡准确率提升+0.030），并利用一致性追踪生存损失训练的深度专家与临床专家等权集成进行预后预测，在验证集上取得加权得分0.6454。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决头颈癌放疗中从FDG-PET/CT和电子健康记录进行肿瘤分割、T/N分期和复发预测的完整自动化流程，并改进传统基于通用放射组学的分期特征效率，设计更紧凑、与AJCC/UICC分期标准对齐的几何特征。

Method: 1) 分割：10折STU-Net Small集成；2) 分期：从预测掩膜提取与AJCC/UICC 7版标准对齐的紧凑几何特征（大小和数量），替代通用放射组学向量；3) 预后：等权集成深度专家与临床风险专家，深度专家使用自研的一致性追踪生存损失函数（CTC生存损失）进行训练。所有组件通过正则化导向协议在袋外预测上选择，无公共验证集调优。

Result: 内部交叉验证中N分期平衡准确率从0.691提升至0.720（+0.030），特征维度更低。HECKTOR 2026验证排行榜上加权得分0.6454（平均Dice 0.641，T分期平衡准确率0.580，N分期平衡准确率0.642，RFS C-index 0.679）。

Conclusion: 基于紧凑几何特征的分期方法和一致性追踪生存损失函数有效提升了模型性能，验证了针对任务定制特征和损失函数优于通用方法的思路，HERMES成功晋级测试阶段。

Original Abstract: We present HERMES (Hybrid Ensemble for Radiotherapy-target segmentation, Malignancy staging, and Event-free Survival), a single containerized algorithm for the three HECKTOR 2026 subtasks: segmentation of the primary tumor (GTVp) and pathological lymph nodes (GTVn), radiological T/N staging, and recurrence-free survival (RFS), computed from a paired FDG-PET/CT scan and an electronic health record. A 10-fold ensemble of STU-Net Small networks produces the segmentation; the predicted mask then drives two downstream tasks. Rather than pass a generic radiomics vector to the staging models, we derive from the predicted masks a compact set of geometry features aligned with the size and number axes of AJCC/UICC 7th-edition radiological N/T staging. On internal cross-validation these features raise N-stage balanced accuracy from 0.691 to 0.720 (+0.030), our largest single design gain, at lower feature dimensionality. For prognosis we combine complementary deep and clinical risk experts in an equal-weight ensemble, and train one deep expert with a concordance-tracking survival loss of our own, whose value approximates the concordance index during training. Every component was selected on honest out-of-fold predictions under a regularization-oriented protocol, with no tuning on the public validation set, and deployed as two decorrelated submissions. On the HECKTOR 2026 validation leaderboard, HERMES achieved a weighted score of 0.6454 (Mean Dice 0.641, T balanced accuracy 0.580, N balanced accuracy 0.642, RFS C-index 0.679) and qualified for the testing phase. Team: AMC_HNC.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了HERMES（Hybrid Ensemble for Radiotherapy-target segmentation, Malignancy staging, and Event-free Survival），一个用于HECKTOR 2026三项子任务（原发性肿瘤GTVp和病理淋巴结GTVn的分割、放射学T/N分期、无复发生存RFS预测）的单一容器化算法，算法输入为配对的FDG-PET/CT扫描和电子健康记录。一个10折的STU-Net Small网络集成产生分割结果；预测的掩膜随后驱动两个下游任务。我们并非将通用的放射组学向量传递给分期模型，而是从预测掩膜中推导出一组紧凑的几何特征，这些特征与AJCC/UICC第7版放射学N/T分期的尺寸和数量轴线对齐。在内部交叉验证中，这些特征以更低的特征维度将N分期平衡准确率从0.691提升到0.720（+0.030），这是我们最大的单一设计增益。对于预后预测，我们将互补的深度专家和临床风险专家以等权集成的方式结合，并使用我们自己设计的一致性追踪生存损失函数训练一个深度专家，该损失函数的值在训练期间近似一致性指数。每个组件都是在正则化导向的协议下，基于诚实的袋外预测进行选择，未在公共验证集上调优，并作为两个去相关提交部署。在HECKTOR 2026验证排行榜上，HERMES取得了加权得分0.6454（平均Dice 0.641，T平衡准确率0.580，N平衡准确率0.642，RFS C-index 0.679），并晋级测试阶段。团队：AMC_HNC。

</details>


### [26] [Semantic-Aware Temporal Adaptation for UAV Anti-UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26511)
*Xiaozhen Qiao,Da Zhang,Yubin Guo,Junyu Gao,Zhiyuan Zhao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SATATrack框架，通过语义感知上下文传播（SACP）和时序感知分布对齐（TADA）解决无人机反无人机跟踪中双动态场景下的外观匹配难题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无人机反无人机跟踪中，相机平台和目标同时运动（双动态），导致视角快速变化、运动模糊、尺度变化和视觉相似干扰，固定视觉表示难以应对快速变化的目标外观和特征分布偏移。文本描述在帧间保持稳定，可作为语义锚点用于时序状态传播，而在线特征分布对齐可减少视频特定的测试时偏移。

Method: 提出SATATrack框架，包含两个核心模块：1）语义感知上下文传播（SACP）：利用目标文本描述引导跨骨干网络阶段的时序上下文传播，在快速外观变化下保持目标身份；训练时使用辅助对比正则化器抑制对语义相似背景区域的响应。2）时序感知分布对齐（TADA）：推理时无需更新模型参数，在线对齐特征分布，结合近期帧估计与训练时统计量以保持稳定性。

Result: 在UAV-Anti-UAV基准上达到最先进性能，同时在Anti-UAV和UAV目标跟踪任务上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用语义描述作为时序传播的锚点并结合在线分布对齐，能有效应对双动态场景下的跟踪挑战，验证了语义信息在极端动态环境下的稳定性优势。

Original Abstract: UAV Anti-UAV tracking is an emerging low-altitude security task for localizing an adversarial UAV using the onboard camera of a moving observer UAV. It differs from conventional UAV tracking and ground-based Anti-UAV tracking because both the camera platform and the target move simultaneously. This dual-dynamic setting induces rapid viewpoint changes, motion blur, scale variation, and visually similar distractors, making reliable appearance matching difficult. Under such rapidly changing conditions, fixed visual representations are often insufficient because target appearance becomes unreliable and feature distributions may deviate from the training domain. The target language description remains stable across frames and can therefore serve as a semantic anchor for temporal state propagation, while online feature-distribution alignment can reduce video-specific test-time shifts. In this paper, we propose \emph{SATATrack}, a Semantic-Aware Temporal Adaptation framework for UAV Anti-UAV tracking. SATATrack introduces Semantic-Aware Context Propagation (SACP), which uses the target description to guide temporal context propagation across backbone stages and preserve target identity under rapid appearance changes. An auxiliary contrastive regularizer is used during training to discourage responses to semantically similar background regions. During inference, Temporal-Aware Distribution Alignment (TADA) aligns feature distributions online without updating model parameters, combining recent-frame estimates with training-time statistics for stability. SATATrack achieves state-of-the-art performance on the UAV-Anti-UAV benchmark while remaining competitive in Anti-UAV and UAV object tracking tasks. The code will be available at https://github.com/XiaozhenQiao/SATATrack.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机反无人机跟踪是一项新兴的低空安全任务，旨在利用移动观察无人机上的机载摄像头定位敌方无人机。该任务不同于传统无人机跟踪和地面反无人机跟踪，因为相机平台和目标同时运动。这种双动态设置会导致视角快速变化、运动模糊、尺度变化以及视觉上相似的干扰物，使得可靠的外观匹配变得困难。在这种快速变化的条件下，固定的视觉表示往往不足，因为目标外观变得不可靠，特征分布可能偏离训练域。目标语言描述在帧间保持稳定，因此可以作为时序状态传播的语义锚点，而在线特征分布对齐可以减少视频特定的测试时偏移。在本文中，我们提出了SATATrack——一种用于无人机反无人机跟踪的语义感知时序自适应框架。SATATrack引入了语义感知上下文传播（SACP），利用目标描述跨骨干网络阶段引导时序上下文传播，在快速外观变化下保持目标身份。训练时使用辅助对比正则化器来抑制对语义相似背景区域的响应。在推理过程中，时序感知分布对齐（TADA）在不更新模型参数的情况下在线对齐特征分布，将近期帧估计与训练时统计量相结合以保持稳定性。SATATrack在UAV-Anti-UAV基准上实现了最先进的性能，同时在Anti-UAV和UAV目标跟踪任务上保持竞争力。代码将在https://github.com/XiaozhenQiao/SATATrack开源。

</details>


### [27] [EgoSafe: A First-Person Mobile-Captured Benchmark for Visual Safety Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26518)
*Yuyun Chen,Tianao Li,TianQuan Feng,Cen Chen,Huiping Zhuang,Hao Peng,Ziqian Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoSafe-Bench基准，用于评估大视觉语言模型在第一人称视频安全场景中的因果推理能力，发现现有模型存在严重的感知-推理解耦问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉安全理解评估主要依赖第三人称监控视频和二元分类指标，无法暴露LVLMs在动态、部分可观测的第一人称体验中区分表面相关性与真实法医逻辑的认知差距。

Method: 构建EgoSafe-Bench基准，包含12,000个评估样本，由3,000个视频片段与基于分层推理评估(HRE)协议的QA链配对生成。HRE强制模型遵循从特征锚定到盲点推理再到意图推断的严格推理轨迹，惩罚基于捷径的预测。

Result: 对Qwen3-VL、Gemini、VideoLLaMA 3等先进LVLMs的广泛评估揭示了显著的感知-推理解耦现象：模型在描述性得分上表现优异，但在因果推理和逻辑闭合性上表现出明显的脆弱性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个具有挑战性的数据集和系统的评估框架，以促进逻辑鲁棒的视频理解系统的发展。

Original Abstract: Reliable visual safety understanding in real-world scenarios demands more than just object recognition; it requires causal reasoning under epistemic uncertainty. While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate impressive semantic alignment on standard benchmarks, they often struggle to distinguish between superficial correlation and genuine forensic logic when grounded in the dynamic, partially observable nature of first-person experiences. Existing evaluations, dominated by third-person surveillance footage and binary classification metrics, fail to expose this cognitive gap. To address this, we introduce EgoSafe-Bench, a benchmark specifically designed to probe forensic reasoning in egocentric safety scenarios. It comprises 12,000 unique evaluation samples, generated by pairing each of the 3,000 video clips with a QA chain governed by our proposed Hierarchical Reasoning Evaluation (HRE) protocol. Unlike standard benchmarks, HRE mandates a rigorous reasoning trajectory from initial feature anchoring to blind-spot deduction and intent inference, thereby enforcing logical consistency and penalizing shortcut-based predictions.Extensive evaluations of state-of-the-art LVLMs (e.g., Qwen3-VL, Gemini, VideoLLaMA 3) reveal a significant perception-reasoning decoupling: models often achieve high descriptive scores but exhibit notable fragility in causal reasoning and logical closure. Our work provides both a challenging dataset and a systematic evaluation framework to foster the development of logically robust video understanding systems.

中文摘要翻译: 现实场景中可靠的视觉安全理解不仅需要物体识别，还需要在认知不确定性下进行因果推理。尽管大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在标准基准上展现出令人印象深刻的语义对齐能力，但当立足于动态、部分可观测的第一人称体验时，它们往往难以区分表面相关性与真实的法医逻辑。现有的评估大多依赖第三人称监控视频和二元分类指标，未能暴露这一认知差距。为解决此问题，我们提出了EgoSafe-Bench——一个专门设计用于探查第一人称安全场景中法医推理能力的基准。该基准包含12,000个独特的评估样本，每个样本由3,000个视频片段与基于我们提出的分层推理评估（HRE）协议生成的QA链配对而成。与标准基准不同，HRE强制要求从初始特征锚定到盲点推理再到意图推断的严格推理轨迹，从而确保逻辑一致性并惩罚基于捷径的预测。对当前先进LVLMs（如Qwen3-VL、Gemini、VideoLLaMA 3）的广泛评估揭示了显著的感知-推理解耦现象：模型在描述性得分上往往表现优异，但在因果推理和逻辑闭合性上表现出明显的脆弱性。本文工作既提供了一个具有挑战性的数据集，也提供了一个系统的评估框架，以促进逻辑鲁棒的视频理解系统的发展。

</details>


### [28] [CineWeaver: Training-Free Reference-Controllable Multi-Shot Long Video Generation for Cinematic Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26529)
*Yuyang Huang,Yabo Chen,Wenrui Dai,Ziyang Zheng,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Junni Zou,Hongkai Xiong,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CineWeaver，一个无需重新训练的统一框架，通过操纵位置编码和注意力模式打破预训练视频扩散模型中的时间连续性偏差，实现参考可控的多镜头长视频生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到视频扩散模型在同时满足多镜头生成、细粒度角色/场景控制以及长时生成方面存在困难，已有方法依赖定制化和重新训练来分别解决特定需求，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 1) 操纵位置编码和注意力模式，在推理时打破时间连续性以实现清晰的镜头切换；2) 提出镜头路由的参考条件机制实现每个镜头的细粒度控制；3) 开发锚点记忆机制以保持全局外观一致性，支持长视频生成。

Result: 实验结果表明CineWeaver能够生成长时长的高质量电影级视频，具有一致的身份特征、稳定的全局外观和清晰的镜头切换。

Conclusion: CineWeaver是首个无需训练即可同时实现长视频、参考可控和多镜头生成的统一框架，为训练自由范式下的视频生成提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Cinematic video generation is challenging for text-to-video diffusion models due to concurrent requirements on multi-shot generation, fine-grained controllability over characters and scenes, and long-form generation across extended temporal horizons. Existing methods rely on customization and retraining to separately address specific requirements, and cannot simultaneously fulfill all the requirements with a unified framework. In this paper, we shed light on the training-free paradigm with the key insight that the difficulty of multi-shot generation arises from a structural bias toward temporal continuity in pretrained video diffusion models, and consequently, propose a unified framework named CineWeaver to achieve reference-controllable multi-shot long-video generation without retraining. We manipulate positional encoding and attention patterns to break temporal continuity during inference to enable clear shot transitions using pretrained video diffusion models.
