Furthermore, we extend the proposed framework with a shot-routed reference conditioning mechanism for per-shot fine-grained controllability, and develop an anchor memory mechanism to allow long-form generation with consistent global appearance cues. To our best knowledge, CineWeaver is the first unified framework to simultaneously enable \textbf{long-form}, \textbf{reference-controllable}, and \textbf{multi-shot} video generation in a training-free fashion. Experimental results demonstrate that CineWeaver produces high-quality cinematic videos of long durations with consistent identities, stable global appearance, and clear shot transitions. The project page is available at: https://cineweaver.github.io.

中文摘要翻译: 电影级视频生成对文本到视频扩散模型而言极具挑战性，因为它同时要求多镜头生成、对角色和场景的细粒度可控性，以及跨扩展时间范围的长视频生成。现有方法依赖定制化和重新训练来分别解决特定需求，无法用统一框架同时满足所有要求。本文聚焦于训练自由范式，核心洞察在于：多镜头生成的困难源于预训练视频扩散模型对时间连续性的结构性偏差。基于此，我们提出统一框架CineWeaver，无需重新训练即可实现参考可控的多镜头长视频生成。我们通过操纵位置编码和注意力模式，在推理时打破时间连续性，从而利用预训练视频扩散模型实现清晰的镜头切换。此外，我们扩展了该框架，引入镜头路由的参考条件机制以实现每个镜头的细粒度可控性，并开发了锚点记忆机制以保持全局外观一致性，支持长视频生成。据我们所知，CineWeaver是首个以训练自由方式同时实现长视频、参考可控和多镜头生成的统一框架。实验结果表明，CineWeaver能够生成长时长的高质量电影级视频，具有一致的身份特征、稳定的全局外观和清晰的镜头切换。

</details>


### [29] [TPCD: Tone-Pressure Contrastive Decoding and the Label-Free Gating Bottleneck in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26536)
*Jinkun Zhao,Kui Zhang,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语调压力对比解码（TPCD），利用高压提示下VLM产生的logits作为对比解码的负分支来缓解承诺偏差，实验表明该方法能大幅降低攻击成功率，但存在正例准确率下降的问题，进一步通过任务先验/分歧门控机制在保持正例准确率的同时降低ASR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高压提示会迫使视觉语言模型（VLM）做出不合理的承诺（如读取难以辨认的文字、报告不确定的时间、确认不存在的物体），本文旨在探究是否能将压力诱导出的分布本身作为对比解码的负分支，从而缓解这种承诺偏差。

Method: 提出语调压力对比解码（TPCD），从安全中性指令下的logits中减去高压指令下的logits；并设计基于任务先验/分歧的门控机制，以及基于类别无关的回答分歧路由器来改进性能。

Result: 在tone-matters基准测试（800例）上，LLaVA-1.5-7B在高压下ASR为66.75%，安全中性降为9.88%，TPCD降至0.50%但正例准确率仅15.56%；加入门控后正例准确率恢复至54.44%，ASR降至1.63%。在GLM-4.6V和Llama-3.2-Vision的留出集上，回答分歧路由器将聚合ASR降至6.93%，优于安全中性（10.98%）和分支分歧（9.67%），正例准确率达79.94%。

Conclusion: 压力是探测承诺偏差和提供缓解信号的有效手段，但当前的门控机制尚未成为独立验证的、基于接地感知的检测器，仍需进一步研究。

Original Abstract: High-pressure prompts can push vision-language models (VLMs) into unsupported commitments, such as reading illegible text, reporting indeterminate times, or affirming absent objects. This paper asks whether the pressure-induced distribution itself can serve as a contrastive-decoding negative branch. Tone-pressure contrastive decoding (TPCD) subtracts logits produced under a high-pressure instruction from logits produced under a safe neutral instruction. On the 800-example tone-matters benchmark, LLaVA-1.5-7B under pressure reaches 66.75% attack success rate (ASR); safe neutralization reduces ASR to 9.88%; full TPCD reaches 0.50% but collapses positives to 15.56%. A benchmark-specific task-prior/disagreement gate preserves measured positive accuracy (54.44%) while lowering ASR to 1.63% on LLaVA. Treating this LLaVA analysis as the design split, full $n=800$ negative and $n=780$ matched-positive held-out runs on GLM-4.6V and Llama-3.2-Vision show that simple gates can improve over safe neutralization, with sensitivity analyses bounding the weak time-positive subtask. A category-prior-free answer-disagreement router reduces held-out aggregate ASR to 6.93%, improving over both safe neutralization (10.98%) and branch disagreement (9.67%) while matching branch disagreement's 79.94% positive accuracy, although it remains post-hoc and surface-form based. We conclude that pressure is a useful probe of commitment bias and a viable mitigation signal, but the current gates are not yet independently validated grounding-aware detectors.

中文摘要翻译: 高压提示可以迫使视觉语言模型（VLM）做出不合理的承诺，例如读取难以辨认的文字、报告不确定的时间或确认不存在的物体。本文探讨压力诱导出的分布本身是否可以作为对比解码的负分支。语调压力对比解码（TPCD）从安全中性指令下的logits中减去高压指令下的logits。在包含800个样本的tone-matters基准测试上，LLaVA-1.5-7B在压力下攻击成功率达到66.75%；安全中性处理将ASR降至9.88%；完整TPCD将ASR降至0.50%，但正例准确率也崩溃至15.56%。基于基准特定任务先验/分歧的门控机制在将ASR降至1.63%的同时，保持了可测量的正例准确率（54.44%）。将LLaVA分析视为设计划分，在GLM-4.6V和Llama-3.2-Vision上进行的全部n=800负例和n=780匹配正例的留出运行显示，简单的门控机制可以优于安全中性处理，敏感性分析界定了薄弱的时间正子任务。基于类别无关注的回答分歧路由器将留出集上的聚合ASR降至6.93%，优于安全中性处理（10.98%）和分支分歧（9.67%），同时匹配了分支分歧的79.94%正例准确率，尽管它仍然是事后性的且基于表层形式。我们得出结论：压力是探测承诺偏差的有效探针和可行的缓解信号，但当前的门控机制尚未成为独立验证的、基于接地感知的检测器。

</details>


### [30] [From Spatial Semantics to Temporal Context: Leveraging Gaze Trajectory for Weakly Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26542)
*Shaoxuan Wu,Xiao Zhang,Xiaodi Zhao,Yunzhi Tian,Yilin Tang,Jun Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TrailNet，利用眼动追踪中的注视点与轨迹进行医学图像分割，通过轨迹引导的时空编码器建模时间上下文、多尺度不确定性解码器处理噪声，并引入循环蒸馏实现无眼动推断。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学图像分割依赖耗时费力的像素级标注，眼动追踪虽可提供低成本解决方案，但有效建模轨迹中的时间上下文以及处理探索性注视带来的噪声仍是挑战。

Method: 提出TrailNet，包含三部分：1）轨迹引导的时空编码器，建模时间上下文并与图像空间语义互补交互；2）多尺度不确定性解码器，利用类别互斥约束产生确定性预测并抑制噪声；3）循环蒸馏策略，通过师生网络传递特征级知识，实现无眼动推理。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上，TrailNet的Dice得分分别达到81.25%和81.85%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合利用注视点与轨迹，TrailNet有效建模时空上下文并抑制噪声，显著提升了眼动监督下的医学图像分割性能。

Original Abstract: Medical image segmentation heavily depends on labor-intensive and time-consuming pixel-level annotations. Eye tracking offers a cost-effective solution that can be naturally integrated into clinical workflows. Recorded by eye trackers, gaze conveys the spatial regions of clinicians' attention through fixations and the temporal context of clinicians' progressive visual perception from trajectories. Nevertheless, effective modeling of temporal trajectories remains challenging, and noise in gaze caused by exploratory fixations greatly limits segmentation performance. To overcome these limitations, we propose the Trajectory-guided Uncertainty-aware Network (TrailNet), which exploits gaze-supervised medical image segmentation from spatial semantics modeling to temporal context by jointly leveraging fixations and trajectories. Specifically, the proposed trajectory-guided spatio-temporal encoder models temporal context and establishes complementary interactions with image spatial semantics to strengthen target perception. Furthermore, the multi-scale uncertainty decoder leverages category mutual-exclusivity constraints to produce deterministic predictions and mitigate supervision uncertainty induced by noise. To enable gaze-free inference, we further introduce a cycle distillation strategy that transfers feature-level knowledge via teacher-student networks. Experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that TrailNet outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving Dice scores of 81.25% and 81.85%, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 医学图像分割严重依赖劳动密集且耗时的像素级标注。眼动追踪提供了一种成本效益高且可自然融入临床工作流程的解决方案。通过眼动仪记录的注视点通过注视区域传达临床医生的空间注意力，而轨迹则反映临床医生渐进视觉感知的时间上下文。然而，时间轨迹的有效建模仍具挑战性，且探索性注视造成的眼动噪声极大限制了分割性能。为克服这些局限，我们提出轨迹引导的不确定性感知网络（TrailNet），通过联合利用注视点与轨迹，从空间语义建模扩展到时间上下文，来挖掘眼动监督下的医学图像分割。具体而言，所提出的轨迹引导时空编码器建模时间上下文，并与图像空间语义建立互补交互以增强目标感知。此外，多尺度不确定性解码器利用类别互斥约束产生确定性预测，并抑制噪声导致的监督不确定性。为实现无眼动推理，我们进一步引入循环蒸馏策略，通过师生网络传递特征级知识。两个公开数据集上的实验结果表明，TrailNet优于现有方法，Dice得分分别达到81.25%和81.85%。

</details>


### [31] [MedARC: Training-Free Adaptive Redundancy Compression of Visual Tokens for 3D Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26554)
*Yitao Zhu,Mengjun Liu,Yingji Fu,Haowen Pang,Anqi Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedARC提出了一种无需训练的自适应冗余压缩框架，通过整合三种互补线索（自注意力、视觉-文本相似度、局部特征偏差）对3D医学视觉语言模型中的视觉令牌进行重要性评估，并采用保留重要令牌、合并冗余令牌的策略，在压缩视觉令牌数量和推理时间的同时保持或提升诊断性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D医学视觉语言模型面临视觉令牌序列过长的问题，而现有压缩方法采用统一降采样或依赖单一重要性信号，容易移除与临床查询相关或结构独特的区域，亟需一种更精细的自适应压缩方法。

Method: MedARC是一个无需训练的自适应冗余压缩框架，通过三种互补线索评估令牌重要性：VLM视觉编码器的自注意力（反映模型视觉焦点）、投影视觉令牌与文本嵌入的相似度（识别查询相关区域）、局部视觉基础模型特征与体素级特征中心的偏差（突出结构独特解剖区域）。之后采用显著性感知的合并策略，保留信息丰富的令牌同时合并冗余令牌。

Result: 在CT-RATE和MR-RATE数据集上的实验表明，MedARC能够减少视觉令牌开销和推理时间，同时保持或提升诊断性能。多线索评分的计算成本被处理更少令牌所节省的成本所抵消，且在大语言模型上收益更大。

Conclusion: MedARC通过多线索自适应压缩有效解决了3D医学VLM中视觉令牌冗余问题，兼具无需训练、诊断性能保持或提升、推理加速等优势，尤其在大语言模型场景下收益更为显著。

Original Abstract: Integrating 3D medical images with vision-language models (VLMs) holds substantial promise for computer-aided diagnosis. However, volumetric images generate prohibitively long visual-token sequences with considerable spatial and inter-slice redundancy. Existing token compression methods typically apply uniform reduction or rely on a single importance signal, increasing the risk of removing regions that are clinically relevant to the query or structurally distinctive. To address this limitation, we propose MedARC, a unified, training-free framework for Adaptive Redundancy Compression of visual tokens in 3D medical VLMs. MedARC estimates token importance by integrating three complementary cues: self-attention from the VLM vision encoder, which reflects the model's intrinsic visual focus; similarity between projected visual tokens and text embeddings, which identifies query-relevant regions; and deviations of local visual foundation model features from the volume-level feature center, which highlight structurally distinctive anatomy. The resulting importance distribution guides a saliency-aware merging strategy that preserves informative tokens while consolidating redundant ones rather than simply discarding them. Experiments on CT-RATE and MR-RATE show that MedARC reduces visual-token overhead and inference time while preserving or improving diagnostic performance. Its multi-cue scoring cost is outweighed by the savings from processing fewer tokens, with greater benefits expected for larger language models.

中文摘要翻译: 将3D医学图像与视觉语言模型（VLM）相结合对计算机辅助诊断具有重要前景。然而，体素图像会产生过长的视觉令牌序列，且存在显著的空间冗余和切片间冗余。现有的令牌压缩方法通常采用统一降采样或依赖单一重要性信号，增加了移除与查询临床相关或结构独特区域的风险。为解决这一局限性，我们提出MedARC，一个统一的、无需训练的自适应冗余压缩框架，用于3D医学VLM中的视觉令牌压缩。MedARC通过整合三种互补线索来估计令牌重要性：VLM视觉编码器的自注意力（反映模型的内在视觉焦点）、投影视觉令牌与文本嵌入之间的相似度（识别查询相关区域）、以及局部视觉基础模型特征与体素级特征中心的偏差（突出结构独特的解剖区域）。由此产生的重要性分布引导一种显著性感知的合并策略，该策略在保留信息丰富令牌的同时合并冗余令牌，而非简单丢弃。在CT-RATE和MR-RATE上的实验表明，MedARC在减少视觉令牌开销和推理时间的同时，保持或提升了诊断性能。其多线索评分的计算成本被处理更少令牌所节省的成本所抵消，且在大语言模型上预期收益更大。

</details>


### [32] [Representation Trajectories Matters: Complementary Evidence for OOD Detection and Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26565)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Hamed Damirchi,Stephen Gould,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过分析视觉模型各层之间的计算路径（而非仅关注最终表示），发现路径中保留了最终表示丢弃的证据信息，这些信息对OOD检测和图像分类任务具有实用价值。基于过渡惊异度和冻结更新探针的方法在多个任务上取得了显著改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常将模型的中间层视为独立的快照，忽略了层间变换过程中样本身份信息的连续性。作者质疑最终表示是否丢弃了计算路径中的有用证据，并探索这些被丢弃的信息能否提升OOD检测和图像分类性能。

Method: 保留样本跨深度的身份信息，研究相邻状态之间的变换。将类一致传输与输入特定创新分离，将协调运动与关系重组分离。提出基于ID数据的过渡惊异度（transition-surprise）分数用于OOD检测，以及冻结更新探针（frozen update probes）用于图像分类。

Result: 1) 路径展现出强样本特异性连续性和跨数据集重复的架构特异性深度轮廓。2) 过渡惊异度分数在OpenOOD网格的131/152次非饱和比较中降低了FPR95，对视觉破坏性和语义远距离偏移增益最大，近OOD检测也保持正向效果。3) 冻结更新探针改进了71/72个干净模型-数据集案例，偏移数据上的增益因架构和腐败类型而异。

Conclusion: 计算路径提供了一种广泛适用的可靠性信号，其价值由模型组织结构和所遇到的数据偏移共同决定。

Original Abstract: Vision models do not form a representation at once; each block revises it. We ask whether the resulting computation path contains evidence that the final representation discards, and whether that evidence improves OOD detection and image classification on clean and shifted data. Unlike approaches that treat intermediate layers as separate snapshots, we retain sample identity across depth and study the transformations connecting successive states. We separate class-coherent transport from input-specific innovation, and coordinate movement from relational reorganization. Across supervised, self-supervised, vision--language, hierarchical, and convolutional encoders, these paths show strong sample-specific continuity and architecture-specific depth profiles that recur across datasets. They are also practically useful. An ID-only transition-surprise score complements strong final-state detectors, reducing FPR95 in 131/152 non-saturated comparisons on a balanced OpenOOD grid; gains are largest for visually disruptive and semantically far shifts, and remain positive on near-OOD for most detectors. Frozen update probes improve 71/72 clean model--dataset cases, while shifted-data gains vary with architecture and corruption type. Computation paths therefore provide a broadly useful reliability signal whose value is determined jointly by model organization and the shift encountered.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉模型并非一次性形成表征；每个模块都会对其进行修正。我们探究由此产生的计算路径是否包含被最终表征丢弃的证据，以及这些证据是否能提升在干净数据和偏移数据上的OOD检测与图像分类性能。与将中间层视为独立快照的方法不同，我们保留样本随深度变化的身份信息，并研究连接连续状态的变换。我们将类一致传输与输入特定创新分离，将协调运动与关系重组分离。在有监督、自监督、视觉-语言、层级和卷积编码器中，这些路径展现出强的样本特异性连续性和跨数据集重复的架构特异性深度轮廓。它们也具有实用价值。基于ID数据的过渡惊异度分数补充了强最终状态检测器，在平衡OpenOOD网格上的131/152次非饱和比较中降低了FPR95；增益在视觉破坏性和语义远距离偏移中最大，且在近OOD检测中对大多数检测器保持正值。冻结更新探针改进了71/72个干净模型-数据集案例，而偏移数据上的增益因架构和腐败类型而异。因此，计算路径提供了一种广泛适用的可靠性信号，其价值由模型组织结构和所遇到的数据偏移共同决定。

</details>


### [33] [3DGBGS: 3D Granular Ball Gaussian Splatting for Compact Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26578)
*Meng Yang,Shuyin Xia,Dawei Dai,YiWang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DGBGS，一种基于3D颗粒球的高斯泼溅框架，通过自适应颗粒球划分替代固定体素化，实现更紧凑的锚点初始化与局部尺度先验，在减少锚点数量和模型存储的同时保持渲染质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于锚点的3DGS方法通过固定体素化从稀疏SfM点云构建锚点，无法适应空间非均匀的点分布，导致锚点数量、模型紧凑性和渲染质量之间存在权衡。

Method: 将SfM点云自适应划分为3D颗粒球，大球紧凑表示平滑冗余区域、小球保留复杂几何与局部细节；基于此表示提出颗粒球锚点初始化（GBAI）用球心初始化锚点位置，以及颗粒球尺度先验（GBSP）用球半径提供高斯生成的局部尺度先验。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，3DGBGS初始锚点减少37.1%、最终锚点减少10.0%、模型存储平均减少9.8%，同时保持相当的渲染质量。

Conclusion: 将颗粒球计算引入锚点式3DGS，通过自适应颗粒球组织有效解决了固定体素化带来的适应性问题，实现了更紧凑高效的场景表示。

Original Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality real-time novel-view synthesis through explicit Gaussian primitives and differentiable rasterization. 3DGS and Granular Ball Computing (GBC), proposed in 2019, share a natural compatibility in adaptive representation. The efficiency of 3DGS partly stems from a coarse-to-fine and on-demand refinement process that draws on the generation principle of GBC. This connection motivates us to further introduce adaptive granular ball organization into anchor-based 3DGS. Existing anchor-based methods typically construct anchors from sparse SfM point clouds through fixed voxelization, which cannot adequately adapt to spatially non-uniform point distributions and leads to a trade-off among anchor count, model compactness, and rendering quality. To address this issue, we propose 3DGBGS (3D Granular Ball Gaussian Splatting), a compact anchor-based framework for novel-view synthesis. 3DGBGS adaptively partitions SfM point clouds into 3D granular balls, using larger balls to compactly represent smooth and redundant regions and smaller balls to preserve complex geometry and local details. Based on this representation, Granular Ball Anchor Initialization (GBAI) uses granular ball centers to initialize compact anchor positions, while the Granular Ball Scale Prior (GBSP) exploits granular ball radii to provide local scale priors for Gaussian generation. Experiments on four benchmarks show that 3DGBGS reduces initial and final anchors by 37.1% and 10.0%, respectively, and model storage by 9.8% on average, while maintaining comparable rendering quality.

中文摘要翻译: 三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）通过显式高斯基元和可微光栅化实现了高质量实时新视角合成。3DGS与2019年提出的颗粒球计算（GBC）在自适应表示上具有天然兼容性。3DGS的效率部分源于一种由粗到细、按需优化的细化过程，该过程借鉴了GBC的生成原理。这一联系促使我们进一步将自适应颗粒球组织引入基于锚点的3DGS。现有基于锚点的方法通常通过固定体素化从稀疏SfM点云构建锚点，这种方法无法充分适应空间非均匀的点分布，导致锚点数量、模型紧凑性和渲染质量之间需要权衡。为解决该问题，我们提出3DGBGS（3D颗粒球高斯泼溅），一种用于新视角合成的紧凑型锚点式框架。3DGBGS将SfM点云自适应划分为3D颗粒球，用大球紧凑表示平滑冗余区域，用小球保留复杂几何与局部细节。基于该表示，颗粒球锚点初始化（GBAI）利用颗粒球中心初始化紧凑的锚点位置，而颗粒球尺度先验（GBSP）利用颗粒球半径为高斯生成提供局部尺度先验。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，3DGBGS在保持相当渲染质量的同时，初始锚点减少37.1%，最终锚点减少10.0%，模型存储平均减少9.8%。

</details>


### [34] [Classification of Disease from Lungs X-ray Images using VGG16, VGG19 and ResNet50 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26580)
*Nand Lal Yadav,Rajesh Kumar,Satyendra Singh,Sudhakar Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了VGG16、VGG19和ResNet50三种深度学习模型在X射线图像上对肺炎、肺结核、肺癌和正常肺部进行分类的性能，发现ResNet50在准确率和效率上表现最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着呼吸系统疾病病例的增加，迫切需要早期检测和准确诊断的方法。卷积神经网络在影像诊断中显示出良好前景，因此本研究探索深度学习算法用于肺部疾病X射线图像分类的潜力。

Method: 使用VGG16、VGG19和ResNet50三种深度学习模型，在大规模X射线图像数据集上进行训练和分类任务，包括肺炎、肺结核、肺癌和正常肺部四种类别。

Result: 三种模型均取得了良好的分类效果，其中ResNet50在准确率和效率方面优于VGG16和VGG19，表现最佳。

Conclusion: 深度学习模型（尤其是ResNet50）可成功应用于肺部疾病的临床诊断实践中，有助于早期检测和改善患者预后。

Original Abstract: With the increase in the number of cases related to respiratory diseases, there is an urgent need to detect them early and diagnose them accurately. Convolutional neural networks have given promising results when used for diagnosing diseases using imaging tests. In this study, we investigate the potential of applying deep learning algorithms such as VGG16, VGG19, and ResNet50 for classification of lung ailments based on X-ray images. A detailed analysis of the aforementioned models' performances was conducted to assess how well they can classify various types of lung ailments, including pneumonia, tuberculosis, lung cancer, and normal lungs. In order to do that, these deep learning models were trained on a vast amount of X-ray images. The results of our study show that while all three models provide good results, ResNet-50 performs best in comparison with other models due to its efficiency and high level of accuracy. We believe that these deep learning models can be successfully implemented in the practice of diagnosing pulmonary diseases in the future. It helps with early disease detection and improves patient outcomes.

中文摘要翻译: 随着呼吸系统疾病相关病例数量的增加，对其进行早期检测和准确诊断的需求变得十分迫切。卷积神经网络在利用影像学检查诊断疾病方面已展现出令人鼓舞的结果。在本研究中，我们探讨了应用VGG16、VGG19和ResNet50等深度学习算法基于X射线图像对肺部疾病进行分类的潜力。我们对上述模型的性能进行了详细分析，以评估它们对各类肺部疾病（包括肺炎、肺结核、肺癌以及正常肺部）进行分类的能力。为此，这些深度学习模型在大量X射线图像上进行了训练。研究结果表明，虽然三种模型均取得了良好效果，但ResNet50由于效率高、准确率高，与其他模型相比表现最佳。我们认为，这些深度学习模型未来可以成功应用于肺部疾病的临床诊断实践中，有助于疾病的早期检测并改善患者的预后。

</details>


### [35] [Level, Sharpness, and Corpus: Why Zero-Shot OOD Detector Rankings Do Not Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26582)
*Ignacio M. De la Jara,Cristian Rodriguez-Opazo,Stephen Gould,Damith Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现零样本OOD检测器的性能排名在不同部署场景中会发生反转，不存在通用的最优检测器。原因在于检测器依赖的互补证据通道（绝对匹配水平、相对/空间锐度、外部语义覆盖）不可相互还原。为此提出CEG，一种无需OOD样本、辅助语料或学习融合的检测器无关包装器，通过非补偿性融合基础检测器、水平和锐度信息，显著提升了OOD检测的跨域鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 零样本OOD检测器在新部署场景中通常基于基准排名选择，但隐含假设排名最优的检测器能跨域迁移。本文通过大规模控制实验揭示这一假设不成立，检测器排名会跨部署反转，且每种检测器在至少一个领域上FPR95超过80%，需要深入诊断其原因并设计鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出Complementary Evidence Guard (CEG)，一种检测器无关的包装器。其核心是通过非补偿性融合（non-compensatory fusion）保留基础检测器、绝对匹配水平（level）和相对/空间锐度（sharpness）三种互补证据通道，仅使用经验性分布内百分位数进行融合，无需OOD样本、辅助语料库或学习机制。

Result: 通过17个分布内数据集、3个视觉语言模型和7个零样本OOD检测器的可控可迁移性审计，发现检测器排名跨部署反转；每个检测器在至少一个领域上FPR95超过80%。CEG将GL-MCM的FPR95从38.1降至28.8，MCM从42.6降至30.5（家族平衡FPR95）。控制实验表明替换证据通道为熵、logit方差或随机噪声无法复现增益。

Conclusion: 零样本OOD检测器在不同部署场景下排名会反转，不存在通用最优检测器，根源在于互补证据通道不可相互还原。CEG通过保留互补证据的非补偿性融合，有效降低检测器对部署场景的敏感性，无需额外数据或学习即可提升OOD检测的跨域鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Selecting a zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) detector for a new deployment is typically based on benchmark rankings, implicitly assuming that the highest-ranked detector will transfer across domains. We show that this assumption does not hold. Through a controlled portability audit across seventeen in-distribution datasets, three vision-language models, and seven representative zero-shot OOD detectors, we find that detector rankings reverse across deployments, every detector exceeds $80\%$ FPR95 on at least one domain, and the preferred detector depends on both the in-distribution data and the underlying VLM. We trace these reversals to complementary evidence channels in vision-language logits. Corpus-free detectors rely on different combinations of absolute match level and relative or spatial sharpness, while WordNet-based methods additionally depend on external semantic coverage. A simple proposition shows that level and sharpness cannot generally be recovered from one another, explaining why no single detector transfers reliably across deployments. Motivated by this diagnosis, we introduce the Complementary Evidence Guard (CEG), a detector-agnostic wrapper that preserves complementary evidence through a non-compensatory fusion of the base detector, level, and sharpness using only empirical in-distribution percentiles. Controls replacing these channels with entropy, logit variance, or random noise do not reproduce the gains. Without OOD samples, auxiliary corpora, or learned fusion, CEG reduces detector sensitivity and improves GL-MCM from $38.1$ to $28.8$ and MCM from $42.6$ to $30.5$ family-balanced FPR95.

中文摘要翻译: 为新的部署场景选择零样本分布外（OOD）检测器通常基于基准排名，这隐含假设排名最高的检测器能够跨领域迁移。我们发现这一假设并不成立。通过一项涵盖17个分布内数据集、3个视觉语言模型和7种代表性零样本OOD检测器的可控可迁移性审计，我们发现检测器排名会跨部署场景发生反转，每种检测器在至少一个领域上FPR95超过80%，且最优检测器取决于分布内数据及底层视觉语言模型。我们将这些反转追溯至视觉语言logits中的互补证据通道。无语料库检测器依赖于绝对匹配水平与相对或空间锐度的不同组合，而基于WordNet的方法还额外依赖于外部语义覆盖。一个简单的命题表明，水平与锐度通常无法相互还原，这解释了为何没有单一检测器能在不同部署场景中可靠迁移。受此诊断启发，我们提出了互补证据守卫（CEG），一种检测器无关的包装器，通过仅使用经验性分布内百分位数对基础检测器、水平和锐度进行非补偿性融合，以保留互补证据。将上述通道替换为熵、logit方差或随机噪声的控制实验无法复现增益。无需OOD样本、辅助语料库或学习融合，CEG降低了检测器敏感性，并将GL-MCM的家族平衡FPR95从38.1降至28.8，MCM从42.6降至30.5。

</details>


### [36] [R-SLPR: Region-based Small-to-Large Point-cloud Registration with Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26583)
*Yusen Wan,Zeyuan Chen,Qianshi Zou,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向规模不匹配点云配准的区域化小到大配准框架（R-SLPR），通过区域提议、区域匹配和迭代精化三阶段架构，显著提升了从局部小点云到全局大点云的配准精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有经典配准算法在处理几何线索有限、不完整或模糊的小规模源点云与大规模参考点云配准时效果不佳，而现有学习方法通常假设点云尺度和重叠度相当，未充分解决实际部署中常见的小到大规模配准问题。

Method: 提出三阶段架构R-SLPR：1）利用Fibonacci网格分割与对比学习目标生成局部几何块并匹配候选区域；2）通过级联锚点选择与精化算法迭代对齐源点云与目标区域。

Result: 在ModelNet40上达到新的最优精度，位置MAE为0.009，旋转MAE为1.104，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: R-SLPR通过将规模不匹配的配准问题重新表述为区域提议、匹配和迭代精化序列，有效解决了小到大尺度点云配准的挑战，实现了高精度对齐。

Original Abstract: Point-cloud (PC) registration is fundamental to three-dimensional (3D) perception in robotic systems. However, classic registration algorithms falter when aligning a source PC containing limited, incomplete, or ambiguous geometric cues against a reference. This challenge of registering a small, partial PC to a significantly larger global reference is pervasive in real-world deployment yet remains insufficiently addressed by existing learning-based approaches, which typically assume comparable scales and significant overlap. To bridge this gap, we propose the Region-based Small-to-Large Point-cloud Registra- tion framework (R-SLPR), a novel three-stage architecture that fundamentally reformulates the scale-mismatched registration problem into a sequence of region proposal, regional matching, and iterative refinement. Unlike conventional methods that fail to localize specific regions, R-SLPR explicitly identifies candidate regions prior to estimating rigid transformations, ensuring robust alignment even under severe scale mismatch. The framework introduces a Fibonacci Grid Segmentation method coupled with a contrastive learning objective to effectively generate and match local geometric patches. Building on this, a novel Cascade Anchor Selection and Refinement algorithm iteratively aligns the source with the target region to maximize precision. Extensive evaluation on ModelNet40 demonstrates that R-SLPR establishes a new state-of-the-art accuracy standard, outperforming prior approaches and significantly reducing position and rotation Mean Absolute Error (MAE) to 0.009 and 1.104, respectively.

中文摘要翻译: 点云配准是机器人系统中三维感知的基础。然而，当包含有限、不完整或模糊几何线索的源点云与参考点云进行配准时，经典配准算法效果不佳。这种将小规模部分点云与显著更大的全局参考点云进行配准的挑战在实际部署中普遍存在，但现有基于学习的方法通常假设点云具有相当的尺度和显著的重叠，未能充分解决这一问题。为弥补这一差距，我们提出区域化小到大点云配准框架（R-SLPR），这是一种新颖的三阶段架构，从根本上将规模不匹配的配准问题重新表述为区域提议、区域匹配和迭代精化序列。与传统方法无法定位特定区域不同，R-SLPR在估计刚体变换之前明确识别候选区域，确保即使在严重尺度不匹配下也能实现鲁棒对齐。该框架引入了Fibonacci网格分割方法，结合对比学习目标，有效生成和匹配局部几何块。在此基础上，一种新颖的级联锚点选择与精化算法通过迭代对齐源点云与目标区域，最大化配准精度。在ModelNet40上的广泛评估表明，R-SLPR建立了新的最优精度标准，超越先前方法，将位置和旋转平均绝对误差分别显著降低至0.009和1.104。

</details>


### [37] [SpatialQ: Understanding 3D Gaussian Splatting Scene Quality via Visual-based MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26595)
*Jingxuan Su,Shenglin Wang,Tiesong Zhao,Ge Li,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对3D高斯泼溅场景的质量评估问题，提出了一种多模态质量评估框架，通过3D感知质量表示学习和基于多模态大语言模型的推理机制，解决现有方法依赖2D感知线索且不适用于质量回归的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法仅依赖2D感知线索，而通用多模态大语言模型无法稳定地进行质量回归，导致对3DGS场景质量的评估不可靠。因此需要一种能感知3D结构、跨视图一致性等场景级因素的专用质量评估方法。

Method: 1) 提出3D感知质量表示学习框架：基于VGGT编码器增加专用质量头，将多视图图像编码为视图特定特征并聚合以捕获跨视图一致性，同时通过深度和点云结构信息的联合建模融入几何线索。2) 构建基于多模态大语言模型的推理机制：将原始图像、深度图、点云渲染和相机参数联合输入Qwen-based MLLM进行推理。

Result: （原文未提供具体实验结果，仅描述了方法框架。）

Conclusion: 本文提出的多模态质量评估框架通过结合3D感知质量表示学习和多模态大语言模型推理，能够更全面地评估3DGS场景质量，弥补了现有方法的不足。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an effective representation for novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction, creating an increasing demand for reliable quality assessment. Unlike conventional image quality assessment (IQA), the quality of a 3DGS scene depends not only on the perceptual fidelity of rendered views, but also on scene-level factors such as spatial structure and cross-view consistency. Existing IQA methods are limited by their reliance on 2D perceptual cues, whereas general multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are not designed for stable quality regression and may produce unreliable judgments. To address these limitations, a multimodal quality assessment framework is developed for 3DGS scene understanding. First, a 3D-aware quality representation learning framework is introduced by augmenting a VGGT-based encoder with a dedicated quality head. Multi-view images are encoded into view-specific features and aggregated to capture cross-view consistency, while geometric cues are incorporated through joint modeling of depth and point-cloud-related structural information, enabling the learning of structure-aware quality representations beyond appearance-driven features. Second, a grounded multimodal reasoning mechanism is constructed by jointly feeding original images, depth maps, point cloud renderings, and camera parameters into a Qwen-based MLLM.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）已成为新视角合成和3D场景重建的有效表示方法，这催生了对可靠质量评估的迫切需求。与传统的图像质量评估（IQA）不同，3DGS场景的质量不仅取决于渲染视图的感知保真度，还取决于空间结构和跨视图一致性等场景级因素。现有的IQA方法受限于对2D感知线索的依赖，而通用的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）并非为稳定的质量回归而设计，可能产生不可靠的判断。为了解决这些局限性，本文开发了一种用于3DGS场景理解的多模态质量评估框架。首先，引入了一种3D感知质量表示学习框架，通过在VGGT编码器上增加专用质量头来增强其能力。多视图图像被编码为视图特定特征并聚加以捕获跨视图一致性，同时通过深度和点云相关结构信息的联合建模融入几何线索，从而能够学习超外观驱动特征的结构感知质量表示。其次，构建了一种基于多模态大语言模型的推理机制，将原始图像、深度图、点云渲染和相机参数联合输入到基于Qwen的多模态大语言模型中进行推理。

</details>


### [38] [Decoupled Visual Processing: Efficient Multimodal Adaptation via Modality-Specific Transformer Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26596)
*Mingkuan Feng,Zhengqi Wen,Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出解耦视觉处理（DVP）框架，通过轻量级单Transformer块独立处理视觉token，在显著减少可训练参数的同时，在多项基准上保持竞争性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉指令微调时全参数微调计算成本高昂，且视觉与文本token在网络深层对表征的需求存在显著差异，因此需要一种参数高效的训练方案。

Method: 将预训练LLM的解码器层分为两部分：前半层共享处理视觉和文本token，之后将两者拆分——视觉token路由至新初始化的单Transformer块，文本token继续通过原始冻结的解码器层，最后在语言建模头前将两流拼接。训练时仅更新单个Transformer块。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5框架上，DVP在MME、POPE和ChartQA基准上取得竞争性能，同时仅训练总参数的一小部分。

Conclusion: MLLMs中的视觉表征可以通过解耦的、参数高效的路径有效学习，无需全参数微调。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities by integrating visual and textual understanding within a unified transformer architecture. However, fine-tuning all parameters of these models for visual instruction tuning is computationally expensive and often unnecessary, as the representation requirements for visual and textual tokens diverge significantly in the deeper layers of the network. In this paper, we propose Decoupled Visual Processing (DVP), an efficient training framework that replaces the upper decoder layers of a pretrained LLM with a lightweight, independently trainable single transformer block dedicated exclusively to visual token processing. Specifically, after shared processing through the first half of the decoder layers, visual and textual tokens are split: visual tokens are routed through a newly initialized single transformer block while textual tokens continue through the original frozen decoder layers. The two streams are then concatenated before the language modeling head. During training, only the single transformer block is updated, dramatically reducing the number of trainable parameters. Experiments on the LLaVA-1.5 framework demonstrate that DVP achieves competitive performance on MME, POPE, and ChartQA benchmarks while training only a fraction of the total parameters, suggesting that visual representations in MLLMs can be effectively learned through a decoupled, parameter-efficient pathway.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通过将视觉和文本理解整合到统一的Transformer架构中，展现了卓越的能力。然而，为视觉指令微调而微调这些模型的所有参数在计算上代价高昂，而且往往没有必要，因为视觉和文本token在网络深层对表征的需求存在显著差异。本文提出解耦视觉处理（DVP），一种高效训练框架，将预训练LLM的上层解码器层替换为一个轻量级、可独立训练的单Transformer块，专门用于视觉token处理。具体而言，在经过解码器前半层的共享处理后，视觉和文本token被拆分：视觉token被路由至一个新初始化的单Transformer块，而文本token继续通过原始冻结的解码器层。两流在语言建模头前进行拼接。训练过程中，仅更新单Transformer块，从而极大减少可训练参数的数量。在LLaVA-1.5框架上的实验表明，DVP在MME、POPE和ChartQA基准上取得了竞争性能，同时仅训练总参数的一小部分，这表明MLLMs中的视觉表征可以通过解耦的、参数高效的路径有效学习。

</details>


### [39] [JEPADepth: Masked Predictive Representation Learning for Self-Supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26600)
*Ionuţ Grigore,Călin-Adrian Popa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出JEPADepth，通过将I-JEPA风格的掩码预测损失引入自监督单目深度估计框架，在不增加部署成本的情况下显著提升性能，在KITTI、Make3D和Cityscapes上达到SOTA水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统自监督单目深度估计依赖光度重建损失，该损失耦合了深度、位姿和外观假设，存在固有局限性。作者希望引入一种互补的训练目标来缓解这一问题。

Method: 在标准光度重建管线基础上，增加一个基于I-JEPA的掩码预测损失。使用预训练的DINOv3 ViT编码器提取表示，通过预测器从可见上下文区域嵌入推断目标区域嵌入，该预测器和目标编码器在推理时丢弃，不增加部署开销。

Result: 在KITTI上，JEPA目标一致优于同等的DINOv3光度基线；与现有方法相比，JEPADepth与基于transformer的SOTA方法竞争力相当，并优于强CNN基线；在零样本迁移到Make3D和Cityscapes上达到最佳或接近最佳性能。

Conclusion: I-JEPA风格的掩码预测损失可作为自监督单目深度估计的有效补充训练目标，在无需额外推理成本的前提下显著提升深度估计性能。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation typically relies on photometric reconstruction losses that couple depth, pose, and appearance assumptions. In this paper, we propose JEPADepth, a self-supervised monocular depth framework that incorporates a complementary training objective inspired by Image Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (I-JEPA) for self-supervised depth learning. Our method augments a standard photometric pipeline with a masked prediction loss computed in the representation space of a pretrained DINOv3 Vision Transformer encoder. A predictor infers target-region embeddings from visible context-region embeddings under structured masking, and is discarded along with the target encoder at inference time, adding no deployment cost. On KITTI, adding the JEPA objective consistently improves performance over the same DINOv3-based photometric baseline, without changing the inference-time architecture. Compared to prior monocular self-supervised methods, JEPADepth is competitive with state-of-the-art transformer-based approaches and outperforms strong CNN-based baselines on the standard benchmark. In zero-shot transfer (trained on KITTI and evaluated without fine-tuning), JEPADepth achieves the best or near-best performance among the compared methods on both Make3D and Cityscapes across multiple metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督单目深度估计通常依赖于光度重建损失，该损失耦合了深度、位姿和外观假设。本文提出JEPADepth，一种自监督单目深度框架，引入了受图像联合嵌入预测架构（I-JEPA）启发的互补训练目标，用于自监督深度学习。我们的方法在标准光度管线的基础上，增加了在预训练DINOv3 Vision Transformer编码器的表示空间中计算的掩码预测损失。预测器在结构化掩码下从可见上下文区域嵌入推断目标区域嵌入，该预测器与目标编码器在推理时被丢弃，不增加部署成本。在KITTI上，添加JEPA目标后，与同等的基于DINOv3的光度基线相比性能持续提升，且推理时架构不变。与先前的自监督单目方法相比，JEPADepth与基于transformer的最先进方法竞争力相当，并在标准基准测试上优于强CNN基线。在零样本迁移（在KITTI上训练，不进行微调直接评估）中，JEPADepth在Make3D和Cityscapes上的多个指标中均取得最佳或接近最佳的性能。

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过提出CDPI探针方法，系统分析了异构多模态大模型在跨尺度融合时的知识迁移特性，发现迁移具有选择性：主要集中在语言侧的高层推理能力，而感知能力几乎不变，且只有在低干扰的小参数注入比例下才有效。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有异构多模态大模型融合研究仅在有限任务集或聚合指标上评估，未揭示小模型在跨尺度融合中究竟继承了哪些具体能力，不同能力能否跨尺度迁移尚不明确。

Original Abstract: Training-free fusion of heterogeneous multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provides a direct route for cross-scale capability transfer, yet improvements in aggregate performance do not reveal what a smaller model actually inherits. Existing studies are largely designed and evaluated on limited task sets or aggregate metrics; as evaluation expands to broader task collections, whether different capabilities can transfer across scales remains poorly understood. To investigate this question, we introduce Cross-Scale Directional Parameter Injection (CDPI), a simple linear probe to analyze cross-scale knowledge transfer during heterogeneous fusion. A local theoretical analysis indicates that knowledge transfer selectivity is determined at first order by capability-dependent responses to a shared injection direction, while second-order curvature effects constrain the effective transfer regime. Across four Qwen3-VL model pairs and twelve multimodal benchmarks, our experiments reveal a consistent pattern of selectivity: gains concentrate on reasoning, particularly high-level reasoning, whereas perception performance remains close to that of the original target model. Component-wise ablations further show that high-level reasoning gains arise primarily from the language model, while ratio analysis finds that positive selective transfer occurs mainly in the small-ratio regime. These findings recast cross-scale heterogeneous MLLM fusion as selective language-side reasoning transfer within a narrow, low-interference regime, rather than broad capability inheritance.

### [41] [FakeIDet3-DB: Refining Digital Attacks and Patch Extraction for Secure ID Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26641)
*Muñoz-Haro Javier,Teruel Andres,Tolosana Ruben,DeAlcala Daniel,Vera-Rodriguez Ruben,Morales Aythami,Fierrez Julian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FakeIDet3-DB，首个基于真实政府签发身份证件的数字篡改综合数据库，并设计了隐私感知的补丁提取算法PACE，用于训练鲁棒的证件伪造检测模型。实验表明现有最先进模型难以检测生成式/经典篡改攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实身份证件包含复杂高频安全图案，但隐私法规限制了使用真实证件训练检测模型，只能依赖缺乏真实图案特征的合成模板。为弥合这一领域鸿沟，需要构建基于真实证件的、符合隐私规范的篡改数据库。

Method: 构建FakeIDet3-DB数据库，涵盖经典（如复制-移动）和生成式AI驱动（如换脸、修复）的篡改，并采用高级图像精炼流程抑制视觉伪影。提出PACE算法，将隐私感知的补丁提取建模为几何约束的图像处理问题，利用积分图映射和距离驱动的非极大值抑制（NMS）生成匿名化掩码，在防止PII泄露的同时最大化语义密度。

Result: 从超过6.4K张真实/伪造身份证图像中提取约520万张补丁。评估显示，现有最先进模型在检测攻击时EER达32.45%，定位攻击时AUC-ROC达83.48%，表明检测任务极具挑战性。

Conclusion: 现有伪造检测模型在面对真实身份证件上的生成式与经典篡改时性能显著不足，FakeIDet3-DB和PACE为未来研究提供了重要的基准和方法支撑。

Original Abstract: Identity document (ID) authentication relies on the structural integrity of complex, high-frequency security patterns. However, advanced Generative AI models can now inject localized, high-fidelity manipulations, creating deceptive attacks that bypass standard verification. Training robust image forensic models to detect these anomalies is hindered by privacy regulations, forcing reliance on synthetic templates lacking the intricate visual patterns of real IDs. To bridge this domain gap, we introduce FakeIDet3-DB, the first comprehensive database of digital manipulations on real, government-issued IDs. FakeIDet3-DB encompasses classical (e.g., copy-move) and Generative AI-driven manipulations (e.g., face-swapping, inpainting) enhanced with advanced image refinement procedures to suppress visual artifacts. In addition, to comply with strict data protection regulations (e.g., GDPR), we adopt a recently-proposed framework based on patches. In order to maximize forensic utility, we formulate privacy-aware patch extraction from a real ID as a geometrically constrained image processing problem. We propose PACE, a Pseudo-Anonymized Contextual patch Extraction algorithm, which leverages Integral Image mapping and distance-driven Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS). PACE efficiently contours anonymization masks that prevent Personally Identifiable Information (PII) leakage while maximizing semantic density in peri-censorship regions, yielding almost 5.2M patches extracted from more than 6.4K images from real/fake IDs. Furthermore, an extensive evaluation of the proposed FakeIDet3-DB is performed using state-of-the-art models, showcasing they all struggle to detect and locate attacks coming from generative and classic techniques (32.45\% EER in detection and 83.48\% AUC-ROC in localization).

中文摘要翻译: 身份证件（ID）认证依赖于复杂、高频安全图案的结构完整性。然而，先进的生成式AI模型现在可以注入局部、高保真的篡改，制造出能绕过标准验证的欺骗性攻击。由于隐私法规的限制，训练鲁棒的图像取证模型来检测这些异常变得困难，这迫使研究人员依赖缺乏真实ID复杂视觉图案的合成模板。为弥合这一领域鸿沟，我们提出FakeIDet3-DB，首个基于真实政府签发身份证件的数字篡改综合数据库。FakeIDet3-DB涵盖了经典（如复制-移动）和生成式AI驱动的篡改（如换脸、图像修复），并通过高级图像精炼流程增强以抑制视觉伪影。此外，为遵守严格的数据保护法规（如GDPR），我们采用了一种基于补丁（patch）的最近提出框架。为最大化取证实用性，我们将从真实ID中提取隐私感知的补丁建模为一个几何约束的图像处理问题。我们提出PACE——一种伪匿名化上下文补丁提取算法，利用积分图映射和距离驱动的非极大值抑制（NMS）。PACE能够高效勾勒出防止个人身份信息（PII）泄露的匿名化掩码，同时最大化审查周边区域的语义密度，最终从超过6.4K张真实/伪造ID图像中提取了约520万张补丁。此外，我们使用最先进模型对FakeIDet3-DB进行了广泛评估，结果表明这些模型在检测和定位生成式及经典攻击方面均存在困难（检测EER为32.45%，定位AUC-ROC为83.48%）。

</details>


### [42] [FPSGen: Flexible Point Cloud Scene Generation with BEV-Supported Transport Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26645)
*Wenzhe He,Meng Wang,JiaWei Qian,Jinfeng Xu,Ying Liu,Ruihui Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FPSGen提出了一种基于BEV点源构建和路径拉直传输的灵活框架，摆脱了对部分扫描的依赖，实现了室外场景的无条件和条件生成，在SemanticKITTI和KITTI-360上取得了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于点的室外场景生成方法依赖于LiDAR条件补全，存在训练-推理不匹配问题，继承了部分扫描的稀疏性和可见性偏差，导致远处区域稀疏、遮挡区域几何不完整；同时依赖部分扫描限制了在无LiDAR观测或仅有布局线索时的生成能力。

Method: FPSGen首先从活动线索预测包含密度、高度和掩码通道的鸟瞰图（BEV）先验；然后对密度图进行采样构建BEV支撑的点源，支持无条件和条件初始化；接着通过师生近似最优传输方案，利用教师预测的端点学习速度场，诱导更直的传输路径。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI补全任务上取得SOTA的JSD和体素IoU性能，且仅需单点传输步数即可保持强性能；在KITTI-360无条件生成任务上取得最佳覆盖率（COV）。

Conclusion: FPSGen通过将BEV点源构建与路径拉直传输相结合，为无条件和灵活的条件室外场景生成提供了统一框架，有效解决了现有方法的训练-推理不匹配和依赖部分扫描的问题。

Original Abstract: Existing point-based generative methods for outdoor scenes primarily focus on LiDAR-conditioned completion. During training, noisy point clouds are constructed by perturbing complete ground-truth scenes, whereas during inference, they are initialized by adding noise to duplicated partial scans. This train-inference mismatch inherits the sparsity and visibility bias of partial scans, leading to sparse distant regions and incomplete geometry in occluded areas. Moreover, the reliance on partial scans restricts generation when LiDAR observations are unavailable or replaced by layout cues. We present FPSGen, a flexible framework that constructs point sources independently of partial scans. FPSGen first predicts a bird's-eye-view (BEV) prior with density, height, and mask channels from the active cues. The density map is then sampled to form a BEV-supported point source, enabling both unconditional and conditioned initialization. A teacher-student approximate optimal transport scheme then uses teacher-predicted endpoints to learn a velocity field that induces straighter transport paths. By integrating BEV point source construction with path-straightening transport, FPSGen provides a unified framework for unconditional and flexible cue-conditioned scene generation. Extensive experiments show that FPSGen achieves state-of-the-art JSD and voxel IoU performance on SemanticKITTI completion while maintaining strong performance with a single point transport step. On KITTI-360 unconditional generation, it also achieves the best Coverage (COV) among the compared methods.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的基于点的室外场景生成方法主要关注于LiDAR条件下的补全。在训练过程中，通过对完整的真实场景进行扰动来构建含噪点云，而在推理过程中，则通过对重复的部分扫描添加噪声来初始化。这种训练-推理不匹配继承了部分扫描的稀疏性和可见性偏差，导致远处区域稀疏且遮挡区域几何不完整。此外，对部分扫描的依赖限制了在LiDAR观测不可用或被布局线索替代时的生成能力。我们提出了FPSGen，一个灵活框架，能够独立于部分扫描构建点源。FPSGen首先从活动线索预测具有密度、高度和掩码通道的鸟瞰图（BEV）先验。然后对密度图进行采样以形成BEV支撑的点源，从而实现无条件和条件初始化。接着，一种师生近似最优传输方案利用教师预测的端点来学习速度场，从而诱导更直的传输路径。通过将BEV点源构建与路径拉直传输相结合，FPSGen为无条件和灵活的条件场景生成提供了统一框架。大量实验表明，FPSGen在SemanticKITTI补全任务上取得了最先进的JSD和体素IoU性能，同时仅需单点传输步数即可保持强大性能。在KITTI-360无条件生成任务上，它在所有比较方法中也取得了最佳覆盖率（COV）。

</details>

*Yongxin Su,Linjie Hou,Feng Wang,Jialin Tang,Zhijun Li,Qian Wang,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Genie Sim PanoWorld，一个两阶段前馈流水线，通过可控制轨迹的全景视频生成与重建，实现从单张360°全景图到高保真3D场景的零样本重建，无需逐场景优化或多视图捕获。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么缺乏度量轨迹控制，阻碍可靠的下游3D重建；要么在大范围相机运动下难以处理大的遮挡区域，且需要高端多GPU服务器。本文旨在实现从单张全景图高效重建可自由漫游的高保真3D场景。

Method: 两阶段前馈流水线：1) 生成阶段：将NavMesh规划的SE(3)漫游轨迹注入潜视频扩散模型，通过密集几何变形条件控制，采用长短轨迹混合训练和基于捷径模型的自一致性目标，4步无CFG去噪生成高保真全景视频；2) 重建阶段：前馈全景重建器将生成的视频提升为高保真3D高斯场景，支持实时自由视点漫游。

Result: 在全景视频生成和下游3D重建任务上均优于几何条件基线方法，能够零样本泛化到未见过的室内场景。

Conclusion: Genie Sim PanoWorld通过显式、可控制轨迹的全景视频生成桥接了生成与重建，实现了高效、高保真的单张全景图3D场景重建，可直接作为具身AI应用的仿真就绪资产。

Original Abstract: We address the problem of reconstructing a high-fidelity, freely navigable 3D scene from a single $360^\circ$ panorama, without per-scene optimization or multi-view capture. Existing methods either lack metric trajectory control, which hinders reliable downstream 3D reconstruction, or struggle with large disocclusions under long-range camera motion while requiring high-end multi-GPU servers.We present Genie Sim PanoWorld, a two-stage feed-forward pipeline that bridges generation and reconstruction via an explicit, trajectory-controllable panoramic video. A NavMesh-planned $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ roaming trajectory is injected into a latent video diffusion model through dense geometry-warped conditioning; long--short trajectory mixed training and a self-consistency objective based on shortcut models together yield high-fidelity video in four CFG-free denoising steps.
  A feed-forward panoramic reconstructor then lifts the generated video into a high-fidelity
  3D Gaussian scene that supports real-time, free-viewpoint roaming and can be directly used as a simulation-ready asset for embodied AI applications. Experiments show that Genie Sim PanoWorld
  outperforms geometry-conditioned baselines in both panoramic video generation and
  downstream 3D reconstruction, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen indoor scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了从单张$360^\circ$全景图重建高保真、可自由漫游的3D场景的问题，无需逐场景优化或多视图捕获。现有方法要么缺乏度量轨迹控制，阻碍了可靠的下游3D重建；要么在长距离相机运动下难以处理大的遮挡区域，同时需要高端多GPU服务器。我们提出了Genie Sim PanoWorld，一个两阶段前馈流水线，通过显式的、轨迹可控的全景视频桥接生成与重建。将NavMesh规划的$\mathrm{SE}(3)$漫游轨迹通过密集几何变形条件注入潜视频扩散模型；长短轨迹混合训练和基于捷径模型的自一致性目标相结合，仅需四步无分类器引导去噪即可生成高保真视频。随后，前馈全景重建器将生成的视频提升为高保真3D高斯场景，支持实时、自由视点漫游，并可直接用作具身AI应用的仿真就绪资产。实验表明，Genie Sim PanoWorld在全景视频生成和下游3D重建方面均优于几何条件基线方法，并能零样本泛化到未见过的室内场景。

</details>


### [44] [Anchoring and Steering Diffusion: Enhancing the Faithfulness of Text-to-Image Generation at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26647)
*Xinyi Wang,Yuyang Huang,Yalin Su,Pengcheng Luan,Tao Zhang,Feiming Wei,Wenxian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnchorSteer是一个无需训练的框架，通过语义锚定（基于CLIP的文本对齐初始化）和反射引导（Think-Erase-Retouch自校正循环）两方面改进扩散模型的推理过程，显著提升文本-图像对齐效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在复杂组合提示下常出现对齐不精准的问题，现有训练-free方法要么只改进初始噪声（成本高或语义注入不可靠），要么只改进去噪轨迹（缺乏及时诊断和纠错机制），缺乏同时控制初始化和去噪轨迹的细粒度方法。

Method: AnchorSteer包括两个协同组件：1) 语义锚定（Semantic Anchoring）：通过CLIP提取先验并用LP-SDS目标将其蒸馏到扩散模型的知识分布中，生成文本对齐的初始噪声；2) 反射引导（Reflective Steering）：通过Think-Erase-Retouch循环，利用VLM诊断语义偏差，对潜在表示进行定向精炼以抑制错误内容并恢复缺失属性。

Result: 在GenEval和T2I-CompBench++上的大量实验表明，AnchorSteer在文本-图像对齐方面持续优于现有基线方法，同时保持了高视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过同时控制初始化和去噪轨迹，AnchorSteer实现了无需额外训练的细粒度推理优化，有效解决了扩散模型在复杂组合提示下的对齐问题。

Original Abstract: While text-to-image diffusion models achieve impressive visual quality, they frequently struggle to maintain precise alignment with complex compositional prompts. An effective strategy is to improve the inference process of diffusion models, thereby better leveraging their pretrained priors to address misalignment. Existing training-free methods can be divided into two categories. The first category focuses on improving the randomly sampled initial noise, either performing costly search over noise pools or manipulating sampled noise without ensuring reliable semantic injection. The second category focuses on improving the denoising trajectory, lacking explicit mechanisms to timely diagnose and correct semantic errors. we propose \textbf{AnchorSteer}, a training-free framework that exerts fine-grained control over \textbf{both initialization} and \textbf{the denoising trajectory}. AnchorSteer consists of two synergistic components: \textbf{Semantic Anchoring} replaces uninformative Gaussian noise with text-aligned initializations via CLIP-based prior extraction and a novel Latent-Prior Score Distillation Sampling (LP-SDS) objective. Specifically, LP-SDS distills CLIP visual priors into the knowledge distribution of diffusion models, mitigating the domain gap between CLIP-based priors and diffusion-based priors. \textbf{Reflective Steering} transforms passive denoising with an active Think--Erase--Retouch loop that enables mid-generation self-correction. It leverages VLM-based diagnosis to detect semantic deviations and performs targeted latent refinement to suppress erroneous content and recover missing attributes. Extensive experiments on GenEval and T2I-CompBench++ demonstrate that AnchorSteer consistently outperforms existing baselines in text--image alignment while preserving high visual quality.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然文本到图像扩散模型实现了令人印象深刻的视觉质量，但它们经常难以与复杂的组合提示保持精确对齐。一个有效的策略是改进扩散模型的推理过程，从而更好地利用其预训练先验来解决对齐问题。现有的无需训练方法可分为两类。第一类专注于改进随机采样的初始噪声，要么在噪声池上进行昂贵的搜索，要么操纵采样的噪声而不确保可靠的语义注入。第二类专注于改进去噪轨迹，缺乏及时诊断和纠正语义错误的显式机制。我们提出了AnchorSteer，一个无需训练的框架，对初始化和去噪轨迹都施加细粒度控制。AnchorSteer由两个协同组件组成：语义锚定（Semantic Anchoring）通过基于CLIP的先验提取和一种新颖的潜在先验分数蒸馏采样（LP-SDS）目标，用文本对齐的初始化替代无信息的高斯噪声。具体来说，LP-SDS将CLIP视觉先验蒸馏到扩散模型的知识分布中，缓解了基于CLIP的先验与基于扩散的先验之间的域差距。反射引导（Reflective Steering）通过一个主动的思考-擦除-润饰（Think-Erase-Retouch）循环将被动的去噪转变为生成中期的自我校正。它利用基于VLM的诊断来检测语义偏差，并执行定向的潜在精炼以抑制错误内容并恢复缺失属性。在GenEval和T2I-CompBench++上的大量实验表明，AnchorSteer在文本-图像对齐方面持续优于现有基线方法，同时保持了高视觉质量。

</details>


### [45] [Physically Real-time Infrared Attack against Optical Flow Estimation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26651)
*Shen You,Wei Jiang,Jiarui Liu,Yijian Ye,Qiuzhen Lin,Xiangtao Li,Ka-Chun Wong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用红外光在物理世界中对光流估计网络进行实时对抗攻击的方法，通过预先生成并实时显示对抗样本，实现对受害者模型精确且有针对性的攻击，克服了数字到物理攻击技术的局限性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光流估计网络作为上游模型在下游任务中至关重要，但其鲁棒性测试不足可能导致安全事故。现有数字到物理攻击技术存在对抗样本失效的局限性，亟需一种能在物理世界中进行实时、精准攻击的方法来评估其鲁棒性。

Method: 预先大量生成对抗样本，通过红外光（利用其隐蔽性）在物理世界中实时计算并动态显示这些对抗样本，从而在不修改受害者系统的情况下对光流估计网络实施精确且有针对性的攻击。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能在不同光照条件、不同物体运动速度和不同物体位置下有效攻击光流估计网络，削弱其准确估计光流的能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了在物理世界中对光流估计网络的实时攻击，克服了传统数字到物理攻击技术的局限，为评估和提升OFEN的鲁棒性提供了有效途径。

Original Abstract: With the promising performance of deep neural networks on image-based tasks, different real-world applications such as autonomous driving and motion detection have become increasingly mature and relevant to human lives. In particular, Optical Flow Estimation Networks (OFENs), as upstream models, play a critical role in different domains. Its outputs are heavily assumed and adopted for different downstream tasks, and it is essential to test its robustness to prevent safety accidents. We present an approach for real-time attacks on OFENs in the physical world, leveraging infrared lights for their stealthiness. By generating a large number of Adversarial Examples in advance, our approach computes AEs in real time and dynamically displays them, which allows our method to facilitate precise and targeted attacks without modifying the victim system. Unlike previous digital-to-physical attack techniques, our method directly attacks victim models within the physical world, thereby overcoming the limitations associated with the ineffectiveness of AEs. Experimental results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach in compromising OFENs across diverse lighting conditions, varying object motion velocities, and different object placements, ultimately impairing the network's ability to accurately estimate optical flow.

中文摘要翻译: 随着深度神经网络在图像任务上展现出卓越性能，自动驾驶和运动检测等不同实际应用已日益成熟并与人类生活息息相关。特别是光流估计网络作为上游模型，在不同领域中扮演着关键角色。其输出被广泛假设并应用于不同的下游任务，因此测试其鲁棒性以防止安全事故至关重要。本文提出了一种在物理世界中利用红外光（因其隐蔽性）对光流估计网络进行实时攻击的方法。通过预先大量生成对抗样本，我们的方法能够实时计算并动态显示这些样本，从而在不修改受害者系统的情况下实现精确且有针对性的攻击。与以往的数字到物理攻击技术不同，我们的方法直接在物理世界中攻击受害者模型，从而克服了对抗样本失效的局限性。实验结果表明，我们的方法能够在不同光照条件、不同物体运动速度和不同物体位置下有效攻击光流估计网络，最终削弱网络准确估计光流的能力。

</details>


### [46] [Visko Orbis 1.0: A Live Model for Real-Time Interactive Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26694)
*Xiangbo Gao,Siyuan Yang,Ping He,Mingyang Wu,Yuheng Wu,Yushen Zuo,Jiongze Yu,Ryan Cui,Hongyuan Hua,Devin Ma,Xiao Jin,Yubo Yuan,Qing Yin,Jie Yang,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Visko Orbis 1.0 是一个实时交互式长视频生成模型，支持用户动态切换提示词，实现小时级连续4K视频生成，在长视频竞技场对比中获得最高偏好和时域稳定性评分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以支持长时间、实时、可交互的生成过程，用户无法在生成过程中动态修改提示并立即看到效果，且长视频生成存在质量漂移和色彩偏移问题。

Method: 采用有界多尺度记忆机制跨块保持主体、场景和风格一致性；基于蒸馏的块式流式生成器与流式视频升频器协同工作；结合优化的GPU推理引擎实现实时4K输出。

Result: 在长视频竞技场对比中，Visko Orbis 1.0 在所有最先进的实时交互式视频生成系统中获得了最高的总体偏好评分和时域稳定性评分。

Conclusion: Visko Orbis 1.0 证明了实时交互式长视频生成的可行性，能够实现小时级无质量漂移的4K视频生成，且支持多语言提示和动态提示切换。

Original Abstract: We present Visko Orbis 1.0, a Live Model for real-time, interactive long-video generation. Users can change the prompt at any moment during generation, and the update becomes visible in real time. Visko Orbis 1.0 supports long-form text-to-video, image-to-video, and video continuation, with multilingual prompts and prompt switching while generation is in progress. A bounded multi-scale memory preserves subjects, scenes, and style across chunks, sustaining hour-scale rollouts without evident quality or color drift. Built on a distilled chunk-wise streaming generator and a streaming video upscaler, Visko Orbis 1.0 delivers real-time 4K video generation at 24 FPS using an optimized GPU serving engine. In long-form Arena comparisons, Visko Orbis 1.0 obtains the highest overall-preference and temporal-stability ratings among state-of-the-art real-time interactive video-generation systems.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Visko Orbis 1.0，一个用于实时、交互式长视频生成的Live Model。用户可以在生成过程中的任意时刻更改提示词，并且更新会实时可见。Visko Orbis 1.0支持长文本到视频、图像到视频以及视频续写，支持多语言提示以及在生成过程中切换提示词。一个有界的多尺度记忆机制在各块之间保持主体、场景和风格的一致性，支撑小时级滚动生成而无明显质量或色彩漂移。基于蒸馏的块式流式生成器和流式视频升频器，Visko Orbis 1.0利用优化的GPU推理引擎实现了24 FPS的实时4K视频生成。在长视频竞技场对比中，Visko Orbis 1.0在所有最先进的实时交互式视频生成系统中获得了最高的总体偏好评分和时域稳定性评分。

</details>


### [47] [Sequence-SOD: Bio-inspired Sequence-aware Spiking ObjectDetection for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26703)
*Katharina Bendig,René Schuster,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sequence-SOD，一种序列感知的脉冲神经网络目标检测器，通过在处理连续事件流时保留膜电位状态，利用多个时间点的标签进行训练，在Gen1数据集上将mAP从23.38提升至26.88，理论预测频率达40Hz。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有SNN目标检测器通常将事件流切分为独立间隔，每个间隔仅用一个标签处理，并在每次预测后重置网络状态，未能充分利用连续事件流中的时序信息。

Method: 将事件累积为短间隔并离散化为时间步，依次输入SSD风格的Spiking DenseNet；在序列内跨间隔保留膜电位，使检测由演化的神经状态驱动而非独立重置的输入窗口，并采用序列感知训练方式利用多个时间点标签。

Result: 在Gen1 Automotive Detection Dataset上，序列感知训练将mAP从单间隔训练的23.38提升至25.30（无数据增强）和26.88（有事件数据增强），模型理论预测频率达40Hz。

Conclusion: 在扩展事件序列上训练和评估SNN目标检测器能提升其利用时序线索的能力，同时保留稀疏脉冲计算带来的能效优势；序列感知训练是事件驱动SNN检测中与架构改进互补的重要方向。

Original Abstract: Event cameras follow a retina-inspired sensing principle, reporting local intensity changes asynchronously with hightemporal resolution and a wide dynamic range. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) complement these sparse event streams through brain-inspired dynamics, using sparse spikes and leaky membrane potentials to integrate information over time. However, many SNN object detectors process isolated event intervals with a single label and reset the network state after each prediction, thereby underusing temporal information in continuous event streams. We introduce Sequence-SOD, a sequence-aware SNN object detector that processes extended event sequences containing labels at multiple time points. Events are accumulated into short intervals, discretized into temporal steps, and fed sequentially to an SSD-style Spiking DenseNet while preserving membrane potentials across intervals within a sequence, so that detection is driven by an evolving neural state instead of independently reset input windows. On the Gen1 Automotive Detection Dataset, sequence-aware training improves mAP from 23.38 for single-interval training to 25.30 without augmentation and to 26.88 withevent-data augmentation. The model achieves a theoretical prediction frequency of 40 Hz. Training and evaluating SNN object detectors on extended event sequences improves their ability to exploit temporal cues while preserving the energy-efficiency benefits of sparse spiking computation. The results highlight sequence-aware training as a complementary direction to architectural improvements for event-based SNN detection.

中文摘要翻译: 事件相机遵循类视网膜感知原理，以高时间分辨率和宽动态范围异步报告局部强度变化。脉冲神经网络（SNN）通过类脑动力学（使用稀疏脉冲和漏电膜电位随时间整合信息）对这些稀疏事件流进行补充。然而，许多SNN目标检测器以单个标签处理孤立的事件间隔，并在每次预测后重置网络状态，从而未能充分利用连续事件流中的时序信息。我们提出Sequence-SOD，一种序列感知的SNN目标检测器，可处理包含多个时间点标签的扩展事件序列。事件被累积为短间隔，离散化为时间步，并顺序输入SSD风格的Spiking DenseNet，同时在序列内跨间隔保留膜电位，使得检测由演化的神经状态驱动，而非独立重置的输入窗口。在Gen1 Automotive Detection Dataset上，序列感知训练将mAP从单间隔训练的23.38提升至25.30（无数据增强）和26.88（有事件数据增强）。该模型实现了40 Hz的理论预测频率。在扩展事件序列上训练和评估SNN目标检测器，可提升其利用时序线索的能力，同时保留稀疏脉冲计算带来的能效优势。结果表明，序列感知训练是与事件驱动SNN检测中架构改进互补的重要方向。

</details>


### [48] [TPD: Temporal Prior Decoupling for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26706)
*Taewon Kang,Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现文本到视频扩散模型存在“时序先验抑制”（TPS）问题，即早期片段的强先验会压制后期片段的事件生成。为此提出时序先验解耦（TPD），一种无需训练、与骨干网络无关的框架，通过构建时序反事实并恢复被抑制的信号方向，显著提升后期事件生成质量，同时保持时序一致性和视觉保真度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本到视频扩散模型在处理“早期场景持续存在、后期新事件涌现”的复杂提示时（如“沙滩上的沙堡被海浪冲走”），经常无法成功生成后期片段的事件。作者发现根本原因是早期片段的主导先验沿时间轴捕获交叉注意力轨迹，压制了后期片段实现所需的引导信号，而现有引导机制未能建模这种竞争关系。

Method: TPD是一个无需训练的训练自由框架，包含三个关键步骤：（1）仅以早期片段为条件构建时序反事实；（2）将完整提示轨迹与反事实轨迹之间的差异定义为被抑制的信号方向；（3）通过帧选择性的下界约束，在扩散时间步和视频帧上联合求解，恢复被抑制的后期信号。与先前减法投影方法去除该方向不同，TPD采用下界可行性约束（而非上界）来保证被抑制信号的贡献，从而在不破坏早期片段连贯性的前提下实现后期事件。

Result: 实验表明：TPD显著提升了后期概念的实现质量，同时保持了时序连贯性和视觉保真度；TPS问题在多种不同的文本到视频骨干网络中普遍存在。

Conclusion: TPD完全运行在标准扩散采样框架内，无需重新训练，且纯粹在无分类器引导空间中定义，因此天然具有骨干网络无关性。该方法有效解决了文本到视频扩散模型中的时序先验抑制问题。

Original Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion models generate temporally coherent content from natural language, yet when a prompt describes an early scene that persists while a new event emerges on top of it---such as "a tall sandcastle standing on a beach where a wave rushes in and washes it away"---generation frequently fails to realize the late-segment event in the corresponding frames. We identify this failure as Temporal Prior Suppression (TPS): the dominant prior of the early segment captures the cross-attention trajectory across the temporal axis and suppresses the guidance signal needed for late-segment realization, a competing tendency existing guidance mechanisms do not model. We introduce Temporal Prior Decoupling (TPD), a training-free framework that restores suppressed late-segment signals during diffusion sampling. TPD constructs a temporal counterfactual by conditioning on the early segment alone, and defines the discrepancy between the full-prompt and counterfactual trajectories as a suppressed signal direction. Rather than removing this direction as in prior subtractive projection methods, TPD restores it through a frame-selective lower-bound constraint resolved jointly over diffusion timestep and video frame, realizing the suppressed event in the late frames without disrupting early-segment coherence: where prior work enforces upper-bound feasibility to remove unwanted semantics, TPD enforces lower-bound feasibility to guarantee suppressed-signal contribution. TPD runs entirely within standard diffusion sampling without retraining, and is defined purely in classifier-free guidance space, making it backbone-agnostic by construction. Experiments show that TPD significantly improves late-concept realization while preserving temporal coherence and visual fidelity, and that the targeted suppression recurs across distinct text-to-video backbones.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到视频扩散模型能够从自然语言生成时序连贯的内容，然而，当提示描述一个早期场景持续存在且在其上涌现新事件时——例如“沙滩上矗立着一座高高的沙堡，一波海浪涌来将其冲走”——模型经常无法在对应帧中成功实现后期片段的事件。我们将这一失败现象识别为时序先验抑制（Temporal Prior Suppression, TPS）：早期片段的主导先验沿时间轴捕获交叉注意力轨迹，并压制了后期片段实现所需的引导信号，现有引导机制未能建模这种竞争倾向。我们提出时序先验解耦（Temporal Prior Decoupling, TPD），一个无需训练的训练自由框架，在扩散采样过程中恢复被抑制的后期片段信号。TPD通过仅以早期片段为条件构建时序反事实，并将完整提示轨迹与反事实轨迹之间的差异定义为被抑制的信号方向。不同于先前减法投影方法去除该方向，TPD通过一个帧选择性的下界约束在扩散时间步和视频帧上联合求解来恢复它，从而在不破坏早期片段连贯性的前提下实现后期被抑制事件：先前工作通过上界可行性约束来移除不想要的语义，而TPD通过下界可行性约束来保证被抑制信号的贡献。TPD完全运行在标准扩散采样框架内，无需重新训练，且纯粹在无分类器引导空间中定义，因此天然具有骨干网络无关性。实验表明，TPD显著改善了后期概念的实现，同时保持了时序连贯性和视觉保真度，并且这种目标性抑制现象在不同文本到视频骨干网络中普遍存在。

</details>


### [49] [FreeShadow: Training-Free Shadow Removal via Illumination Transfer and Selective Content Preservation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26715)
*Yinan Wang,Yan Huang,Yong Xu,Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeShadow是一种基于预训练扩散模型的免训练阴影去除方法，通过光照传递注意力（ITA）恢复光照、选择性保留光照不变成分保持内容一致性，以及局部纹理保持重光照（LTPR）解决纹理错位问题，无需训练或优化即可实现强泛化能力和逼真的无阴影图像。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有有监督和无监督阴影去除方法因训练数据集多样性不足而泛化能力有限，零样本方法则容易产生伪影且需要耗时的测试时优化。

Method: 1) 光照传递注意力（ITA）：对扩散模型中的自注意力图重新加权，将非阴影区域的光照线索传递到阴影区域；2) 内容保持：分析光照变化对自注意力图和潜在高频特征的影响，选择性地保留光照不变成分以维持内容保真度并抑制残留阴影；3) 局部纹理保持重光照（LTPR）：缓解VAE压缩导致的局部纹理未对齐问题。

Result: 大量实验表明FreeShadow实现了强泛化能力，并生成逼真的无阴影图像。

Conclusion: FreeShadow通过利用预训练扩散模型中的先验知识，无需任何训练或测试时优化，即可高效完成阴影去除任务，在泛化性和图像质量上表现优异。

Original Abstract: Existing supervised and unsupervised shadow removal methods often suffer from limited generalization due to the insufficient diversity of available training datasets, while zero-shot methods tend to produce artifacts and require time-consuming test-time optimization. To address these issues, we propose FreeShadow, a training-free shadow removal method built upon pretrained diffusion models, which exploits diffusion priors for shadow removal without any training or optimization. For illumination recovery, we propose an illumination transfer attention (ITA), which re-weights the self-attention maps in diffusion model to transfer illumination cues from non-shadow to shadow regions. For content preservation, we analyze the effects of illumination variations on self-attention maps and latent high-frequency features in diffusion model, and selectively preserve illumination-invariant components to maintain content fidelity while suppressing residual shadows. We further propose local texture-preserving relighting (LTPR) to mitigate local texture misalignment caused by VAE compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong generalization and produces realistic shadow-free images.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的有监督和无监督阴影去除方法通常因可用训练数据集的多样性不足而泛化能力有限，而零样本方法则容易产生伪影且需要耗时的测试时优化。为解决这些问题，我们提出了FreeShadow——一种基于预训练扩散模型的免训练阴影去除方法，它利用扩散先验进行阴影去除，无需任何训练或优化。在光照恢复方面，我们提出了光照传递注意力（ITA），该方法对扩散模型中的自注意力图进行重新加权，将非阴影区域的光照线索传递到阴影区域。在内容保持方面，我们分析了光照变化对扩散模型中自注意力图和潜在高频特征的影响，并选择性地保留光照不变成分以维持内容保真度，同时抑制残留阴影。我们进一步提出了局部纹理保持重光照（LTPR），以缓解VAE压缩导致的局部纹理未对齐问题。大量实验表明，我们的方法实现了强泛化能力，并生成逼真的无阴影图像。

</details>


### [50] [CASIAL: Geometric Distortion Robust Image Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26729)
*Yupeng Qiu,Han Fang,Ee-Chien Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CASIAL水印框架，通过全局消息扩散（CAS）和几何不变表示对齐（IAL）两个核心设计，同时抵御区域移除和去同步两类几何失真，在六种几何变换下显著优于11种基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习水印方法在非几何失真下表现良好，但对几何变换（如裁剪、缩放、旋转）鲁棒性不足。几何变换导致两类根本性失效模式：区域移除（信息丢失）和去同步（像素错位）。需要同时实现全局消息扩散和几何不变表示两种性质才能解决该问题。

Method: 提出CASIAL框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 封面图像感知的消息扩散策略（CAS），将水印比特与封面图像特征紧密耦合，自适应地分布到全图，增强每像素信息容量和对区域移除的鲁棒性；(2) 不变性对齐学习模块（IAL），利用空间注意力捕获跨像素依赖关系，将扰动后的特征对齐到共享的几何不变表示空间，缓解去同步导致的解码失败。

Result: 在六种挑战性几何变换下，CASIAL显著优于11种基线方法，同时保持高视觉质量；在六种信号失真和四种光度变换下保持竞争性能；仅使用白盒失真训练，即展现出对未见黑盒失真的强迁移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过全局消息扩散和几何不变表示对齐两个关键设计，CASIAL实现了对几何变换的强鲁棒性，兼具广泛的失真鲁棒性和优越的视觉质量。

Original Abstract: Deep learning-based watermarking has shown strong robustness against non-geometric distortions, yet its performance under geometric transformations remains limited. Such transformations induce two fundamental failure modes: region removal, such as cropping or masking, which eliminates the information carried by removed pixels, and desynchronization, such as scaling or rotation, which misaligns pixel positions and disrupts decoding. We argue that achieving geometric robustness requires two essential properties: (1) global spread of the watermark message, ensuring resilience even when large regions are removed, and (2) geometry-invariant representations, enabling decoding to remain synchronized despite spatial transformations. Building on these insights, we propose CASIAL, a geometric distortion-robust watermarking framework with cover image-aware message spreading (CAS) strategy and invariance alignment learning (IAL) module. CAS tightly couples watermark bits with cover image features and distributes them adaptively across the entire image, enhancing per-pixel information capacity and robustness to region removal. IAL leverages spatial attention to capture cross-pixel dependencies and align perturbed features into a shared geometry-invariant representation space, mitigating failures due to desynchronization. Across six challenging geometric transformations, CASIAL achieves substantially stronger robustness than eleven prior baselines while preserving high visual quality. It also maintains competitive performance under six signal distortions and four photometric transformations. Notably, although trained only with white-box distortions, CASIAL also exhibits strong transfer robustness to unseen black-box distortions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the broad robustness and superior visual quality of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度学习的数字水印在非几何失真下展现出强鲁棒性，但在几何变换下的性能仍然有限。此类变换会引发两种根本性失效模式：区域移除（如裁剪或掩膜），会移除像素所携带的信息；以及去同步（如缩放或旋转），会错乱像素位置并破坏解码。我们认为，实现几何鲁棒性需要两个关键性质：（1）水印消息的全局扩散，确保即使大块区域被移除仍能保持韧性；（2）几何不变表示，使解码在空间变换下保持同步。基于上述见解，我们提出CASIAL——一种几何失真鲁棒的水印框架，包含封面图像感知的消息扩散（CAS）策略和不变性对齐学习（IAL）模块。CAS将水印比特与封面图像特征紧密耦合，并自适应地将它们分布到整幅图像，从而提升每像素信息容量和对区域移除的鲁棒性。IAL利用空间注意力捕获跨像素依赖关系，将扰动后的特征对齐到共享的几何不变表示空间，缓解去同步导致的解码失败。在六种具有挑战性的几何变换下，CASIAL的鲁棒性显著强于11个先前基线方法，同时保持高视觉质量。在六种信号失真和四种光度变换下，它也保持了具有竞争力的性能。值得注意的是，尽管仅使用白盒失真训练，CASIAL对未见过的黑盒失真也展现出强迁移鲁棒性。全面的实验证明了我们方法的广泛鲁棒性和优越的视觉质量。

</details>


### [51] [Online Handwriting Trajectory Reconstruction from Kinematic Sensors using Temporal Convolutional Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26733)
*Wassim Swaileh,Florent Imbert,Yann Soullard,Romain Tavenard,Eric Anquetil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于数字笔传感器信号重建在线手写轨迹的处理流程，采用动态时间规整对齐信号，并设计了一种类时间卷积网络的专用神经网络架构，在基准数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用配备传感器的数字笔可以在任何表面书写并获取数字化轨迹，有助于学习书写、协作会议等多种应用，但需要从传感器信号中准确重建在线手写轨迹。

Method: 提出一个新型处理流程：首先利用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐数字笔传感器信号与平板（提供真值）的采样率差异；然后设计一种受时间卷积网络（TCN）启发的专用神经网络架构，从对齐后的传感器信号重建在线手写轨迹。

Result: 在新的基准数据集上进行了定性和定量评估，结果显示该方法相较于最具竞争力的现有方法有显著改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于DTW对齐和类TCN网络的处理流程能有效从数字笔传感器信号中重建在线手写轨迹，性能优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Handwriting with digital pens is a common way to facilitate human-computer interaction through the use of Online Handwriting (OH) trajectory reconstruction. In this work, we focus on a digital pen equipped with sensors from which one wants to reconstruct the OH trajectory. Such a pen allows to write on any surface and to get the digital trace, which can help learning to write, by writing on paper, and can be useful for many other applications such as collaborative meetings, etc. In this paper, we introduce a novel processing pipeline that maps the sensor signals of the pen to the corresponding OH trajectory. Notably, in order to tackle the difference of sampling rates between the pen and the tablet (which provides ground truth information), our preprocessing pipeline relies on Dynamic Time Warping to align the signals. We introduce a dedicated neural network architecture, inspired by a Temporal Convolutional Network, to reconstruct the online trajectory from the pen sensor signals. Finally, we also present a new benchmark dataset on which our method is evaluated both qualitatively and quantitatively, showing a notable improvement over its most notable competitor.

中文摘要翻译: 使用数字笔进行手写是通过在线手写轨迹重建促进人机交互的常见方式。本文聚焦于一款配备传感器、旨在重建在线手写轨迹的数字笔。该笔可在任何表面书写并获取数字轨迹，有助于通过纸质书写学习写字，也可用于协作会议等多种应用。本文提出一种新型处理流程，将笔的传感器信号映射为对应的在线手写轨迹。为处理笔与平板（提供真值信息）之间的采样率差异，我们的预处理流程采用动态时间规整对齐信号。我们引入了一种受时间卷积网络启发的专用神经网络架构，从笔传感器信号中重建在线轨迹。最后，我们还展示了一个新的基准数据集，并在该数据集上对方法进行了定性和定量评估，结果显示相较于最具竞争力的现有方法有显著改进。

</details>


### [52] [Dual Inversion for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models: From Both Prompt and Noise Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26735)
*Xiaolong Liu,Junjian Li,Yuan Xiao,Jiaqi Deng,Dayong Ye,Tianqing Zhu,Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有提示反演方法梯度不稳定、视觉保真度低的问题，提出Dualin双阶段框架，联合恢复目标图像的语义提示和潜在噪声，实现高质量反演与图像编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有提示反演方法存在两大局限：(1)基于梯度的方法不稳定、不可解释，生成图像有严重伪影；(2)无梯度方法虽生成可读提示，但缺乏细粒度细节对齐，视觉保真度差。原因是将提示反演视为逆向工程的充分条件，忽略了编码结构信息的潜在噪声的关键作用。

Method: 提出Dualin（双反演）两阶段方法。第一阶段：融合视觉语言模型CLIP和大语言模型，反演生成忠实可解释的硬提示（hard prompt）。第二阶段：采用无条件DDIM反演重建目标图像的确切潜在噪声，保证结构信息层面的一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，Dualin能够同时生成高质量的反演提示，并达到最先进的图像保真度。此外，Dualin可为精确可控的图像编辑奠定坚实基础。

Conclusion: 理论上证明了反演后的噪声无需重新优化即可实现灵活的图像编辑。Dualin通过联合恢复语义提示和潜在噪声，克服了传统方法的不足，实现了高质量反演与编辑。

Original Abstract: Prompt inversion, as a typical reverse engineering technique, enables text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models to generate the desired target images without extensive prompt engineering. However, existing prompt inversion methods suffer from significant limitations: (1) gradient-based methods are unstable and uninterpretable, often resulting in generated images with severe artifacts; (2) gradient-free methods yield human-readable prompts but still fail to preserve visual fidelity due to the lack of fine-grained detail alignment. We contend that the limitations stem from treating prompt inversion as a sufficient condition for reverse engineering, ignoring the critical role of the latent noise that encodes structural information. Consequently, we propose Dualin (Dual inversion), a two-stage method that jointly recovers both the semantic prompt and latent noise of the target image. In the first stage, we integrate vision-language model, CLIP and large language model to invert a faithful, human-interpretable hard prompt. In the second stage, unconditional DDIM inversion reconstructs the exact latent noise of the target image, guaranteeing the consistency at the structural information level. Theoretically, we prove that the inverted noise enables flexible image editing without re-optimization. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets demonstrate that Dualin simultaneously generates high-quality inverted prompts and achieves state-of-the-art image fidelity. Additionally, Dualin can establish a robust foundation for the precise and controllable image editing.

中文摘要翻译: 提示反演作为一种典型的逆向工程技术，使得文本到图像扩散模型无需大量提示工程即可生成所需的目标图像。然而，现有的提示反演方法存在显著局限：(1)基于梯度的方法不稳定且不可解释，生成的图像往往带有严重伪影；(2)无梯度方法能生成人类可读的提示，但由于缺乏细粒度细节对齐，仍无法保持视觉保真度。我们认为这些局限源于将提示反演视为逆向工程的充分条件，而忽略了编码结构信息的潜在噪声的关键作用。为此，我们提出Dualin（双反演），一种两阶段方法，联合恢复目标图像的语义提示和潜在噪声。在第一阶段，我们融合视觉语言模型CLIP和大语言模型，反演生成忠实且人类可解释的硬提示。在第二阶段，无条件DDIM反演重建目标图像的确切潜在噪声，保证结构信息层面的一致性。理论上，我们证明了反演后的噪声无需重新优化即可实现灵活的图像编辑。在多个数据集上的大量实验表明，Dualin能同时生成高质量的反演提示，并达到最先进的图像保真度。此外，Dualin可为精确可控的图像编辑奠定坚实基础。

</details>


### [53] [Multimodal fusion of visual and morphometric features for avian bone classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26743)
*Nevio Dubbini,Lisa Yeomans,Marco Pavia,Ramazan Parmaksiz,Ayse Atas Hooglugt,Gabriele Gattiglia,Beatrice Demarchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合卷积神经网络图像分析与骨学测量的多模态框架，用于鸟类骨骼的骨片类型和科级分类。在超过10,000张图像的数据集上，骨片类型分类准确率达86%，科级分类top-1和top-3准确率分别为51%和75%，验证了多模态深度学习在动物考古学中应用的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 人工智能在考古学中展现出潜力，但在动物考古学（尤其是鸟类骨骼识别）中的应用仍然有限。现有方法依赖专家手工鉴定，耗时且难以规模化，因此需要开发可扩展、可解释的自动化识别工具。

Method: 提出了一种多模态框架：首先使用BiRefNet和SAM2两阶段流水线对图像进行自动分割；然后利用预训练的EfficientNet_V2_S骨干网络提取视觉特征，并与标准化骨学测量数据通过特征级多模态架构进行融合；最后对两个分类任务（骨骼元素识别和科级分类）进行训练和评估。

Result: 骨片类型分类测试集准确率为86%；科级分类top-1准确率为51%，top-3准确率为75%，表明正确分类常出现在最可能的预测结果中。

Conclusion: 研究表明将视觉信息与形态测量信息在统一深度学习框架中融合是可行的，为未来AI辅助动物考古学识别建立了方法学基线，有助于开发可扩展、可解释且具有考古学意义的鸟类遗存分析工具。

Original Abstract: Artificial intelligence has shown considerable potential for archaeological applications, yet its use in zooarchaeology remains limited, particularly for the identification of avian skeletal remains. This study presents a proof-of-concept multimodal framework that integrates convolutional neural network-based image analysis with osteometric measurements for the classification of bird bones. Using a dataset of more than 10,000 images from multiple museum and research collections, two classification tasks were investigated: skeletal element identification and family-level taxonomic classification. Prior to classification, images were automatically segmented using a two-stage pipeline combining BiRefNet and SAM2. Visual features extracted with a pre-trained EfficientNet_V2_S backbone were fused with standardized morphometric data through a feature-level multimodal architecture. The model achieved 86% accuracy on the test set for bone-type classification, demonstrating reliable recognition of skeletal elements. Family-level classification proved more challenging, reaching 51% top-1 accuracy but 75% top-3 accuracy, indicating that correct taxa were frequently included among the most probable predictions. These results demonstrate the feasibility of combining visual and morphometric information within a unified deep-learning framework and establish a methodological baseline for future AI-assisted zooarchaeological identification. The approach contributes to ongoing efforts to develop scalable, interpretable, and archaeologically meaningful tools for the study of avian remains.

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能在考古应用中已展现出相当大的潜力，但其在动物考古学中的使用仍然有限，尤其是在鸟类骨骼遗存的识别方面。本研究提出了一种概念验证的多模态框架，该框架将基于卷积神经网络的图像分析与骨学测量相结合，用于鸟类骨骼的分类。利用来自多个博物馆和研究机构收藏的超过10,000张图像的数据集，研究了两个分类任务：骨骼元素识别和科级分类。在分类之前，图像通过一个结合BiRefNet和SAM2的两阶段流水线进行自动分割。使用预训练的EfficientNet_V2_S骨干网络提取的视觉特征，通过特征级多模态架构与标准化形态测量数据相融合。该模型在测试集上的骨片类型分类准确率达到86%，展示了可靠的骨骼元素识别能力。科级分类更具挑战性，top-1准确率为51%，但top-3准确率达到75%，表明正确分类常出现在最可能的预测结果中。这些结果证明了在统一深度学习框架内结合视觉与形态测量信息的可行性，并为未来AI辅助的动物考古学识别建立了方法学基线。该方法有助于持续推进可扩展、可解释且具有考古学意义的鸟类遗存分析工具的开发。

</details>


### [54] [StatePlay: State-Aware Game World Models for Mechanics-Consistent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26754)
*Zijun Lin,Zeqing Wang,Cheston Tan,Bihan Wen,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StatePlay，一种状态感知的游戏世界模型，通过联合预测视觉内容和游戏状态来生成符合游戏机制的画面，采用混合Transformer架构实现跨模态交互。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型仅基于像素生成视觉内容，忽略了游戏依赖精确内部状态（如血量、技能计时器）的显式机制，导致生成的画面虽视觉合理但违反底层游戏规则。

Method: 采用混合Transformer（MoT）架构，分别维护视觉和状态表示并支持跨模态交互，使预测的状态指导帧生成；每个分支使用适配其模态的独立优化目标。

Result: 状态预测的平均归一化L1距离低于0.06；相比无显式状态建模的模型，生成游戏序列的机制保真度提升了18.6%。

Conclusion: 状态感知的游戏世界建模至关重要，推动游戏生成从像素级真实感向完整且机制保真的方向发展。

Original Abstract: Recent game world models can generate visually realistic and interactive environments conditioned on player actions. However, games are not defined by pixels alone; they are governed by explicit mechanics, namely state-dependent rules that control health reduction, skill activation, and game termination. These mechanics depend on precise internal states, such as health points, skill meters, and timers, which are tightly coupled with visual observations and determine how gameplay evolves. Without modeling these state dynamics, existing game world models may generate visually plausible rollouts but violate the underlying game rules. In this paper, we propose StatePlay, a novel state-aware game world model that jointly predicts visual content and game states to promote mechanics-consistent generation. StatePlay adopts a mixture-of-transformers (MoT)-style architecture that preserves specialized visual and state representations while enabling cross-modal interaction, allowing predicted states to guide frame generation. Each branch is further optimized with a distinct objective suited to its modality. Experiments show that StatePlay achieves an average normalized L1 distance below 0.06 for state prediction. Furthermore, compared with models without explicit state modeling, our method improves mechanics fidelity in generated game rollouts by 18.6%. Overall, our work highlights the importance of state-aware game world modeling and advances beyond pixel-level realism toward complete and mechanically faithful game generation.

中文摘要翻译: 近期游戏世界模型能够根据玩家动作生成视觉逼真且可交互的环境。然而，游戏并非仅由像素定义，而是受显式机制——即控制血量减少、技能激活和游戏终止的状态依赖规则——所支配。这些机制依赖于精确的内部状态（如血量、技能条和计时器），这些状态与视觉观察紧密耦合，决定了游戏进程的演变。若不对此类状态动态进行建模，现有游戏世界模型可能生成视觉合理的序列，但违反了底层游戏规则。本文提出StatePlay，一种新颖的状态感知游戏世界模型，它联合预测视觉内容和游戏状态以促进机制一致的生成。StatePlay采用混合Transformer（MoT）架构，在保持专业化的视觉和状态表征的同时实现跨模态交互，使预测状态能够指导帧生成。每个分支进一步使用适合其模态的独立目标进行优化。实验表明，StatePlay在状态预测上的平均归一化L1距离低于0.06。此外，与无显式状态建模的模型相比，我们的方法在生成游戏序列中的机制保真度提升了18.6%。总体而言，我们的工作凸显了状态感知游戏世界建模的重要性，推动游戏生成从像素级真实感迈向完整且机制保真的境界。

</details>


### [55] [Long-Tailed 3D Point Cloud Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26763)
*Jiahao You,Xu Han,Jinfeng Xu,Xianzhi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次提出面向长尾分布的点云数据集蒸馏方法，通过自适应合成预算分配和3D长尾分布匹配两大模块，在ShapeNet55上分类精度提升7.0个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有点云数据集蒸馏方法仅关注几何和表示特征，忽略了点云数据集中普遍存在的类别分布不平衡（长尾分布）问题。

Method: 提出两个核心模块：1) 自适应合成预算分配（Adaptive Synthetic Budgeting）：根据类别数量和额外合成样本的预期收益分配类级合成预算；2) 3D长尾分布匹配（3D Long-Tailed Distribution Matching）：通过全局-局部特征对齐（Global-Local Feature Alignment）保留全局类分布和多样化类内结构，以及先验感知监督（Prior-Aware Supervision）提供类依赖的专家监督，使尾类样本保持可识别性同时维持头类多样模式。

Result: 在ShapeNet55数据集上，相较于现有最优方法，分类准确率提升7.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模长尾类分布的点云数据集蒸馏方法有效提升了分类性能，验证了处理数据不平衡问题在点云蒸馏中的重要性。

Original Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses large-scale datasets into compact synthetic sets while preserving their training utility, enabling efficient 3D point cloud training. Current point cloud dataset distillation methods only tackle geometric and representation challenges while ignoring the distributional imbalance prevalent in point cloud datasets where both training and test splits follow long-tailed class distributions. To our knowledge, we present the first study on long-tailed point cloud dataset distillation. Rather than focusing primarily on geometric and representation properties or simply constructing a class-balanced synthetic set, our framework explicitly accounts for long-tailed class distributions via two core modules. First, we design Adaptive Synthetic Budgeting to allocate class-wise synthetic budgets according to class quantity and the expected benefit of additional synthetic samples. Given the allocated budgets, we further design 3D Long-Tailed Distribution Matching to optimize synthetic point clouds through Global-Local Feature Alignment and Prior-Aware Supervision. The former preserves both global class distributions and diverse intra-class structures, while the latter provides class-dependent expert supervision to keep tail-class samples recognizable while maintaining diverse head-class patterns. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, lifting classification accuracy by 7.0 points on ShapeNet55 against state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 数据集蒸馏将大规模数据集压缩为紧凑的合成集，同时保留其训练效用，从而实现高效的3D点云训练。当前的点云数据集蒸馏方法仅解决了几何和表示层面的挑战，而忽略了点云数据集中普遍存在的分布不平衡问题——训练集和测试集均遵循长尾类分布。据我们所知，本文提出了首个关于长尾点云数据集蒸馏的研究。我们的框架并非主要关注几何和表示属性，也非简单地构建类平衡合成集，而是通过两个核心模块显式地考虑长尾类分布。首先，我们设计了自适应合成预算分配（Adaptive Synthetic Budgeting），根据类别数量和额外合成样本的预期收益来分配类级合成预算。在分配的预算基础上，我们进一步设计了3D长尾分布匹配（3D Long-Tailed Distribution Matching），通过全局-局部特征对齐（Global-Local Feature Alignment）和先验感知监督（Prior-Aware Supervision）来优化合成点云。前者保留了全局类分布和多样化的类内结构，而后者则提供类依赖的专家监督，使尾类样本保持可识别性，同时维持头类的多样模式。大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性，在ShapeNet55数据集上，相较于现有最优方法，分类准确率提升了7.0个百分点。

</details>


### [56] [Searching for Robust Augmentations to Improve Out-of-Domain Generalization in Dermoscopic Skin Cancer Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26765)
*Alexander Kozachok,Ilya Latyshev,Evgeny Karpulevich,Elena Kozachok,Egor Ushakov,Oleg Samovarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了数据增强对皮肤镜病变分类器在域迁移下鲁棒性的影响，发现模拟真实域迁移的增强策略（尤其是混合策略和光度变换）能显著提升OOD泛化性能（AUC提升+0.053），但指出策略选择与评估同源会导致偏差，需要源分离的选择协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 皮肤镜病变分类器在面临成像设备、光照和采集伪影等域迁移时准确率显著下降，现有研究缺乏对数据增强如何提升分类器在域外（OOD）泛化鲁棒性的系统分析。

Method: 以ConvNeXt-Large为骨干网络，在ISIC Archive多源数据集上搜索单一增强、光度组合及复合增强策略，以ROC-AUC为指标，在病变ID级别划分训练/测试集，并使用HAM10000和ISIC 2019-2020作为主要源分离的OOD测试集进行验证。

Result: 混合策略（mix policy）在OOD上增益最大，光度变换是最有效的OOD操作；在扩展的留出源池上AUC提升+0.053（95% CI +0.045至+0.061，p<0.001），四个训练种子下一致；小型临床集合上单检查点灵敏度从0.591提升至0.818，但基于22例恶性病例且未跨种子保持。

Conclusion: 建模真实域迁移源的增强比最大化域内准确率更重要；但由于增强策略与评估集同源，该效果量存在偏差，需采用源分离的选择协议才能获得无偏估计。

Original Abstract: Background/Objectives: Dermoscopic skin lesion classifiers often lose accuracy under domain shift across imaging devices, illumination, and capture artifacts. We study how data augmentation improves the robustness of a binary malignant-versus-non-malignant classifier, with emphasis on out-of-domain (OOD) generalization. Methods: Single augmentations, photometric combinations, and composite policies were searched on a multi-source ISIC Archive collection with Derm7pt, using a ConvNeXt-Large backbone and ROC-AUC. Splits were made at the lesion-ID level, and HAM10000 and ISIC 2019-2020 were held out as a predominantly source-disjoint OOD test. Results: The largest OOD gain came from the mix policy, and photometric transformations dominated the most useful OOD operations. On an expanded pool from the same held-out sources the gain was +0.053 (95% CI +0.045 to +0.061, p<0.001), consistent across four training seeds (per-seed ROC-AUC: baseline 0.761-0.775, mix 0.806-0.829). On a small independent clinical collection, single-checkpoint sensitivity rose from 0.591 to 0.818, but this rested on 22 malignant cases and did not persist across seeds. Conclusions: Augmentations modelling real sources of domain shift can matter more than maximizing in-domain accuracy. Because the policy was selected on the same sources used to evaluate it, a source-disjoint selection protocol is needed before this effect size can be read as unbiased.

中文摘要翻译: 背景/目标：皮肤镜病变分类器在面临跨成像设备、光照和采集伪影的域迁移时常常损失准确率。我们研究数据增强如何提高二元恶性与非恶性分类器的鲁棒性，重点关注域外（OOD）泛化。方法：在包含Derm7pt的多源ISIC Archive数据集上，以ConvNeXt-Large为骨干网络，使用ROC-AUC指标搜索单一增强、光度组合及复合策略。按病变ID级别划分数据，并留出HAM10000和ISIC 2019-2020作为主要源分离的OOD测试集。结果：最大的OOD增益来自混合策略（mix policy），光度变换主导了最有用的OOD操作。在来自相同留出源的扩展池上，增益为+0.053（95% CI +0.045至+0.061，p<0.001），四个训练种子下结果一致（每种子ROC-AUC：基线0.761-0.775，混合策略0.806-0.829）。在一个小型独立临床数据集上，单检查点灵敏度从0.591提升至0.818，但该结果基于22例恶性病例且未跨种子保持。结论：建模真实域迁移源的增强可能比最大化域内准确率更重要。由于增强策略是在用于评估的同一源上选择的，因此需要源分离的选择协议，才能将该效果量解读为无偏估计。

</details>


### [57] [See2Think: Do Multimodal Models Really Use Intermediate Visual States?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26769)
*Siyu Yan,Zhuoran Yan,Haiying Xu,Panhao Zhou,Jingyu Chen,Chenhao Ji,Shuo Cao,Yongheng Zhang,Haoze Liu,Siyu Zhang,Xiwen Gu,Yihao Liu,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出See2Think统一评估框架，包含1200个视觉依赖问题的基准（See2ThinkBench）和视觉行动思维链（VAoT）诊断方法，发现多模态大模型的视觉推理高度依赖模型与环境，忠实渲染是最大瓶颈，且在视觉状态被干扰时准确率下降超10个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准存在两个局限：任务集合覆盖狭窄且部分样本可纯文本求解；评估仅关注最终答案，不诊断中间视觉状态（如草图、标注、工具、中间图像）的生成、渲染和使用过程。

Method: 构建See2ThinkBench（涵盖12类任务、1200个开放型视觉依赖问题，横跨2D结构化、3D场景和真实世界推理），提出Visual Action-of-Thought（VAoT）方法记录文本思维、视觉动作、渲染状态及后续推理，在四种受控推理设置下评估模型。

Result: 视觉推理强烈依赖模型和环境，无单一设置持续占优；过程分析表明模型通常能选择相关视觉操作，但忠实渲染是清晰瓶颈，高反馈采纳率不一定转化为准确率提升；在任务相关的损坏反馈下，模型表现出对视觉状态的行为依赖，受控干预中准确率下降超10个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型的视觉推理存在严重缺陷，视觉状态的忠实渲染是当前最关键的瓶颈环节。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly use sketches, annotations, tools, and intermediate images during reasoning, but it remains unclear whether they truly rely on these visual states. Existing benchmarks are limited both by task collections with narrow coverage or partially text-solvable samples and by evaluations that emphasize final answers without diagnosing how intermediate visual states are generated, rendered, and used. We introduce See2Think, a unified evaluation framework comprising See2ThinkBench and Visual Action-of-Thought (VAoT). See2ThinkBench contains 1,200 open-ended, visually dependent problems across 12 task categories spanning 2D structured, 3D scene, and real-world reasoning. VAoT records textual thoughts, visual actions, rendered states, and subsequent reasoning under four controlled inference settings.
