Evaluating representative proprietary and open-source multimodal models, we find that visual reasoning is strongly model- and environment-dependent, with no single setting consistently dominating across tasks. Process analysis further shows that models usually select relevant visual operations, while faithful rendering remains the clearest bottleneck and high feedback uptake does not necessarily translate into accuracy gains. Under task-relevant corrupted feedback, models exhibit behavioral dependence on visual states, with accuracy dropping by over 10 percentage points in controlled interventions.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型在推理过程中越来越多地使用草图、标注、工具和中间图像，但目前尚不清楚它们是否真正依赖这些视觉状态。现有基准存在两方面局限：任务集合覆盖狭窄或部分样本可纯文本求解，且评估仅关注最终答案，未诊断中间视觉状态如何生成、渲染和使用。我们提出See2Think，一个统一的评估框架，包含See2ThinkBench和视觉行动思维链（VAoT）。See2ThinkBench包含1200个开放型、视觉依赖的问题，涵盖12个任务类别，横跨二维结构化、三维场景和真实世界推理。VAoT在四种受控推理设置下记录文本思维、视觉动作、渲染状态及后续推理。评估代表性闭源和开源多模态模型后发现，视觉推理强烈依赖于模型和环境，没有单一设置能在所有任务上持续占优。过程分析进一步表明，模型通常能选择相关的视觉操作，而忠实渲染仍是最明显的瓶颈，高反馈采纳率并不一定转化为准确率提升。在任务相关的损坏反馈下，模型表现出对视觉状态的行为依赖，受控干预中准确率下降超过10个百分点。

</details>


### [58] [PRISM-Net: Patient-specific reference-guided inter-breast symmetry matching for three-class breast DCE-MRI classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26799)
*Boya Zhang,Shuaiwen Zhou,Di Kong,Mingxu Wang,Wenbiao Du,Yiman Zhong,Yuexin Duan,Xiawei Yue,Liuquan Cheng,Xiru Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM-Net 是一种无配准的双侧乳腺DCE-MRI分类框架，通过对侧乳腺特征作为患者特异性参考进行背景感知表示学习，有效提升了无病灶、良性、恶性三类乳腺分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有乳腺DCE-MRI AI方法主要聚焦于单侧或病灶中心分析，而双侧方法对空间自适应跨乳腺对应关系的显式建模有限。患者特异性背景变异是分类任务中的重要混淆因素，亟需一种能利用对侧乳腺作为患者自身参考进行背景感知学习的方法。

Method: PRISM-Net 是一种无配准的双侧框架，通过双侧特征匹配和不对称注意力机制建立自适应跨乳腺对应关系，增强对判别性不对称模式的表示学习，实现背景感知的表示学习。

Result: 在ODELIA数据集上，分布内测试集的Macro AUC、Micro AUC和二次加权kappa分别为84.11±2.33、90.64±1.61、60.94±5.64；在外部机构上分别为68.51±4.54、80.74±2.68、43.45±7.10，均优于基线方法。消融实验证实双侧关系建模和不对称感知重加权均有助于提升分类性能。

Conclusion: 患者特异性双侧参考建模是一种具有临床可行性的DCE-MRI判读策略，通过显式建模背景复杂性提升不对称模式判别能力。

Original Abstract: Breast DCE-MRI AI is increasingly being explored for breast-level classification of no-lesion, benign, and malignant findings, beyond conventional lesion-centered diagnosis. Within this broader diagnostic scope, however, patient-specific background variability remains a major source of imaging confounding across classification tasks. Existing approaches predominantly focus on unilateral or lesion-centric analysis, whereas bilateral methods offer limited explicit modeling of spatially adaptive cross-breast correspondence. We propose PRISM-Net, a registration-free bilateral framework that leverages contralateral breast features as patient-specific references for background-aware representation learning. PRISM-Net integrates bilateral feature matching and asymmetry-aware attention to establish adaptive inter-breast correspondence and enhance representations of discriminative asymmetric patterns. On ODELIA, Macro AUC, Micro AUC, and quadratic weighted kappa were $84.11 \pm 2.33$, $90.64 \pm 1.61$, and $60.94 \pm 5.64$ on the in-distribution test set, and $68.51 \pm 4.54$, $80.74 \pm 2.68$, and $43.45 \pm 7.10$ on the held-out institution, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baseline methods across the primary evaluation metrics. PRISM-Net further demonstrated performance on independent institutional and background-complexity evaluations. Ablation experiments revealed that both bilateral relation modeling and asymmetry-aware reweighting contributed to improved classification performance. These findings highlight patient-specific bilateral reference modeling as a clinically grounded strategy for DCE-MRI interpretation, improving asymmetric pattern discrimination through explicit modeling of background complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 乳腺DCE-MRI人工智能正越来越多地被探索用于超越传统病灶中心诊断的乳腺水平分类（无病灶、良性、恶性）。然而，在这一更广泛的诊断范围内，患者特异性背景变异仍然是跨分类任务的主要影像混淆来源。现有方法主要聚焦于单侧或病灶中心分析，而双侧方法对空间自适应跨乳腺对应关系的显式建模有限。我们提出PRISM-Net，一种无配准的双侧框架，利用对侧乳腺特征作为患者特异性参考进行背景感知表示学习。PRISM-Net整合了双侧特征匹配和不对称感知注意力机制，以建立自适应的跨乳腺对应关系并增强判别性不对称模式的表示。在ODELIA数据集上，分布内测试集的Macro AUC、Micro AUC和二次加权kappa分别为84.11±2.33、90.64±1.61和60.94±5.64，在外部留存机构上分别为68.51±4.54、80.74±2.68和43.45±7.10，在主要评估指标上均优于所评估的基线方法。PRISM-Net还在独立机构和背景复杂度评估中展示了性能。消融实验表明，双侧关系建模和不对称感知重加权均有助于提升分类性能。这些发现表明，患者特异性双侧参考建模是一种具有临床可行性的DCE-MRI判读策略，通过显式建模背景复杂性提升不对称模式判别能力。

</details>


### [59] [DistillAlign: Coordinating Mode Covering and Mode Seeking in Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26811)
*Jiaxing Li,Kai Zou,Cindy Zhou,Kaichen Huang,Junyao Gao,Zile Wang,Yang Liu,Bin Liu,Bo An,Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从分布对齐的视角重新审视了自回归视频蒸馏中的多阶段流水线，指出现有方法在初始化与DMD阶段存在目标分布不一致的问题，并提出联合蒸馏方法，结合DMD的mode-seeking与一致性蒸馏的mode-covering约束，有效提升了生成质量、覆盖度和多样性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自回归视频蒸馏方法采用基于DMD的多阶段流水线，但初始化阶段与DMD阶段追求不同的目标分布，且仅依赖视觉指标（如VBench）评估中间学生模型。作者从分布视角发现，由于DMD损失具有mode-seeking特性，好的初始化应匹配目标DMD教师的模式覆盖范围，而非仅追求高视觉质量；某些初始化虽精度高但覆盖度低，导致后续精炼效果不佳。

Method: 提出联合蒸馏（Joint Distillation）方法，将DMD的mode-seeking目标与一致性蒸馏（Consistency Distillation）的mode-covering约束相结合，在单一训练过程中同时兼顾分布对齐的精度与覆盖度。

Result: 实验表明该方法在生成质量、覆盖度和多样性上均有提升；即便使用Wan-1.3B DMD教师，也能超越使用Wan-14B教师精炼的基线方法，凸显了分布对齐在自回归视频蒸馏中的重要性。

Conclusion: 自回归视频蒸馏中分布对齐至关重要，所提出的联合蒸馏方法能有效解决现有方法中初始化与DMD阶段目标分布不一致、以及DMD后期训练覆盖度降低的问题，实现更优的生成性能。

Original Abstract: Existing autoregressive video distillation methods commonly adopt a Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-based multi-stage pipeline. However, they typically decouple the initialization and DMD stages -- which then pursue different target distributions -- and judge the intermediate student mainly by visual scores such as VBench. In this paper, we revisit this design from a distributional perspective. Given the mode-seeking nature of the distribution matching loss, a good initialization should match the mode coverage of the target DMD teacher, rather than merely pursuing high quality. To analyze this, we introduce a distributional evaluation protocol that measures precision and coverage between student and teacher distributions in a shared latent space. It exposes differences hidden by visual scores: some initializations reach high precision but low coverage, leading to suboptimal refinement, while mode-covering ones preserve broader support. Furthermore, even when the target distributions are aligned, DMD's reverse-KL objective can still drive the student toward high-probability teacher regions in late training, reducing coverage and diversity. To address this, we propose joint distillation, which combines DMD's mode-seeking objective with a Consistency Distillation-based mode-covering constraint. Experiments show that our method improves generation quality, coverage, and diversity; notably, even with a Wan-1.3B DMD teacher, it outperforms baselines refined with Wan-14B, underscoring the importance of distributional alignment in autoregressive video distillation.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的自回归视频蒸馏方法普遍采用基于分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）的多阶段流水线。然而，它们通常将初始化阶段与DMD阶段解耦——这两个阶段追求不同的目标分布——并主要依据VBench等视觉分数来评判中间学生模型。本文从分布视角重新审视了这一设计。鉴于分布匹配损失具有mode-seeking特性，一个好的初始化应匹配目标DMD教师的模式覆盖范围，而非仅仅追求高质量。为分析此问题，我们引入了一个分布评估协议，在共享隐空间中衡量学生分布与教师分布之间的精度和覆盖度。该协议揭示了视觉分数所隐藏的差异：某些初始化达到了高精度但低覆盖度，导致精炼效果不佳；而覆盖模式的初始化则保留了更广泛的支撑集。此外，即使目标分布对齐，DMD的反向KL目标在训练后期仍可能将学生推向教师的高概率区域，降低覆盖度和多样性。为解决此问题，我们提出了联合蒸馏方法，将DMD的mode-seeking目标与基于一致性蒸馏的mode-covering约束相结合。实验表明，我们的方法提升了生成质量、覆盖度和多样性；值得注意的是，即使使用Wan-1.3B DMD教师，它也优于使用Wan-14B精炼的基线方法，凸显了分布对齐在自回归视频蒸馏中的重要性。

</details>


### [60] [Ripple: Real-Time Streaming Audio-Video Generation With Cross-Modal Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26818)
*Yanbo Ding,Zhizhi Guo,Quanyue Song,Yishan He,Zhixiang He,Yongxiang Li,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ripple系统，通过跨模态循环记忆机制实现实时联合音视频生成，在480P分辨率下达到~28 FPS，支持长序列连贯生成，性能优于现有离线和在线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音视频生成模型质量高但延迟大，无法用于实时应用；已有的流式方法成本高且不支持长序列生成。因此需要一种既能高效流式推理又能保持长期上下文一致性的实时联合音视频生成系统。

Method: 提出Ripple系统，核心包括：(1) 固定长度滑动窗口注意力结合模态特定的记忆状态（持续总结音视频上下文）；(2) 跨模态记忆交互增强音视频同步；(3) 三阶段训练策略：双向教师模型适配块级因果注意力（模拟记忆）→ 端到端蒸馏优化记忆构建与交互 → 在线强化后训练用于流式音视频生成。

Result: Ripple在480P分辨率下达到~28 FPS（远超教师模型），在短视频和长视频基准上均优于现有离线和在线联合音视频生成方法，且能生成连贯的长序列内容。

Conclusion: Ripple通过跨模态循环记忆机制成功实现了实时、长序列连贯的联合音视频生成，在效率和质量上均超越现有方法，为实时音视频生成应用提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Audio-video generative models achieve impressive quality but suffer from high latency, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Although several streaming audio-video generation methods have been proposed, they remain costly and fail to support long-form generation. To address this, we propose \textbf{Ripple}, a real-time joint audio-video generation system with a cross-modal recurrent memory mechanism. To enable efficient streaming inference while preserving long-term context, Ripple combines a fixed-length sliding-window attention with modality-specific memory states that continuously summarize audio and video context. Cross-modal memory interaction is further introduced to enhance audio-visual synchronization. To learn this memory-augmented model effectively, we devise a three-stage training recipe: (1) adapting a bidirectional audio-video teacher to block-wise causal attention with simulated memory, (2) optimizing the memory construction and interaction pipeline through end-to-end distillation, and (3) applying online reinforcement post-training tailored for streaming audio-video generation. As a result, Ripple achieves ~28 FPS at 480P resolution, over faster than the teacher, while capable of coherent long-form generation. Extensive experiments on both short-video and long-video benchmarks demonstrate our superior performance over existing offline and online joint audio-video generation methods.

中文摘要翻译: 音频-视频生成模型虽然取得了令人印象深刻的质量，但存在高延迟问题，使其不适用于实时应用。尽管已有多种流式音频-视频生成方法被提出，它们仍然成本高昂且无法支持长序列生成。为了解决这一问题，我们提出\textbf{Ripple}，一种具有跨模态循环记忆机制的实时联合音频-视频生成系统。为了实现高效的流式推理同时保持长期上下文，Ripple将固定长度的滑动窗口注意力与持续总结音频和视频上下文的模态特定记忆状态相结合。进一步引入跨模态记忆交互以增强音视频同步。为了有效学习这种记忆增强模型，我们设计了三阶段训练方案：(1) 将双向音频-视频教师模型适配到带有模拟记忆的块级因果注意力；(2) 通过端到端蒸馏优化记忆构建与交互流程；(3) 针对流式音频-视频生成应用在线强化后训练。最终，Ripple在480P分辨率下达到约28 FPS，远快于教师模型，同时能够生成连贯的长序列内容。在短视频和长视频基准上的大量实验表明，我们在现有离线和在线联合音频-视频生成方法中均取得了优越性能。

</details>


### [61] [BATS: Resource-Efficient Volumetric Segmentation with Boundary-Aware Mixed-Resolution Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26829)
*David Hagerman,Roman Naeem,Fredrik Kahl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BATS是一种3D医学图像分割架构，通过在预测边界附近集中精细分辨率处理，使用边界感知令牌选择和混合分辨率层次结构，在保持高精度的同时显著降低GPU内存消耗（>53%）并加速推理（最高30%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高性能3D体素分割模型依赖密集多尺度特征图，导致激活内存和推理成本过高，亟需一种能在保持精度的同时降低计算开销的方法。

Method: 提出边界感知令牌选择（BATS）架构：1）密集边界预测器定位需要额外分辨率的区域；2）精细优先的上下文级联构建输入依赖的混合分辨率层次结构，在边界、薄结构和小目标周围保留精细令牌，同质区域使用粗表示；3）在每个分辨率级别独立预测边界相关性，防止粗尺度错误抑制细尺度证据；4）父簇注意力将层次化祖先令牌注入局部注意力邻域，提供跨尺度上下文。

Result: 在5个公共CT和MRI数据集上，BATS的LiTS Dice分数达到最高，平均Dice分数比最强密集基线MedNeXt-L低0.37以内。在KiTS、LiTS和BraTS上，峰值GPU内存减少超过53%，KiTS和LiTS推理速度提升高达30%。

Conclusion: 混合分辨率处理能持续节省内存，运行时间和精度的提升取决于数据集边界密度；该方法在内存效率和推理速度上优势显著，精度与密集基线相当。

Original Abstract: Many high-performing volumetric segmentation models maintain dense multi-scale feature maps, leading to high activation memory and inference cost. We present BATS (Boundary-Aware Token Selection), a 3D medical image segmentation architecture that concentrates fine-resolution processing near predicted class boundaries. A dense boundary predictor identifies where additional resolution is needed, while a fine-first context cascade constructs an input-dependent mixed-resolution hierarchy. Homogeneous regions are represented coarsely, with finer tokens retained around boundaries, thin structures, and small targets. The sparse hierarchy is refined and rasterised into a dense segmentation.
  BATS predicts boundary relevance independently at every resolution level, preventing an erroneous coarse-scale decision from suppressing fine-scale evidence. Parent cluster attention further injects hierarchical ancestor tokens into local attention neighbourhoods, providing cross-scale context without dense multi-scale feature maps or cross-scale neighbour search.
  We evaluate BATS on five public CT and MRI datasets using the standardised nnU-Net Revisited protocol. BATS achieves the highest LiTS Dice among the compared methods and averages within 0.37 Dice points of the strongest dense baseline, MedNeXt-L, across the five datasets. Relative to MedNeXt-L, it reduces peak allocated GPU memory by more than 53% on KiTS, LiTS, and BraTS. Inference is up to 30% faster on KiTS and LiTS, which retain fewer tokens, but slower on the more token-dense BraTS. Mixed-resolution processing therefore provides consistent memory savings, while runtime and accuracy gains depend on dataset boundary density.

中文摘要翻译: 许多高性能体素分割模型维护密集的多尺度特征图，导致激活内存和推理成本高昂。我们提出BATS（边界感知令牌选择），一种3D医学图像分割架构，将精细分辨率处理集中在预测的类别边界附近。密集边界预测器定位需要额外分辨率的区域，同时精细优先的上下文级联构建一个输入依赖的混合分辨率层次结构。同质区域被粗略表示，而边界、薄结构和小目标周围保留更精细的令牌。稀疏层次结构经过精炼并栅格化为密集分割。BATS在每个分辨率级别独立预测边界相关性，防止错误的粗尺度决策抑制细尺度证据。父簇注意力进一步将层次化祖先令牌注入局部注意力邻域，提供跨尺度上下文，无需密集多尺度特征图或跨尺度邻域搜索。我们在五个公共CT和MRI数据集上使用标准化的nnU-Net Revisited协议评估BATS。BATS在所比较方法中取得了最高的LiTS Dice分数，并且在五个数据集上的平均Dice分数与最强密集基线MedNeXt-L相差不超过0.37。相对于MedNeXt-L，它在KiTS、LiTS和BraTS上减少了超过53%的峰值分配GPU内存。在保留较少令牌的KiTS和LiTS上推理速度提升高达30%，但在令牌密度更高的BraTS上较慢。因此，混合分辨率处理提供了持续的内存节省，而运行时和精度的提升取决于数据集边界密度。

</details>


### [62] [ICDAR 2026 Competition on Information Extraction from Atomic Layer Deposition/Etching (ALD/E) Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26848)
*Fahad Ahmed,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sci-ImageMiner基准数据集和竞赛，用于评估多模态AI在科学图表理解与推理方面的能力，发现现有最先进模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理（尤其是视觉问答）方面存在明显不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 科学图表理解需要融合视觉感知与领域特定推理，而现有基准在端到端、专家标注的综合性评估方面存在不足，因此需要构建更全面、更严格的评估平台来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了一个包含专家标注的Sci-ImageMiner基准数据集，涵盖四项互补的端到端任务，并组织了一场社区驱动的竞赛（2026年1月9日至4月8日），吸引了68名活跃参与者和1,263次提交。

Result: 最先进的多模态模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理（尤其是视觉问答）方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: Sci-ImageMiner基准和竞赛为科学图表理解与推理研究建立了一个严格的评估平台，揭示了当前模型的局限性，并为改进领域感知的多模态AI系统指明了方向和机遇。

Original Abstract: Scientific figure comprehension and reasoning using multimodal AI requires integrating visual perception with domain-specific reasoning to extract meaningful knowledge, often not presented in the text of a research publication. The Sci-ImageMiner benchmark dataset, accompanied by a community-driven competition, raises the bar over prior scientific competitions by curating a comprehensive, expert-annotated dataset across four end-to-end complementary tasks. The competition attracted 68 active participants and 1,263 public/private submissions from 9th January 2026 to 8th April 2026. Our results show that state-of-the-art multimodal models perform well on classification and summarization tasks but struggle with data extraction and scientific reasoning, particularly in visual question-answering. These findings reveal key limitations and highlight challenges and opportunities for improving domain-aware multimodal AI systems. Overall, the Sci-ImageMiner benchmark and competition establish a rigorous platform for advancing research in scientific figure comprehension and reasoning and demonstrate the potential of state-of-the-art approaches for a challenging and complex research area.

中文摘要翻译: 利用多模态AI进行科学图表理解和推理，需要将视觉感知与领域特定的推理相结合，以提取有意义的知识，而这些知识通常不在研究论文的文本中呈现。Sci-ImageMiner基准数据集及其配套的社区驱动竞赛，通过整理一个涵盖四项端到端互补任务的、由专家标注的综合性数据集，提升了以往科学竞赛的标准。该竞赛从2026年1月9日到2026年4月8日吸引了68名活跃参与者和1,263次公开/私有提交。我们的结果表明，最先进的多模态模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理（尤其是视觉问答）方面存在困难。这些发现揭示了关键的局限性，并突出了改进领域感知多模态AI系统的挑战与机遇。总体而言，Sci-ImageMiner基准和竞赛为推进科学图表理解与推理研究建立了一个严格的平台，并展示了最先进方法在这一具有挑战性和复杂性的研究领域中的潜力。

</details>


### [63] [SCALPEL: Semantic Cross-modal Alignment via LLM-Powered Encoder Learning for Medical Vision-Language Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26885)
*Yunzhan Fu,Enyu Bao,Xiangyu Shen,Yihao Wu,Chunbo Jiang,Fangli Guan,Liqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCALPEL提出了一种语义跨模态对齐框架，通过LLM驱动的编码器学习，解决医学视觉-语言预训练中生成式LLM的各向异性坍缩、大batch端到端训练的内存开销和医学幻觉三大瓶颈，在跨模态检索、零样本分类和医学VQA任务上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学VLP框架受限于轻量级文本编码器的有限上下文窗口和浅层表示能力，难以处理冗长且术语密集的临床报告。虽然引入医学LLM带来了强大的临床推理能力，但引出三个关键瓶颈：(i) 生成式LLM在标准对比学习目标下的各向异性表示坍缩；(ii) 端到端联合训练中大batch size带来的巨大内存开销；(iii) 忽略细粒度解剖侧向性和否定修饰词的对比损失导致的医学幻觉。

Method: 提出SCALPEL框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 临床报告对比微调，通过领域特异性临床文本适应将生成式LLM转化为各向同性编码器；(2) 非对称对齐策略，利用离线特征缓存实现高效训练；(3) 解剖-否定感知目标函数，显式惩罚涉及侧向性混淆或错误否定的不匹配图文对。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert和IU X-Ray基准上，SCALPEL在跨模态检索、零样本疾病分类和医学视觉问答任务中均达到最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过LLM驱动的编码器学习与细粒度语义对齐目标，SCALPEL有效解决了医学VLP中LLM集成的关键瓶颈，为医学多模态表示学习提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Vision-language pre-training (VLP) serves as a cornerstone for medical multimodal representation learning. However, existing medical VLP frameworks are often constrained by the limited context windows and shallow representational capacities of lightweight text encoders when processing lengthy, terminology-dense clinical reports. While integrating medical large language models (LLMs) offers unprecedented clinical reasoning capabilities, it introduces three major bottlenecks: (i) the anisotropic representational collapse of generative LLMs under standard contrastive objectives, (ii) the prohibitive memory overhead of joint end-to-end training with large batch sizes, and (iii) the medical hallucinations induced by vanilla contrastive losses that ignore fine-grained anatomical laterality and negation modifiers. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{SCALPEL}, a \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{A}lignment framework via \textbf{L}LM-\textbf{P}owered \textbf{E}ncoder \textbf{L}earning. First, Clinical Report Contrastive fine-tuning converts a generative LLM into an isotropic encoder via domain-specific clinical text adaptation. Second, an asymmetric alignment strategy leverages offline feature caching to enable efficient training. Critically, we formulate an Anatomy-Negation Aware Objective that explicitly penalizes mismatched image-text pairs involving laterality confusion or false negations. Extensive experiments across MIMIC-CXR, CheXpert, and IU X-Ray benchmarks demonstrate that SCALPEL achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-modal retrieval, zero-shot disease classification and medical visual question answering.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言预训练（VLP）是医学多模态表示学习的基石。然而，现有的医学VLP框架在处理冗长且术语密集的临床报告时，往往受限于轻量级文本编码器的有限上下文窗口和浅层表示能力。虽然集成医学大语言模型（LLM）带来了前所未有的临床推理能力，但同时也引入了三个主要瓶颈：（i）生成式LLM在标准对比学习目标下的各向异性表示坍缩，（ii）大batch size下联合端到端训练带来的过高内存开销，以及（iii）忽略细粒度解剖侧向性和否定修饰词的普通对比损失所导致的医学幻觉。针对这些挑战，我们提出SCALPEL——一种基于LLM驱动编码器学习的语义跨模态对齐框架。首先，临床报告对比微调通过领域特异性临床文本适应将生成式LLM转化为各向同性编码器。其次，非对称对齐策略利用离线特征缓存实现高效训练。关键地，我们设计了解剖-否定感知目标函数，显式惩罚涉及侧向性混淆或错误否定的不匹配图文对。在MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert和IU X-Ray基准上的大量实验表明，SCALPEL在跨模态检索、零样本疾病分类和医学视觉问答任务中均取得了最优性能。

</details>


### [64] [Hearsay: Vision-Language Medical Diagnoses Without an Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26886)
*Siddharth Vohra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 前沿视觉-语言模型在没有输入医学图像时不会拒绝回答，而是编造诊断；这种编造并非随机，而是受患者人口统计学描述（年龄、种族、性别）系统性影响。研究揭示了两种结构性缺陷：结构化输出与文本不一致的“对冲模式”，以及特定疾病对探针词（如“皮肤痣”vs“皮肤病变”）的高度敏感。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证前沿VLM在面对缺失医学图像时是否会拒绝回答，并探究其编造诊断是否受到患者人口统计学属性（年龄、种族、性别）的系统性影响，从而揭示模型在临床部署中的潜在偏见与安全隐患。

Method: 仅向三个前沿VLM（Claude Opus-4.7、GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro）提供人口统计学描述（如“65岁白人男性”“32岁黑人女性”）和检查类型（胸部X光、脑MRI、皮肤科），但不提供任何实际医学图像，然后分析模型输出的诊断分布及其与人口统计学因素的关联。同时对比分析自由文本输出与结构化诊断字段的一致性，以及更换探针词（“皮肤痣”vs“皮肤病变”）对诊断结果的影响。

Result: 1. 所有模型均会编造诊断，且诊断结果随人口统计学描述系统性地变化。2. Claude对白人老年男性的皮肤痣几乎每次输出黑色素瘤；对年轻黑人女性的胸部X光频繁输出类肉瘤病。3. GPT-5.4的影响范围更广，在所有测试的人口统计学单元格中均有编造。4. 存在“对冲模式”：文本承认图像缺失，但结构化诊断字段仍给出疾病名称。5. Claude对“皮肤痣”的效果在换成“皮肤病变”后完全消失，而GPT-5.4的效果保持不变，表明这是多种不同的失败模式。

Conclusion: 可信赖的VLM在临床管线中的部署需要直接审计结构化输出通道，探针词敏感性应作为一级评估维度。模型在无图像时的编造行为具有系统性偏差，不能仅通过文本一致性来检测。

Original Abstract: When asked to describe a medical image that was never attached, frontier vision-language models do not abstain: they confabulate a diagnosis. We show that this confabulation is not random. It is structured by who the patient is said to be. Across chest X-ray, brain MRI, and dermatology, Claude Opus-4.7, GPT-5.4, and Gemini-3.1-Pro are each queried with only a demographic descriptor and no image, and changing the descriptor systematically shifts the diagnosis returned. Claude concentrates sharply: a 65-year-old white man asking about a skin mole receives Melanoma in nearly every response, and a 32-year-old Black woman asking about her chest X-ray receives a Sarcoidosis diagnosis whose reasoning reads "suspected, based on demographics and classic pattern.'' GPT-5.4's effect is broader, fabricating across every demographic cell we test, most conspicuously naming Sarcoidosis for young Black patients on chest X-ray. Two structural findings sharpen the problem. A hedged regime appears in which the prose acknowledges the missing image while the structured diagnosis field nevertheless names a disease, a dissociation invisible to prose-only audits. And Claude's dermatology effect collapses entirely when 'skin mole' is swapped for 'skin lesion' while GPT-5.4's is preserved, indicating that mirage is a family of distinct failure modes rather than a single phenomenon. Trustworthy VLM deployment in clinical pipelines requires auditing the structured output channel directly, and probe-word sensitivity should be treated as a first-class evaluation dimension

中文摘要翻译: 当被要求描述一张从未附上的医学图像时，前沿视觉-语言模型并不会拒绝回答：它们会编造出一个诊断。我们表明这种编造并非随机，而是受到所谓患者身份的系统性影响。在胸部X光、脑部MRI和皮肤科三个领域，分别对Claude Opus-4.7、GPT-5.4和Gemini-3.1-Pro仅提供人口统计学描述而不提供任何图像，改变该描述会系统性地改变模型返回的诊断结果。Claude表现出高度集中的倾向：一位65岁的白人男性询问皮肤痣时，几乎每次回答都得到黑色素瘤的诊断；而一位32岁的黑人女性询问其胸部X光时，得到的类肉瘤病诊断的推理描述为“基于人口统计学特征和经典模式疑似诊断”。GPT-5.4的影响更为广泛，在我们测试的每一种人口统计学组合中均出现编造，最显著的是在胸部X光上对年轻黑人患者给出类肉瘤病诊断。两项结构性发现使问题更加严峻。一种“对冲模式”出现了：文字表述承认图像缺失，但结构化诊断字段却仍然给出了疾病名称——这种分离现象是仅通过文本审计无法发现的。此外，当“皮肤痣”替换为“皮肤病变”时，Claude在皮肤科的效果完全消失，而GPT-5.4的效果则保持不变，这表明幻影并非单一现象，而是一系列不同的失败模式。可信赖的VLM在临床管线中的部署需要直接审计结构化输出通道，并且探针词敏感性应被作为一级评估维度来对待。

</details>


### [65] [CinemaTraj: Composing Atomic Camera Trajectories for 3D Scenes with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26910)
*Qianru Li,Xuyang Chen,Erkin Türköz,Lu Liu,Xuqin Wang,Liqiu Meng,Tao Wu,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CinemaTraj 提出将相机轨迹规划转化为基于语言的空间推理问题，利用结构化3D场景图和大语言模型代理，从自然语言描述中自动生成具有电影级表达力的相机运动轨迹，并同步生成配音和字幕。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么缺乏真正的3D空间感知（依赖2D图像先验），要么将轨迹生成视为脱离电影语义的几何路径规划问题，无法同时保证空间准确性和电影表现力。

Method: CinemaTraj 框架包含：(1) 将用户提示分解为原子电影运动序列（推拉、环绕、升降、摇摄、俯仰、变焦、弧线）的LLM代理；(2) 参数化轨迹表示，兼具电影表现力和碰撞可优化性；(3) 结构化3D场景图作为空间先验，引导代理的语义和几何推理；(4) 与相机运动同步的配音和字幕生成。

Result: 在真实ScanNet++环境上的实验表明，CinemaTraj 生成的轨迹忠实于提示、无碰撞且具有高电影质量，在提示对齐、轨迹质量和安全性指标上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将相机轨迹规划重新定义为语言引导的空间推理问题，结合结构化场景图和原子化电影运动描述，能够有效生成兼具空间准确性和电影表现力的高质量相机轨迹。

Original Abstract: Automatically generating cinematically expressive camera trajectories through 3D scenes from natural language descriptions is a challenging task of high practical value, with applications ranging from real-estate advertising to virtual tour creation. Existing methods either lack true 3D spatial awareness by relying on 2D image priors, or treat trajectory generation as a geometric path planning problem divorced from cinematographic semantics. We present CinemaTraj, a framework that reframes camera trajectory planning as a language-grounded spatial reasoning problem. Given a set of RGB-D images and a user prompt, CinemaTraj equips an LLM agent with a structured 3D scene graph: the agent decomposes the prompt into a sequence of atomic cinematographic movements (dolly, orbit, crane, pan, tilt, zoom, arc). Each movement is instantiated via a novel parametric trajectory representation that is both cinematographically expressive and optimizable for collision avoidance. The scene graph acts as a structured spatial prior, grounding the agent's reasoning in accurate geometric and semantic knowledge of the environment. CinemaTraj further generates synchronized voiceover and subtitles aligned with camera motion, producing narrated cinematic video outputs. We evaluate CinemaTraj on real-world ScanNet++ environments, and show that it produces prompt-faithful, collision-free trajectories with high cinematographic quality, outperforming existing approaches on prompt alignment, trajectory quality, and safety metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 从自然语言描述中自动生成具有电影级表现力的相机轨迹，使其能够穿行于3D场景之中，是一项极具实用价值的挑战性任务，应用范围涵盖房地产广告到虚拟导览创建。现有方法要么依赖2D图像先验而缺乏真正的3D空间感知，要么将轨迹生成视为脱离电影语义的几何路径规划问题。我们提出 CinemaTraj，一个将相机轨迹规划重新定义为基于语言的空间推理问题的框架。给定一组RGB-D图像和用户提示，CinemaTraj 为大语言模型代理配备结构化的3D场景图：该代理将提示分解为一系列原子化电影运动（推拉、环绕、升降、摇摄、俯仰、变焦、弧线）。每个运动通过一种新颖的参数化轨迹表示进行实例化，该表示兼具电影级表现力和碰撞规避的可优化性。场景图作为结构化的空间先验，将代理的推理锚定在环境的准确几何和语义知识之上。CinemaTraj 还能生成与相机运动同步的配音和字幕，输出带旁白的电影化视频。我们在真实ScanNet++环境上评估了 CinemaTraj，结果表明它能生成忠实于提示、无碰撞且具有高电影质量的轨迹，在提示对齐、轨迹质量和安全性指标上均优于现有方法。

</details>


### [66] [Prior Directions: Why GUI Grounding Gets Locked in the Past](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26913)
*Weile Gong,Zijian Lu,Mingcai Chen,Yiping Zuo,Xin He,Weibei Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言模型中因先前语言描述过时导致的“视觉锁定”现象，发现锁定强度与模型表示中Prior Directions的集中程度相关，通过移除这些方向可恢复视觉基础能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当场景变化时，视觉-语言模型中过时的语言描述（未经更新的先前状态描述）会干扰模型对当前场景的正确视觉判断，导致模型被“锁定”在旧答案上。现有研究缺乏对这一现象的深入机制理解。

Method: 在受控的grounding场景中，仅改变语言先验（verbalized prior），分析模型表示变化与视觉锁定之间的关系。通过识别Prior Directions（跨样本反复出现的、由先验诱导的表示变化方向），并进行干预实验（移除对齐/正交于Prior Directions的成分）验证因果关系。

Result: 更强的视觉锁定伴随着先验诱导的表示变化更集中于紧凑的方向集合（Prior Directions）；这些方向在未见样本上可重复出现；跨4个模型的比较表明更大的集中度与更强锁定相关；干预实验发现移除Prior Directions方向上的成分可恢复视觉grounding，而移除等量正交成分几乎无影响。

Conclusion: 先验控制（prior control）的强弱取决于先验诱导的表示变化是否形成一致且可复用的模式（Prior Directions）。当这种模式高度集中时，模型难以被纠正；反之则易于修正。这解释了为何相同的先验在不同模型中可修正性不同。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models often use descriptions of earlier visual states to make decisions about the current scene. When the scene changes, stale language can redirect an otherwise correct visual judgment toward an outdated answer. We study this failure as visual lock-in in a controlled grounding setting where only the verbalized prior varies. Across models, stronger lock-in accompanies smaller changes in the model representation before the final answer. This reversal suggests that lock-in depends not on how far this representation moves, but on how that movement is organized. In models that are harder to correct, prior-induced changes concentrate along a compact set of directions that repeatedly appear across examples. We call these recurrent axes the Prior Directions. They recur on held-out examples, while a descriptive four-model comparison associates greater concentration with stronger lock-in. Controlled interventions show that removing the component aligned with the Prior Directions restores visual grounding, whereas removing an equally large orthogonal component has little effect. Prior control thus arises when prior-induced changes form a coherent and reusable pattern in the representation used to produce the answer. This account explains why the same prior remains revisable in one model yet becomes dominant in another.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型常常利用对先前视觉状态的描述来对当前场景做出决策。当场景发生变化时，过时的语言描述会将原本正确的视觉判断引向一个过时的答案。我们在一个受控的grounding环境中研究这种失败现象（称为“视觉锁定”），其中只有口头化的先验发生变化。跨模型来看，更强的锁定伴随着最终答案前模型表示空间中更小的变化。这一反转表明，锁定并不取决于该表示移动了多少，而取决于这些移动是如何组织的。在更难以纠正的模型中，先验诱发的变化集中在一组紧凑的方向上，这些方向在不同样本中反复出现。我们将这些重复出现的轴称为“先验方向”（Prior Directions）。它们在未见样本上也会重复出现，而一项包含四个模型的描述性比较表明，更强的集中度与更严重的锁定相关。受控干预实验表明，移除与先验方向对齐的成分可以恢复视觉基础能力，而移除同样大小的正交成分则几乎没有影响。因此，先验控制产生于先验诱发的变化在用于生成答案的表示空间中形成了连贯且可复用的模式。这一解释说明了为什么相同的先验在一个模型中仍然可修正，而在另一个模型中却变得主导。

</details>


### [67] [From Keypoints to Predictive Distributions: Post-Hoc Uncertainty for YOLO-Pose Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26921)
*Alexej Klushyn,Juan Rivero Sesma,Florian Seligmann,Richard Kurle,Kinh Tieu,Jayant Sen Gupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级后验概率扩展方法，为YOLO-Pose模型的关键点预测增加校准的双变量不确定性估计，并设计了包含分布校准诊断和AKP指标的评估协议，在COCO和飞机着陆任务上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的YOLO-Pose模型能够高效定位关键点，但无法量化关键点预测的空间不确定性，限制了其在安全关键应用（如飞机着陆）中的可靠性。

Method: 在训练好的YOLO-Pose模型上添加轻量级概率头，通过重要性加权负对数似然训练预测输入相关的2×2色散矩阵；分别采用高斯校准（兼容下游任务）和Student-t校准（保持分布保真度）；提出结合分布校准诊断和AKP（关键点平均精度）的评估协议。

Result: COCO实验表明：学习到的不确定性估计能有效进行关键点级可靠性排序；Student-t校准最能拟合经验残差分布；基于不确定性的剪枝可移除不可靠关键点。飞机着陆演示验证了校准协方差支持不确定性感知的位姿估计和传感器融合。

Conclusion: 该后验概率扩展能以轻量级方式为YOLO-Pose提供校准的不确定性估计，Student-t分布在保真度上优于高斯分布，不确定性信息在实际应用中具有重要价值。

Original Abstract: YOLO-Pose models provide efficient keypoint localization, but do not quantify the associated spatial uncertainty. We introduce a lightweight post-hoc probabilistic extension that augments a trained YOLO-Pose model with calibrated bivariate predictive distributions over keypoint locations, centered at the model's original predictions. Concretely, we train additional probabilistic heads with an importance-weighted negative log-likelihood to predict an input-dependent $2\times2$ dispersion matrix for each keypoint, followed by Gaussian calibration for broad downstream compatibility or Student-$t$ calibration for distributional fidelity. Complementing this, we propose an evaluation protocol that combines a suite of distributional calibration diagnostics with average keypoint precision (AKP), a keypoint-level extension of the COCO AP protocol for assessing reliability rankings. Experiments on COCO show that the learned uncertainty estimates enable effective keypoint-level reliability ranking, Student-$t$ calibration best captures the empirical residual distribution, and uncertainty-based pruning removes unreliable keypoints. A central application-level demonstration is vision-based aircraft landing, where calibrated covariances for runway keypoints support uncertainty-aware aircraft position estimation and downstream sensor fusion.

中文摘要翻译: YOLO-Pose模型能够高效定位关键点，但无法量化相关的空间不确定性。我们提出一种轻量级后验概率扩展方法，为训练好的YOLO-Pose模型增广校准的双变量预测分布，该分布以模型原始预测为中心，覆盖关键点位置。具体而言，我们训练额外的概率头，通过重要性加权负对数似然为每个关键点预测输入相关的2×2色散矩阵，随后进行高斯校准（适用于广泛的下游任务）或Student-t校准（保持分布保真度）。在此基础上，我们提出一套评估协议，将一组分布校准诊断指标与关键点平均精度（AKP）相结合——AKP是COCO AP协议在关键点层面的扩展，用于评估可靠性排序。在COCO上的实验表明：学习到的不确定性估计能够实现有效的关键点级可靠性排序；Student-t校准最能拟合经验残差分布；基于不确定性的剪枝可以移除不可靠的关键点。一个核心的应用展示是基于视觉的飞机着陆场景，其中跑道关键点的校准协方差支持不确定性感知的飞机位姿估计及下游传感器融合。

</details>


### [68] [Progressive Multimodal Alignment for Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26947)
*Duzhen Zhang,Yahan Yu,Qiaoyi Su,Jiahua Dong,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出渐进式多模态对齐（PMA）框架，通过检测分布偏移并渐进扩展投影仪专家，解决多模态持续指令微调中被忽视的投影仪级遗忘问题，在保持稳定性的同时实现子线性参数增长，显著提升现有方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）中主要关注LLM骨干网络的遗忘问题，但忽视了投影仪（projector）因视觉分布变化和指令语义演变而发生的投影仪级遗忘，该问题限制了跨模态对齐的持续适应性。

Method: PMA框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级表示描述子用于检测多模态分布偏移；(2) 仅在必要时渐进扩展投影仪专家；(3) 可扩展路由器基于多模态特征整合专家输出，同时保留原始预训练投影仪作为稳定对齐锚点。该机制实现稳定性与可塑性的平衡，且参数呈子线性增长。

Result: 在两个MCIT基准上的大量实验表明，结合PMA缓解投影仪级遗忘能够在现有最先进方法基础上取得一致的性能提升。PMA还能扩展到多种不同的MLLM骨干网络，展现出鲁棒且广泛适用的MCIT性能。

Conclusion: 投影仪级遗忘是MCIT中一个被忽视但关键的问题，PMA作为一种方法无关的即插即用模块，通过渐进式扩展投影仪有效解决了该问题，为多模态持续学习提供了新的研究视角。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) rely on a projector to align visual representations with the language embedding space, making it central to cross-modal understanding. In Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT), however, shifting visual distributions and evolving instruction semantics cause this shared projector to drift, leading to projector-level forgetting, an issue largely overlooked by methods that focus primarily on the LLM backbone. We introduce Progressive Multimodal Alignment (PMA), a framework that enables the projector to adapt continually while preserving previously learned alignment. PMA detects multimodal distribution shifts via a lightweight representation descriptor and progressively expands projector experts only when needed. An expandable router integrates expert outputs based on multimodal features, while the original pretrained projector is retained as a stable alignment anchor. This progressive mechanism balances stability and plasticity with sub-linear parameter growth and serves as a method-agnostic add-on to existing MCIT approaches. Extensive experiments on two recent MCIT benchmarks demonstrate that mitigating projector-level forgetting yields consistent gains over prior state-of-the-art methods when combined with PMA. Moreover, PMA scales across diverse MLLM backbones, demonstrating robust and broadly applicable MCIT performance.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）依赖投影仪将视觉表示与语言嵌入空间对齐，这使得投影仪成为跨模态理解的核心。然而，在多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）中，不断变化的视觉分布和演进的指令语义会导致这一共享投影仪发生漂移，从而引发投影仪级遗忘——这一被主要关注LLM骨干网络的方法所严重忽视的问题。我们提出渐进式多模态对齐（PMA）框架，该框架使投影仪能够持续适应，同时保留先前学到的对齐知识。PMA通过轻量级表示描述子检测多模态分布偏移，并仅在必要时渐进扩展投影仪专家。一个可扩展路由器基于多模态特征整合专家输出，同时保留原始预训练投影仪作为稳定对齐锚点。这种渐进机制在实现子线性参数增长的同时平衡了稳定性与可塑性，并可作为方法无关的即插即用模块集成到现有MCIT方法中。在两个最新MCIT基准上的大量实验表明，当结合PMA时，缓解投影仪级遗忘能够在现有最先进方法基础上取得一致的性能提升。此外，PMA可扩展到多种不同的MLLM骨干网络，展现出鲁棒且广泛适用的MCIT性能。

</details>


### [69] [Robust RPC Bundle Adjustment for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery with Season-Invariant Correspondences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26973)
*Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Xavier Bou,Thibaud Ehret,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种外观感知的RPC精化流程，利用学习型局部特征匹配和全局图像描述符，在多日期卫星影像中实现无地面控制点的相对RPC精化，显著提升几何一致性和匹配效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统RPC光束法平差依赖手工特征匹配，在多日期影像中因季节、光照和地表覆盖变化而不可靠，亟需一种对时相外观变化鲁棒的RPC精化方法。

Method: 提出外观感知的RPC精化流程：首先利用学习型局部特征匹配（如SuperGlue等）获取季节不变的点对应关系，再结合全局图像描述符（如NetVLAD）筛选视觉兼容的图像对，减少冗余和错误匹配的同时保持匹配图连通性，最后进行光束法平差实现RPC精化。

Result: 在季节多样的WorldView-3影像上，该方法相比开源基线方法实现了更低的几何一致性误差，并在39-42景影像的数据集上大幅缩短了匹配时间。

Conclusion: 该方法使RPC精化对跨时相外观变化更具鲁棒性，从而更有效地利用多日期卫星影像进行地理定位。

Original Abstract: Accurate refinement of Rational Polynomial Camera (RPC) models is essential for high-quality satellite image geolocation. In ground control point (GCP)-free multi-view pipelines, this refinement is commonly performed through bundle adjustment from automatically extracted image correspondences. However, conventional RPC bundle adjustment pipelines rely on handcrafted feature matching, which becomes unreliable in multi-date collections affected by seasonal, illumination, and land-cover changes. We propose an appearance-aware RPC refinement pipeline that combines learned local feature matching for season-invariant correspondences with global image descriptors for selecting visually compatible image pairs. This reduces redundant and error-prone matching while preserving the connectivity of the matching graph. Experiments on seasonally diverse WorldView-3 images show that our pipeline improves GCP-free relative RPC refinement over open-source baselines, achieving lower geometric consistency errors while substantially reducing matching time on collections with 39-42 views. By making RPC refinement more robust to diachronic appearance variation, our approach enables more effective use of multi-date satellite imagery.

中文摘要翻译: 精确精化有理多项式相机（RPC）模型对于高质量卫星影像地理定位至关重要。在无地面控制点（GCP）的多视角流程中，这种精化通常通过从自动提取的图像对应关系进行光束法平差来实现。然而，传统的RPC光束法平差流程依赖手工特征匹配，这在受到季节、光照和土地覆盖变化影响的多日期影像集合中变得不可靠。我们提出了一种外观感知的RPC精化流程，将学习型局部特征匹配用于季节不变的点对应，同时结合全局图像描述符来筛选视觉兼容的图像对。这减少了冗余和错误匹配，同时保持了匹配图的连通性。在季节多样的WorldView-3影像上的实验表明，我们的流程在无GCP的相对RPC精化上优于开源基线方法，实现了更低的几何一致性误差，同时在39-42景影像的数据集上大幅减少了匹配时间。通过使RPC精化对跨时相外观变化更具鲁棒性，我们的方法能够更有效地利用多日期卫星影像。

</details>


### [70] [Mitigating Compounding Error via Video Representation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27036)
*Taiye Chen,Qi Zhang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视频扩散世界模型在自回归推理中由于隐层表示维度坍塌导致误差累积，提出视频表示正则化方法，有效提升了长视频生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 滑窗自回归推理存在严重误差累积导致帧质量随时间下降，但其底层机制尚不明确，主流的数据缩放范式也未能解决该问题。

Method: 提出视频表示正则化（video representation regularization），一种轻量级训练约束，用于稳定潜层表示并抑制迭代误差累积。

Result: 在VBench的Aesthetic Quality指标上从38.65提升至55.56，Imaging Quality指标从44.37提升至72.08；发现训练数据缩放无法提高模型抗误差漂移能力，与主流缩放范式相悖。

Conclusion: 首次建立了自回归视频漂移与模型内部表示之间的关联，采用有效秩（erank）作为误差累积的定量指标，揭示了视频世界模型的缩放局限性，并提出了有效的正则化策略。

Original Abstract: Video diffusion-based world models enable long autoregressive video generation for robotics, autonomous driving and simulation tasks, yet sliding-window autoregressive inference suffers from severe error accumulation that degrades frame quality over time. Although this phenomenon has been widely observed, the underlying mechanism of compounding error and how to achieve stable long-horizon generation remain largely unresolved. In this paper, we investigate the internal representation dynamics of video world models and discover that compounding error is tightly coupled with dimensional collapse of hidden representations. Specifically, the effective rank of model representations sharply decreases at the onset of generation drift, revealing a strong connection between representational degradation and long-term rollout instability. Furthermore, we find that pure training data scaling fails to boost model resistance to error drift, contradicting mainstream scaling paradigms. To address this problem, we propose video representation regularization, a lightweight training constraint that stabilizes latent representations and suppresses iterative error accumulation. Compared with Diffusion Forcing, our method achieves improvements from 38.65 to 55.56 and from 44.37 to 72.08 on the Aesthetic Quality and Imaging Quality metrics of VBench. Our work establishes the first connection between autoregressive video drifting and model internal representations, adopts erank as a quantitative metric for error accumulation, reveals counterintuitive scaling limitations for video world models, and presents a simple yet effective regularization strategy to improve long video generation robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 基于视频扩散的世界模型可实现用于机器人、自动驾驶和仿真任务的长程自回归视频生成，然而滑窗自回归推理存在严重的误差累积，导致帧质量随时间推移而退化。尽管该现象已被广泛观察，但复合误差的底层机制以及如何实现稳定的长程生成仍悬而未决。本文研究视频世界模型的内部表示动态，发现复合误差与隐层表示的维度坍塌密切相关。具体而言，模型表示的有效秩（erank）在生成漂移开始时刻急剧下降，揭示了表示退化与长程推演不稳定性之间的强关联。此外，我们发现纯训练数据缩放无法增强模型对误差漂移的抵抗能力，这与主流缩放范式相矛盾。为解决该问题，我们提出视频表示正则化，一种轻量级训练约束，能够稳定潜层表示并抑制迭代误差累积。与Diffusion Forcing相比，我们的方法在VBench的美学质量（Aesthetic Quality）和成像质量（Imaging Quality）指标上分别从38.65提升至55.56，从44.37提升至72.08。本文首次建立了自回归视频漂移与模型内部表示之间的联系，采用erank作为误差累积的定量指标，揭示了视频世界模型反直觉的缩放局限性，并提出了一种简单有效的正则化策略以提升长视频生成的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [71] [Object Detection for Autonomous Driving in Chinese Rural Scenes: An Experimental Study on Real-Synthetic Data Mixing and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27058)
*Danning Zhu,Ziyan Lin,Jing Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对中国农村交通场景下自动驾驶目标检测面临的数据稀缺和泛化挑战，本文构建了一个融合真实与合成数据的混合检测数据集（涵盖14类农村特有目标），并系统评估了13种主流检测器在不同虚实数据比例下的性能表现。实验表明，适度注入合成数据（1:0.5）可提升检测效果，YOLO11m达到最高mAP@0.5为0.758，但过高比例（1:1）会引入域偏移；模型对长尾非标准目标（如摊位、栏杆）仍存在感知瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前自动驾驶目标检测模型在复杂的中国农村交通场景中面临严重的数据稀缺和泛化能力不足问题，亟需构建针对中国农村道路的专用数据集并系统评估不同数据策略对检测器性能的影响，为模型选择和训练数据设计提供实证依据。

Method: 构建了一个真-合成混合目标检测数据集：真实图像采集自河南尉氏县农村道路，合成场景通过虚幻引擎参数化生成；定义了涵盖电动三轮车、低速车辆（LSV）、路边摊位等14类农村特有目标的类别体系；在统一训练协议下，系统评估了YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11、YOLO26系列及RT-DETR-L共13种主流检测器在三种数据配置（全真实、1:0.5虚实混合、1:1虚实混合）下的性能表现。

Result: 适度注入合成数据（1:0.5比例）能有效提升检测性能，YOLO11m取得最佳mAP@0.5为0.758；但过高的合成数据比例（1:1）会引入域偏移，抵消数据规模扩大带来的收益；多数模型能可靠识别本地车辆，但对摊位、栏杆等长尾非标准目标仍存在显著的感知瓶颈。

Conclusion: 本研究为农村自动驾驶场景下的模型选择与合成数据策略提供了关键实证依据和新的见解，有助于推动自动驾驶感知系统在农村地区的实际部署。

Original Abstract: Currently, autonomous driving object detection models face significant data scarcity and generalization challenges when navigating complex Chinese rural traffic scenarios. To address these limitations, we propose a novel real-synthetic mixed object detection dataset tailored specifically for Chinese rural roads and systematically evaluate the performance of 13 mainstream detectors under different real-to-synthetic data ratios, thereby providing empirical evidence for model selection and data strategy design in rural autonomous driving scenarios. Our dataset combines real-world images captured in Weishi County, Henan Province, with parameterized virtual scenes generated via Unreal Engine. To accurately reflect the unique realities of rural traffic, we define a comprehensive 14-category object system encompassing region-specific elements such as electric tricycles, low-speed vehicles (LSVs), and roadside stalls. Under a unified training protocol, we systematically evaluate 13 mainstream detectors -- spanning the YOLOv5, YOLOv8, YOLO11, and YOLO26 series, as well as RT-DETR-L -- across three data configurations: an all-real baseline, a 1:0.5 real-to-virtual mix, and a 1:1 mix. Experimental results demonstrate that a moderate injection of synthetic data (1:0.5 ratio) effectively enhances detection performance, with YOLO11m achieving the highest mAP@0.5 of 0.758. However, a higher proportion of synthetic data (1:1) introduces domain shifts that offset the benefits of data scaling. While most models reliably identify distinct local vehicles, significant perceptual bottlenecks remain for long-tail, non-standard objects like stalls and railings. This research provides crucial empirical evidence and novel insights for model selection and synthetic data strategies, facilitating the practical deployment of autonomous driving perception systems in rural areas.

中文摘要翻译: 当前，自动驾驶目标检测模型在复杂的中国农村交通场景中面临严重的数据稀缺和泛化挑战。为解决这些局限，我们提出一个专门针对中国农村道路的新型真实-合成混合目标检测数据集，并系统评估了13种主流检测器在不同真实与合成数据比例下的性能，从而为农村自动驾驶场景下的模型选择和数据策略设计提供实证依据。我们的数据集结合了河南省尉氏县采集的真实世界图像与通过虚幻引擎生成的参数化虚拟场景。为准确反映农村交通的独特现实，我们定义了一个包含14种类别的综合目标体系，涵盖了电动三轮车、低速车辆（LSV）和路边摊位等区域特有元素。在统一训练协议下，我们系统评估了13种主流检测器——涵盖YOLOv5、YOLOv8、YOLO11和YOLO26系列以及RT-DETR-L——在三种数据配置下的表现：全真实基线、1:0.5虚实混合和1:1混合。实验结果表明，适度注入合成数据（1:0.5比例）能有效提升检测性能，其中YOLO11m取得了最高mAP@0.5为0.758。然而，过高的合成数据比例（1:1）会引入域偏移，抵消了数据规模扩大带来的收益。虽然大多数模型能可靠识别不同的本地车辆，但对摊位和栏杆等长尾非标准目标仍存在显著的感知瓶颈。本研究为模型选择和合成数据策略提供了关键的实证依据和新的见解，有助于推动自动驾驶感知系统在农村地区的实际部署。

</details>


### [72] [ScratchSim: A Procedural Synthetic Data Pipeline for Surface Scratch Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27065)
*Paul Julius Kühn,Saptarshi Neil Sinha,Tiago Kleist,Richard Hoffmann,Arjan kuijper,Michael Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于BlenderProc的程序化渲染管线，用于生成大规模带标注的合成缺陷检测数据，并通过微调与混合训练策略在轻量化边缘部署检测器上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业缺陷检测中标注数据稀缺，使得自动化缺陷检测任务具有挑战性。

Method: 利用BlenderProc构建程序化渲染管线，可配置材质外观、相机模式和域随机化，自动生成COCO格式标注的合成训练数据；在此基础上比较了四种训练策略（纯合成、纯真实、混合、从合成权重微调），并在两种不同材质物体和三种轻量级边缘部署检测器（YOLOX、YOLO26、LW-DETR）上进行评估。

Result: 从合成权重微调始终优于纯真实数据训练；混合训练在真实数据稀缺条件下有效恢复性能；该结论在卷积和Transformer两类架构上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在不依赖大规模真实标注数据集的情况下实现可扩展的缺陷检测，适用于设备端工业检测场景。

Original Abstract: While automated defect detection such as the detection of surface scratched is an important aspect in industrial quality control, the scarcity of annotated defect data make this task challenging. This paper presents a procedural rendering pipeline that generates large-scale annotated synthetic training data using BlenderProc, with configurable material appearance, camera modes, and domain randomization, producing automatic COCO-format annotations. To show the potential of our approach, we evaluate four training strategies, namely synthetic-only, real-only, mixed, and fine-tuning from synthetic weights, across two objects with different material properties and three lightweight edge-deployable detectors, YOLOX, YOLO26, and LW-DETR. Our evaluation show that fine-tuning from synthetic weights consistently outperforms real-only training, and that mixed training effectively recovers performance under scarce real-data conditions, with findings validated across both convolutional and transformer-based architectures. The proposed approach enables scalable defect detection without the burden of large real annotated datasets, making it practical for on-device industrial inspection. The pipeline scripts, 3D model, and both synthetic and real annotated scratch datasets for a glossy toy Ferrari car will be made available through the project website upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然自动化缺陷检测（如表面划痕检测）是工业质量控制中的重要环节，但标注缺陷数据的稀缺使得该任务具有挑战性。本文提出了一种基于BlenderProc的程序化渲染管线，可生成大规模带标注的合成训练数据，支持可配置的材质外观、相机模式和域随机化，并自动生成COCO格式的标注。为展示该方法潜力，我们在两种不同材质属性的物体和三种轻量级边缘部署检测器（YOLOX、YOLO26和LW-DETR）上评估了四种训练策略：纯合成数据、纯真实数据、混合训练以及从合成权重微调。实验结果表明，从合成权重微调始终优于纯真实数据训练，且混合训练在真实数据稀缺条件下能有效恢复性能，该结论在卷积架构和Transformer架构上均得到验证。该方法无需大量真实标注数据集即可实现可扩展的缺陷检测，使其适用于设备端工业检测场景。管线脚本、3D模型以及用于光泽玩具法拉利汽车的合成和真实标注划痕数据集将在论文接收后通过项目网站公开。

</details>


### [73] [SciFigAlign: Scoring Scientific Figures by Fine-tuned Alignment of Visuals with Manuscript Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27066)
*Chuanzhi Xu,Zihan Deng,Huiqi Liang,Chengkun Yue,Zhanlin Cui,Pengfei Ye,Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了SciFigAlign，一种融合CLIP和SciBERT的多模态评分器，用于科学论文图表质量评估，在3857张标注图表数据集上实现了比LLM基线低59%的误差，并证明了基于稿件证据的图文对齐是关键。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 同行评审中的科学图表质量评估与通用图像质量评估有本质区别，现有方法（传统IQA、CLIP、零样本LLM/VLM）均无法有效判断图表是否服务于论文的科学论证，缺乏对稿件上下文的深入理解，且往往产生过于集中的评分。

Method: 提出SciFigAlign，一个微调的多模态评分器：以图表裁剪、图注、引用段落和轻量论文上下文为输入，端到端微调CLIP和SciBERT，使用逐模态交叉注意力（per-modality cross-attention）和CubeMLP融合，联合优化SmoothL1回归损失和论文内排序合页损失。

Result: 在396张测试集上，宏观MAE为0.3524，论文内成对准确率81.64%；相比最佳LLM基线（MAE 0.864）相对误差降低59%。消融实验证实稿件上下文、引用段落去噪和排序监督均至关重要。

Conclusion: 科学图表质量评估需要视觉内容与稿件证据之间的学习对齐，而非仅依靠提示工程，即便使用最先进的VLM也是如此。

Original Abstract: Scientific figure assessment in peer review differs fundamentally from general image quality evaluation: a figure must be visually legible, faithfully support the manuscript's claims, and communicate evidence with a clear visual hierarchy. However, if we apply traditional image assessment methods to scientific figure quality assessment, limitations emerge: classic IQA models capture perceptual quality or aesthetics but cannot judge whether a figure serves the paper's scientific argument; CLIP-based methods assess generic image-text correspondence, yet lack understanding of manuscript context; and zero-shot LLM/VLM judges, when repurposed for figure scoring, often yield overly concentrated scores with limited fusion of visual and textual evidence. We introduce an annotated dataset of 3,857 scientific figures from peer-reviewed conference papers, each rated along four peer-review-oriented dimensions: Clarity, Relevance, Informativeness, and Structure. We propose SciFigAlign, a fine-tuned multimodal scorer that grounds figure quality assessment in manuscript evidence. Given a figure crop, caption, citing paragraphs, and light paper context, SciFigAlign fine-tunes CLIP and SciBERT end-to-end with per-modality cross-attention and CubeMLP fusion, jointly optimizing SmoothL1 regression with a within-paper ranking hinge loss. Under paper-level splits, SciFigAlign achieves a macro MAE of 0.3524 and a within-paper pairwise accuracy of 81.64% on the test set with n = 396, a 59% relative error reduction over the best LLM-as-judge baseline with MAE 0.864. Ablations confirm that manuscript-grounded inputs, citing-context denoising, and ranking supervision are all critical, showing that scientific figure assessment requires learned alignment between visual content and manuscript evidence rather than prompting alone, even with state-of-the-art VLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 同行评审中的科学图表评估与通用图像质量评估存在根本差异：图表必须视觉可读、忠实支持稿件主张、并以清晰的视觉层次传递证据。然而，将传统图像评估方法应用于科学图表质量评估时会出现局限性：经典IQA模型能捕捉感知质量或美学，但无法判断图表是否服务于论文的科学论证；基于CLIP的方法评估通用图文对应关系，但缺乏对稿件上下文的理解；而零样本LLM/VLM评判器在被用于图表评分时，往往产生过于集中的分数且视觉与文本证据融合有限。我们引入了一个包含3857张来自同行评审会议论文的科学图表标注数据集，每张图表沿四个同行评审导向维度进行评分：清晰度、相关性、信息量和结构。我们提出SciFigAlign，一个基于稿件证据对图表质量评估进行 grounding 的微调多模态评分器。给定图表裁剪、图注、引用段落和轻量论文上下文，SciFigAlign端到端微调CLIP和SciBERT，采用逐模态交叉注意力和CubeMLP融合，联合优化SmoothL1回归与论文内排序合页损失。在论文级别划分下，SciFigAlign在n=396的测试集上实现了宏观MAE为0.3524和论文内成对准确率为81.64%，相比最佳LLM评判基线（MAE 0.864）相对误差降低了59%。消融实验证实，基于稿件的输入、引用上下文去噪和排序监督均至关重要，表明科学图表评估需要视觉内容与稿件证据之间的学习对齐，而非仅依靠提示工程，即便使用最先进的VLM也是如此。

</details>


### [74] [Visual Credit Audit for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27069)
*Feixiang Liu,Qiang Qiu,Lanbo Sun,Nan Wei,Huawei Shen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉信用审计（VCA）框架，用于评估多模态大语言模型在空间基准测试中是否真正利用了图像证据，而非仅依赖文本或盲猜。实验发现12.73%-26.25%的正确决策缺乏图像支撑，并通过图像置换、关系对比等分析揭示了模型对视觉关系的真实响应程度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的封闭式空间基准测试中，模型即使在没有图像支持的情况下也能答对，导致无法区分正确回答是源于真正的视觉理解还是文本先验或随机猜测。因此需要一种方法对模型的视觉信用进行审计。

Method: 提出视觉信用审计（VCA）框架，包含两个审计目标：(1) 比较模型在有图像、纯文本和空白控制条件下的决策支持度，无需训练和标签；(2) 评估模型是否对关系特定的视觉证据作出响应。通过引入标签得到依赖信用正确性（D-CC），用图像置换、固定像素关系对比和3x3证据来源因子设计进行审计。

Result: 在4个开源MLLM和2个空间基准上，12.73%-26.25%的正确决策未获得图像信用；匹配的同分割图像置换使D-CC下降21.25-47.80个百分点；在可控的正确但无信用的同意决策中，对关系反转的响应达81.57%-100.00%，但仅有32.11%改变了答案。

Conclusion: VCA框架能够将基准测试的成功分解为正确性、额外图像支持和关系一致性响应三个维度，揭示了模型在空间任务中表面正确但缺乏真正视觉理解的普遍现象。

Original Abstract: Closed yes/no spatial benchmarks can reward a correct answer even when the image adds little support beyond no-image contexts. Under a fixed forced-choice interface, Visual Credit Audit (VCA) separates two estimands: whether the benchmark image gives the model's declared decision more support than text-only and blank controls, and whether the model responds to relation-specific visual evidence. The first audit is training- and label-free and does not require an answer flip. Applying labels yields dependence-credited correctness (D-CC); on correct items, it equals same-control gold-aligned positive gain, while prediction alignment extends the audit to errors. Across four open MLLMs and two spatial benchmarks, 12.73-26.25% of decisions are correct yet uncredited. Matched same-split image permutation reduces D-CC by 21.25-47.80 points, with every paired 95% interval above zero. Fixed-pixel relation contrasts and a 3x3 evidence-source factorial show why null controls cannot identify relation response. Among controlled correct-but-uncredited agreement decisions, response to relation reversal spans 81.57-100.00%, while 32.11% pooled change answer. Independently audited outcomes on 108 geometry-compatible edits provide a bounded natural-image correspondence check. VCA thereby decomposes benchmark success into correctness, additional image support, and relation-consistent response.

中文摘要翻译: 封闭式是与非空间基准测试在图像提供的支持甚至不优于无图像上下文时，仍可能奖励正确答案。在固定二选一接口下，视觉信用审计（VCA）分离了两个估计目标：基准图像是否为模型的声明决策提供了比纯文本和空白对照更多的支持，以及模型是否对特定关系的视觉证据作出响应。第一个审计无需训练和标签，也不要求答案翻转。应用标签得到依赖信用正确性（D-CC）；在正确项上，它等同于对照对齐的金标准正增益，而预测对齐将审计扩展到错误项。在四个开源多模态大语言模型和两个空间基准测试上，12.73%-26.25%的决策正确但未获信用。匹配的同分割图像置换使D-CC下降21.25-47.80个百分点，每对95%置信区间均高于零。固定像素关系对比和3x3证据来源因子设计表明，空对照无法识别关系响应。在可控的正确但无信用的同意决策中，对关系反转的响应范围达81.57%-100.00%，而32.11%的合并改变了答案。基于108个几何兼容编辑的独立审计结果提供了有界自然图像对应性检查。由此，VCA将基准测试的成功分解为正确性、额外图像支持和关系一致性响应。

</details>


### [75] [SciFigQual-Bench: A Benchmark for Scientific Figure Quality Assessment with Full-Manuscript Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27084)
*Zihan Deng,Chuanzhi Xu,Huiqi Liang,Haoyang Li,Xiaozhen Zhong,Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了面向科学论文图像的全文本上下文基准SciFigQual-Bench及分阶段跨模态评估框架SFQ-Agent，在五个维度上实现了对科学图像的质量评估，实验表明SFQ-Agent（F3）结合GPT-5.6-Sol取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法主要针对自然照片或AI生成内容设计，无法直接用于科学论文；少数关于学术图表的研究仅停留在视觉表面比较，未能验证标题匹配度、引用相关性或视觉误导性。因此需要一种能够结合全文本上下文的科学图像质量评估方法。

Method: 提出了SciFigQual-Bench基准数据集，覆盖2020-2025年顶级计算机科学会议，包含6,308张图像，由多名领域专家在清晰度、布局、标题匹配度、上下文相关性和误导风险五个维度独立评分并聚合为黄金标准标注。同时设计了分阶段跨模态评估框架SFQ-Agent，通过收集和融合模态证据实现可审计的精细化评分。

Result: 在eval1200测试子集上，SFQ-Agent（F3）配备GPT-5.6-Sol取得了最低的总体平均绝对误差（0.418）和最高的一致性率（93.4%），持续优于直接评估和辅助视觉语言模型评估方案。

Conclusion: 提出的SciFigQual-Bench和SFQ-Agent有效解决了科学论文图像质量评估中缺乏全文本上下文验证的问题，多模态证据融合的分阶段评估框架显著提升了评估准确性和一致性。

Original Abstract: Scientific images are the core elements of presenting experimental conclusions, elaborating system architecture, and supporting comparative arguments in scientific papers. However, existing image quality assessment (IQA) methods are predominantly designed for natural photographs or AI-generated content, which cannot be directly applied to scientific papers. The few existing studies on scholarly charts remain confined to visual-surface comparisons, failing to verify caption alignment, citation relevance, or visual misleadingness. To address this, we propose SciFigQual-Bench, a full-text contextual benchmark that evaluates scientific images across five dimensions (clarity, layout, caption fit, context relevance, and misleading risk). The data covers top computer-science conferences from 2020 to 2025; 6,308 images were independently scored by multiple domain experts in five dimensions and aggregated into gold-standard annotations. Unlike previous scientific figure benchmarks, our dataset binds each image to its caption, citing sentence, and manuscript context. To enable automated evaluation on this benchmark, we designed a staged cross-modal evaluation framework SFQ-Agent to achieve auditable and refined scoring through the collection and fusion of modal evidence. Multiple mainstream large models were evaluated on the test subset eval1200, and SFQ-Agent (F3) equipped with GPT-5.6-Sol achieved the lowest overall average absolute error (0.418) and the highest consistency rate (93.4%), consistently outperforming both direct evaluation and auxiliary (Sidecar) visual language model evaluation schemes.

中文摘要翻译: 科学图像是科学论文中呈现实验结论、阐述系统架构和支持比较论证的核心要素。然而，现有的图像质量评估方法主要针对自然照片或AI生成内容设计，无法直接应用于科学论文。少数关于学术图表的研究仍局限于视觉表面比较，未能验证标题匹配度、引用相关性或视觉误导性。为解决这一问题，我们提出了SciFigQual-Bench——一个全文本上下文基准，从五个维度（清晰度、布局、标题匹配度、上下文相关性和误导风险）评估科学图像。数据涵盖2020至2025年顶级计算机科学会议；6,308张图像由多名领域专家在五个维度上独立评分，并聚合为黄金标准标注。与以往的科学图表基准不同，我们的数据集将每张图像与其标题、引用句子和稿件上下文相绑定。为实现对该基准的自动评估，我们设计了一个分阶段跨模态评估框架SFQ-Agent，通过收集和融合模态证据实现可审计且精细化的评分。在eval1200测试子集上对多个主流大模型进行了评估，配备GPT-5.6-Sol的SFQ-Agent（F3）取得了最低的总体平均绝对误差（0.418）和最高的一致性率（93.4%），持续优于直接评估和辅助（Sidecar）视觉语言模型评估方案。

</details>


### [76] [Step-Attention Refinement of DINOv3 Features for Efficient Anterior Eye Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27087)
*Philippe Baumstimler,Jean-Mathieu Gagnon,Sébastien Gagné,Mathieu Duchesneau,Clément Playout,Lama Séoud*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于蒸馏DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干的轻量级前眼节（AES）分割架构，通过步注意力特征精炼模块逐步适配多层级Transformer表征，在私有数据集上达到85.55% mIoU，并在域迁移场景中展现出最强的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AES分割方法在临床场景下面临采集条件异质和标注稀缺两大挑战，导致泛化性不足；虽然基础模型（如DINOv3）具备强迁移能力，但如何高效适配其表征到密集预测任务仍是一个难题。

Method: 基于蒸馏后的DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络，设计了一个步注意力特征精炼模块（Step-Attention Feature Refinement Module），在卷积解码前逐步适配多层级Transformer特征，以少量参数高效利用预训练特征。

Result: 在含333张临床AES图像（涵盖8种采集协议、7类解剖结构）的私有数据集上，全微调后达到85.55% mIoU，优于卷积、Transformer及DINOv3基线方法；在4个未见过的公开AES分割数据集上展现出最强的域迁移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为临床场景下鲁棒的AES分割建立了强基线，同时揭示了解码器设计对有效适配基础模型表征到医学分割任务的关键作用。

Original Abstract: Anterior eye segment (AES) segmentation is a key component of both ocular biometrics and emerging clinical image analysis applications. However, heterogeneous acquisition conditions and limited annotations in medical settings hinder the robustness and generalization of existing methods. Foundation models (FMs) such as DINOv3 offer strong transfer capabilities, but efficiently adapting their representations to dense prediction tasks remains challenging. In this study, we investigate robust AES segmentation in clinical settings, and propose a lightweight architecture built upon a distilled DINOv3 ViT-Small backbone. We introduce a step-attention feature refinement module that progressively adapts multi-level transformer representations before convolutional decoding, enabling efficient exploitation of pretrained features with few parameters. We evaluate the proposed approach on a private dataset of 333 clinically acquired AES images spanning eight ophthalmic acquisition protocols and annotated for seven anatomical classes. Compared with convolutional and transformer-based baselines, including DINOv3-based methods, our approach achieves the best overall performance, reaching 85.55\% mIoU when fully fine-tuned. It also demonstrates the strongest robustness to domain shift across four unseen public AES segmentation datasets. These results establish a strong baseline for robust AES segmentation in clinical settings and highlight the importance of decoder design for effectively adapting FMs representations to medical segmentation tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 前眼节（AES）分割是眼科生物识别和新兴临床图像分析应用的关键组成部分。然而，异质的采集条件和医学场景中有限的标注阻碍了现有方法的鲁棒性和泛化能力。诸如DINOv3之类的基础模型（FMs）具备强大的迁移能力，但如何高效地将其表征适配到密集预测任务仍具挑战性。在本研究中，我们探究了临床场景下的鲁棒AES分割问题，并提出了一种轻量级架构，该架构基于蒸馏后的DINOv3 ViT-Small骨干网络构建。我们引入了一个步注意力特征精炼模块，该模块在卷积解码之前逐步适配多层级Transformer表征，从而能够以少量参数高效利用预训练特征。我们在一个包含333张临床采集的AES图像（涵盖8种眼科采集协议，标注了7类解剖结构）的私有数据集上对所提方法进行了评估。与卷积和Transformer基线方法（包括基于DINOv3的方法）相比，我们的方法在全微调后达到了85.55%的平均交并比（mIoU），取得了最优的整体性能。此外，该方法在四个未见过的公开AES分割数据集上展现出对域迁移的最强鲁棒性。这些结果为临床场景下的鲁棒AES分割建立了强基线，并凸显了解码器设计对有效适配基础模型表征到医学分割任务的重要性。

</details>


### [77] [FreqForcing: Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27110)
*Jiatong Li,Leo Liang,Linghe Kong,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FreqForcing，一种无需训练的视频生成长时稳定性方法，通过频谱自锚定机制抑制低频能量漂移，实现5秒到2分钟（24倍外推）的长时间视频生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时自滚动生成中误差累积，导致颜色漂移、运动停滞和视觉崩溃。本文从频域角度刻画了该现象——低频段能量漂移，并发现注意力汇虽能缓解但无法完全解决该问题。

Method: 提出FreqForcing框架，核心为频谱自锚定（SSA）：利用锚定注意力的低频分量维持长期视觉稳定性，同时利用局部注意力的高频分量保留动态运动。该方法无需训练，基于Self-Forcing预训练模型进行扩展。

Result: 将Self-Forcing从5秒片段扩展到2分钟生成，实现24倍外推；实验结果表明FreqForcing在定量和定性上优于现有免训练方法，并与代表性训练方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 从频域视角揭示了视频生成中误差累积的本质，并证明了无需训练即可通过频域操作有效解决长视频生成的稳定性问题。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable real-time streaming video generation. However, errors introduced during self-rollout accumulate over long horizons, manifesting as color drift, motion stagnation, and eventual visual collapse. In this paper, we characterize this phenomenon from a frequency-domain perspective: error accumulation appears as a pronounced energy drift in the low-frequency bands. We further investigate the effectiveness of attention sink in the frequency domain, and find that it improves the video quality by alleviating the spectral energy drift to some extent, but cannot fully resolve it. Motivated by the above analysis, we propose FreqForcing, a training-free framework that addresses error accumulation in long-video generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring (SSA). The proposed SSA leverages the low-frequency components of anchor attention to maintain long-horizon visual stability, while preserving dynamic motion through the high-frequency components of local attention. Our FreqForcing extends Self-Forcing pretrained on 5s clips to two-minute generation, achieving 24x extrapolation. Extensive experiments show that FreqForcing outperforms existing training-free methods quantitatively and qualitatively while remaining competitive with representative training-based approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归视频扩散模型能够实现实时流式视频生成。然而，在自滚动生成过程中引入的误差会随着时间步累积，表现为颜色漂移、运动停滞以及最终的视觉崩溃。本文从频域角度刻画了这一现象：误差累积表现为低频段中显著的能量漂移。我们进一步研究了注意力汇在频域中的有效性，发现它通过一定程度上缓解频谱能量漂移来提升视频质量，但无法完全解决该问题。基于上述分析，我们提出FreqForcing，一种无需训练的框架，通过频谱自锚定（SSA）解决长视频生成中的误差累积问题。所提出的SSA利用锚定注意力的低频分量来维持长时视觉稳定性，同时通过局部注意力中的高频分量保留动态运动。我们的FreqForcing将预训练于5秒片段的Self-Forcing模型扩展到两分钟生成，实现了24倍的外推。大量实验表明，FreqForcing在定量和定性上均优于现有的免训练方法，并与代表性的基于训练的方法保持竞争力。

</details>


### [78] [Veritas++: Value-aware On-Policy Distillation for Perception-Enhanced AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27113)
*Hao Tan,Jun Lan,Zichang Tan,Ajian Liu,Zijian Yu,Chuanbiao Song,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jun Wan,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Veritas++，一个基于感知增强的推理框架，通过面向感知的学习（PoRL）和价值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD）来弥合多模态大语言模型在AI生成图像检测中的感知瓶颈，实现了良好的泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前基于MLLM的AI生成图像检测器在捕捉细粒度异常方面存在显著的感知瓶颈，主要集中在组织视觉证据的方式上，而内在感知能力未得到充分优化。

Method: 提出Veritas++框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 面向感知的学习（PoRL），用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督，显式增强细粒度视觉细节、语义异常和像素级差异三种基本感知能力；(2) 价值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD），一种自适应蒸馏机制，优先选择高价值蒸馏信号，通过特权自教师内化感知感知推理。

Result: 在标准、野外和新兴基准上的大量实验表明，Veritas++实现了有前景的泛化性能。感知学习有效弥合了感知差距，在检测上取得了无缝提升；VaOPD在不牺牲现有性能的情况下进一步实现了高效的能力演进。

Conclusion: 将可靠的感知能力作为真实性推理的基础，通过显式增强基本感知能力并配合自适应蒸馏，可以有效提升MLLM在AIGI检测任务上的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: The growing capability of image generation models has made synthetic images a routine presence in open media, making robust and generalizable AI-Generated Image (AIGI) detection increasingly essential. While multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) offer a transparent alternative to black-box binary scoring, we observe that current MLLM-based detectors still exhibit notable perception bottlenecks in capturing fine-grained anomalies. They primarily focus on how visual evidence is organized and synthesized, leaving the intrinsic perception less optimized. To mitigate this gap, we present Veritas++, a perception-enhanced reasoning framework that establishes reliable perception as the foundation of authenticity reasoning. Rather than directly optimizing the model's explanatory ability, we ground AIGI detection on three basic perception abilities, i.e., capturing fine-grained visual details, semantic anomalies and pixel-level differences. Building on this insight, we introduce Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL), which replaces open-ended description supervision with verifiable rewards to explicitly strengthen these capacities. To further integrate enhanced perception with reasoning, we introduce Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD), an adaptive distillation mechanism that prioritizes high-value distillation signals over uniform supervision, internalizing perception-aware reasoning through a privileged self-teacher. Extensive experiments across standard, in-the-wild and emerging benchmarks demonstrate that Veritas++ achieves promising generalization. The perception learning effectively bridges the perception gap and yields seamless gains on detection, while VaOPD further enables efficient capability evolvement without sacrificing existing performance. Code and checkpoints are available at https://github.com/EricTan7/VeritasPP.

中文摘要翻译: 随着图像生成模型能力的不断提升，合成图像已频繁出现在开放媒体中，使得鲁棒且泛化的AI生成图像（AIGI）检测变得日益重要。虽然多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）为黑箱二分类评分提供了一种透明的替代方案，但我们观察到当前基于MLLM的检测器在捕捉细粒度异常方面仍存在显著的感知瓶颈。它们主要关注视觉证据的组织和综合方式，而内在感知能力尚未得到充分优化。为弥补这一差距，我们提出Veritas++，一个感知增强的推理框架，将可靠的感知能力作为真实性推理的基础。与其直接优化模型的解释能力，我们将AIGI检测建立在三种基本感知能力之上，即捕捉细粒度视觉细节、语义异常和像素级差异。基于这一见解，我们引入面向感知的学习（PoRL），用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督，以显式增强这些能力。为进一步将增强的感知与推理相结合，我们提出价值感知在线策略蒸馏（VaOPD），一种自适应蒸馏机制，优先选择高价值蒸馏信号而非均匀监督，通过特权自教师内化感知感知推理。在标准、野外和新兴基准上的大量实验表明，Veritas++实现了有前景的泛化性能。感知学习有效弥合了感知差距，在检测上取得了无缝提升；而VaOPD进一步在不牺牲现有性能的情况下实现了高效的能力演进。代码和检查点可在https://github.com/EricTan7/VeritasPP获取。

</details>


### [79] [Towards Grounded GI Endoscopy VQA via Multi-Task Learning on Small VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27122)
*Itbaan Safwan,Ramail Khan,Muhammad Annas Shaikh,Muhammad Atif Tahir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种多任务微调方法，在VQA任务基础上引入辅助的定位与描述任务，通过复用息肉掩码和弱监督Grad-CAM标注，提升VLM在胃肠道内镜图像问答上的准确率与可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在胃肠道内镜VQA任务上取得了不错的效果，但临床部署要求模型不仅能给出正确答案，其内部表征还应反映答案背后的视觉证据，现有方法缺乏对这种可解释性的保证。

Method: 从现有VQA数据集出发，以最小额外标注构建辅助的定位和描述任务：直接复用专家标注的息肉掩码，对于无真值掩码的类别则利用GI领域预训练分类器结合Grad-CAM定位提供弱监督。采用低秩适配（LoRA）对三个小型VLM骨干进行微调，在匹配的计算量下比较仅VQA任务与多任务两种微调策略。

Result: 在Kvasir-VQA-x1数据集上，多任务微调策略在分布内和分布外数据上均一致提升了VQA准确率，同时改善了答案token与相关图像区域之间的隐式对齐程度。

Conclusion: 多任务微调可以有效提升VLM在胃肠道内镜VQA任务上的准确率和可解释性，且所需额外标注成本极低，有助于推动该技术在临床场景中的应用。

Original Abstract: Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopic image analysis has shifted from single-label classification toward visual question answering (VQA), where a model must answer free-form clinical questions about an image. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) achieve promising answer accuracy on this task, clinical adoption also requires the model's internal representations to reflect the visual evidence behind its answers. We propose a simple multi-task fine-tuning recipe that constructs auxiliary grounding and description tasks from an existing VQA dataset with minimal additional annotation: expert-annotated polyp masks are reused directly, while a GI-domain pretrained classifier with Grad-CAM localization provides weak supervision for finding categories that lack ground-truth masks. Three small VLM backbones are fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation under matched VQA-only and multi-task recipes on Kvasir-VQA-x1, and we show consistent accuracy gains together with improved implicit alignment between answer tokens and the relevant image region, evaluated on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data.

中文摘要翻译: 胃肠道（GI）内窥镜图像分析已从单标签分类转向视觉问答（VQA），模型需要回答关于图像的自由形式临床问题。尽管最近的视觉语言模型（VLM）在此任务上取得了不错的答案准确率，但临床采纳还要求模型的内部表征能够反映其答案背后的视觉证据。我们提出一种简单的多任务微调方法，从现有VQA数据集中以最小的额外标注构建辅助的定位与描述任务：专家标注的息肉掩码被直接复用，而对于缺乏真值掩码的类别，则利用GI领域预训练分类器结合Grad-CAM定位提供弱监督。我们在Kvasir-VQA-x1上使用低秩适配对三个小型VLM骨干进行匹配的仅VQA和多任务两种微调，结果显示，在分布内和分布外数据上，多任务策略均一致提升了准确率，同时改善了答案token与相关图像区域之间的隐式对齐程度。

</details>


### [80] [SeasonStereo: Robust Dense Stereo Matching for Multi-Date Satellite Imagery via Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27139)
*Álvaro Díaz-Laureano,Roger Marí,Elías Masquil,Pablo Arias,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeasonStereo 提出一个可扩展框架，通过合成具有可控季节性外观变化的图像对来训练立体匹配模型，并结合基础模型的零样本几何先验，实现多日期卫星图像的视差估计，无需真实多日期标注数据或LiDAR标签即可达到与最先进监督方法相当的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星图像的精确3D重建通常依赖近同时立体像对，难以适用于存在季节和光照变化的多日期图像；而训练鲁棒于外观变化的密集立体匹配模型需要大量对齐的多日期图像和地面真值几何数据，获取成本高昂且难以规模化。

Method: 提出SeasonStereo框架，包括：(1) 使用合成图像对进行训练，通过控制季节性外观变化生成多样化的训练数据；(2) 利用基础模型提供的零样本几何先验增强模型泛化能力；(3) 在不依赖真实对齐的多日期训练产品或LiDAR标签的情况下，实现跨日期卫星图像的视差估计。

Result: SeasonStereo在精度上与最先进的LiDAR监督模型相当，同时能生成更锐利的几何细节，且无需对齐的真实多日期训练数据或LiDAR标签。

Conclusion: SeasonStereo为从异构卫星图像进行大规模3D重建提供了一条实用的路径，显著降低了监督成本。

Original Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction from satellite imagery typically relies on near-simultaneous stereo pairs, limiting its applicability to diachronic settings where multi-date images exhibit varying seasonal and illumination conditions. Training dense stereo matching models robust to appearance changes is a long-standing challenge, as aligned multi-date imagery and ground-truth geometry are costly to obtain at scale. We propose SeasonStereo, a scalable framework that addresses disparity estimation from diachronic satellite images by training on synthetic image pairs with controlled seasonal appearance variation, while leveraging zero-shot geometric priors from foundation models. SeasonStereo matches the accuracy of state-of-the-art LiDAR-supervised models, while producing sharper geometric details without requiring aligned real multi-date training products or LiDAR-derived labels. As a result, SeasonStereo offers a practical path toward large-scale 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous satellite images with reduced supervision cost.

中文摘要翻译: 从卫星图像进行精确的三维重建通常依赖于近同时获取的立体像对，这限制了其在多日期场景中的适用性——其中不同日期的图像呈现出不同的季节和光照条件。训练对外观变化具有鲁棒性的密集立体匹配模型是一个长期存在的挑战，因为对齐的多日期图像和地面真值几何数据难以大规模获取且成本高昂。我们提出SeasonStereo，一个可扩展的框架，通过使用具有可控季节性外观变化的合成图像对进行训练，并结合基础模型提供的零样本几何先验，来解决多日期卫星图像的视差估计问题。SeasonStereo在精度上与最先进的LiDAR监督模型相当，同时能生成更锐利的几何细节，且无需对齐的真实多日期训练产品或LiDAR标签。因此，SeasonStereo为从异构卫星图像进行大规模三维重建提供了一条降低监督成本的实用路径。

</details>


### [81] [Explainable and Resource-Efficient Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs for Decision-Critical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27145)
*Piyush Jain,Kousik Dasgupta,Rajarshi Roy,Subarna Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ByDeWay-V2通过将显式空间关系谓词（如"左侧"、"内部"）与深度线索结合，以零训练方式增强多模态大语言模型的空间推理能力，在VSR和BLINK基准上取得显著提升，并支持低预算实时部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLM在决策关键型管线（如机器人、具身AI）中缺乏空间判断的可审计性，先前的ByDeWay（LDP方法）虽用深度分层减少幻觉，但无法区分同一几何平面内物体间的投影关系（如"左侧"、"上方"）和拓扑关系（如"内部"、"接触"）。

Method: 提出ByDeWay-V2框架：使用开放词汇目标检测器（YOLO-World-L）计算检测对象间的成对几何关系，将其转化为人类可读的结构化空间谓词，与深度线索共同注入MLLM提示中，无需训练即可桥接3D场景深度与2D空间语义。

Result: 在BLINK空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2相较于LDP在Qwen2.5-VL上实现46%的相对F1提升；将BLIP-Base在VSR上的空间推理从近随机水平恢复至0.53的F1值；最轻量配置在CPU上仅需40 token上下文预算即可运行。

Conclusion: 显式空间关系谓词能有效弥补纯深度分层方法的不足，为MLLM提供可审计的空间证据，该框架轻量且无需训练，适用于资源受限的实时决策支持场景。

Original Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in decision-critical pipelines such as robotics, embodied AI, and safety monitoring, the opacity of their spatial judgments limits operator trust and auditability. MLLMs demonstrate strong reasoning but often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and object hallucination. Prior work, ByDeWay, introduced Layered-Depth-Based Prompting (LDP), a training-free framework that mitigates hallucinations by structuring prompts using monocular depth estimation. However, coarse depth layering falls short in resolving object-to-object spatial relationships within the same geometric plane, such as projective ("left of", "above") and topological ("inside", "touching") relations. We propose ByDeWay-V2, which integrates explicit spatial relational context alongside depth cues, expressed as human-readable predicates that serve as auditable evidence for downstream decision support. Using an open-vocabulary object detector (YOLO-World-L), our framework computes pairwise geometric relations between detected objects and injects them as structured spatial predicates into the MLLM prompt, bridging 3D scene depth and 2D spatial semantics without any training. We evaluate ByDeWay-V2 on the Visual Spatial Reasoning (VSR) and BLINK benchmarks across multiple MLLMs, with hallucination grounding assessed via POPE. On the BLINK spatial subset, ByDeWay-V2 achieves a 46 percent relative F1 improvement over LDP for Qwen2.5-VL, and recovers BLIP-Base's spatial reasoning on VSR from near-random performance to a competitive F1 of 0.53. Our lightest configuration operates under a strict 40-token context budget on CPU, showing the framework's suitability for resource-constrained, real-time decision-support settings.

中文摘要翻译: 随着多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在机器人、具身AI和安全监控等决策关键型管线中的部署日益增多，其空间判断的不透明性限制了操作人员的信任度和可审计性。MLLMs展现出强大的推理能力，但在细粒度空间理解和物体幻觉方面仍存在困难。先前的工作ByDeWay引入了基于分层深度的提示方法（LDP），这是一种无训练框架，通过使用单目深度估计构建提示来缓解幻觉。然而，粗粒度的深度分层在解析同一几何平面内物体间的空间关系（如投影关系"左侧"、"上方"和拓扑关系"内部"、"接触"）方面表现不足。我们提出ByDeWay-V2，该方法将显式空间关系上下文与深度线索相结合，以人类可读的谓词形式作为下游决策支持的可审计证据。利用开放词汇目标检测器（YOLO-World-L），我们的框架计算检测对象间的成对几何关系，并将其作为结构化空间谓词注入MLLM提示中，在无需任何训练的情况下桥接3D场景深度与2D空间语义。我们在Visual Spatial Reasoning（VSR）和BLINK基准上对ByDeWay-V2进行了评估，涉及多种MLLM，并通过POPE评估了幻觉定位。在BLINK空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2相较于LDP在Qwen2.5-VL上实现了46%的相对F1提升，并将BLIP-Base在VSR上的空间推理从接近随机水平恢复至具有竞争力的F1值0.53。我们最轻量的配置在CPU上仅需40 token的严格上下文预算即可运行，展示了该框架在资源受限的实时决策支持场景中的适用性。

</details>


### [82] [Anatomy Contextualized Adaption of CT Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27154)
*Roshan Kenia,Stephanie L McNamara,William Lotter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ACA框架，通过轻量级适配冻结的CT基础模型实现解剖级视觉-语言对齐，同时增强全局上下文信息，在零样本分类任务上优于现有方法且训练效率极高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CT视觉-语言基础模型使用全卷表示，稀释了细粒度解剖信号；而现有的细粒度预训练方法丢弃了全局上下文，且从头训练计算成本高昂。

Method: ACA利用TotalSegmentator将CT卷分解为解剖级嵌入，通过跨解剖Transformer捕捉解剖间关系进行细化，并与放射学报告中提取的每个解剖和扫描级文本进行对齐。

Result: 在Merlin和CT-RATE上，ACA在零样本发现分类中一致优于冻结基础模型基线和现有细粒度方法，且嵌入缓存后训练时间不到1小时；跨解剖Transformer的注意力权重还显示了合理的跨解剖上下文路由。

Conclusion: ACA是一种轻量级方法，能够在保留并增强全局解剖上下文的同时，将CT基础模型适配到解剖基础的视觉-语言对齐任务中。

Original Abstract: CT vision-language foundation models have demonstrated promising performance across downstream tasks, but are typically trained with whole-volume representations that dilute fine-grained anatomical signals. Fine-grained vision-language pre-training addresses this by aligning anatomy-level visual features with anatomy-specific text, but in doing so discards the global context that whole-volume models provide. Furthermore, existing fine-grained approaches train from scratch, making them computationally expensive. We introduce Anatomy Contextualized Adaptation (ACA), a lightweight framework that adapts frozen CT foundation model representations for anatomy-level vision-language alignment while enhancing global contextualization. ACA uses TotalSegmentator to decompose CT volumes into anatomy-level embeddings, which are refined via a transformer that captures cross-anatomy relationships, and aligned to both per-anatomy and scan-level text extracted from radiology reports. Evaluated on Merlin and CT-RATE, ACA consistently outperforms both the frozen foundation model baselines and existing fine-grained methods in zero-shot finding classification, while requiring less than one hour of training once embeddings are cached. The attention weights learned by ACA's inter-anatomy transformer additionally indicate plausible cross-anatomy context routing. Altogether, these results support ACA as a lightweight approach for adapting CT foundation models to anatomically grounded vision-language alignment while preserving and enhancing global anatomical context.

中文摘要翻译: CT视觉-语言基础模型在下游任务中展现出有前景的性能，但通常使用全卷表示进行训练，这稀释了细粒度的解剖信号。细粒度视觉-语言预训练通过将解剖级视觉特征与解剖特定文本对齐来解决这一问题，但这样做丢弃了全卷模型所提供的全局上下文。此外，现有的细粒度方法从头开始训练，导致计算成本高昂。我们提出了解剖上下文适配（ACA），这是一个轻量级框架，它适配冻结的CT基础模型表示以实现解剖级视觉-语言对齐，同时增强全局上下文化。ACA使用TotalSegmentator将CT卷分解为解剖级嵌入，通过捕捉跨解剖关系的Transformer进行细化，并与从放射学报告中提取的每个解剖和扫描级文本对齐。在Merlin和CT-RATE上评估，ACA在零样本发现分类中一致优于冻结基础模型基线和现有细粒度方法，且在嵌入缓存后训练时间不到一小时。ACA跨解剖Transformer学习到的注意力权重还指示了合理的跨解剖上下文路由。总体而言，这些结果支持ACA作为一种轻量级方法，用于将CT基础模型适配到解剖基础的视觉-语言对齐，同时保留并增强全局解剖上下文。

</details>


### [83] [HumanCLAW: Can Vision-Language Models Act Through a Body?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27180)
*Siyao Li,Jiawei Gu,Shuai Liu,Kairui Hu,Zekun Li,Linjie Li,Chengcheng Tang,Po-Chen Wu,Ivan Shugurov,Lingni Ma,Michael Zollhoefer,Sizhe An,Abhay Mittal,Amy Zhao,Ranjay Krishna,Manling Li,Ziwei Liu,Chuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了HumanCLAW评估框架，将VLM的动作决策与底层执行解耦，通过1,218个长期任务基准测试发现当前最佳VLM仅达16.8%成功率，主要瓶颈在于缺乏具身自我意识（无法感知自身位置、是否到达目标或撞到障碍物）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在物理世界中执行任务时，任务失败难以区分是VLM决策错误还是电机控制执行失败（如失去平衡），因此需要将动作决策与底层执行解耦的评估框架。

Method: 提出HumanCLAW评估框架：在每个时间步，VLM发出原子技能指令，该指令被转换为亚秒级的连续全身运动块，在真实物理环境（含重力和碰撞）中执行，同时将执行侧的干扰（平衡、电机误差）因素排除，从而只测量VLM的动作智能（即每一步选择下一步应该执行什么）。

Result: 构建HumanCLAW-Bench基准（1,218个长期、自我中心的找物-导航-交互任务，覆盖41个室内场景），测试9个最先进VLM，最佳模型成功率仅16.8%，且发现识别目标并非瓶颈，当前VLM缺乏具身自我意识。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在具身任务中的主要局限不是视觉识别能力，而是缺乏对自己身体状态的感知意识（无法判断自身位置、是否到达目标、是否撞到障碍物），这为未来具身智能研究指明了方向。

Original Abstract: Evaluating whether a vision-language model (VLM) can act through a physical body is challenging. The outcome of an action couples the VLM's decision with motor control. When a task fails, it is hard to tell whether the VLM made a bad choice or the motor controller simply failed to execute it, e.g., losing balance and falling. In this work, we introduce HumanCLAW, an evaluation framework that decouples action decision-making from low-level execution. At every step, a harnessed, off-the-shelf VLM issues an atomic skill command, and the command is translated into a sub-second chunk of continuous full-body motion with real physical consequences, including gravity and collisions. The body can therefore act freely in the physical world, while execution-side disturbances, balance and motor errors, are factored out. What remains measurable is the model's action intelligence: its moment-to-moment choice of what the body should execute next. Based on this framework, we build HumanCLAW-Bench: 1,218 long-horizon, egocentric find-navigate-interact episodes across 41 indoor scenes. We test nine state-of-the-art VLMs and find that none solves the benchmark; the best model reaches only a 16.8% success rate. Recognizing the target is not the bottleneck. What current VLMs lack is embodied self-awareness: they lose track of their own body, failing to tell where it is, whether it has reached the goal, or whether it has hit an obstacle.

中文摘要翻译: 评估一个视觉-语言模型(VLM)能否通过物理身体执行动作具有挑战性。动作的结果耦合了VLM的决策与运动控制。当任务失败时，很难判断是VLM做出了错误选择，还是运动控制器未能成功执行（例如失去平衡而摔倒）。在这项工作中，我们提出了HumanCLAW，一个将动作决策与底层执行解耦的评估框架。在每个时间步，一个受控的、现成的VLM发出一个原子技能指令，该指令被转换为亚秒级的连续全身运动片段，并产生真实的物理后果，包括重力和碰撞。因此，身体可以在物理世界中自由行动，而执行侧的干扰、平衡和运动误差被排除在外。可测量的仅仅是模型的动作智能：它在每个时刻选择身体下一步应该执行什么的能力。基于该框架，我们构建了HumanCLAW-Bench：包含41个室内场景中的1,218个长期、以自我为中心的找物-导航-交互任务。我们测试了九个最先进的VLM，发现没有一个能解决该基准；最佳模型仅达到16.8%的成功率。识别目标并不是瓶颈。当前VLM缺乏的是具身自我意识：它们无法追踪自己的身体状态，无法判断身体在哪里、是否到达目标、或是否撞到了障碍物。

</details>


### [84] [VidMap: Exploiting Temporal Structure for Video-Based Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27194)
*Zador Pataki,Paul-Edouard Sarlin,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合SLAM时序约束与SfM全局优化的系统，用于任意无约束视频的相机标定和度量位姿恢复，在极端运动和视觉对称场景下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SLAM对初始化敏感、易产生瞬时故障，且通常需要已知相机标定；而SfM缺乏对图像顺序的利用，对视觉对称和极端运动鲁棒性不足。现有方法无法有效恢复任意未标定长视频的度量重建。

Method: 结合SLAM的强时序约束与离线SfM的灵活性和全局优化能力，利用宽基线密集图像匹配的最新进展，将时间顺序作为闭环检测的核心信息，并用度量单目深度先验增强全局优化。

Result: 在包含极端运动和视觉对称的多种挑战性数据集上，该方法在鲁棒性和准确性上显著优于最先进的SLAM和SfM方法（无论是经典方法还是学习方法，也无论相机标定已知与否）。

Conclusion: 融合时序约束与全局优化的混合策略能够有效恢复任意未标定长视频的度量重建，为导航和场景理解的大规模训练数据提供可行方案。

Original Abstract: Accurately recovering the camera's calibration and metric poses for any unconstrained video would unlock large-scale training data for navigation and scene understanding. The dominant approaches to this problem are severely limited: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is sensitive to initialization and transient failures due to its causal, incremental nature; it is often over-optimized for real-time operation and generally requires known camera calibration; while Structure-from-Motion (SfM) typically forgoes any image ordering, enabling optimal initialization and global optimization, but lacks robustness to visual symmetries and extreme motions. To bridge this gap, we introduce a system that combines the strong sequential constraints of SLAM with the flexibility and global optimization of offline SfM, enabling the metric reconstruction of arbitrary, long, uncalibrated videos. This system leverages recent advances in wide-baseline dense image matching, treats temporal ordering as a first-class citizen for reliable loop closure, and augments global optimization with metric monocular depth priors. As a result, thorough evaluations on diverse, challenging datasets that exhibit extreme motion and visual symmetries reveal that our approach is significantly more robust and accurate than both state-of-the-art SLAM and SfM, classical or learned, with given or unknown camera calibration. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/cvg/vidmap.

中文摘要翻译: 准确恢复任意无约束视频的相机标定和度量位姿，将为导航和场景理解解锁大规模训练数据。当前解决该问题的主流方法存在严重局限：同步定位与地图构建（SLAM）由于其因果增量特性，对初始化和瞬时故障敏感，通常过于针对实时运行优化，且一般需要已知的相机标定；而运动恢复结构（SfM）通常放弃图像顺序信息，虽然能实现最优初始化和全局优化，但对视觉对称和极端运动缺乏鲁棒性。为弥合这一差距，我们引入一个结合SLAM强时序约束与离线SfM灵活性和全局优化的系统，能够实现任意、长序列、未标定视频的度量重建。该系统利用宽基线密集图像匹配的最新进展，将时间顺序作为闭环检测的核心要素，并用度量单目深度先验增强全局优化。最终，在包含极端运动和视觉对称的多种挑战性数据集上的全面评估表明，我们的方法在鲁棒性和准确性上均显著优于最先进的SLAM和SfM方法——无论是经典方法还是学习方法，也无论相机标定已知与否。代码已在https://github.com/cvg/vidmap公开。

</details>


### [85] [TurboVLA: Real-Time Vision-Language-Action Model at 32 Hz on an RTX 4090 with <1 GB VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27205)
*Hengyi Xie,Chenfei Yao,Xianjin Wu,Xuanyang Xi,Yiping Tang,Di Xu,Yingying Zhu,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai,Han Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TurboVLA 提出了一种新的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）范式，将传统的 V→L→A 路径改为直接的 V+L→A 映射，通过轻量级双向视觉-语言交互和紧凑解码器实现高效机器人操控，在 LIBERO 上以 0.2B 参数、31.2 ms 延迟、0.9 GB 显存达到 97.7% 的成功率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统 VLA 模型采用以大语言模型为中心的 V→L→A 架构，每次策略调用都会产生巨大的计算和内存开销，效率低下，不适合实际部署。

Method: TurboVLA 摒弃了大语言模型作为感知与动作的中间接口，独立编码视觉观测和语言指令，通过轻量级双向视觉-语言交互直接在二者之间交换信息，并使用紧凑解码器预测连续的动作片段，实现 V+L→A 的直接映射。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上，TurboVLA 仅用 0.2B 参数，在消费级 RTX 4090 上实现 31.2 ms 推理延迟和 0.9 GB 推理显存，平均成功率达到 97.7%，匹配或超越参数量大得多的 VLA 策略。

Conclusion: TurboVLA 以其简洁高效的设计，为当前以大语言模型为中心的 VLA 范式提供了一种有力的替代方案，为视觉、语言和动作的高效连接提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models commonly adopt an LLM-centric $V \to L \to A$ pathway, where visual observations are projected into the representation space of a large language model before being decoded into robot actions. Although effective, this design incurs substantial computation and memory overhead at every policy invocation. In this work, we introduce TurboVLA, a new VLA paradigm that reformulates the conventional $V \to L \to A$ pathway as a direct $V + L \to A$ mapping. Instead of using a large language model as the central interface between perception and action, TurboVLA independently encodes visual observations and language instructions, directly exchanges information between them through lightweight bidirectional vision-language interaction, and predicts continuous action chunks with a compact decoder. This simple design constructs task-conditioned representations directly from visual and linguistic features, significantly reducing the computational and memory costs of VLA inference. On LIBERO, TurboVLA achieves 97.7% average success with only 0.2B parameters, 31.2 ms inference latency, and 0.9 GB inference VRAM on a consumer-grade RTX 4090, matching or outperforming substantially larger VLA policies.
