Result: 在合成数据上，IRIS生成稳定的画像并能区分个体用户，揭示了纯记忆方法在召回指标上的局限性。在Reddit AITA数据上，针对100位作者，IRIS决策预测准确率达61.0%，优于静态画像、纯记忆检索和无个性化基线。

Conclusion: 隐式行为建模为LLM个性化提供了可扩展的替代方案，可替代显式偏好学习，为需要持续演化用户模型的自适应对话系统和具身智能体提供实用基础。

Original Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) to individual users is essential for improving user experience, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit preference supervision such as pairwise comparisons or demographic attributes, limiting their applicability in natural interaction settings. We propose IRIS, a framework that learns dynamic user personas directly from implicit interaction streams by extracting behavioral signals from everyday conversations and iteratively refining persona representations through a prediction-driven closed loop without requiring explicit feedback. We introduce an evaluation protocol based on behavior prediction, persona stability, and decision prediction. A proof-of-concept study on a synthetic interaction stream derived from public-domain autobiographical text shows that IRIS produces stable personas and distinguishes individual users while revealing limitations of memory-only approaches on recall-oriented metrics. We then validate IRIS on anonymized real-world Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA) data, with personas built solely from each author's historical interactions. Across 100 authors, IRIS achieves the highest decision prediction accuracy among all evaluated methods (61.0%), outperforming static personas, memory-only retrieval, and no-personalization baselines. These results suggest that implicit behavioral modeling provides a scalable alternative to explicit preference learning for personalized LLMs and offers a practical foundation for adaptive conversational systems and embodied agents that require continuously evolving models of their users.

中文摘要翻译: 将大型语言模型（LLM）个性化应用于个体用户对于提升用户体验至关重要，但现有方法通常依赖显式偏好监督（如成对比较或人口统计属性），限制了其在自然交互场景中的适用性。我们提出IRIS框架，该框架直接从隐式交互流中学习动态用户画像，通过从日常对话中提取行为信号，并经由预测驱动的闭环迭代精炼画像表示，无需显式反馈。我们引入了一套基于行为预测、画像稳定性和决策预测的评估协议。在基于公开领域自传文本构建的合成交互流上进行的概念验证研究表明，IRIS能生成稳定的画像并区分个体用户，同时揭示了纯记忆方法在召回导向指标上的局限性。随后，我们在匿名化的真实Reddit r/AmItheAsshole (AITA)数据上验证了IRIS，其画像仅基于每位作者的历史交互构建。在100位作者中，IRIS在所有评估方法中取得了最高的决策预测准确率（61.0%），优于静态画像、纯记忆检索和无个性化基线。这些结果表明，隐式行为建模为个性化LLM提供了一种可扩展的显式偏好学习替代方案，并为需要持续演化用户模型的自适应对话系统和具身智能体提供了实用基础。

</details>


### [115] [From Conceptual Hydrologic Models to Conceptually Interpretable Neural Networks: A Snow-Water Mass-Conserving-Perceptron Framework for Discovering Catchment-Scale Precipitation-Storage-Runoff Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26492)
*Yuan-Heng Wang,Hoshin V. Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了雪水质量守恒感知机（SNOW-MCP）框架，在513个流域上验证了将概念水文模型重构为受物理约束的可解释神经网络的有效性。结果表明两状态网络在性能与复杂度之间取得最佳平衡，且MCP网络能以更少参数达到与LSTM相当的预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索如何将概念性水文模型（特别是雪水过程）重构为既满足物理守恒定律又具有可解释性的神经网络结构，并系统评估不同状态数量和类型对水文模拟性能的影响，为水文建模中状态的数量、类型和交互方式提供实证基础。

Method: 首先将耦合的两状态SOIL-MCP和SNOW-MCP概念模型重铸为质量守恒神经网络；然后分析两状态HYDROMCP架构中的跨节点状态信息共享；最后构建由1到5种可解释MCP单元组成的单层网络，并在美国513个CAMELS流域上进行系统评估。

Result: 水文模型与神经网络形式性能相当；中位KGEss从单状态网络的0.82提升至两状态网络的0.89和五状态网络的0.90，表明两状态后增益递减；流域特定的MCP与LSTM选择得到相同的中位KGEss（0.90），但MCP网络参数更少；基于AIC和KGE的选择能识别出平衡精度与复杂度的紧凑有向图表示。

Conclusion: 质量守恒感知机框架能有效将概念水文模型转化为可解释的神经网络，两状态网络在预测性能与模型复杂度之间达到最佳权衡。未来研究应测试针对多种水文响应（如径流、雪水当量和地下水储量）的联合训练。

Original Abstract: The Mass-Conserving Perceptron (MCP) establishes a modeling paradigm in which conceptual hydrologic models can be reformulated as physically constrained, conceptually interpretable neural networks. Here, we develop a snow-water MCP network framework and evaluate it across 513 CAMELS-US basins. We first recast a coupled two-state SOIL-MCP and SNOWMCP conceptual model as a mass-conserving neural network and show that the hydrologic-model and neural-network formulations achieve comparable predictive performance. We then examine cross-node state-information sharing within two-state HYDROMCP architectures and evaluate broader single-layer networks constructed from three types of interpretable MCP units with one to five states. Across CONUS, the median KGEss increases from 0.82 for one-state networks to 0.89 for two-state networks and 0.90 for five-state networks, suggesting diminishing aggregate gains beyond two states. Basin-specific MCP and LSTM selection yields the same median KGEss of 0.90, while the selected MCP networks use fewer parameters on average. Complementary AIC- and KGE-based selection identifies compact, basin-specific directed-graph representations that balance predictive accuracy and model complexity. These analyses provide an empirical basis for identifying the numbers, types, and interactions of states needed for hydrologic representation. Future studies should test joint training against multiple hydrologic responses, such as streamflow, snow water equivalent, and groundwater storage.

中文摘要翻译: 质量守恒感知机（MCP）建立了一种建模范式，使得概念性水文模型可以被重构为受物理约束、概念可解释的神经网络。本文开发了一个雪水MCP网络框架，并在513个CAMELS-US流域上进行了评估。我们首先将耦合的两状态SOIL-MCP和SNOW-MCP概念模型重铸为质量守恒神经网络，并表明水文模型和神经网络形式达到了可比的预测性能。接着，我们分析了双状态HYDROMCP架构中的跨节点状态信息共享，并评估了由1到5种可解释MCP单元构成的更广泛的单层网络。在全美范围内，中位KGEss从单状态网络的0.82增加到两状态网络的0.89和五状态网络的0.90，表明两状态以外的聚合增益递减。流域特定的MCP和LSTM选择得到了相同的中位KGEss值0.90，而选中的MCP网络平均使用更少的参数。互补的AIC和KGE选择识别出紧凑的、流域特定的有向图表示，这些表示在预测精度和模型复杂度之间取得了平衡。这些分析为确定水文表征所需的状态数量、类型及其相互作用提供了实证基础。未来研究应测试针对多种水文响应（如径流、雪水当量和地下水储量）的联合训练。

</details>


### [116] [From Interface to Inference: Eliciting Any-Order Inference from Any-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26504)
*Seunggeun Kim,Jaeyeon Kim,Taekyun Lee,Yuyuan Chen,Yilun Du,Sham Kakade,Sitan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出自回归语言模型缺乏原生任意顺序推理能力，发现掩码扩散模型的任意顺序接口与推理之间存在“位置不确定性”鸿沟，并提出两种方法（基于插入的FlexMDM和潜在空间掩码扩散LatentMDM）弥合该鸿沟，实验表明两者均能诱导任意顺序推理行为并提升下游性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多离散推理任务（如代码生成）本质上是非因果的，但自回归语言模型缺乏原生的任意顺序推理接口。尽管掩码扩散模型的训练目标天然提供任意顺序预测接口，但该接口并不能自动转化为任意顺序推理能力，两者之间存在鸿沟。本文旨在弥合这一接口-推理差距。

Method: 提出两种互补方法：(1) 基于插入的掩码扩散（FlexMDM），通过插入操作放松固定位置承诺，实现跨非连续区域的生成；(2) 潜在空间掩码扩散（LatentMDM），将预测转移到更粗粒度的语义片段，支持在潜在生成顺序上进行搜索。

Result: 训练了一个7B参数的FlexMDM用于Python代码生成，以及一个125M参数的LatentMDM用于GSM8K推理任务。实验表明两种方法均能诱导出不同的任意顺序推理行为，并提升了下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 固定画布、词元级别的模型即使知道应该出现什么语义成分，却不知道将其放置于何处（位置不确定性）。通过插入机制和潜在空间方法可以有效解决该问题，使模型真正支持任意顺序推理。

Original Abstract: Many discrete reasoning tasks, such as code generation, are inherently non-causal: programmers move between high-level structure and local details, a process we call any-order inference. For autoregressive language models, which lack a native any-order interface, non-causal abilities such as infilling and next-edit prediction require hand-designed mechanisms. Can we instead design models that natively support any-order inference? Masked diffusion models have recently emerged as compelling candidates, as their any-order training objective naturally offers an any-order prediction interface. This interface, however, does not automatically yield any-order inference. We demonstrate that this interface-inference gap stems from positional uncertainty: fixed-canvas, token-level models may know what semantic component should appear without knowing where to place it. In light of this, we propose two complementary approaches: (1) Insertion-based masked diffusion, building on FlexMDM (Kim et al, 2025), relaxes fixed-position commitments via insertions, enabling generation across non-contiguous regions. (2) Latent-space masked diffusion shifts prediction to coarser semantic segments, enabling search over latent generation orders. Empirically, we train a 7B FlexMDM for Python coding and a 125M LatentMDM for GSM8K and show that both approaches induce distinct any-order inference behaviors and improve downstream performance. We release our codebase at https://github.com/SeunggeunKimkr/genuine-any-order.

中文摘要翻译: 许多离散推理任务，如代码生成，本质上是非因果的：程序员在高层次结构和局部细节之间来回切换，这一过程我们称为任意顺序推理。对于自回归语言模型而言，其本身缺乏原生的任意顺序接口，因而诸如填充和下一编辑预测等非因果能力需要手工设计的机制。我们能否设计出原生支持任意顺序推理的模型？掩码扩散模型最近成为引人注目的候选方案，因为其任意顺序训练目标天然提供了任意顺序预测接口。然而，该接口并不能自动产生任意顺序推理。我们证明，这种接口-推理差距源于位置不确定性：固定画布上的词元级模型可能知道应该出现什么语义成分，却不知道将其放置在何处。基于此，我们提出两种互补方法：(1) 基于插入的掩码扩散（FlexMDM，Kim等人, 2025），通过插入操作放宽固定位置约束，实现跨非连续区域的生成；(2) 潜在空间掩码扩散，将预测转移到更粗粒度的语义片段，支持在潜在生成顺序上进行搜索。实验方面，我们训练了一个7B参数的FlexMDM用于Python代码生成，以及一个125M参数的LatentMDM用于GSM8K推理任务，结果表明两种方法均能诱导出独特的任意顺序推理行为，并提升了下游任务性能。我们已将代码开源至 https://github.com/SeunggeunKimkr/genuine-any-order。

</details>


### [117] [Collaborative Weighting with Pessimistic Critic for Mitigating Overestimation in Off-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26509)
*Gong Gao,Xiao Lai,Ziqi Xie,Guojie Chen,Xianhui Liu,Weidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic (CWAC)框架，通过分布化价值函数和协作加权机制，显式建模预测不确定性以抑制过估计偏差，提升连续控制任务的训练稳定性与性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 离策略强化学习中，TD学习引入噪声导致非平稳优化，贪心策略更新放大早期估计误差，递归传播造成持久过估计偏差和训练不稳定。现有方法通过优先级采样或修改价值学习目标来缓解，但过度关注高不确定性转移，反而加剧偏差。

Method: 提出CWAC框架：1）使用分布化评论家建模回报不确定性；2）引入协作加权机制，联合重加权TD误差和不确定性，从可靠样本中学习并抑制噪声更新；3）通过从回报分布中采样实现随机悲观价值估计，缓解策略改进中的误差传播。可无缝集成到SAC、TD3、DDPG等框架中。

Result: 在多种模拟连续控制任务上，CWAC显著提升了现有算法（SAC、TD3、DDPG）的性能，有效抑制了过估计偏差并改善了训练稳定性。

Conclusion: CWAC通过显式建模价值估计中的预测不确定性并引入协作加权机制，为离策略演员-评论家方法提供了一种统一、轻量且有效的偏差缓解方案，显著提升了连续控制任务的性能。

Original Abstract: Deep off-policy reinforcement learning algorithms for continuous control typically rely on neural value function approximation to guide policy improvement. However, temporal-difference (TD) learning introduces noisy targets, resulting in non-stationary optimization, while greedy policy updates amplify early-stage estimation errors. The recursive propagation of such errors leads to persistent overestimation bias and degraded training stability in actor-critic methods. Existing approaches attempt to alleviate this issue via prioritized sampling or modified value learning objectives, but often overemphasize high-uncertainty transitions caused by limited data coverage or bootstrapping errors, thereby further amplifying bias.In this paper, we propose Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic (CWAC), a unified framework that explicitly accounts for predictive uncertainty in value estimation. CWAC employs distributional critic to model return uncertainty and introduces a collaborative weighting mechanism that jointly reweights TD-errors and uncertainty, enabling robust learning from reliable samples while suppressing noisy updates. In addition, we incorporate a stochastic pessimistic value estimation scheme via sampling from the return distribution, which effectively mitigates error propagation during policy improvement. CWAC can be seamlessly integrated into existing off-policy algorithm frameworks such as SAC, TD3, and DDPG with minimal overhead. Empirical results demonstrate that our proposed method significantly enhances performance across a diverse range of simulated tasks. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CWAC-348E.

中文摘要翻译: 用于连续控制的深度离策略强化学习算法通常依赖神经价值函数近似来指导策略改进。然而，时序差分（TD）学习引入噪声目标，导致非平稳优化，而贪心策略更新则会放大早期阶段的估计误差。这种误差的递归传播会导致演员-评论家方法中出现持久的过估计偏差和训练稳定性下降。现有方法尝试通过优先级采样或修改价值学习目标来缓解这一问题，但往往过度关注因有限数据覆盖或自举误差导致的高不确定性转移，从而进一步放大了偏差。本文提出协作加权演员-评论家（Collaborative Weighting Actor-Critic, CWAC），这是一个统一框架，显式地考虑了价值估计中的预测不确定性。CWAC采用分布化评论家对回报不确定性进行建模，并引入一种协作加权机制，联合重加权TD误差和不确定性，从而能够从可靠样本中进行鲁棒学习，同时抑制噪声更新。此外，我们通过从回报分布中采样引入随机悲观价值估计方案，有效减轻了策略改进过程中的误差传播。CWAC可以无缝集成到现有的离策略算法框架（如SAC、TD3和DDPG）中，且开销极低。实验结果表明，我们提出的方法在多种模拟任务上显著提升了性能。我们的代码已在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/CWAC-348E公开。

</details>


### [118] [HiFloat4 Format for End-To-End Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26515)
*Hei Yi Mak,Shadan Golestan,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Yaoyuan Wang,Yao Wang,Junsong Wang,Tianchi Hu,Fengchen He,Guipeng Hu,Tanzila Rahman,Anandharaju Durai Raju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个端到端FP4强化学习后训练方法，发现FP4 RL性能下降的主因是rollout激活量化中的离群值导致下溢，而非训练端量化误差。为此提出Rollout-ResQ（rollout残差量化）结合HiF4格式，在保持计算效率的同时大幅缩小了与BF16精度的差距。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有FP4量化研究多聚焦于预训练或推理阶段，尚未有工作探索端到端FP4强化学习后训练（即rollout和训练策略的前向、反向传播均在4-bit精度下运行）。作者希望填补这一空白，并系统性地诊断FP4 RL性能退化的根本原因。

Method: 提出Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ)：在FP4 rollout矩阵乘法中引入一个具有硬件友好稀疏模式的单一残差校正项，用于恢复因离群值驱动的下溢而丢失的精度，且不会增加rollout的计算开销。同时采用HiFloat4 (HiF4)格式，其三层级分层缩放机制在4-bit预算下保持分辨率。

Result: 1) 在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，Rollout-ResQ+HiF4将FP4 RL与BF16的精度差距从4.9%缩小至1.1%；2) 应用于MXFP4格式时，差距从13.6%缩小至5.3%，表明FP4格式选择是可恢复精度的关键上限因素。

Conclusion: HiF4是实现端到端FP4 RL后训练的使能格式，Rollout-ResQ是缩小与BF16精度差距的激活侧机制。二者结合使得全量化FP4 RL接近全精度性能。

Original Abstract: We present, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, in which both the rollout and training policies, including their forward and backward passes, operate at 4-bit precision. A systematic study reveals that the dominant source of degradation in FP4 RL is not training-side quantization error but rollout activation quantization: outliers stretch the dynamic range so far that a large number of activation values underflow to zero under FP4. Counterintuitively, restoring the training policy to higher precision while keeping the rollout in FP4 makes accuracy worse than full FP4 baseline, exposing rollout-training mismatch as the principal failure mode and ruling out standard pretraining-style fixes. We address this with Rollout Residual Quantization (Rollout-ResQ): a single residual correction term constrained to a hardware-friendly sparsity pattern, added only to the FP4 rollout matmul -- a lightweight correction that recovers most of the precision lost to outlier-driven underflow without inflating the rollout's compute footprint. On Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-Math-7B, Rollout-ResQ paired with the HiFloat4 (HiF4) format -- whose three-level hierarchical scaling preserves resolution under FP4's tight 4-bit budget -- closes the accuracy gap to BF16 from 4.9% to 1.1%, bringing fully quantized FP4 RL within striking distance of full precision. Applied to the open-standard MXFP4, the same recipe narrows the gap from 13.6% to 5.3%, revealing that FP4 format choice is a key factor that determines the ceiling on recoverable accuracy. Together, these results establish HiF4 as the enabling format for end-to-end FP4 RL post-training, and Rollout-ResQ as the activation-side mechanism that makes the gap to BF16 closable.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了据我们所知首个端到端FP4强化学习后训练方法，其中rollout和训练策略（包括其前向和反向传播）均在4-bit精度下运行。系统性研究表明，FP4 RL性能退化的主要来源并非训练侧量化误差，而是rollout激活量化：离群值将动态范围拉伸至过远，导致大量激活值在FP4下下溢为零。反直觉的是，将训练策略恢复为更高精度而保持rollout为FP4，会使准确率比全FP4基线更差，这揭示了rollout-训练不匹配是主要的失效模式，并排除了标准的预训练风格修复方案。我们通过Rollout残差量化（Rollout-ResQ）来解决这一问题：一个具有硬件友好稀疏模式的单一残差校正项，仅添加到FP4 rollout矩阵乘法中——这是一种轻量级校正，无需增加rollout的计算开销即可恢复大部分因离群值驱动的下溢而丢失的精度。在Qwen2.5-3B和Qwen2.5-Math-7B上，Rollout-ResQ与HiFloat4（HiF4）格式（其三层级分层缩放机制在FP4紧凑的4-bit预算下保持分辨率）相结合，将精度差距从4.9%缩小至1.1%，使全量化FP4 RL接近全精度性能。将该方案应用于开放标准MXFP4，差距从13.6%缩小至5.3%，表明FP4格式选择是决定可恢复精度上限的关键因素。综上，这些结果确立了HiF4作为端到端FP4 RL后训练的使能格式，以及Rollout-ResQ作为缩小与BF16差距的激活侧机制。

</details>


### [119] [From Unsupervised Subgroups to Hypothetical State-Intervention Policies: An Evaluation of Selected Subgrouping Methods in Observational Health Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26521)
*Vasundhara Acharya,Bulent Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合因果发现、无监督聚类和双重稳健策略评估的框架，用于在预算约束下构建可解释的子组策略优先排序，并在三个健康数据集上验证了其有效性，尽管统计显著性有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的亚组分析方法在处理观察性生物医学数据时，由于个体仅在一个暴露状态下被观测、真实个体治疗效果未知且因果结构不确定，容易产生不稳定且难以解释的结论。因此，需要一种能够构建可解释、稳定的子组以支持预算约束下的政策优先级排序的方法。

Method: 提出一个五步框架：(1) 基于因果发现的协变量选择；(2) 发现-评估样本分割；(3) 归纳式无监督聚类（比较K-means、硬/隶属度加权/随机Fuzzy C-means、贝叶斯高斯混合模型及作为对比的CATE树）；(4) 不确定性感知的子组选择；(5) 留出集上的双重稳健策略评估。在PIMA印第安人糖尿病数据集和NHANES数据集上进行政策评估。

Result: BMI政策使用贝叶斯GMM获得最高未约束效用0.799，血糖政策使用硬/隶属度加权FCM获得0.735，吸烟史政策使用K-means获得0.775。所有策略风险差异的配对95%置信区间均包含零，经Holm校正后无统计显著结果。贝叶斯池化保留未约束分配，Empirical Bernstein门控更保守。效用相似的策略会优先考虑不同的个体。

Conclusion: 该框架提供的应被解释为假设依赖的决策支持证据，而非干预收益的证明。预算约束下的子组优先排序是可行的，但结果依赖于因果假设且统计显著性有限。

Original Abstract: Conventional subgroup analyses can yield unstable and difficult-to-interpret conclusions, especially in observational biomedical data where each individual is observed under only one exposure state, true individual treatment effects are unavailable, and causal structure is uncertain. We investigate whether subgroups constructed from pretreatment characteristics, without using exposure, outcome, or estimated treatment-effect information, can serve as interpretable units for budget-constrained policy prioritization. We propose a framework combining causal-discovery-informed covariate selection, discovery-evaluation sample splitting, inductive unsupervised clustering, uncertainty-aware subgroup selection, and held-out doubly robust policy evaluation. We compare K-means, hard, membership-weighted, and stochastic Fuzzy C-means, Bayesian Gaussian mixture models, and a supervised causal-forest-derived CATE-tree comparator. Policies are evaluated under a 70% budget for hypothetical obesity-to-non-obesity and elevated-to-lower-glucose state shifts in the PIMA Indians Diabetes dataset and for a lifetime-smoking-history contrast in NHANES. The highest estimated ungated utilities were 0.799 for the BMI policy using Bayesian GMM, 0.735 for the glucose policy using hard or membership-weighted FCM, and 0.775 for the smoking-history policy using K-means. All paired 95% confidence intervals for policy-risk differences included zero, and no comparison remained statistically significant after Holm adjustment. Bayesian pooling generally preserved ungated allocations, whereas Empirical Bernstein gating was more conservative. Policies with similar estimated utility could nevertheless prioritize different individuals. The findings should be interpreted as assumption-dependent decision-support evidence for hypothetical state contrasts rather than proof of intervention benefit.

中文摘要翻译: 传统的亚组分析可能产生不稳定且难以解释的结论，尤其是在观察性生物医学数据中——每个个体仅在一个暴露状态下被观测、真实的个体治疗效果不可得、且因果结构不确定。我们探究了仅基于预处理特征（不利用暴露、结局或估计的治疗效应信息）构建的子组是否可以作为预算约束下政策优先级排序的可解释单元。我们提出一个结合了因果发现驱动的协变量选择、发现-评估样本分割、归纳式无监督聚类、不确定性感知的子组选择以及留出集上双重稳健策略评估的框架。我们比较了K-means、硬聚类、隶属度加权和随机Fuzzy C-means、贝叶斯高斯混合模型，以及作为对照的基于监督因果森林的CATE树。在PIMA印第安人糖尿病数据集中，针对假设的肥胖到非肥胖和血糖升高到降低的状态转变，以及在NHANES中针对终生吸烟史对比，在70%预算下评估各项政策。各政策估计的最高未约束效用分别为：BMI政策使用贝叶斯GMM为0.799，血糖政策使用硬聚类或隶属度加权FCM为0.735，吸烟史政策使用K-means为0.775。所有政策风险差异的配对95%置信区间均包含零，经Holm校正后无任何比较保持统计显著。贝叶斯池化通常保留未约束分配，而Empirical Bernstein门控则更为保守。估计效用相似的政策仍可能优先考虑不同的个体。这些发现应被解释为针对假设状态对比的、依赖于假设的决策支持证据，而非干预收益的证明。

</details>


### [120] [The Art of Not Forgetting A Local Learning Architecture for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26523)
*Ashmith Atmuri,Yashaswini Rao Bhogarajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CMP（认知记忆原语）架构，通过稀疏关系编码、双层竞争记忆和局部更新实现持续学习，在域增量语言建模协议上相比基于EWC的Transformer表现出更低的灾难性遗忘，但存在单域精度差距和视觉任务上的空结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆三者的结合是否能够相比传统的基于反向传播的持续学习方法减少灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出CMP架构：将输入表示为稀疏关系编码，存储于双层竞争记忆系统中，通过局部更新规则进行学习，避免在特征生成系统中使用端到端反向传播。

Result: 在字节级语言建模的域增量协议上，CMP的后向迁移（遗忘）显著低于使用在线弹性权重巩固（EWC）的参数匹配Transformer；在15域实验中遗忘行为稳定；但存在较大的单域精度差距，且在视觉基准上得到空结果，同时无法与独立精度提升机制结合。

Conclusion: 稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆的组合是持续学习中有前景的方向，但需要进一步研究学习规则、表示和架构设计在缓解灾难性遗忘中的各自作用。

Original Abstract: We introduce CMP (Cognitive Memory Primitive), a continual-learning architecture that repre?sents inputs as sparse relational codes, stores them in a two-tier competitive memory, and learns through local updates without end-to-end backpropagation through its feature-generating system. We investigate whether combining sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory can reduce catastrophic forgetting relative to conventional backpropagation-based continual?learning approaches. On a controlled domain-incremental byte-level language modeling protocol, CMP demonstrates substantially lower backward transfer than a parameter-matched Trans?former trained with online Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC). Across a three-seed replicated 15-domain experiment, CMP exhibits stable forgetting behavior, while separate head-to-head comparisons and domain-order analyses show consistently lower forgetting than the evaluated Transformer baseline under the reported experimental settings. We report these findings alongside a substantial single-domain accuracy gap relative to the Transformer, a null result on a vision benchmark, and a documented failure to combine CMP with an independent accuracy-improving mechanism, reflecting our commitment to reporting both positive and negative outcomes. These results suggest that the combination of sparse representations, local learning, and persistent memory is a promising direction for continual learning, while motivating further investigation into the respective roles of learning rules, representations, and architectural design in mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了CMP（认知记忆原语），一种持续学习架构，它将输入表示为稀疏关系编码，存储于双层竞争记忆系统中，并通过局部更新进行学习，无需在特征生成系统中使用端到端反向传播。我们研究了结合稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆是否能够在减少灾难性遗忘方面优于传统的基于反向传播的持续学习方法。在一个受控的域增量字节级语言建模协议上，CMP相比使用在线弹性权重巩固（EWC）的参数匹配Transformer表现出显著更低的后向迁移。在三个随机种子的15域重复实验中，CMP表现出稳定的遗忘行为，而独立的头对头比较和域顺序分析均显示，在所报告的实验设置下，CMP的遗忘程度一致地低于所评估的Transformer基线。我们同时报告了CMP相对于Transformer存在较大的单域精度差距、在视觉基准上得到空结果，以及未能成功将CMP与独立精度提升机制结合，这体现了我们对报告正面和负面结果的承诺。这些结果表明，稀疏表示、局部学习和持久记忆的组合是持续学习中一个富有前景的研究方向，同时激励进一步探究学习规则、表示方法和架构设计在缓解灾难性遗忘中各自的作用。

</details>


### [121] [AgentGFM: A Graph Foundation Model with Node-Agent Information-Flow Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26533)
*Jingbo Cui,Jitao Zhao,Di Jin,Dongxiao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentGFM，将每个节点视为智能体，通过端到端可训练的策略实现自适应信息流控制，解决图基础模型中固定传播模式不适应多样化拓扑结构的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFMs）依赖人工设计的固定传播方案，难以适应不同图甚至同一图中不同节点之间多样化的局部结构模式，缺乏让节点自主决定信息如何传播的能力。

Method: 提出AgentGFM框架，将每个节点视为智能体，所有节点共享一个端到端可训练的策略。通过"预测-行动-观察-修正"循环实现自适应信息流控制，其中行动阶段包括三个决策：源接收（source reception）、信号通道选择（signal-channel selection）和增益感知节点级停止（gain-aware node-wise halting）。

Result: 在节点级、图级和大规模迁移场景下的广泛实验表明，AgentGFM在多种图拓扑结构上均有效。

Conclusion: AgentGFM通过将节点建模为智能体并赋予其自主决策信息传播的能力，有效解决了图基础模型中固定传播模式不适应多样化拓扑结构的问题，在多种迁移场景下表现优异。

Original Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) aim to learn transferable knowledge from multi-domain graphs and adapt to unseen scenarios. As a fundamental source of relational semantics in graphs, the transferability of topological patterns has long been central to GFM research. However, local structural patterns may vary across graphs and even among nodes within the same graph. Despite such structural variation, most existing GFMs rely on manually designed propagation schemes and apply them to new graphs largely unchanged. Such fixed schemes may not suit the diverse structural patterns of different nodes. This raises a key question: can each node autonomously determine how information should be propagated through the graph? We refer to this capability as information-flow control. Inspired by recent advances in agent technology, we formulate this problem as agent-based decision making and treat each node as an agent. Accordingly, we propose AgentGFM, in which all node agents follow a shared end-to-end trainable policy rather than using independent models. For adaptive information-flow control, each node interacts with the graph through a predict-act-observe-correct process. During the act stage, the node makes three decisions: source reception, signal-channel selection and gain-aware node-wise halting. The resulting observation is compared with the prediction and their discrepancy is used to correct the node state and guide subsequent interactions. Extensive experiments across node-level, graph-level and large-scale transfer scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of AgentGFM across diverse graph topologies.

中文摘要翻译: 图基础模型（GFMs）旨在从多领域图中学习可迁移知识，并适应未见场景。作为图中关系语义的基本来源，拓扑模式的可迁移性长期以来一直是GFM研究的核心。然而，局部结构模式可能在不同图之间甚至同一图中的不同节点之间发生变化。尽管存在这种结构差异，现有的大多数GFM仍依赖人工设计的传播方案，并在应用于新图时基本保持不变。这种固定方案可能不适合不同节点的多样化结构模式。这引出一个关键问题：每个节点能否自主决定信息应如何通过图进行传播？我们将这种能力称为信息流控制。受近期智能体技术进展的启发，我们将该问题形式化为基于智能体的决策问题，并将每个节点视为一个智能体。据此，我们提出AgentGFM，其中所有节点智能体遵循一个共享的端到端可训练策略，而非使用独立模型。为了实现自适应的信息流控制，每个节点通过"预测-行动-观察-修正"过程与图进行交互。在行动阶段，节点做出三个决策：源接收、信号通道选择和增益感知的节点级停止。将产生的观察与预测进行比较，它们的差异用于修正节点状态并指导后续交互。在节点级、图级和大规模迁移场景上的广泛实验证明了AgentGFM在多种图拓扑结构上的有效性。

</details>


### [122] [From Tokens to Watt-hours: Analytical Energy Estimation for LLM Inference on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26571)
*Tina Vartziotis,Rodopi Kosteli,Elli Vartziotis,George Dasoulas,Michael Keckeisen,Konstantinos Skianis,Sotirios Kotsopoulos,Francesca Dominici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需直接运行时功耗测量的、基于解析模型和经验校准的GPU级LLM推理能耗估算方法，适用于NVIDIA H100加速器，支持对预填充和解码阶段进行细粒度能耗分解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型推理的能耗正成为AI系统环境足迹的重要组成部分，但直接测量需要硬件遥测或功率仪表等条件，限制了其在对比研究、早期系统设计和可持续性报告中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种解析结构化、经验校准的GPU级能耗估算方法：结合参数缩放后的Transformer FLOP计算、校准的内存流量因子、FP16/BF16张量核心计算和HBM数据搬移的硬件特定能耗系数，明确分离提示预填充和自回归解码阶段，并将总能耗分解为计算、参数访问、KV缓存写入和注意力读取四个分量。

Result: 该方法能够估算输入token、输出token及完整推理请求的能耗，并分析能耗随模型规模、上下文长度和生成token数量的缩放行为。估算结果旨在提供透明、可复现的近似值，适用于模型比较、绿色编码分析和设计阶段的推理工作负载评估。

Conclusion: 该估算方法并非替代物理功耗测量，而是提供一种透明、可复现、假设明确的近似方法，适用于模型比较、绿色编码分析和推理工作负载的设计阶段评估。

Original Abstract: The operational energy consumption of large language model (LLM) inference is becoming an increasingly important component of the environmental footprint of deployed AI systems. However, direct measurement of inference energy often requires hardware telemetry, power instrumentation, or infrastructure-specific monitoring, limiting its applicability in comparative studies, early-stage system design, and sustainability reporting. This report presents an analytically structured, empirically calibrated, GPU-level methodology for estimating LLM inference energy on NVIDIA H100-class accelerators without direct runtime measurement. The proposed estimator combines parameter-scaled transformer FLOP accounting, calibrated memory-traffic factors, and hardware-specific energy coefficients for FP16/BF16 tensor-core computation and high-bandwidth-memory movement. It explicitly separates prompt prefill from autoregressive decoding, enabling energy estimates for input tokens, output tokens, and complete inference requests. The methodology further decomposes total energy into compute, parameter-access, key-value-cache write, and attention-read components, allowing the scaling behavior with model size, context length, and generated-token count to be analyzed. The resulting estimates are not intended to replace physical power measurements; rather, they provide transparent, reproducible, and assumption-explicit approximations suitable for model comparison, green-coding analysis, and design-time evaluation of LLM inference workloads.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型推理的运行能耗正成为已部署AI系统环境足迹中日益重要的组成部分。然而，直接测量推理能耗通常需要硬件遥测、功率仪表或基础设施特定的监控手段，这限制了其在对比研究、早期系统设计和可持续性报告中的适用性。本文提出了一种解析结构化、经验校准的、无需直接运行时测量的GPU级方法论，用于估算NVIDIA H100级加速器上的大语言模型推理能耗。所提出的估算器结合了参数缩放后的Transformer FLOP计算、校准的内存流量因子，以及FP16/BF16张量核心计算和高带宽内存数据搬移的硬件特定能耗系数。该方法明确区分了提示预填充和自回归解码阶段，从而能够估算输入token、输出token以及完整推理请求的能耗。该方法论进一步将总能耗分解为计算、参数访问、键值缓存写入和注意力读取四个分量，使得能耗随模型规模、上下文长度和生成token数量的缩放行为得以分析。最终的估算结果并非旨在替代物理功耗测量，而是提供一种透明、可复现且假设明确的近似方法，适用于模型比较、绿色编码分析以及大语言模型推理工作负载的设计阶段评估。

</details>


### [123] [Simultaneous Coverage and Efficiency Guarantee in Online Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26577)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自适应共形推断（ACI）的三个根本缺陷——符号化长期覆盖误差掩盖持续错误、缺乏预测集效率保证、以及静态基准在分布漂移下失去意义，提出了统一在线学习框架。在完全对抗、随机全分数反馈和协变量相关随机三种场景下，分别实现了覆盖误差与动态基准效率的同时控制，并给出了最优性理论保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ACI方法存在三个根本限制：1) 仅控制符号化长期覆盖误差，持续性错误可被后续误差掩盖；2) 缺乏对预测集大小的控制，导致平凡过宽预测集；3) 效率保证针对固定后见预测器，在分布漂移下意义有限。需要同时解决绝对覆盖违规和动态基准下的预测集效率问题。

Method: 1) 完全对抗场景：利用ACI更新等价于投影在线梯度下降优化pinball损失，推导任意单调Lipschitz效率目标下的同时覆盖与效率保证；2) 随机全分数反馈场景：提出滑动窗口分位数追踪器，并证明匹配极小化最优下界；3) 协变量相关随机场景：提出分区ACI算法追踪函数值预言机阈值，同时保证覆盖与效率。

Result: 1) 对抗场景下无需分布或凸性假设即可实现同时覆盖与效率保证；2) 随机场景下滑动窗口分位数追踪器达到率最优（匹配极小化下界）；3) 协变量相关场景下分区ACI算法追踪函数值阈值并给出同时保证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一在线学习框架有效解决了ACI方法在分布漂移下的覆盖误差掩盖和效率退化问题，在三种重要模型下均实现了非抵消性覆盖违规与动态基准效率的同时控制，理论最优性通过匹配下界得到验证。

Original Abstract: Adaptive conformal inference (ACI) of Gibbs and Cand{è}s and its variants are the standard approach to online conformal prediction under distribution shift, but they suffer from three fundamental limitations. First, their guarantees control only the \emph{signed} long-run coverage error: persistent miscoverage in one direction can be masked by compensating errors later, so a method can satisfy the theoretical guarantee while being badly wrong for extended periods. Second, existing guarantees say nothing about prediction-set size, so validity can be achieved trivially at the cost of unduly wide prediction sets. Third, the efficiency guarantees that do exist compare against a \emph{fixed} predictor chosen in hindsight, a benchmark that becomes increasingly less meaningful once the data-generating distribution shifts, since the very notion of an optimal threshold then changes over time.
  We consider a unified online learning framework that simultaneously controls absolute, non-cancelling coverage violation and prediction-set efficiency against a dynamically evolving benchmark for three important models. In the fully adversarial setting, exploiting the fact that the standard ACI update is exactly projected online gradient descent on the pinball loss, we derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees for arbitrary monotone Lipschitz efficiency objectives, with no distributional or {\it convexity} assumptions. In the stochastic setting with full-score feedback, we propose a sliding-window quantile tracker and establish a matching minimax lower bound showing our algorithm is rate-optimal. In the covariate-dependent stochastic setting, we develop a partitioned ACI algorithm that tracks a function-valued oracle threshold, and derive simultaneous coverage and efficiency guarantees.

中文摘要翻译: Gibbs和Candès提出的自适应共形推断（ACI）及其变体是分布漂移下在线共形预测的标准方法，但它们存在三个根本缺陷。首先，其保证仅控制符号化长期覆盖误差：一个方向的持续错误覆盖可被后续的补偿误差掩盖，因此方法在满足理论保证的同时可能长时间严重失效。其次，现有保证未涉及预测集大小，因此可通过迫使其过宽来平凡地实现有效性。第三，现有的效率保证仅与事后选定的固定预测器比较，一旦数据生成分布发生漂移，这一基准便愈发失去意义，因为最优阈值本身也会随时间变化。
本文考虑一个统一的在线学习框架，在三种重要模型下同时控制非抵消的绝对覆盖违规和针对动态演化基准的预测集效率。在完全对抗场景中，利用标准ACI更新恰好是投影在线梯度下降在pinball损失上的更新这一事实，我们推导出任意单调Lipschitz效率目标下的同时覆盖与效率保证，无需任何分布或凸性假设。在具有全分数反馈的随机场景中，我们提出一个滑动窗口分位数追踪器，并建立匹配的极小化最优下界，证明算法是率最优的。在协变量依赖的随机场景中，我们开发了一个分区ACI算法来追踪函数值的预言机阈值，并推导出同时的覆盖与效率保证。

</details>


### [124] [Benchmarking ConvLSTM for One-Day-Ahead IMDAA Rainfall-Field Prediction across Four Indian Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26581)
*Tanmay Ghosh,Shaurabh Anand,Rakesh Gomaji Nannewar,Nithin Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了ConvLSTM与多种基线方法在基于小尺度日再分析格点数据上的降雨预测性能，发现ConvLSTM并未持续优于简单方法，FC-LSTM在多数城市表现更好，而持续性预测在高降雨日检测中最佳。结果表明，网格化输入本身不足以证明ConvLSTM的合理性，架构选择需结合全面基准测试。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有证据大多来自密集高频雷达序列，ConvLSTM在小尺度日降雨格点预测上的实际优势尚未被充分验证，因此需要系统评估卷积循环结构在此场景下的有效性。

Method: 基于IMDAA再分析数据（1998-2020年6-9月），针对印度四个城市，采用十种方法（包括朴素方法、统计方法、树基方法和神经网络）进行一日提前降雨场预测，比较仅大气输入和降雨历史加大气输入两种设定，从完整场、区域均值、空间异常和高降雨日四个维度评估性能。

Result: ConvLSTM未持续优于简单方法；FC-LSTM在班加罗尔、加尔各答和孟买取得最低区域均雨量误差；持续性预测在德里最优；ConvLSTM仅在孟买取得最低空间异常误差；所有神经模型低估降雨量且在高降雨日预测阈值超标过少；持续性预测在所有城市取得最高检测性能；模型对最新输入日最敏感。

Conclusion: 网格化输入本身不足以证明ConvLSTM的合理性，架构选择应依据对平均性能、空间性能和高降雨性能的全面基准测试。

Original Abstract: Convolutional long short-term memory networks (ConvLSTMs) are widely used for precipitation forecasting, but most evidence for their performance comes from dense, high-frequency radar sequences. This study tests whether convolutional recurrence improves one-day-ahead rainfall-field prediction on small daily reanalysis grids. Indian Monsoon Data Assimilation and Analysis (IMDAA) fields for June-September 1998-2020 were analysed for Bengaluru, Delhi, Kolkata and Mumbai. Ten naive, statistical, tree-based and neural approaches were compared using atmospheric-only and rainfall-history-plus-atmospheric inputs. Performance was assessed for complete fields, domain-mean rainfall, spatial anomalies and high-rainfall days.
  ConvLSTM did not consistently outperform simpler alternatives. FC-LSTM produced the numerically lowest domain-mean rainfall error in Bengaluru, Kolkata and Mumbai, whereas persistence performed best in Delhi. ConvLSTM produced the numerically lowest spatial-anomaly error only in Mumbai, where rainfall fields showed greater short-term spatial continuity and rainfall-history inputs improved all three neural architectures. The difference between ConvLSTM and FC-LSTM was nevertheless small. Neural models underestimated rainfall magnitude and predicted too few threshold exceedances on high-rainfall days, while persistence achieved the highest detection performance in every city. Post-hoc analyses showed that the selected models were most sensitive to the latest input day, with broader recent-lag sensitivity in Mumbai. These findings show that gridded inputs alone do not justify ConvLSTM and that architecture choice should follow strong benchmarking across average, spatial and high-rainfall performance.

中文摘要翻译: 卷积长短期记忆网络（ConvLSTM）被广泛用于降水预报，但支持其性能的大部分证据来自密集的高频雷达序列。本研究测试了在小尺度日再分析格点数据上，卷积循环是否能够改善一日提前的降雨场预测。分析了印度季风数据同化与分析（IMDAA）1998-2020年6-9月的场数据，涵盖班加罗尔、德里、加尔各答和孟买四个城市。比较了十种朴素方法、统计方法、树基方法和神经网络方法，使用仅大气输入以及降雨历史加大气输入两种设定。从完整场、区域平均降雨量、空间异常和高降雨日四个维度评估了性能。ConvLSTM并未持续优于简单替代方法。FC-LSTM在班加罗尔、加尔各答和孟买取得了数值最低的区域平均降雨量误差，而持续性预测在德里表现最佳。ConvLSTM仅在孟买取得了数值最低的空间异常误差，该城市降雨场表现出更大的短期空间连续性，且降雨历史输入改善了所有三种神经架构的性能。尽管如此，ConvLSTM与FC-LSTM之间的差异很小。神经模型低估了降雨量，并在高降雨日预测了过少的阈值超标次数，而持续性预测在所有城市取得了最高的检测性能。事后分析表明，所选模型对最新输入日最敏感，孟买具有更宽的近期滞后敏感性。这些发现表明，网格化输入本身不足以证明ConvLSTM的合理性，架构选择应依据对平均性能、空间性能和高降雨性能的全面基准测试。

</details>


### [125] [Uncertainty-Guided LLM Semantic Augmentation for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26599)
*Jialu Xu,Mengkun Liang,Guannan Liu,Xiaojie Mao,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CURL方法，利用大语言模型和估计不确定性，通过分离的分配效应和异质性效应两条路径构建表征，提升条件平均处理效应(CATE)估计的稳定性与准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始数值和类别编码无法充分表达语义关系和高阶交互，导致有限样本下CATE估计存在局部不稳定性；进一步发现这种不稳定性通过部分可分离的分配侧和异质性侧渠道表现出来。

Method: 提出CURL，一种即插即用的适配器。查询冻结的LLM并通过两个角色条件提示，从观测协变量构建分配导向和异质性导向的表征，经分离通路路由，利用估计不确定性将预训练语义容量分配给局部不稳定单元。

Result: 在四个基准上，CURL使十种基础学习器在大多数设置下表现提升；消融、细化动态、路径重分配和探针分析支持两通道的设计意图和各自作用。

Conclusion: 利用LLM语义知识和不确定性引导的表征分离策略，能有效缓解CATE估计中的局部不稳定问题，提升异构处理效应估计性能。

Original Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects is central to targeted interventions, such as personalized promotions and precision medicine. We focus on the conditional average treatment effect (CATE), a standard estimand for characterizing such heterogeneity. Even under standard identification conditions, finite-sample CATE estimation requires learning the nuisance structure for covariate adjustment and treatment-effect heterogeneity, often together with an effective representation of X. Raw numerical and categorical encodings can leave semantic relations and higher-order interactions implicit, making this joint task locally unstable. A motivating study further shows that this instability appears through partially separable assignment- and heterogeneity-side channels. Building on this observation, we propose CURL (Causal Uncertainty-guided Representation Learning), a plug-in adapter that uses estimator uncertainty to allocate pretrained semantic capacity to locally unstable units. CURL queries a frozen LLM through two role-conditioned prompts, constructs assignment- and heterogeneity-oriented representations from the observed covariates, and routes them through separated pathways. On four benchmarks, CURL improves ten host learners in most settings, while ablation, refinement-dynamics, route-reassignment, and probe analyses support the intended design and roles of the two channels.

中文摘要翻译: 估计异质性处理效应对于定向干预（如个性化推荐和精准医疗）至关重要。本文关注条件平均处理效应（CATE），这是刻画此类异质性的标准估计量。即使在标准识别条件下，有限样本的CATE估计也需要学习用于协变量调整和处理效应异质性的 nuisance 结构，同时往往还需要结合X的有效表征。原始数值和类别编码会隐式地保留语义关系和更高阶的交互，使得这一联合任务在局部上不稳定。一项动机研究进一步表明，这种不稳定性通过部分可分离的分配侧和异质性侧渠道表现出来。基于这一发现，我们提出CURL（因果不确定性引导的表征学习），一种即插即用的适配器，利用估计器的不确定性将预训练语义容量分配给局部不稳定的单元。CURL通过两个角色条件提示查询冻结的大语言模型，从观测协变量构建分配导向和异质性导向的表征，并通过分离的通路进行路由。在四个基准测试上，CURL在大多数设置下提升了十种基础学习器的表现，同时消融、细化动态、路径重分配和探针分析支持了两通道的预期设计及其各自的作用。

</details>


### [126] [FedWeave: Rethinking the Unit of Specialization in Heterogeneous Federated MoE-LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26618)
*Donghang Duan,Xu Zheng,Lizong Zhang,Chong Mu,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedWeave提出非对称聚合策略，分别优化专家（保持纯度）和路由器（保持对比度），通过无监督原型发现实现原型级专家聚合，解决联邦PEFT中任务异构导致的跨任务干扰问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦MoE-LoRA方法通常以客户端粒度进行专精化，隐式假设客户端内任务一致，但实际中客户端存在任务异构，导致跨任务干扰和梯度冲突。核心洞察是专家需要纯度（模式一致的更新）而路由器需要对比度（混合任务观察），现有方法无法同时满足这两个需求。

Method: FedWeave采用非对称聚合框架：(1) 将专家聚合与路由器优化分离；(2) 通过无监督原型发现形成局部桶，并在客户端间对齐，实现原型级专家聚合；(3) 保留混合任务的客户端轨迹用于路由器训练；(4) 推理时采用稀疏推理（单专家激活），保持近乎完整的软路由性能。

Result: 在异构多任务基准测试中，使用主流大语言模型骨干，FedWeave持续优于强基线方法；消融实验验证了各设计的有效性；理论分析解释了非对称聚合的优势：控制专家收敛驻点、识别路由器轨迹碎片化导致的共识误差、界定了稀疏推理风险。

Conclusion: FedWeave通过分离专家聚合与路由器优化的非对称聚合策略，有效解决了联邦PEFT中任务异构带来的跨任务干扰问题，同时保持了良好的推理效率。

Original Abstract: Federated PEFT enables LLMs to collaboratively adapt to decentralized private data without sharing raw examples. However, task heterogeneity across clients can cause cross-task interference and gradient conflicts during aggregation. Federated MoE-LoRA addresses this challenge through specialized LoRA experts and conditional routing. Yet existing methods typically specialize at client granularity, implicitly assuming task-coherent clients. Our core insight is that experts need purity, namely pattern-coherent updates that preserve specialization, whereas routers need contrast, namely mixed-task observations that support expert comparison. We propose FedWeave, a framework that adopts asymmetric aggregation, separating expert aggregation from router optimization to meet these two requirements. FedWeave uses unsupervised prototype discovery to form local buckets and align them across clients, enabling prototype-level expert aggregation while retaining mixed-task client trajectories for router training. At inference, FedWeave performs sparse inference with one active expert while preserving nearly all soft-routing performance. Our theoretical analysis explains why asymmetric aggregation is advantageous: it controls expert convergence in stationarity through off-pattern contamination, identifies the consensus error induced by fragmented router trajectories, and bounds sparse-inference risk. On a heterogeneous multi-task benchmark with mainstream LLM backbones, FedWeave consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablations verify the effectiveness of our design.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦参数高效微调使大语言模型能够在共享原始样本的情况下，协作地适配分散的私有数据。然而，客户端之间的任务异质性可能导致聚合过程中的跨任务干扰和梯度冲突。联邦MoE-LoRA通过专门的LoRA专家和条件路由来应对这一挑战。然而，现有方法通常以客户端粒度进行专精化，隐式假设客户端内部任务一致。我们的核心洞察是：专家需要纯度，即保持专精化的模式一致更新；而路由器需要对比度，即支持专家比较的混合任务观察。我们提出FedWeave框架，该框架采用非对称聚合策略，将专家聚合与路由器优化分离，以满足以上两个需求。FedWeave利用无监督原型发现形成局部桶，并在客户端间对齐，实现原型级专家聚合，同时保留混合任务的客户端轨迹用于路由器训练。在推理时，FedWeave执行稀疏推理，仅激活一个专家，同时保留几乎全部的软路由性能。我们的理论分析解释了非对称聚合的优势：它通过离模污染控制专家在驻点处的收敛，识别由碎片化路由器轨迹引起的共识误差，并界定了稀疏推理风险。在采用主流大语言模型骨干的异构多任务基准测试中，FedWeave持续优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了我们设计的有效性。

</details>


### [127] [Enhancing Automated Machine Learning via Homogeneous Train-Test Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26625)
*Yearn Tan Yin Tze,Charles Grellois*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究训练-测试集划分中的统计相似性对AutoML模型评估的影响，提出将相似性作为显式优化目标的Optimised-Distribution方法，在MMD相似性上达到89.0%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准随机划分假设训练集和测试集服从相同分布，但这一假设在类别不平衡、自然聚类或空间自相关的数据集中常被违反，从而影响模型评估的准确性。

Method: 比较五种划分策略（随机划分、分层采样、Kennard-Stone、Duplex、SPXY），使用卡方检验、Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验和最大均值差异（MMD）评估统计相似性，并提出Optimised-Distribution方法将相似性作为显式优化目标。

Result: 基于几何的方法（Kennard-Stone、Duplex、SPXY）产生接近零的MMD分数，导致下游性能估计不稳定；提出的Optimised-Distribution方法在所有策略中取得了最高的平均MMD相似性89.0%。

Conclusion: 将统计相似性作为显式优化目标进行训练-测试集划分，能够显著提升数据划分的分布一致性，从而改善AutoML模型评估的可靠性。

Original Abstract: Accurate model evaluation in machine learning depends critically on how datasets are split into training and testing subsets. Standard random splitting assumes that both partitions share the same underlying distribution, an assumption often violated in datasets with class imbalance, natural clustering, or spatial autocorrelation. This paper investigates the role of statistical similarity in train-test splitting and its consequences for AutoML model evaluation. Five established strategies are compared across fifteen UCI benchmark datasets: random splitting, stratified sampling, Kennard-Stone, Duplex, and SPXY. Similarity is assessed using chi-square, Kolmogorov-Smirnov, and Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) tests. Geometry-based methods consistently produce near-zero MMD scores, introducing instability into downstream performance estimates. The proposed Optimised-Distribution method treats similarity as an explicit optimisation objective and achieves the highest mean MMD similarity, 89.0%, across all strategies evaluated.

中文摘要翻译: 机器学习中准确的模型评估关键取决于数据集如何划分为训练集和测试集。标准的随机划分假设两个分区共享相同的底层分布，这一假设在具有类别不平衡、自然聚类或空间自相关性的数据集中经常被违反。本文研究了训练-测试划分中统计相似性的作用及其对AutoML模型评估的影响。在15个UCI基准数据集上比较了五种已有策略：随机划分、分层采样、Kennard-Stone、Duplex和SPXY。使用卡方检验、Kolmogorov-Smirnov检验和最大均值差异（MMD）测试评估相似性。基于几何的方法持续产生接近零的MMD分数，给下游性能估计引入不稳定性。提出的优化分布（Optimised-Distribution）方法将相似性作为显式优化目标，在所有评估策略中取得了最高的平均MMD相似性，达到89.0%。

</details>


### [128] [Understanding Context Sampling in TabPFN on Small Tabular Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26628)
*Mohammed Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了TabPFN在小表格数据上的上下文选择（大小与组成）对预测稳定性、准确率和选择成本的影响，发现大上下文更准确稳定，随机采样因能覆盖特征空间而优于昂贵的K-Means等方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: TabPFN通过上下文学习进行分类，但在小表格数据上，实践者仍需选择上下文大小和组成行，这些选择如何影响预测稳定性、准确率和选择成本尚不清楚。

Method: 在15个OpenML数据集上重复采样上下文，比较不同上下文大小下的预测变异性和准确率；通过控制实验分离分布代表性、特征均值匹配和多样性/覆盖度的影响；对比均匀随机采样与K-Means、最远点采样等昂贵选择方法的成本与效果。

Result: 大上下文更准确稳定，AUC变异系数从k=16时的6-18%降至大上下文时的1-4%；匹配特征均值会降低上下文多样性，导致AUC下降最多0.5；多样性和覆盖度是准确率的更强预测因子（diversity beta=+0.23, p=3×10⁻¹²）；K-Means和最远点采样在准确率上与随机采样相当，但选择成本高出2-3个数量级。

Conclusion: 随机采样之所以有效，是因为它在期望上覆盖了特征空间，而非因为它再现了底层数据分布；昂贵的上下文选择方法不具成本效益。

Original Abstract: TabPFN performs classification through in-context learning: it conditions on a set of labeled training rows (the context, or prototypes) and predicts test labels without gradient updates. On small tabular datasets, practitioners must still choose the context size and which rows constitute the context. We study how these choices affect prediction stability, accuracy, and selection cost using repeated context sampling on 15 OpenML datasets. Specifically, we investigate (i) whether larger contexts reduce prediction variability across random draws, (ii) whether accuracy depends on preserving the training distribution or on feature-space coverage, and (iii) whether expensive selection methods such as K-Means and farthest-point sampling provide benefits over uniform random sampling.
  We find that larger contexts are both more accurate and substantially more stable, with AUC coefficient of variation decreasing from roughly 6 to 18% at k=16 to 1 to 4% at larger context sizes on datasets with room for improvement. Although accuracy correlates with distribution representativeness in random contexts, controlled experiments show that matching feature means alone can reduce accuracy by up to 0.5 AUC because it reduces context diversity. Mixed-effects analysis identifies diversity and coverage, rather than feature-mean matching, as the stronger predictor of accuracy (diversity beta=+0.23, p=3x10^-12; feature-mean shift beta=-0.01, p=0.71). K-Means and farthest-point sampling achieve similar accuracy to random selection while requiring two to three orders of magnitude more selection cost. These results show that random sampling succeeds because it provides feature-space coverage in expectation, not because it reproduces the underlying data distribution.

中文摘要翻译: TabPFN通过上下文学习进行分类：它基于一组标注的训练行（上下文，或原型）进行条件预测，无需梯度更新即可预测测试标签。在处理小型表格数据集时，实践者仍需选择上下文大小以及哪些行构成上下文。我们通过在15个OpenML数据集上重复上下文采样，研究了这些选择如何影响预测稳定性、准确率和选择成本。具体而言，我们探究了(i)更大的上下文是否能减少随机抽取之间的预测变异性，(ii)准确率是否依赖于保持训练分布还是依赖于特征空间覆盖，以及(iii)K-Means和最远点采样等昂贵的选择方法是否优于均匀随机采样。我们发现，更大的上下文既更准确又显著更稳定，在具有改进空间的数据集上，AUC变异系数从k=16时的约6-18%降至更大上下文大小时的1-4%。虽然随机上下文中的准确率与分布代表性相关，但控制实验表明，仅匹配特征均值即可使AUC降低多达0.5，因为它降低了上下文多样性。混合效应分析识别出多样性和覆盖度，而非特征均值匹配，是准确率的更强预测因子（多样性beta=+0.23，p=3×10⁻¹²；特征均值偏移beta=-0.01，p=0.71）。K-Means和最远点采样在准确率上与随机选择相当，但选择成本高出2到3个数量级。这些结果表明，随机采样之所以成功，是因为它在期望上提供了特征空间覆盖，而非因为它再现了底层数据分布。

</details>


### [129] [RAG-HAR+: Towards Cost-Efficient LLM-Based Human Activity Recognition for Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26631)
*Hansi Karunarathna,Nirhoshan Sivaroopan,Chamara Madarasingha,Anura Jayasumana,Kanchana Thilakarathna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAG-HAR+ 是一种无需训练、检索优先的人体活动识别方法，通过离线设计特征组和基于检索的多数投票（仅对不确定案例调用LLM），在六个基准上保持竞争力，同时大幅降低LLM使用量、Token消耗和推理时间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习HAR方法需要数据集特定训练、大量标注数据和反复适应新传感器设置或活动分类体系，缺乏灵活性和可迁移性。

Method: 提出RAG-HAR+框架：离线阶段使用检索设计器代理（Retrieval Designer Agent）从多样化运动描述子池中设计数据集特定特征组；推理阶段对检索证据充分的样本采用多数投票，仅将不确定案例交由基于LLM的模糊解析器代理（Ambiguity Resolver Agent）处理。

Result: 在六个HAR基准数据集上，RAG-HAR+在保持竞争性甚至更优性能的同时，显著降低了LLM调用次数、Token消耗和推理时间。

Conclusion: 检索优先、LLM辅助的范式在移动传感场景中具有实际可行性，为无需训练、可迁移的人体活动识别提供了有效路径。

Original Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) from wearable sensors supports applications in healthcare, rehabilitation, fitness tracking, and smart environments. Yet, existing deep learning approaches require dataset-specific training, large labeled corpora, and repeated adaptation to new sensor settings or activity taxonomies. Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Human Activity Recognition (RAG-HAR) addresses this by framing HAR as a training-free, retrieval-augmented task, in which statistical descriptions of sensor windows are used to retrieve similar labeled examples that guide LLM-based classification. We introduce RAG-HAR+, a retrieval-first and cost-optimized extension that strengthens retrieval while reducing dependence on LLM-based inference. RAG-HAR+ uses an offline Retrieval Designer Agent to design dataset-specific feature groups from a diverse pool of motion descriptors, enabling sensor windows to be compared using features better aligned with dataset-specific activity patterns. During inference, RAG-HAR+ uses majority voting over retrieved neighbors for samples with strong retrieval evidence and defers only uncertain cases to an LLM-based Ambiguity Resolver Agent. Across six HAR benchmarks, RAG-HAR+ maintains competitive or improved performance while reducing LLM usage, token consumption, and inference time. We further extend the RAG-HAR mobile prototype to demonstrate the practical feasibility of retrieval-first, LLM-assisted HAR in mobile sensing scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 可穿戴传感器上的人体活动识别（HAR）支持医疗健康、康复、健身追踪和智能环境等应用。然而，现有深度学习方法需要针对特定数据集进行训练、大量标注语料库，并需反复适应新的传感器设置或活动分类体系。检索增强生成用于人体活动识别（RAG-HAR）通过将HAR构建为一个无需训练、检索增强的任务来解决这一问题，其中利用传感器窗口的统计描述来检索相似的有标注样本，以引导基于大语言模型（LLM）的分类。我们提出RAG-HAR+，一种检索优先且成本优化的扩展方法，它在增强检索能力的同时减少了对LLM推理的依赖。RAG-HAR+使用离线的检索设计器代理（Retrieval Designer Agent），从多样化的运动描述子池中设计数据集特定的特征组，使得传感器窗口能够使用与数据集特定活动模式更对齐的特征进行比较。在推理阶段，RAG-HAR+对检索证据充分的样本采用近邻多数投票，仅将不确定案例交由基于LLM的模糊解析器代理（Ambiguity Resolver Agent）处理。在六个HAR基准数据集上，RAG-HAR+在保持竞争性或更优性能的同时，减少了LLM使用量、Token消耗和推理时间。我们进一步扩展了RAG-HAR移动原型，以展示检索优先、LLM辅助的HAR在移动传感场景中的实际可行性。

</details>


### [130] [AIGen: Automating AI Bill of Materials Generation Through Hybrid MLOps Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26652)
*Federica Pepe,Daniele Bifolco,Costantino Martignetti,Aureliano D'Amici,Fabiano Izzo,Damian A. Tamburri,Massimiliano Di Penta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AIGen是一个模块化的AI物料清单生成器，基于MLflow框架并结合挖掘启发式与LLM，自动生成符合SPDX 3.0标准的可互操作AI组件清单，支持多种AI框架，旨在促进AI供应链治理与法规合规。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管SPDX 3.0标准已支持AI和数据集描述，但缺乏能够自动化、可扩展地生成符合标准的AI物料清单（AIBoM）的实际工具，阻碍了AI系统的透明治理与法规合规。

Method: AIGen基于MLflow MLOps框架，结合挖掘启发式算法与大语言模型（LLM）自动生成AIBoM；通过插件接口支持领域特定收集器，无需修改核心代码即可扩展至Hugging Face、PyTorch、TensorFlow等多种AI框架。

Result: AIGen能够生成机器可读、可互操作的SPDX 3.0兼容AIBoM，支持多框架扩展，为欧盟AI法案、NIST AI风险管理框架和ISO/IEC 42001等法规合规提供了可复用的基础工具。

Conclusion: AIGen为AI供应链治理提供了一种具体、可复用的自动化解决方案，填补了SPDX 3.0标准在实际工具支持上的空白，有助于实现透明、负责任的AI系统文档化与合规管理。

Original Abstract: The responsible development and deployment of artificial intelligence (AI) systems requires rigorous documentation of their constituent artifacts, e.g., datasets, model weights, training pipelines, and runtime dependencies. Although the Software Package Data Exchange (SPDX) 3.0 standard introduced native support for AI and dataset profiles, practical tooling capable of generating standards-compliant AI Bills of Materials (AIBoMs) in an automated and extensible manner remains scarce. This paper presents AIGen, a modular AIBoM generator that produces machine-readable, interoperable inventories of AI system components that comply with the SPDX 3.0 AI profile. AIGen works on top of the MLflow MLOps framework and combines mining heuristics with Large Language Models to generate AIBoMs. A plugin interface allows practitioners to extend the tool with domain-specific collectors without modifying the core codebase, supporting heterogeneous AI frameworks such as Hugging Face, PyTorch, and TensorFlow. AIGen is designed to facilitate compliance with the European Union AI Act, the NIST AI Risk Management Framework, and ISO/IEC 42001, providing a concrete, reusable foundation for transparent, accountable AI supply chain governance. Tool URL: https://github.com/danielebifolco/AIGen Tool Video: https://youtu.be/\_nAbXDWfVL4

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能（AI）系统的负责任开发与部署要求对其组成构件（如数据集、模型权重、训练流水线和运行时依赖）进行严格的文档化。尽管软件包数据交换（SPDX）3.0标准已原生支持AI与数据集描述文件，但能够以自动化、可扩展方式生成符合标准的AI物料清单（AIBoM）的实际工具仍然稀缺。本文提出AIGen，一个模块化的AIBoM生成器，可生成符合SPDX 3.0 AI描述文件的、机器可读且可互操作的AI系统组件清单。AIGen基于MLflow MLOps框架构建，融合了挖掘启发式算法与大语言模型（LLM）来生成AIBoM。其插件接口允许从业者在无需修改核心代码的情况下扩展领域特定的收集器，支持Hugging Face、PyTorch和TensorFlow等多种异构AI框架。AIGen旨在促进对欧盟AI法案、NIST AI风险管理框架以及ISO/IEC 42001的合规性，为透明、可问责的AI供应链治理提供具体且可复用的基础。工具URL：https://github.com/danielebifolco/AIGen 工具视频：https://youtu.be/_nAbXDWfVL4

</details>


### [131] [Efficient Heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization for Risk-Aware AutoRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26680)
*Mingxuan Che,Tsung-Yuan Tseng,Theresa Eimer,Marius Lindauer,Alexander von Rohr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ERAHBO，一种风险厌恶的异方差贝叶斯优化方法，用于高效搜索强化学习超参数配置，在保证高平均回报的同时降低训练方差，并通过自适应重采样提升样本效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习结果具有高度随机性，且期望性能与方差高度依赖超参数配置，现有方法在优化超参数时未充分兼顾均值与方差，且样本效率低下。

Method: 提出风险厌恶的异方差贝叶斯优化方法ERAHBO，同时对学习结果的均值和方差建模作为超参数配置的函数；采用自适应重采样策略替代固定预算，提升超参数优化的样本效率。

Result: 在多种RL算法和环境中，ERAHBO在风险厌恶回报上优于风险中性和风险厌恶基线方法，展现出更优的样本效率。

Conclusion: ERAHBO通过异方差建模和自适应重采样，能够高效识别高平均回报且低方差的超参数配置，是一种有效的风险厌恶型超参数优化方法。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has shown remarkable success across a wide range of complex tasks. However, RL outcomes can be highly stochastic, and both expected performance and variability often depend on hyperparameter (HP) configurations. We propose efficient and risk-averse heteroscedastic Bayesian Optimization (ERAHBO), a Bayesian optimization method that models both the mean and variance of learning outcomes as functions of the HP configurations. ERAHBO aims to identify HP configurations that achieve high average return while reducing variability across training runs, and it improves the sample efficiency of the HP optimization via adaptive re-sampling rather than a fixed budget per HP. Empirical evaluations across diverse RL algorithms and environments demonstrate that ERAHBO generally outperforms both risk-neutral and risk-averse baselines, delivering improved sample efficiency for risk-averse returns.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习在广泛的复杂任务中展现出显著的成功。然而，RL结果可能具有高度随机性，且期望性能与变异性通常依赖于超参数配置。我们提出了高效且风险厌恶的异方差贝叶斯优化方法（ERAHBO），这是一种同时对学习结果的均值和方差作为超参数配置的函数进行建模的贝叶斯优化方法。ERAHBO旨在识别能够实现高平均回报同时降低训练间变异性的超参数配置，并通过自适应重采样（而非每个超参数的固定预算）来提高超参数优化的样本效率。跨多种RL算法和环境的实验评估表明，ERAHBO在风险厌恶回报上普遍优于风险中性和风险厌恶基线方法，展现出更优的样本效率。

</details>


### [132] [Universality and Approximation Rates of Graph Neural Networks with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26699)
*Lukas Gonon,Thilo Meyer-Brandis,Niklas Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了带有随机节点特征的置换等变神经网络（PENNs）可以在概率意义下以任意精度逼近任意有向图上的置换不变或置换等变函数，并给出了可微函数情形下的逼近率上界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随机节点特征已被理论和实践证实能增强图神经网络的表达能力，但此前缺乏关于其通用逼近能力的严格理论保证，尤其是针对置换等变神经网络这一涵盖多种主流GNN架构的统一框架。

Method: 作者从理论上分析了将部分随机节点特征与PENNs相结合时的函数逼近能力，针对固定大小的有向图，建立了针对置换不变函数和置换等变函数的概率意义下任意逼近的通用性结论，并对k次连续可微函数推导了逼近率上界。

Result: 1) PENNs结合部分随机节点特征可在概率意义下任意逼近任意可测的置换不变/置换等变函数；2) 对于k≥2的k次连续可微函数，给出了逼近率上界，揭示了前馈组件复杂度（层深度和非零权重的数量）与逼近精度之间的关系。

Conclusion: 部分随机节点特征赋予PENNs强大的通用逼近能力，逼近率上界为设计高效的图神经网络架构提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: We investigate message-passing graph neural networks with random node features. Random node features are known to enhance the expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) both theoretically and empirically. Here, we establish a novel universality result focusing on permutation-equivariant neural networks (PENNs), a class of GNNs built from feedforward neural network components that subsumes many prominent GNN architectures. We show that PENNs, combined with partially random node features, can approximate arbitrarily well in probability any measurable permutation-invariant or permutation-equivariant function on directed graphs of fixed size with multidimensional node and edge features. For $k$-times continuously differentiable functions, $k\geq 2$, we also derive upper bounds on the approximation rates, relating the complexity of the feedforward components of a PENN in terms of layer depth and number of nonzero weights to the desired approximation accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了带有随机节点特征的传递消息图神经网络。随机节点特征已知在理论和实践上都能增强图神经网络（GNNs）的表达能力。本文针对置换等变神经网络（PENNs）建立了一个新的通用性结果，PENNs是一类由前馈神经网络组件构建的GNNs，涵盖了许多主流GNN架构。我们证明，PENNs结合部分随机节点特征，可以在概率意义下以任意精度逼近任意定义在固定大小有向图上的可测置换不变函数或置换等变函数（图中包含多维节点特征和边特征）。对于k次连续可微函数（k≥2），我们还推导了逼近率的上界，将PENN前馈组件的复杂度（层深度和非零权重的数量）与期望的逼近精度联系起来。

</details>


### [133] [Mixture-of-experts for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26708)
*Florent Imbert,Eric Anquetil,Yann Soullard,Romain Tavenard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合专家模型（MOE）的数字笔手写轨迹重建方法，分别用触摸专家和悬停专家处理笔触与悬停阶段的轨迹，并构建了新的公开基准数据集，显著提升了重建精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在手写轨迹重建中，对笔触与悬停阶段的处理不够精细，尤其是悬停轨迹的分析不足，导致后续笔触定位不准确。因此需要一种能够分别优化触摸和悬停阶段轨迹重建的方法。

Method: 采用混合专家模型（MOE）框架，包含两个专家模型：触摸专家模型（touching expert model）负责重建笔触轨迹，悬停专家模型（hovering expert model）负责分析悬停阶段轨迹以正确定位下一笔触位置。每个专家基于额外的上下文或特定样本进行独立优化训练。

Result: 在引入的新型公开基准数据集上，该方法相较于主要竞争对手取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的MOE双专家架构能有效提升在线手写轨迹重建的质量，尤其改善了悬停阶段的分析与笔触定位，且公开数据集将促进该领域的后续研究。

Original Abstract: The use of digital pens for online handwriting trajectory reconstruction is a prevalent method for human-computer interaction. In this study, we focus on a digital pen equipped with sensors where we aim at reconstructing the online handwriting trajectory. This pen enables writing on any surface and preserving the digital trace of handwriting. This type of pen could be used as an aid to learning to write in classroom. In this paper, we propose a new approach learning to finely reconstruct the touching trajectories while precisely analyzing the hovering part in order to position the next touching trace correctly. This relies on a Mixture-Of-Experts (MOE) approach. The first expert is dedicated for the pencil touch, and is named touching expert model. The second one is dedicated for the hovering pen trajectory, and is named hovering expert model. We improve on the learning of each of these experts based on additional context or specific examples. In addition we introduce a novel public benchmark dataset, to enable future research and comparisons in the field of handwriting reconstruction. The results demonstrates a significant enhancement compared to its primary competitors.

中文摘要翻译: 数字笔用于在线手写轨迹重建是人机交互中一种普遍的方法。在本研究中，我们聚焦于一支配备传感器的数字笔，旨在重建在线手写轨迹。这支笔可以在任何表面上书写并保留手写的数字痕迹。此类笔可作为课堂学习书写的辅助工具。在本文中，我们提出了一种新方法，能够精细地重建触摸轨迹，同时精确分析悬停部分，以便正确定位下一触摸轨迹。该方法基于混合专家模型（MOE）。第一个专家专门处理笔触，称为触摸专家模型；第二个专家专门处理悬停笔迹，称为悬停专家模型。我们基于额外的上下文或特定样本改进了每个专家的学习过程。此外，我们引入了一个新颖的公开基准数据集，以促进手写轨迹重建领域的未来研究和比较。实验结果表明，该方法相较于其主要竞争对手有显著提升。

</details>


### [134] [PowerAtlas: Towards Electricity-Computing Co-Scheduling for Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26710)
*Kaiwen Jiang,Siya Xu,Ziyue Zhu,Chao Yang,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerAtlas是一个面向数据中心电力-计算协同调度的LLM智能体框架，通过集成历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，在满足电网运行规则和计算任务SLA的前提下生成联合调度决策，实验表明其在真实物理条件下具有良好的可行性和成本优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI工作负载的快速增长使数据中心成为大规模、波动性强且具有时空灵活性的电网负荷，急需电力-计算协同调度方案。然而，通用大语言模型生成的调度方案在严格的电网约束下往往不可行，会导致线路潮流越限和负荷损失。

Method: 提出PowerAtlas框架，这是一个LLM智能体框架，通过集成历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，生成同时满足电网运行规则和计算任务服务等级协议（SLA）的联合调度决策。

Result: 与某省级电力公司合作构建实验性电力-计算网络并在真实数据中心数据上验证决策闭环；从脱敏运营数据中构建了包含2000个调度实例的基准测试ECBench（含最优解标注）；在11个LLM上的实验表明，PowerAtlas在真实物理运行条件下有效，且在三个开放权重骨干模型上展现出一致的可实现性和成本收益。

Conclusion: PowerAtlas框架能够有效解决数据中心电力-计算协同调度问题，在满足电网物理约束的前提下实现可行且经济的调度决策，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is turning data centers into large-scale, volatile, yet spatiotemporally flexible grid loads, creating an urgent need for coordinated electricity-computing scheduling. Under stringent grid constraints, schedules from general-purpose large language models (LLMs) are often infeasible, causing line-flow violations and unserved load. We present PowerAtlas, an LLM-agent framework for electricity-computing co-scheduling that integrates historical instances, domain knowledge, and physical constraints to produce joint decisions satisfying both grid operational rules and the service-level agreements (SLAs) of computing tasks. Working with a provincial power utility in China, we built an experimental electricity-computing network and validated the decision loop on real data-center data; from de-identified operational data we further constructed ECBench, a benchmark of 2,000 scheduling instances with oracle-optimal solutions. Experiments across eleven LLMs demonstrate the effectiveness of PowerAtlas under realistic physical operating conditions, with consistent feasibility and cost gains across three open-weight backbones. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/JAVA-Jiang/PowerAtlas.

中文摘要翻译: AI工作负载的快速增长正将数据中心转变为大规模、波动性强且具有时空灵活性的电网负荷，从而催生了对电力-计算协同调度的迫切需求。在严格的电网约束下，通用大语言模型（LLM）生成的调度方案往往不可行，会导致线路潮流越限和负荷损失。我们提出了PowerAtlas，一个用于电力-计算协同调度的LLM智能体框架，该框架集成了历史实例、领域知识和物理约束，以生成同时满足电网运行规则和计算任务服务等级协议（SLA）的联合决策。我们与某省级电力公司合作，构建了一个实验性电力-计算网络，并在真实数据中心数据上验证了决策闭环；从脱敏运营数据中，我们进一步构建了ECBench基准测试，包含2000个调度实例及其最优解标注。在11个LLM上的实验表明，PowerAtlas在真实物理运行条件下具有有效性和鲁棒性，在三个开放权重骨干模型上展现出一致的可实现性和成本收益。我们的代码已在 https://github.com/JAVA-Jiang/PowerAtlas 开源。

</details>


### [135] [Domain adaptation for handwriting trajectory reconstruction from IMU sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26736)
*Florent Imbert,Romain Tavenard,Yann Soullard,Eric Anquetil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种领域自适应方法，用于解决儿童与成人因书写速度和自信度差异导致的传感器信号差异问题，从而构建统一的中间特征表示以重建数字笔的书写轨迹。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 儿童与成人在书写手势的速度和自信度上存在差异，导致即使手写轨迹相似，传感器捕获的信号也有较大差异，这影响了基于运动传感器的数字笔在儿童书写学习中的轨迹重建效果。

Method: 采用领域自适应方法构建统一的中间特征表示，以弥合成人（源域）与儿童（目标域）之间的传感器信号差异，并与从头训练模型和微调模型两种方法进行对比。

Result: 领域自适应方法在利用已有知识适应不同应用场景方面优于从头训练和微调两种方法，能够更有效地实现轨迹重建。

Conclusion: 领域自适应方法能够有效利用现有知识，解决跨年龄段（成人到儿童）传感器信号差异问题，为数字笔在教育场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Digital pens are commonly used to write on digital devices, providing the handwriting trace and enhancing human-computer interation. This study focuses on a digital pen equipped with kinematic sensors, allowing users to write on any surface while simultaneously preserving a digital trajectory of handwriting. This technology holds significant potential as a valuable educational tool, particularly in classrooms where it can facilitate the process of learning to write. A major issue is based on the difference in captured signals between adults and children. For similar handwriting trace, we have large differences in sensor signals due to differences in speed and confidence in the handwriting gesture of children. To address this, we investigate a domain adaptation approach to build a unified intermediate feature representation aimed at facilitating the trajectory reconstruction. We demonstrate the interest of domain adaptation methods in leveraging existing knowledge for application in different contexts. Specifically, we compare our domain adaptation approach with two other methods: training the model from scratch and fine-tuning the model.

中文摘要翻译: 数字笔通常用于在数字设备上书写，能够提供书写轨迹并增强人机交互。本研究聚焦于一种配备运动传感器的数字笔，用户可以在任何表面上书写，同时保留手写的数字轨迹。该技术作为一种有价值的教育工具具有巨大潜力，尤其是在课堂环境中，它可以促进书写学习过程。一个主要问题在于成人和儿童之间捕获信号的差异。对于相似的手写轨迹，由于儿童在手写手势的速度和自信度上的差异，传感器信号存在较大差异。为解决这一问题，我们研究了一种领域自适应方法，构建统一的中间特征表示，以促进轨迹重建。我们证明了领域自适应方法在利用已有知识适应不同应用场景方面的优势。具体地，我们将领域自适应方法与其他两种方法进行了对比：从头训练模型和微调模型。

</details>


### [136] [CalTwin: Towards Calibrated, Shift-Robust Medical World Models via Fisher-Information Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26752)
*Behraj Khan,Shabir Ahmad,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari,Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CalTwin，一个结合Fisher信息协变量偏移惩罚与置信度错配惩罚的统一正则化目标，用于提升医疗世界模型在碎片化训练数据和部署时分布偏移下的可靠性。在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症数据集上，CalTwin将OOD下一步潜状态MSE降低9.1%，ECE轻微改善。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医疗世界模型在临床部署中面临两个关键失败模式：(i) 协变量偏移——训练数据因医院、扫描仪和时间不同而碎片化，导致潜动态预测器所见特征分布不一致；(ii) 置信度错配——多步预测在临床风险最高处往往过度自信。现有方法分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: 提出CalTwin，一个轻量级正则化目标，包含两个惩罚项：一是基于Fisher信息的协变量偏移惩罚（改编自先前对碎片化协变量偏移修复的工作），二是置信度错配惩罚（改编自先前对标定视觉-语言分类的工作）。两者共同应用于基于GRU的医疗世界模型的潜状态转移预测器。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症挑战数据集上，将两个医院系统作为顺序训练片段，未见系统作为OOD测试。CalTwin将OOD下一步潜状态MSE相对无惩罚基线降低9.1%（其中FIM惩罚单独贡献7.0%）；置信度错配惩罚带来的ECE降低真实但幅度较小（CalTwin降低0.7%，单独CMP降低1.3%）。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移和置信度错配可以通过一个统一的正则化目标进行有效处理；Fisher信息惩罚是性能提升的主要来源，而置信度错配惩罚提供额外的标定改善；该框架可推广到其他医疗世界模型架构和数据集。

Original Abstract: Medical world models aim to learn a latent state of patient or organ physiology and a transition function that forecasts how that state evolves under interventions, supporting downstream tasks from imaging-based diagnosis to digital-twin treatment planning. Two failure modes threaten the reliability of such models in clinical deployment: (i)~\emph{covariate shift}, because training data are fragmented across hospitals, scanners, and time, so the feature distribution seen by the latent-dynamics predictor differs across fragments and from the distribution at deployment; and (ii)~\emph{confidence misalignment}, because multi-step forecasts are often overconfident exactly where clinical risk is highest. We argue that both problems admit a unified treatment via a single lightweight regularisation objective, \textbf{CalTwin}, which combines a Fisher-Information-based shift penalty adapted from our prior work on fragmented covariate-shift remediation~\cite{khan2025mitigating,khan2025causal} with a Confidence Misalignment Penalty adapted from our prior work on calibrated vision-language classification~\cite{khan2025confidence}, applied here to a GRU-based medical world model's latent transition predictor. We derive the combined objective, establish which proof steps transfer from the classification setting without modification and which require adaptation, and evaluate it on the PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge, treating the two hospital systems as sequential training fragments and the unseen system as an out-of-distribution test. CalTwin reduces OOD next-step latent-state MSE by 9.1\% relative to the no-penalty baseline (FIM penalty alone accounts for 7.0\%); the ECE reduction from the Confidence Misalignment Penalty is real but small (0.7\% for CalTwin, 1.3\% for CMP alone).

中文摘要翻译: 医疗世界模型旨在学习患者或器官生理学的潜状态，以及预测该状态在干预下如何演化的转移函数，从而支持从基于影像的诊断到数字孪生治疗规划的下游任务。两种失败模式威胁着此类模型在临床部署中的可靠性：(i) 协变量偏移——由于训练数据分散在不同医院、扫描仪和时间段，潜动态预测器所见的特征分布在各片段间以及部署时各不相同；(ii) 置信度错配——多步预测在临床风险最高处往往过度自信。我们认为这两个问题可以通过一个统一的轻量级正则化目标——CalTwin——来处理，该目标结合了从我们先前关于碎片化协变量偏移修复工作中改编的基于Fisher信息的偏移惩罚，以及从我们先前关于标定视觉-语言分类工作中改编的置信度错配惩罚，并将其应用于基于GRU的医疗世界模型的潜状态转移预测器。我们推导了组合目标函数，确定了哪些证明步骤可以直接从分类场景迁移而无需修改、哪些需要调整，并在PhysioNet 2019脓毒症挑战数据集上进行了评估，将两个医院系统作为顺序训练片段，未见系统作为分布外测试。CalTwin将分布外下一步潜状态均方误差相对无惩罚基线降低了9.1%（其中Fisher信息矩阵惩罚单独贡献7.0%）；置信度错配惩罚带来的期望标定误差降低真实但幅度较小（CalTwin降低0.7%，单独CMP降低1.3%）。

</details>


### [137] [Journey Operators for Structured Multi-Axis Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26775)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个多轴数据结构的统一框架，证明轴变换可交换性是路径独立性的充要条件，并由此设计出JoFormer模型，将RoPE类方法解释为块状旋转的自然结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多轴数据（如图像、视频、3D体积）在不同轴向上应保持组合与移动的顺序无关性（即交换不变性），但现有位置编码方法（如RoPE）缺乏对这一对称性的严格理论解释和泛化框架。

Method: 1) 建立多轴变换框架：每个数据项携带内容与每个轴上的小变换；2) 定义路径算子为沿路径的逐轴变换乘积；3) 证明当且仅当轴变换可交换时，跨轴的组合与移动是路径无关的；4) 在环面框架对称性、余循环、双线性和范数保持假设下，证明成对评分规则必为块状旋转形式；5) 基于该理论设计JoFormer模型进行值聚合。

Result: 理论方面：严格证明了轴变换可交换性是路径独立性的充要条件，并推导出RoPE类方法必然以块状旋转形式出现。实验方面：在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上的初步实验表明，这些归纳偏置在实际中具有可观察的效果。

Conclusion: 多轴结构中的交换不变性自然导出块状旋转形式的评分规则（即RoPE类方法），JoFormer模型将这一理论与注意力机制和状态空间模型联系起来，为在多种模态中引入可学习的结构性归纳偏置提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: Many kinds of data have structure along one or more axes: words in a sentence, pixels in an image, nodes in a tree, frames in audio, or cells in a 3D volume. Along one axis, order matters: "the dog bit the man" is different from "the man bit the dog." Across independent axes, however, neither composition nor movement should depend on the order of axes: in an image, composing right then down should give the same result as composing down then right, and moving right then down should describe the same relative position as moving down then right.
  We develop a framework for modeling this kind of multi-axis structure. Each data item carries its content together with a small transformation for each axis. A path connecting two positions defines a journey; the journey operator is the product of per-axis transformations along that path, governing both how data composes along the path and how relative position is described. When the transformations are fixed, our framework recovers Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and its multi-dimensional variants. When they depend on the data, the model gains a content-adaptive positional inductive bias.
  We show exactly when these paths are well-defined: both composition and movement across axes are path-independent precisely when the axis transformations commute. We also prove that, under the stated toral-frame symmetry, cocycle, bilinearity, and norm-preservation assumptions, the resulting pairwise scoring rule must take the form of block-wise rotations, explaining why RoPE-like methods arise naturally.
  Finally, we use this theory to design JoFormer, a model for value aggregation, and relate it to attention and state-space models (SSMs).
  Initial experiments across vision, language, and length generalization suggest that these inductive biases can have observable consequences in
  practice.

中文摘要翻译: 许多类型的数据沿一个或多个轴具有结构：句子中的词语、图像中的像素、树中的节点、音频中的帧或3D体积中的体素。沿单一轴，顺序至关重要："狗咬了人"与"人咬了狗"截然不同。然而，在独立轴之间，组合与移动都不应依赖于轴的顺序：在图像中，先右后下的组合应与先下后右的组合得到相同结果，先右后下的移动与先下后右的移动应描述相同的相对位置。
我们提出了一个用于建模这种多轴结构的框架。每个数据项携带其内容以及每个轴上的一个小变换。连接两个位置的路径定义了一次行程；行程算子是沿该路径的各轴变换的乘积，它同时控制数据沿路径的组合方式以及相对位置的描述方式。当变换固定时，我们的框架可以恢复旋转位置编码（RoPE）及其多维变体。当变换依赖于数据时，模型获得了内容自适应的位置归纳偏置。
我们精确刻画了这些路径何时是良定义的：当且仅当轴变换可交换时，跨轴的组合与移动是路径无关的。我们还证明，在给定的环面框架对称性、余循环、双线性及范数保持假设下，产生的成对评分规则必然采取块状旋转的形式，这解释了为什么RoPE类方法会自然出现。
最后，我们利用该理论设计了JoFormer——一个用于值聚合的模型，并将其与注意力机制和状态空间模型（SSM）联系起来。
在视觉、语言和长度泛化上的初步实验表明，这些归纳偏置在实践中可以产生可观察的影响。

</details>


### [138] [SkillRise: Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Cross-Task Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26784)
*Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Zhengxi Lu,Zishan Xu,Yueqing Sun,Yifu Guo,Yuquan Lu,Zhengzhou Cai,Kangning Zhang,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Ziang Ye,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiwen Liu,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillRise提出了一种统一的强化学习框架，让LLM智能体通过跨任务学习可复用的技能，无需多阶段流水线，在三个基准上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么将任务视为独立片段进行标准强化学习，要么在多阶段流水线中纠缠技能的提取、检索和执行，缺乏高效的跨任务技能学习框架。

Method: SkillRise将相关任务组织为渐进式难度序列，使用单一策略交替执行任务求解和维护一份随任务传递的演化技能文档；通过解耦的信用分配机制分别用当前任务结果监督求解、用折扣后的下游结果监督技能文档的更新。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上，SkillRise的Pass@1比最强基线高出2.3~8.5个百分点；其学习到的技能文档在同一任务的重复尝试中仍有效；测试时序列越长性能越好，表明是跨任务复用技能而非重复采样同一任务；同时大幅降低了多阶段流水线的运行时开销。

Conclusion: SkillRise提供了一种简单高效的训练范式，使LLM智能体能够跨任务提取、精炼和复用可迁移技能，兼具强性能和低运行时开销。

Original Abstract: Large language model agents often encounter related yet distinct tasks that share reusable solution patterns. Yet standard agentic reinforcement learning treats tasks as independent episodes, while existing approaches to skill learning either focus on repeated attempts of one task or use pipelines with multiple stages that entangle extraction, retrieval, and execution. We introduce SkillRise, a unified reinforcement learning framework for learning skills across tasks. SkillRise organizes related instances into progressively challenging sequences and uses a single policy to alternate between task solving and curating an evolving skill document passed directly to the next task. Decoupled credit assignment across tasks supervises solving with the current task outcome and curation with discounted downstream outcomes. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld show that SkillRise achieves the strongest Pass@1 performance among the compared methods, with gains over the strongest baseline ranging from 2.3 to 8.5 percentage points. Although trained across distinct tasks, its learned curation policy remains effective for repeated attempts on the same task. Further analysis reveals scaling at test time across tasks: performance improves with longer sequences of related tasks even when each task is attempted only once. This trend suggests that SkillRise reuses transferable skills across tasks rather than benefiting from repeated sampling of the same task. SkillRise further retains strong performance while substantially reducing the runtime overhead of skill learning pipelines with multiple stages. Together, these results provide a simple and efficient training paradigm for LLM agents to extract, refine, and reuse transferable skills across tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型智能体经常遇到相关但不同的任务，这些任务共享可复用的解决方案模式。然而，标准的智能体强化学习将任务视为独立片段，而现有的技能学习方法要么专注于重复尝试同一任务，要么使用多阶段流水线来纠缠技能的提取、检索和执行。我们提出了SkillRise，一个统一的跨任务技能学习强化学习框架。SkillRise将相关实例组织为渐进式难度序列，并使用单一策略在任务求解与维护一份不断演化的技能文档之间交替，该文档直接传递给下一个任务。跨任务的解耦信用分配通过当前任务结果监督求解过程，并通过折扣后的下游结果监督技能文档的更新。在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上的实验表明，SkillRise在对比方法中取得了最强的Pass@1性能，相比最强基线的提升幅度在2.3至8.5个百分点之间。尽管是在不同任务上训练的，其学到的技能文档更新策略在同一任务的重复尝试中仍然有效。进一步分析揭示了测试时跨任务的扩展能力：即使每个任务只尝试一次，性能也会随着相关任务序列的变长而提升。这一趋势表明SkillRise是在跨任务复用可迁移技能，而非受益于同一任务的重复采样。SkillRise在大幅降低多阶段技能学习流水线运行时开销的同时，保持了强劲的性能。综合而言，这些结果提供了一种简单高效的训练范式，使LLM智能体能够跨任务提取、精炼和复用可迁移技能。

</details>


### [139] [FedTopo: Relation-Level Topology Sharing for Model-Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26801)
*Zhaoyang Ma,Zhihao Wu,Xin Gao,Lipo Wang,Youfang Lin,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedTopo提出了一种关系级别的联邦学习框架，通过传递类别关系拓扑而非绝对空间中的参数、蒸馏结果或原型，解决了异构骨干网络下全局知识对齐困难的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法在客户端模型架构异构时，共享的全局知识（参数、蒸馏预测、类别原型）编码在绝对空间中，需要跨客户端对齐表示空间，而异构骨干网络破坏了这种对齐，导致共享知识不可靠，误导本地训练。

Method: FedTopo将全局知识编码为类别关系拓扑，每个客户端从本地原型构建关系拓扑并上传，服务器以可靠性感知方式聚合拓扑（降低弱支持关系的权重），广播全局拓扑给客户端，指导本地训练时强调拓扑相似的负类别。

Result: 在三种数据集和八种异构骨干网络上，FedTopo在性能上一致优于参数共享、蒸馏共享和原型共享的基线方法，且通信开销低、无推理开销。

Conclusion: 关系级别的知识传递（类别关系拓扑）比绝对空间中的知识传递更能有效应对异构联邦学习中的表示空间不对齐问题。

Original Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative learning over decentralized data silos without centralizing raw data. However, heterogeneous local architectures often induce non-aligned representation spaces, making it difficult to transfer global knowledge across silos. Existing paradigms share this knowledge as model parameters, distilled predictions, or class prototypes, yet all encode it in an absolute space that must be aligned across clients. Heterogeneous backbones break this alignment, so the shared knowledge becomes unreliable and misleads local training. We propose FedTopo, a relation-level framework that encodes global knowledge as class relation topology, capturing how classes relate within each client rather than where they lie in feature space. Each client builds its relation topology from local prototypes and uploads it with class statistics. The server then aggregates these relations in a reliability-aware manner that down-weights weakly supported ones, and broadcasts the global topology to clients. The global topology guides local training by emphasizing topology-similar negative classes. Experiments on three datasets under eight heterogeneous backbones show that FedTopo consistently outperforms parameter-, distillation-, and prototype-sharing baselines, with low communication and no inference overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zhaoyang-Ma/FedTopo.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习（FL）能够在去中心化的数据孤岛之间实现协作学习，而无需集中原始数据。然而，异构的本地架构常常导致表示空间不对齐，使得全局知识难以在数据孤岛间传递。现有的范式通过模型参数、蒸馏预测或类别原型来共享全局知识，但这些方法都将知识编码在需要跨客户端对齐的绝对空间中。异构骨干网络破坏了这种对齐，导致共享知识不可靠，从而误导本地训练。我们提出FedTopo，一个关系级别的框架，将全局知识编码为类别关系拓扑，捕捉每个客户端内部类别之间的关联关系，而非它们在特征空间中的位置。每个客户端从本地原型构建其关系拓扑，并连同类别统计信息一起上传。服务器随后以可靠性感知的方式聚合这些关系，降低弱支持关系的权重，并将全局拓扑广播给客户端。全局拓扑通过强调拓扑相似负类别来指导本地训练。在三种数据集和八种异构骨干网络上的实验表明，FedTopo在性能上一致优于参数共享、蒸馏共享和原型共享的基线方法，且通信开销低、无推理开销。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/Zhaoyang-Ma/FedTopo 获取。

</details>


### [140] [Forecasting Trajectory-Level Safety Risks in Black-Box Multi-Turn Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26820)
*Shi Lin,Peng Qian,Dinghao Liu,Renjie Sun,Sifan Wu,Dezhang Kong,Chenpei Wang,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Recast是一个面向LLM智能体的安全风险预测框架，通过轨迹级别的风险演化建模，在安全违规发生前平均提前2.41个回合预测88.3%的未来安全故障，超越传统的回合级风险检测方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM安全防护机制多为被动反应式检测，只能在风险已显性化后发现问题，缺乏在长期多轮交互中预测潜在风险演化轨迹并进行主动预警的能力。

Method: Recast包含三个核心模块：1) 双尺度轨迹视图，从短期对话进程和长期历史上下文检索风险相关证据；2) 组合风险演化建模，捕捉当前风险配置及其时间动态；3) 因果时序编码器，学习潜在风险演化模式并预测未来风险出现的回合分布。

Result: 在7类风险类别上，Recast预测88.3%的未来安全故障，平均提前2.41回合发出预警，误报率为12.3%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级别的风险预测能够有效在安全违规发生前识别新兴风险，相比回合级检测实现了从被动防护到主动预警的范式转变。

Original Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) evolve from standalone assistants into autonomous agents, ensuring their safety requires shifting beyond pointwise risk assessment to understand how risks emerge and unfold over long-horizon trajectories. In multi-turn interactions, malicious intent can be decomposed across seemingly harmless turns and gradually reconstructed through interaction trajectories, eventually resulting in safety failures. Existing safeguards remain largely reactive, detecting manifested violations while lacking the ability to predict latent risk evolution and enable preemptive prevention. To address this limitation, we propose Recast, a safety risk forecasting framework that advances LLM safeguarding beyond turn-level violation detection to trajectory-level risk prediction. Recast first retrieves risk-relevant evidence from both short-term dialogue progression and long-term historical context via a dual-scale trajectory view. It then models compositional risk evolution by capturing the current risk configuration and its temporal dynamics. Finally, a causal temporal encoder learns latent risk evolution patterns and predicts the distribution of future risk emergence turns. Extensive experiments across 7 risk categories show that Recast predicts 88.3% of future safety failures with an average lead time of 2.41 turns, while maintaining a false alarm rate of 12.3%, showcasing the effectiveness of trajectory-level forecasting in identifying emerging risks before safety violations occur.

中文摘要翻译: 随着大型语言模型从独立助手演变为自主智能体，确保其安全性需要超越点状风险评估，理解风险如何在长时间跨度的交互轨迹中产生和演变。在多轮交互中，恶意意图可以被分解到看似无害的回合中，通过交互轨迹逐步重建，最终导致安全故障。现有的防护机制主要停留在反应式检测层面，只能发现已显性化的违规行为，而缺乏预测潜在风险演化从而实现主动预防的能力。为解决这一局限，我们提出Recast，一个安全风险预测框架，将LLM安全防护从回合级违规检测提升到轨迹级风险预测。Recast首先通过双尺度轨迹视图，从短期对话进程和长期历史上下文中检索风险相关证据；然后通过捕捉当前风险配置及其时间动态，对组合风险演化进行建模；最后，一个因果时序编码器学习潜在风险演化模式，并预测未来风险出现回合的分布。覆盖7个风险类别的大量实验表明，Recast能够预测88.3%的未来安全故障，平均预警提前量为2.41个回合，同时保持12.3%的误报率，展示了轨迹级预测在安全违规发生前识别新兴风险的有效性。

</details>


### [141] [Budget-Aware LLM Discovery via Cost-Calibrated Frontier Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26828)
*Yansen Zhang,Yilu Liu,Tianyu Liu,Jiamin Chen,Xiaokun Zhang,Kai Xie,Xue Liu,Chen Ma,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CostAda，一种成本校准的自适应搜索控制器，通过成本校准的前沿效用解决大语言模型在推理时搜索中因忽视不同搜索动作的token成本差异而导致质量损失的问题。CostAda在16个基准-骨干网络对中的12个上，以最多一半的预算达到最强基线的全预算质量，并在所有8个基准上达到最强平均最终质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自适应发现控制器仅基于得分进展分配信用，忽略了提示长度、重试和引导调用导致的不同搜索动作具有不同token成本。论文证明，随着搜索前沿增多和成本发散，忽略成本的信用分配会损失几乎所有可达到的质量。

Method: CostAda基于成本校准的前沿效用（cost-calibrated frontier utility），该效用量化前沿进展相对于实际动作成本，并根据剩余预算条件化该信用。CostAda使用该信号控制：局部探索强度、前沿分配和预算导向的战术干预。成本和剩余预算因此塑造搜索过程，而不仅仅是作为记账变量或停止规则。

Result: 在16个基准-骨干网络对中的12个上，CostAda以最多一半的预算达到最强基线的全预算质量；在GLM-5和GPT-5.4下，在所有8个基准上取得最强平均最终质量。

Conclusion: 将成本信息融入搜索过程的信用分配中，可以显著提升预算效率，表明成本和剩余预算应积极塑造搜索行为而非仅作为后验统计。

Original Abstract: Large language models increasingly support scientific and algorithmic discovery through inference-time search over evaluated candidates. Existing adaptive discovery controllers assign credit based only on score progress, even though prompt length, retries, and guidance calls cause search actions to incur different token costs. We prove that cost-blind credit can forfeit all but a vanishing fraction of attainable quality as frontiers multiply and costs diverge. Under a fixed search-side token budget, the controller must decide which frontier is improving and whether its gain justifies the realized cost before the budget is exhausted. We introduce \textbf{CostAda}, a cost-calibrated adaptive controller built around \emph{cost-calibrated frontier utility}. The utility values frontier progress relative to realized action cost and conditions that credit on the remaining budget. CostAda uses this signal to control local exploration intensity, frontier allocation, and budgeted tactic intervention. Cost and remaining budget therefore shape the search rather than serving only as accounting variables or a stopping rule. CostAda reaches the strongest baseline's full-budget quality with at most half the budget on twelve of sixteen benchmark--backbone pairs while achieving the strongest mean final quality on all eight benchmarks under GLM-5 and GPT-5.4.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型越来越依赖推理时搜索来支持科学和算法发现，通过评估候选方案进行搜索。现有的自适应发现控制器仅基于得分进展来分配信用，尽管提示长度、重试和引导调用导致不同搜索动作产生不同的token成本。我们证明了，随着搜索前沿增多且成本发散，忽视成本的信用分配会损失几乎所有可达到的质量。在固定的搜索端token预算下，控制器必须判断哪个前沿正在改善，以及其收益能否在预算耗尽前证明已产生的成本是合理的。我们提出了\textbf{CostAda}，一种成本校准的自适应控制器，其核心是\textit{成本校准的前沿效用}。该效用量化前沿进展相对于实际动作成本，并根据剩余预算条件化该信用。CostAda利用这一信号来控制局部探索强度、前沿分配和预算导向的战术干预。因此，成本和剩余预算塑造了搜索过程，而不仅仅是作为记账变量或停止规则。在16个基准-骨干网络对中的12个上，CostAda以最多一半的预算达到最强基线的全预算质量，同时在GLM-5和GPT-5.4下，在所有8个基准上实现了最强的平均最终质量。

</details>


### [142] [Kairos: Numerically Robust News Recommendation under Item Cold-Start via Cholesky-based LinUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26832)
*Finn Hertsch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kairos框架，通过结合上下文在线学习（LinUCB）、Cholesky秩1更新和MRL嵌入，解决区域新闻个性化推荐中因数据稀疏和TTL短导致的物品冷启动问题，在Tagesschau API上实现了4.85倍的效率提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 区域新闻个性化推荐面临结构性物品冷启动问题：深度学习模型需要大量交互数据，但真实新闻的TTL极短（<48小时）且文章池浅，协同过滤因数据不足而失效。

Method: 采用上下文在线学习（LinUCB）处理数据稀疏性；用Cholesky因子直接秩1更新替代易出错的Sherman-Morrison求逆，保证协方差矩阵正定性；集成Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）降低推理延迟，利用特征空间语义冗余提升效率。

Result: 基于Tagesschau API的实验表明，该方法在不显著牺牲排序精度的前提下，实现了4.85倍的效率提升。

Conclusion: Kairos为资源和数据受限环境下的高性能推荐系统提供了可参考的设计蓝图。

Original Abstract: Algorithmic news personalization in regional markets often fails because modern deep learning models require massive interaction data while real-world news has a short Time-to-Live (TTL < 48 h) and shallow article pools. This structural item cold-start deprives collaborative filtering of the data needed for robust modeling. This paper presents Project Kairos, a framework that bridges this data scarcity through a contextual online learning approach (LinUCB). To ensure numerical integrity for continuous operation, Kairos replaces error-prone Sherman-Morrison inversions with direct rank-1 updates of Cholesky factors. This preserves the positive definiteness of the covariance matrix even under ill-conditioned data scenarios.
