Motivation: 目前学界普遍将LLMs视为与人类认知根本不同的“外星智能”，认为其与人类的相似性只是拟人化投射的结果。作者认为这种框架是错误的，旨在揭示LLM与人类认知在深层次结构原则上的本质对应关系。

Method: 论文通过跨学科对比分析，从五个维度（推理组织、计算架构、表征结构、预测驱动学习、支持目标导向行为的强化学习-like机制）系统性地识别了LLM系统与人类认知之间的结构对应关系，并借鉴认知科学中已有长期支持的原理进行论证。

Result: 论证了LLMs虽然在物理基质、学习历史和交互环境等方面与人类存在显著差异，但在五个关键的认知组织维度上与人脑认知原理存在结构性趋同，这些对应关系支持了一个更广泛的智能认知模型。

Conclusion: LLMs并非完全异质于人类认知的“外星智能”，核心认知科学原理（如推理组织、预测驱动学习等）既可解释人类智能，也可解释当代基于LLM的系统，这为构建统一的智能认知理论提供了支撑。

Original Abstract: LLMs are widely regarded as alien intelligences, systems whose cognitive operations are fundamentally unlike our own. Apparent similarities to human cognition are therefore often seen as the result of anthropomorphic projection. We argue that this framing is mistaken. LLMs clearly differ from humans in important respects, including their physical substrate, learning history, and the environments with which they interact. These differences make it all the more striking that contemporary LLM-based systems converge with human cognition on a number of principles of cognitive organization with longstanding support in cognitive science. We identify structural correspondences across five dimensions: inferential organization, computational architecture, representational structure, prediction-driven learning, and reinforcement-learning-like mechanisms supporting goal-directed action. These correspondences support a broader model of intelligent cognition in which core principles long used to explain human intelligence also characterize contemporary LLM-based systems.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛视为外星智能——其认知运作方式与人类根本不同。因此，它们与人类认知表观上的相似性常被视为拟人化投射的结果。我们认为这种框架是错误的。LLMs在物理基质、学习历史和交互环境等重要方面确实与人类存在差异。这些差异使得一个现象更加引人注目：当代基于LLM的系统在多个认知组织原则上与人类认知趋同，而这些原则在认知科学中已获长期支持。我们在五个维度上识别了结构性对应关系：推理组织、计算架构、表征结构、预测驱动学习，以及支持目标导向行为的强化学习样机制。这些对应关系支持了一个更广泛的智能认知模型——其中长期用于解释人类智能的核心原理同样也刻画了当代基于LLM的系统。

</details>


### [172] [Three Failures of Pain Location: Why the Diagnostic Utility of Symptom Localization Is Not One Thing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26297)
*Adam Y Shavit*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了疼痛位置报告的诊断效用的新理论框架，将传统单一"解剖复杂性"梯度分解为三种认知上不同的定位失败类型，并建立了统一的贝叶斯推理模型来解释它们。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有理论将患者报告的疼痛位置的诊断效用简化为单一梯度（由解剖复杂性决定），但这种解释混淆了三种认知上不同的定位失败类型，每种类型有其自身的数学结构、最优工具和公共健康后果，需要更精确的区分和建模。

Method: 通过逆问题、信息论、决策论和神经场模型对三种定位失败分别建模，并构建空间贝叶斯模型来区分"疼痛被报告的位置"与"疼痛被感受的位置"。

Result: 三种定位失败（解剖多路复用、去定位放大、牵涉/非典型位移）具有不同的数学行为，需要不同的应对策略；它们统一于贝叶斯推理框架中不同节点（似然、模型类、先验与损失）的失败；已发表的"高效用"准确度区间夸大了特异性，实际梯度更平缓。

Conclusion: 疼痛位置报告的诊断效用不是一个由解剖复杂性决定的单一梯度，而是一个"为什么定位会失败"的维度轴，这一框架与传统的伤害性/神经病理性/伤害可塑性分类法不同，具有独立的综合价值。

Original Abstract: Patient-reported pain location is diagnostically decisive for some presentations and nearly uninformative for others. The prevailing account treats this as a single gradient of diagnostic utility governed by anatomical complexity. That explanation conflates three epistemically distinct failures of localization, each with its own mathematical structure, optimal instrument, and public-health consequence. In anatomical multiplexing (a), many structures share one location: a non-identifiable inverse problem. In delocalized amplification (b) --- clinically, central sensitization or nociplastic pain --- a centrally driven pain-behaviour pattern replaces the peripheral generator: a change of generative model. In referred/atypical displacement (c), location shifts in a systematic, person-dependent way, as in referred pain and in atypical presentations: a covariate-dependent bias. The three behave differently under inverse-problem, information-theoretic, decision-theoretic, and neural-field models, and demand different remedies. A single principle unifies them: they are one Bayesian inference problem failing at different nodes, meaning different points in one generative model: the likelihood, the model class, and the prior and loss. Observation over time increases the recoverable information. A fourth node, the report itself, carries this paper's main formal contribution --- a spatial Bayesian model of where pain is said to be, distinct from where it is felt. Re-examination further finds that the published "high-utility" accuracy band leans on overstated specificity, so the gradient is real but flatter than drawn. The account sits on a "why-location-fails" axis, a synthesis distinct from the nociceptive/neuropathic/nociplastic taxonomy (Kosek et al., 2016).

中文摘要翻译: 患者报告的疼痛位置对某些临床表现具有诊断决定性，而对其他表现则几乎毫无信息价值。当前的主流理论将其视为一个由解剖复杂性决定的单一诊断效用梯度。这种解释混淆了三种认知上不同的定位失败，每种失败有其自身的数学结构、最优工具和公共健康后果。(a) 解剖多路复用：多个结构共享同一位置，构成一个不可识别的逆问题；(b) 去定位放大——临床上的中枢敏化或伤害可塑性疼痛——中枢驱动的疼痛行为模式取代了外周发生器，即生成模型的改变；(c) 牵涉/非典型位移：位置以系统性的、因人而异的方式发生偏移，如牵涉痛和非典型表现中的情况，即协变量依赖的偏倚。这三种失败在逆问题、信息论、决策论和神经场模型下表现出不同的行为，并需要不同的应对策略。一个统一的原则将它们联系起来：它们都是同一个贝叶斯推理问题在不同节点上的失败，即同一个生成模型中的不同点——似然、模型类、先验与损失。随时间推移的观察可以增加可恢复的信息。第四个节点——报告本身——承载了本文的主要形式贡献：一个空间贝叶斯模型，用于描述"疼痛被说出的位置"区别于"疼痛被感受到的位置"。进一步检验发现，已发表的"高效用"准确度区间夸大了特异性，因此该梯度是真实存在的，但比所描绘的更为平缓。该框架位于一个"为何定位会失败"的轴线上，这是一种与伤害性/神经病理性/伤害可塑性分类法（Kosek et al., 2016）截然不同的综合观点。

</details>


### [173] [Artificial intelligence in deep brain stimulation for movement disorders: a systematic review and technology readiness assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26666)
*Zohra Souei,Muhammad Mushhood Ur Rehman,Harith Akram,Jocelyne Bloch,Stephan Chabardes,Alfonso Fasano,Marwan Hariz,Joachim K. Krauss,Andrea A. Kühn,Patricia Limousin,Daniel E. Lumsden,Eduardo M. Moraud,Elena Moro,Martin M. Reich,Ludvic Zrinzo,Xavier Vasques,Laura Cif*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了2000年至2025年间239篇关于AI用于脑深部电刺激（DBS）治疗运动障碍的同行评审研究，发现大多数系统仍处于早期到中期的转化阶段，主要受限于外部验证不足而非算法能力，但新兴的前瞻性研究正推动该领域向临床成熟迈进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管AI在DBS治疗运动障碍中的应用日益增多，但当前系统是否接近实际部署尚不明确，因此需要系统性地评估其范围、验证成熟度和转化就绪程度。

Method: 对2000年至2025年间发表的239篇同行评审研究进行系统评估，分析AI方法、验证实践以及制约临床转化的障碍。

Result: 研究以帕金森病和丘脑底核靶点为主，对其他疾病和靶点覆盖有限；大多数研究报告了良好的内部性能，但外部验证罕见，评估主要为回顾性和单中心，超过四分之一的研究存在小样本、高维数据导致的过拟合风险；技术就绪度评估显示大多数系统处于早期至中期转化阶段，主要受限于验证不足而非算法缺陷。

Conclusion: 当前AI用于DBS的系统仍处于临床转化的早期到中期阶段，制约因素主要是验证不足而非算法能力不足，叠加DBS固有的生物异质性和动态复杂性；但新兴的外部验证和前瞻性研究表明该领域正走向临床成熟，在靶点定位、参数编程、结果预测和自适应治疗递送方面具有应用前景。

Original Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly explored across deep brain stimulation (DBS) for movement disorders, yet whether current systems are approaching deployment remains unclear. To characterise their scope, validation maturity, and translational readiness, we systematically evaluated 239 peer-reviewed studies published between 2000 and 2025, assessing AI methods, validation practices, and barriers constraining clinical translation. Research was dominated by Parkinson's disease and subthalamic nucleus targeting, with limited coverage of other disorders and targets. Most studies reported encouraging internal performance; however, external validation was rare, evaluations remained predominantly retrospective and single-centre, and more than one-quarter involved small-sample, high-dimensional datasets with elevated overfitting risk. Technology readiness assessment revealed that most systems remain at early-to-intermediate translational stages, constrained more by limited validation than by algorithmic inadequacy, compounded by the biological heterogeneity and dynamic complexity inherent to DBS. Nevertheless, emerging external and prospective studies suggest a field moving toward clinical maturity, with promising applications in targeting, programming, outcome prediction, and adaptive therapy delivery.

中文摘要翻译: 人工智能（AI）在脑深部电刺激（DBS）治疗运动障碍中的应用日益广泛，但当前系统是否接近实际部署仍不明确。为表征其研究范围、验证成熟度和转化就绪程度，我们系统评估了2000年至2025年间发表的239篇同行评审研究，分析了AI方法、验证实践及制约临床转化的障碍。研究以帕金森病和丘脑底核靶点为主，对其他疾病和靶点的覆盖有限。大多数研究报告了良好的内部性能；然而，外部验证极为罕见，评估仍主要为回顾性和单中心，且超过四分之一的研究涉及小样本、高维数据集，存在较高的过拟合风险。技术就绪度评估显示，大多数系统仍处于早期至中期的转化阶段，制约因素更多来自于有限的验证而非算法能力的不足，同时DBS固有的生物异质性和动态复杂性进一步加剧了转化难度。尽管如此，新兴的外部验证和前瞻性研究表明该领域正逐步走向临床成熟，在靶点定位、参数编程、结果预测和自适应治疗递送方面展现出有前景的应用。

</details>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Evolutionary AP Switch ON/OFF Techniques for Energy-efficient Cell-free Massive MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26209)
*Jan García-Morales,Alejandro de la Fuente,David Gualda,Leopoldo Carro-Calvo,Felip Riera-Palou,Guillem Femenias*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出两种进化算法（CGA和PDGA）用于无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络中接入点动态开关选择，相比现有贪婪启发式方法，在能效和频谱效率权衡方面取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AP开关机制（如最坏情况维度设计或贪婪启发式）对组合激活空间的探索不充分，导致能效优化效果欠佳，难以在时变非均匀流量下平衡服务质量与能耗。

Method: 提出两种进化AP选择策略：1) 约束遗传算法（CGA），在固定激活基数下寻找近优AP子集，外层搜索确定全局最优解；2) 帕累托驱动遗传算法（PDGA），通过进化帕累托前沿联合优化频谱效率和能效。

Result: 在空间非均匀流量场景下，结合共轭波束成形和MMSE处理，两种方法均优于现有贪婪基准方法，显著提升能效并改善能效-频谱效率权衡。

Conclusion: 进化优化是一种强大且可靠的能效CF-mMIMO部署方法，能有效提升网络能效且不引入其他性能损失。

Original Abstract: Cell-free massive multiple input multiple output (CF-mMIMO) is an emerging technology for next-generation wireless systems, where dynamically adapting the set of active access points (APs) is crucial to balance quality of service (QoS) requirements and network energy consumption under highly time-varying and spatially non-uniform traffic loads. Existing AP ON/OFF mechanisms--typically based on worst-case dimensioning or greedy heuristics--explore the combinatorial activation space inadequately, leading to suboptimal energy-efficiency outcomes. This paper introduces two evolutionary AP-selection strategies tailored to CF-mMIMO networks. The first, a constrained genetic algorithm (CGA), identifies the near-optimal subset of active APs for any fixed activation cardinality, while an outer search determines the globally optimal operating point. The second, a Pareto-driven genetic algorithm (PDGA), jointly optimizes spectral and energy efficiency by evolving a Pareto front over all feasible activation patterns. A detailed computational-complexity analysis is provided for both techniques. Simulations conducted under realistic spatially inhomogeneous traffic and considering both conjugate beamforming (CB) and minimum mean square error (MMSE) processing confirm consistent performance gains. The proposed methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art greedy benchmarks, delivering noticeable improvements in energy efficiency for both CB and MMSE schemes, while simultaneously enhancing the energy-spectral efficiency tradeoff, which is typically difficult to improve without incurring penalties elsewhere. These results highlight the strong potential of evolutionary optimization as a powerful and reliable approach for energy-efficient CF-mMIMO deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 无蜂窝大规模多输入多输出（CF-mMIMO）是下一代无线系统中的新兴技术，其中动态调整活跃接入点（AP）集合对于在高度时变和空间非均匀流量负载下平衡服务质量（QoS）要求与网络能耗至关重要。现有的AP开关机制——通常基于最坏情况维度设计或贪婪启发式——对组合激活空间的探索不充分，导致能效优化结果欠佳。本文介绍了两种专为CF-mMIMO网络设计的进化AP选择策略。第一种是约束遗传算法（CGA），它在任意固定激活基数下识别近优的活跃AP子集，同时通过外层搜索确定全局最优工作点。第二种是帕累托驱动遗传算法（PDGA），它通过在所有可行激活模式上演化帕累托前沿，联合优化频谱效率和能效。本文为这两种技术提供了详细的计算复杂度分析。在现实空间非均匀流量场景下，结合共轭波束成形（CB）和最小均方误差（MMSE）处理进行的仿真验证了一致的性能增益。所提出的方法持续优于最先进的贪婪基准方法，在CB和MMSE方案下均显著提升能效，同时改善了能效-频谱效率权衡——这一权衡通常难以在不引入其他性能损失的情况下得到改善。这些结果突显了进化优化作为能效CF-mMIMO部署的一种强大且可靠方法的巨大潜力。

</details>


### [175] [SWIPT-Enhanced Cell-Free Massive MIMO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26303)
*Guillem Femenias,Jan García-Morales,Felip Riera-Palou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SWIPT增强的无小区大规模MIMO网络，通过分布式接入点协同服务能量收集型和传统移动站，在空间相关Rician衰落信道下推导了能量收集和频谱/能量效率的解析表达式，并通过联合UL/DL优化问题最大化最差加权SINR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SWIPT技术有望为IoT无线网络中的低功耗设备提供近乎永续的供电，但现有研究缺乏对SWIPT与无小区大规模MIMO网络结合的系统级分析，特别是空间相关Rician信道下的性能权衡。

Method: 提出SWIPT增强的无小区大规模MIMO网络架构，采用不同信道估计器（基于对视距分量假设的差异）和预编码方案，推导下行能量收集阶段收集能量的解析表达式以及上行数据传输阶段可达频谱效率和能量效率表达式，构建耦合的UL/DL优化问题以最大化最小加权SINR。

Result: 通过大量数值结果揭示了可达频谱效率与能量效率、收集能量、上行导频传输能量及系统配置之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: SWIPT增强的无小区大规模MIMO网络能够有效兼顾能量收集型和传统移动站的通信需求，但需要在频谱效率、能量效率和收集能量之间进行权衡设计。

Original Abstract: Simultaneous wireless information and power transfer (SWIPT) has been advocated as a highly promising technology to provide near-perpetual operation to low-powered wireless devices in Internet-of-Things (IoT)-based wireless networks. In this paper, a SWIPT-enhanced cell-free massive MIMO network is proposed. In such a network, a large set of spatially distributed access points (APs) interconnected via a central processing unit (CPU) can collaboratively serve a large number of both energy harvesting mobile stations (MSs) (requiring wireless energy transfer) and conventional MSs (not requiring wireless energy transfer) on the same time-frequency resources. We consider spatially correlated Rician fading channels and the use of different precoding schemes that are based on different channel estimators differing on the assumed knowledge of the line-of-sight component. Mathematically manageable expressions are derived for the harvested energy during the downlink (DL) energy harvesting phase and the achievable spectral and energy efficiencies during the uplink (UL) payload transmission phase. A coupled UL/DL optimization problem is formulated aiming at finding the power control coefficients that maximize the minimum of the weighted achievable UL signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratios (SINRs) of all MSs. Extensive numerical results are presented that serve to highlight the existing trade-offs among the achievable spectral and energy efficiencies, the harvested energy, the energy dedicated to UL pilot transmission or the system configuration.

中文摘要翻译: 同步无线信息和功率传输（SWIPT）被认为是面向基于物联网（IoT）的无线网络中为低功耗无线设备提供近乎永续运行的一项极具前景的技术。本文提出了一种SWIPT增强的无小区大规模MIMO网络。在该网络中，大量空间分布的接入点（AP）通过中央处理单元（CPU）互联，可以在相同的时频资源上协同服务大量能量收集型移动站（MS）（需要无线能量传输）和传统MS（不需要无线能量传输）。我们考虑了空间相关的Rician衰落信道，并采用了基于不同信道估计器（对视距分量假设的知识不同）的多种预编码方案。推导出了下行（DL）能量收集阶段收集能量的数学易处理表达式，以及上行（UL）有效载荷传输阶段可达频谱效率和能量效率的表达式。构建了一个耦合的UL/DL优化问题，旨在寻找功率控制系数，以最大化所有MS的加权可达上行信干噪比（SINR）中的最小值。大量数值结果揭示了可达频谱效率与能量效率、收集能量、上行导频传输能量或系统配置之间存在的权衡关系。

</details>


### [176] [Joint Beamforming, Energy Management, and Trajectory Optimization for Figure-Eight Loitering in Solar-Powered HAPS-Enabled ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26325)
*Xue Zhang,Bang Huang,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种太阳能高空平台站（HAPS）支持的集成感知与通信（ISAC）框架，采用8字形盘旋架构实现昼夜可持续运行，通过联合优化通信、感知、移动性和能量管理，在最大化日间通信性能的同时最小化夜间推进能耗。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有HAPS研究多聚焦于通信或感知单一方面，缺乏对可持续昼夜ISAC运行的系统性设计。太阳能HAPS面临昼夜交替导致的能量供需不匹配问题，亟需一种能同时协调通信、感知和能量管理的协同框架。

Method: 提出了统一的通信-感知-能量模型，联合刻画太阳能采集、电池动态、推进功耗、通信传输和合成孔径雷达（SAR）成像。分别建立日间运行（DTO）和夜间运行（NTO）的耦合优化问题，通过电池状态桥接两个运行阶段，并设计高效迭代算法求解非凸优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果验证了所提通信-感知-能量协同设计的有效性，证明了该框架能够有效支持可持续的昼夜ISAC运行。

Conclusion: 本文提出的太阳能HAPS-ISAC框架通过通信、感知与能量管理的联合优化，成功实现了昼夜可持续的集成感知与通信服务，为高空平台在ISAC领域的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Solar-powered high-altitude platform stations (HAPSs) provide a promising platform for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) owing to their wide-area coverage and long-endurance operation. This paper proposes a solar-powered HAPS-enabled ISAC framework for sustainable day-night operation, where a figure-eight loitering architecture is adopted to provide persistent ISAC services over geographically separated regions while harvesting solar energy. A unified communication-sensing-energy model is developed by jointly characterizing solar energy harvesting, battery dynamics, propulsion power consumption, communication transmission, and synthetic aperture radar (SAR) imaging. Based on this model, coupled optimization problems are formulated for daytime operation (DTO) and nighttime operation (NTO), where the battery state bridges the two operational phases through a long-term energy budget. The proposed framework jointly optimizes communication, sensing, mobility, and energy management to maximize daytime communication performance while minimizing nighttime propulsion energy consumption. Efficient iterative algorithms are developed to solve the resulting non-convex optimization problems. Simulation results verify the effectiveness of the proposed communication-sensing-energy co-design and demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively supports sustainable day-night ISAC operation.

中文摘要翻译: 太阳能高空平台站（HAPS）因其广域覆盖和长续航能力，为集成感知与通信（ISAC）提供了有前景的平台。本文提出了一种太阳能HAPS支持的ISAC框架，用于可持续昼夜运行，其中采用8字形盘旋架构，在采集太阳能的同时为地理上分离的区域提供持久的ISAC服务。通过联合刻画太阳能采集、电池动态、推进功耗、通信传输和合成孔径雷达（SAR）成像，建立了统一的通信-感知-能量模型。基于该模型，分别为日间运行（DTO）和夜间运行（NTO）制定了耦合优化问题，其中电池状态通过长期能量预算桥接两个运行阶段。该框架联合优化通信、感知、移动性和能量管理，以最大化日间通信性能同时最小化夜间推进能耗。开发了高效迭代算法来求解由此产生的非凸优化问题。仿真结果验证了所提通信-感知-能量协同设计的有效性，并表明该框架能够有效支持可持续的昼夜ISAC运行。

</details>


### [177] [MVLA-GR: A Phase-Free Multipath-Based Geometry Reconstruction Method via Multi-View Likelihood Accumulation for ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26421)
*Bowei Xing,Yuxiang Zhang,Jianhua Zhang,Yifeng Xiong,Hongbo Xing,Li Yu,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信道冲激响应（CIR）测量的多视角似然累积几何重建（MVLA-GR）方法，仅利用延迟和功率信息（无需相位）即可在ISAC系统中重建目标几何结构，通过软距离匹配核与联合阈值策略，在仿真和真实36GHz车载实验中验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统ISAC几何重建方法依赖相干处理和精确相位信息，但在实际通信系统（尤其高频段）中相位信息难以保证，因此需要一种无需相位的、低复杂度的几何重建方法。

Method: MVLA-GR方法基于CIR测量，仅使用延迟和功率观测。首先从每次观测中提取主径多径分量，然后对每个候选空间位置，跨视角累积传播距离与之匹配的分量作为支撑证据。引入软距离匹配核以容忍距离估计误差和视角相关散射偏移，并以接收功率作为可靠性权重。最后采用响应幅度与角度支撑连续性相结合的联合阈值策略，将连续支撑图转化为二值几何估计。

Result: 在典型目标与复杂目标的射线追踪仿真，以及36 GHz真实车载测量实验中，MVLA-GR能够有效恢复目标几何结构，验证了其作为低复杂度、无需相位的ISAC几何重建方案的有效性。

Conclusion: MVLA-GR为ISAC系统提供了一种无需相位信息的几何重建解决方案，具备低复杂度优势，适用于实际通信系统中高频段等相位难以保证的场景。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables wireless systems to reuse communication signals for environmental sensing, where reconstructing the geometry of surrounding objects is a representative sensing task. However, many conventional methods rely on coherent processing and require accurate phase information, which is often hard to guarantee in practical communication systems, particularly at high carrier frequencies. To address this problem, this paper proposes a Multi-View Likelihood Accumulation Geometry Reconstruction (MVLA-GR) method based on channel impulse response (CIR) measurements, which uses only delay and power observations without requiring phase information. The method extracts dominant multipath components from each observation, and for each candidate spatial location, accumulates components across views whose propagation distances match the location as supporting evidence. A soft distance-matching kernel is introduced to tolerate range estimation errors and viewpoint-dependent scattering migration, and the received power of each component is used as a reliability weight. A joint thresholding strategy combining response magnitude and angular support continuity then converts the continuous support map into a binary geometry estimate. Ray-tracing simulations on canonical and complex targets, as well as real-world vehicle measurements at 36 GHz, demonstrate that MVLA-GR can effectively recover target geometry, providing a low-complexity phase-free solution for ISAC.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）使得无线系统能够复用通信信号进行环境感知，其中重建周围物体的几何结构是一项代表性的感知任务。然而，许多传统方法依赖相干处理并需要精确的相位信息，而这在实际通信系统中往往难以保证，特别是在高载波频率下。为了解决这一问题，本文提出了一种基于信道冲激响应（CIR）测量的多视角似然累积几何重建（MVLA-GR）方法，该方法仅利用延迟和功率观测，无需相位信息。该方法从每次观测中提取主径多径分量，并对每个候选空间位置，跨视角累积传播距离与之匹配的分量作为支撑证据。引入软距离匹配核以容忍距离估计误差和视角相关的散射迁移，并以每个分量的接收功率作为可靠性权重。结合响应幅度与角度支撑连续性的联合阈值策略，将连续支撑图转化为二值几何估计。针对典型目标与复杂目标的射线追踪仿真，以及36 GHz真实车载测量实验表明，MVLA-GR能够有效恢复目标几何结构，为ISAC提供了一种低复杂度、无需相位的解决方案。

</details>


### [178] [Energy-Efficient Access-Point Sleep-Mode Techniques for Cell-Free mmWave Massive MIMO Networks With Non-Uniform Spatial Traffic Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26449)
*Jan García-Morales,Guillem Femenias,Felip Riera-Palou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对无蜂窝毫米波大规模MIMO网络中现有AP开关策略仅考虑均匀空间流量分布的不足，本文提出基于拟合优度检验的非均匀空间流量分布感知的AP休眠模式策略，通过动态开关AP来适应MS的数量和统计分布，显著提升能效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无蜂窝大规模MIMO网络研究大多仅考虑重流量负载条件且假设均匀空间流量分布，导致低流量期间AP利用率低下、资源浪费和能效低下。需要更实际的考虑非均匀空间流量分布的AP开关策略来提升能效。

Method: 提出基于拟合优度（GoF）检验的AP开关（ASO）策略，通过GoF测试动态感知网络中移动站（MS）的数量和统计分布，据此决定AP的开启或关闭，以适应非均匀空间流量分布。

Result: 数值结果表明，在非均匀空间流量分布下，采用精心设计的基于GoF的ASO策略可以显著提高可达能效。

Conclusion: 基于GoF检验的AP休眠模式策略能够有效捕捉无线网络中空间流量分布的非均匀特性，通过动态开关AP显著提升无蜂窝毫米波大规模MIMO网络的能效。

Original Abstract: Cell-free massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) is a novel beyond 5G (B5G) and 6G paradigm that, through the use of a common central processing unit (CPU), coordinates a large number of distributed access points (APs) to coherently serve mobile stations (MSs) on the same time/frequency resource. By exploiting the characteristics of new less-congested millimeter wave (mmWave) frequency bands, these networks can improve the overall system spectral and energy efficiencies by using low-complexity hybrid precoders/decoders. For this purpose, the system must be correctly dimensioned to provide the required quality of service (QoS) to MSs under different traffic load conditions. However, only heavy traffic load conditions are usually taken into account when analysing these networks and, thus, many APs might be underutilized during low traffic load periods, leading to an inefficient use of resources and waste of energy. Aiming at the implementation of energy-efficient AP switch on/off strategies, several approaches have been proposed in the literature that only consider rather unrealistic uniform spatial traffic distribution in the whole coverage area. Unlike prior works, this paper proposes energy efficient AP sleep-mode techniques for cell-free mmWave massive MIMO networks that are able to capture the inhomogeneous nature of spatial traffic distribution in realistic wireless networks. The proposed framework considers, analyzes and compares different AP switch ON-OFF (ASO) strategies that, based on the use of goodness-of-fit (GoF) tests, are specifically designed to dynamically turn on/off APs to adapt to both the number and the statistical distribution of MSs in the network. Numerical results show that the use of properly designed GoF-based ASO strategies under a non-uniform spatial traffic distribution can serve to considerably improve the achievable energy efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 无蜂窝大规模多输入多输出（MIMO）是一种新颖的超越5G（B5G）和6G范式，通过使用公共中央处理单元（CPU），协调大量分布式接入点（AP）在同一时间/频率资源上协同服务移动站（MS）。通过利用新的较少拥堵的毫米波（mmWave）频段的特性，这些网络可以使用低复杂度混合预编码器/解码器来提高整体频谱效率和能效。为此，系统必须正确配置，以在不同流量负载条件下为MS提供所需的服务质量（QoS）。然而，在分析这些网络时通常只考虑重流量负载条件，因此在低流量负载期间许多AP可能未被充分利用，导致资源利用效率低下和能源浪费。为了实现能效型AP开关策略，现有文献中提出了几种方法，但这些方法仅考虑整个覆盖区域内相当不现实的均匀空间流量分布。与以往工作不同，本文提出了适用于无蜂窝毫米波大规模MIMO网络的能效型AP休眠模式技术，该技术能够捕捉现实无线网络中空间流量分布的非均匀特性。所提出的框架考虑、分析并比较了不同的AP开关（ASO）策略，这些策略基于拟合优度（GoF）检验，专门设计用于动态开启/关闭AP，以适应网络中MS的数量和统计分布。数值结果表明，在非均匀空间流量分布下，使用精心设计的基于GoF的ASO策略可以显著提高可达能效。

</details>


### [179] [Calibrating the Digital Twin Channel: Statistics-Consistent Sim-to-Lab Adaptation for W-Band Industrial OFDM Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26501)
*Pulok Tarafder,Abigail O. Oyekola,Jasni Areepatta Mannil,Imtiaz Ahmed,Zoheb Hassan,Danda B. Rawat,Wenjie Che*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种统计一致性仿真到实验室适配（SC-SLA）框架，通过生成对抗网络（GAN）启发的循环一致性架构，对齐信道冲激响应统计量（平均PDP、时延扩展分布、子载波统计），显著提升了95 GHz射线追踪信道孪生的保真度，使τ_rms KS统计量从0.86降至0.050，并泛化至92-94 GHz频段。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字孪生可降低工业无线网络的空口验证成本，但其效用取决于底层信道孪生（CT）的保真度。在W波段（95 GHz以上），射线追踪虽能捕获确定性传播几何，但其信道频率响应（CFR）无法复现实验室OFDM测量中观察到的小尺度损伤和捕获间变异性，因此需要一种校准方法提升射线追踪CT的保真度。

Method: 提出SC-SLA框架，采用GAN启发的循环一致性架构，包含ResNet生成器和批次级信道统计损失函数（包括PDP、子带PDP、均方根时延扩展τ_rms的矩和分位数、以及平均CFR幅度谱的归一化均方误差NMSE）。该框架是非对抗性的，不需要配对的仿真/测量样本，也不使用判别器。

Result: 在95 GHz留出数据上，SC-SLA将τ_rms的KS统计量从0.86降至0.050（相比损伤增强的射线追踪输入），比四个监督基线（FCNN、CNN1D、BiLSTM、UNet1D）中最强者降低44%（从0.089降至0.050）。无需重新训练，同一检查点泛化至92-94 GHz载波，在所有基线中取得最低的PDP和CFR幅度误差，并减少τ_rms分布失配。

Conclusion: SC-SLA能够以统计一致的方式有效校准射线追踪信道孪生，显著提升其与实测信道在关键统计量上的匹配程度，且具备跨频段泛化能力，为高频工业无线网络中数字孪生的实用化提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Digital twins (DTs) can reduce over-the-air validation cost in industrial wireless networks, but their utility depends on the fidelity of the underlying channel twin (CT). At W-band, site-specific ray tracing captures deterministic propagation geometry, yet its channel frequency responses (CFRs) do not reproduce the small-scale impairments and capture-to-capture variability observed in laboratory orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) measurements above 90 GHz. This paper proposes Statistics-Consistent Sim-to-Lab Adaptation (SC-SLA), a calibration framework that improves the fidelity of a 95 GHz Sionna ray-traced CT toward that of the testbed by aligning the mean power delay profile (PDP), the distribution of root-mean-square delay spread ($τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$), and per-subcarrier statistics at 50 MHz sampling bandwidth. SC-SLA uses a generative adversarial network (GAN)-inspired, cycle-consistent architecture with ResNet generators and batch-level channel-statistics losses on the PDP, sub-band PDP, $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$ moments and quantiles, and normalized mean-square error (NMSE) of the mean CFR-magnitude profile. The framework is non-adversarial and requires neither paired simulated/measured samples nor discriminators. On held-out 95 GHz data, SC-SLA reduces the $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$ Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS) statistic from 0.86 to 0.050 relative to the impairment-augmented ray-traced input, and by 44% (from 0.089 to 0.050) relative to the strongest of four supervised baselines (FCNN, CNN1D, BiLSTM, and UNet1D). Without retraining, the same checkpoint also generalizes to 92-94 GHz carriers, where it achieves the lowest PDP and CFR-magnitude errors among all baselines while reducing the $τ_{\mathrm{rms}}$-distribution mismatch relative to the uncalibrated twin.

中文摘要翻译: 数字孪生（DTs）可以降低工业无线网络中的空口验证成本，但其效用取决于底层信道孪生（CT）的保真度。在W波段，特定地点的射线追踪能够捕获确定性传播几何，但其信道频率响应（CFR）无法复现90 GHz以上实验室正交频分复用（OFDM）测量中观察到的小尺度损伤和捕获间变异性。本文提出统计一致性仿真到实验室适配（SC-SLA）——一种校准框架，通过对齐平均功率时延谱（PDP）、均方根时延扩展（τ_rms）的分布以及50 MHz采样带宽下的每子载波统计量，提升95 GHz Sionna射线追踪CT相对于测试台的保真度。SC-SLA采用生成对抗网络（GAN）启发的循环一致性架构，包含ResNet生成器和基于批次的信道统计损失函数，覆盖PDP、子带PDP、τ_rms的矩和分位数，以及平均CFR幅度谱的归一化均方误差（NMSE）。该框架是非对抗性的，既不需要配对的仿真/测量样本，也不使用判别器。在95 GHz留出数据上，SC-SLA将τ_rms的Kolmogorov-Smirnov（KS）统计量从0.86降至0.050（相对于损伤增强的射线追踪输入），相比四个监督基线（FCNN、CNN1D、BiLSTM和UNet1D）中最强者降低44%（从0.089降至0.050）。无需重新训练，同一检查点还能泛化至92-94 GHz载波，在这些频段上取得所有基线中最低的PDP和CFR幅度误差，同时减少τ_rms分布相对于未校准孪生的失配。

</details>


### [180] [A Data-Driven Vibration Analysis Framework for Micro-Motor Fault Diagnosis and Quality Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26519)
*Xuan Chen,Xinjun Zuo,Yancheng Bi,Shunli Yu,Yunqi Cao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于振动的电动牙刷微电机故障检测方法，结合RF特征选择与SVM分类，实现了94.44%的平衡精度，为制造中的质量控制提供了可靠方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 内部微电机的可靠性对电动牙刷的性能和寿命至关重要，需要一种有效的故障检测方法来识别微电机缺陷。

Method: 设计专用信号采集装置捕捉振动信号；从时域和频域提取综合特征；使用随机森林评估特征重要性，选取最重要的三个特征构成最优子集；最后用支持向量机模型对电机状态进行分类。

Result: 平衡精度94.44%，缺陷召回率88.89%，缺陷F1分数94.12%，马修斯相关系数93.74%，几何均值94.28%，ROC曲线下面积100.00%。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够准确高效地检测电动牙刷微电机故障，为制造中的质量控制和状态监测提供了实用可靠的解决方案。

Original Abstract: The reliability of the internal micro-motors is crucial for the performance and lifespan of electric toothbrushes. In this paper, a vibration-based fault detection method is proposed to identify micro-motor defects in electric toothbrushes. A dedicated signal acquisition device was designed and developed to capture the vibration signals of micro-motors using a high-precision accelerometer. To effectively characterize the micro-motor conditions, comprehensive features were extracted from the raw vibration data in both the time and frequency domains. A random forest (RF) algorithm was then employed to evaluate the importance of all extracted features. To better interpret the extracted features based on fault mechanisms, and to reduce dimensionality and computational overhead while avoiding overfitting, the top three features with the highest importance scores were selected to form the optimal feature subset. Finally, a support vector machine (SVM) model was utilized to classify the motor states based on the selected features. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method, combining RF-based feature selection and SVM classification, achieves outstanding diagnostic performance. Specifically, the model yields a balanced accuracy of 94.44%, a defect recall of 88.89%, a defect F1-score of 94.12%, a Matthews correlation coefficient of 93.74%, a geometric mean of 94.28%, and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 100.00%. These robust metrics confirm that the proposed approach can accurately and efficiently detect micro-motor faults in electric toothbrushes, providing a practical and reliable solution for quality control and condition monitoring in manufacturing.

中文摘要翻译: 内部微电机的可靠性对电动牙刷的性能和寿命至关重要。本文提出了一种基于振动的故障检测方法，用于识别电动牙刷中的微电机缺陷。设计并开发了专用信号采集装置，利用高精度加速度计捕捉微电机的振动信号。为了有效表征微电机状态，从原始振动数据中提取了时域和频域的综合特征。随后采用随机森林（RF）算法评估所有提取特征的重要性。为了更好地基于故障机制解释所提取的特征，并降低维度和计算开销同时避免过拟合，选取重要性评分最高的三个特征构成最优特征子集。最后，利用支持向量机（SVM）模型基于所选特征对电机状态进行分类。实验结果表明，所提出的结合RF特征选择与SVM分类的方法实现了出色的诊断性能。具体而言，该模型的平衡精度为94.44%，缺陷召回率为88.89%，缺陷F1分数为94.12%，马修斯相关系数为93.74%，几何均值为94.28%，受试者工作特征曲线下面积为100.00%。这些稳健的指标证实，所提方法能够准确高效地检测电动牙刷中的微电机故障，为制造中的质量控制和状态监测提供了实用可靠的解决方案。

</details>


### [181] [Secure Relay Low-Altitude Networks via Hybrid Fixed-Position and Rotatable Antenna Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26531)
*Maolin Li,Qi Zhang,Riqing Chen,Wei Gao,Feng Shu,Liang Yang,Cunhua Pan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合固定位置与可旋转天线阵列的中继网络，通过在基站和中继站部署两种天线分别服务于地面用户和空中用户，并利用可重构智能表面解决多用户干扰，通过联合波束赋形、功率分配和天线方向设计，最大化最差情况下的安全速率。提出了单用户场景的逐步泄露方案和多用户场景的DSAC-T强化学习方案，仿真显示安全速率提升约两倍，节省约71.4%的功率和55%的天线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统中继网络在低空经济应用中面临安全通信挑战，现有固定天线阵列难以同时高效服务地面用户和空中用户，且多用户干扰严重。本文旨在通过引入可旋转天线阵列和可重构智能表面，提升安全通信性能并降低部署成本。

Method: 提出混合固定位置与可旋转天线阵列的中继网络架构：基站和中继站同时配备固定天线阵列（服务地面用户）和可旋转阵列（服务空中用户）；利用低成本可重构智能表面作为候选路径缓解多用户干扰；在发射功率、服务质量、旋转范围和路径选择约束下，联合优化波束赋形、功率分配和天线方向。单用户场景采用逐步泄露方案；多用户场景提出基于DSAC-T（具有三项改进的分布式软演员-评论家）的强化学习方案，支持混合离散-连续动作空间。

Result: 仿真结果表明：所提方案相比各向同性天线，安全速率性能提升约两倍；系统可节省约71.4%的发射功率和55%的天线数量，并能服务更多用户。

Conclusion: 本文验证了在低空经济通信中引入可旋转天线阵列和可重构智能表面的有效性，所提方案在安全速率、功率效率和天线利用率方面均取得显著提升，为未来低空经济安全通信提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: In this paper, a relay network with hybrid fixed-position and rotatable antenna arrays is proposed. The deployment of rotatable arrays in conventional relay networks is considered to provide more secure communications for low-altitude economy applications. Specifically, both the base station and the relay station are equipped with fixed-position antenna arrays and rotatable arrays to serve ground users and aerial users, respectively. To address the challenge of multi-user interference, a low-cost reconfigurable intelligent surface is exploited as a candidate path. Accordingly, under constraints on transmit power, user quality of service, rotatable range, and path selection, the objective is to maximize the worst-case secrecy rate (SR) through joint beamforming, power allocation, and rotatable antenna orientation design. First, the SR performance in the single-user scenario is investigated, and a step-by-step leakage-based scheme is proposed. Then, the general multi-user scenario is studied, and a Distributional Soft Actor-Critic with Three refinements (DSAC-T)-based learning scheme, which supports hybrid discrete and continuous actions, is proposed to maximize the worst-case SR. Simulation results validate the effectiveness of the proposed schemes. The proposed schemes achieve approximately a twofold improvement in SR performance compared to isotropic antennas. The proposed system achieves approximately 71.4\% power saving, 55\% antenna saving, and can serve more users.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种混合固定位置与可旋转天线阵列的中继网络。考虑在传统中继网络中部署可旋转阵列，以在低空经济应用中提供更安全的通信。具体而言，基站和中继站均配备固定位置天线阵列和可旋转阵列，分别服务地面用户和空中用户。为解决多用户干扰挑战，利用低成本的智能超表面作为候选路径。据此，在发射功率、用户服务质量、旋转范围和路径选择的约束下，目标是通过联合波束赋形、功率分配和可旋转天线方向设计，最大化最差情况下的安全速率。首先，研究单用户场景下的安全速率性能，提出了一种逐步泄露方案。然后，研究一般多用户场景，提出了一种基于分布式软演员-评论家与三项改进（DSAC-T）的学习方案，该方案支持混合离散与连续动作，以最大化最差情况安全速率。仿真结果验证了所提方案的有效性。相比各向同性天线，所提方案实现了约两倍的安全速率性能提升。所提系统可实现约71.4%的功率节省、55%的天线节省，并能服务更多用户。

</details>


### [182] [Stay or Switch: Online Conformal Bayesian Optimization Guided Fluid Antenna Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26547)
*Gangyong Zhu,Jia Yan,Shijian Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对流体天线系统(FAS)在空地网络通感一体化(ISAC)中存在的物理开销和切换成本问题，提出了一个代价感知的多目标FAS切换优化框架，并设计了在线共形贝叶斯优化(OCBO)算法，在不确定环境下实现稳健的切换决策，显著提升了长期ISAC性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究在FAS中常忽略或简化物理开销和切换成本，而实际中端口切换会产生不可忽略的时间延迟，导致通信和感知性能下降。这带来两个关键挑战：(1)难以刻画时隙级且代价感知的ISAC指标；(2)大端口空间和急剧的环境变化需要更可靠的在线决策方法。

Method: 提出了一个代价感知的多目标FAS切换问题，联合考虑时隙级ISAC性能和切换能量；然后提出在线共形贝叶斯优化(OCBO)算法，用于学习未知的灰箱ISAC目标，并校准代理不确定性以实现鲁棒的保持或切换决策。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的代价感知优化框架相比现有基线方法，实现了显著更优的长期ISAC性能。

Conclusion: 考虑FAS切换物理代价的优化框架能够有效提升空地网络中通感一体化系统的长期性能，OCBO算法在不确定环境下具有良好的鲁棒决策能力。

Original Abstract: Fluid antenna systems (FAS) introduce additional spatial degrees of freedom to enable integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) in air-ground networks. However, conventional studies often overlook or simplify the physical overheads and switching costs of FAS. In practice, port switching incurs non-negligible time, during which communication and sensing may continue but with potentially degraded slot-level performance. This leads to two key challenges: (1) the characterization of a slot-level, cost-aware ISAC metric is difficult, and (2) the large port space and accompanying abrupt environmental variations demand more reliable online decision-making. To address these challenges, a cost-aware multi-objective FAS switching problem is formulated, jointly considering slot-level ISAC performance and switching energy. The online conformal Bayesian optimization (OCBO) algorithm is then proposed to learn the unknown gray-box ISAC objectives and calibrate surrogate uncertainty for robust stay-or-switch decisions. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed cost-aware optimization framework achieves substantially improved long-term ISAC performance compared to existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 流体天线系统(FAS)引入了额外的空间自由度，以支持空地网络中的通感一体化(ISAC)。然而，现有研究常常忽视或简化FAS的物理开销和切换成本。实际上，端口切换会产生不可忽略的时间开销，在此期间通信与感知可能仍在继续，但时隙级性能可能因此下降。这带来了两个关键挑战：(1)难以刻画一种时隙级、代价感知的ISAC度量指标；(2)大端口空间及伴随的急剧环境变化要求更可靠的在线决策方法。为应对这些挑战，本文建立了一个代价感知的多目标FAS切换问题，同时考虑时隙级ISAC性能与切换能量。进而提出在线共形贝叶斯优化(OCBO)算法，用于学习未知的灰箱ISAC目标，并校准代理不确定性以实现鲁棒的保持或切换决策。仿真结果表明，所提出的代价感知优化框架相比现有基线方法，实现了显著更优的长期ISAC性能。

</details>


### [183] [Multi-User Localization via Active Sensing with Electromagnetically Reconfigurable Antennas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26605)
*Ruizhi Zhang,Yuchen Zhang,Ying Zhang,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电磁可重构天线（ERA）的主动感知框架，通过LSTM和GNN学习历史导频观测，实现上行多用户定位的渐进式精度提升，优于传统非可重构阵列。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统定位方案中，基站无法利用历史观测数据自适应调整天线配置以优化定位性能。本文旨在利用ERA的动态可调性，结合历史导频观测来逐步精化多用户位置估计。

Method: 构建了两种ERA范式（基于球谐基函数的合成模型和基于测量辐射码本的有限状态模型）的统一宽带几何信号模型；提出基于学习的主动感知框架：先用LSTM模块压缩并累积宽带回波特征，再用图神经网络（GNN）捕获多用户共享孔径耦合，最后通过专用输出头生成ERA配置或定位结果。

Result: 在特定信道分布下的数值结果表明，所提ERA主动感知框架能在多个感知阶段实现渐进式定位精化，性能优于传统非可重构阵列及多种消融基线。

Conclusion: 基于ERA的主动感知框架可有效利用历史观测信息自适应调整天线配置，实现多用户定位性能的逐步提升，验证了可重构天线在智能定位中的潜力。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates multi-user localization in uplink wireless systems assisted by electromagnetically reconfigurable antennas (ERAs). Unlike traditional localization schemes, we formulate an active sensing problem where a base station (BS) exploits historical pilot observations accumulated over previous sensing stages to adapt the shared ERA configuration and progressively refine position estimates. To capture both theoretical flexibility and practical hardware constraints, we establish a unified wideband geometric signal model accommodating two complementary ERA paradigms: a synthesis-based model utilizing spherical-harmonic basis functions, and a finite-state model based on measured radiation codebooks. Because analytically solving the resulting joint design problem is highly intractable due to the high-dimensional observation and the shared-aperture coupling among multiple users, we develop a learning-based active sensing framework. Specifically, pilot-matched wideband observations are compressed into compact user-wise features and sequentially accumulated by a long short-term memory (LSTM) module. These temporal features are then processed by a graph neural network (GNN) to capture multi-user shared-aperture coupling. Model-specific output heads generate either continuous synthesis coefficients or finite-state ERA selections, while a localization head produces stage-wise position estimates. Numerical results under a specific channel distribution show that the proposed ERA-assisted active sensing framework achieves progressive localization refinement across sensing stages and obtains better performance than conventional non-reconfigurable arrays and representative ablation baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究电磁可重构天线（ERA）辅助的上行无线系统中的多用户定位问题。与传统定位方案不同，我们构建了一个主动感知问题：基站利用先前感知阶段积累的历史导频观测来调整共享ERA配置，从而逐步精化位置估计。为了兼顾理论灵活性与实际硬件约束，我们建立了一个统一的宽带几何信号模型，涵盖两种互补的ERA范式：基于球谐基函数的合成模型和基于测量辐射码本的有限状态模型。由于高维观测和多用户共享孔径耦合使得联合设计问题的解析求解高度棘手，我们开发了一个基于学习的主动感知框架。具体地，将匹配导频的宽带观测压缩为紧凑的用户级特征，由长短期记忆（LSTM）模块顺序累积；随后使用图神经网络（GNN）处理这些时序特征以捕获多用户共享孔径耦合；模型专用的输出头生成连续的合成系数或有限状态的ERA选择，同时定位头产生逐阶段的位置估计。在特定信道分布下的数值结果表明，所提出的ERA辅助主动感知框架能在感知阶段实现渐进式定位精化，且性能优于传统非可重构阵列及代表性的消融基线。

</details>


### [184] [An Informativeness-based Clustered Federated Learning Method for Reliable Traffic Prediction in Managed Wi-Fi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26682)
*Luca Barbieri,Gianluca Fontanesi,Lorenzo Galati Giordano,Alfonso Fernandez Duran,Thorsten Wild*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于两步聚类的联邦学习方法（CFL），用于Wi-Fi网络中接入点（AP）的流量预测，通过最大化差分熵选择最具信息量的聚类方案，在预测性能、通信与能耗之间取得平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在集中式Wi-Fi管理方案中，使用分布式AI预测AP的运营统计数据并优化网络性能时，聚类联邦学习（CFL）虽能生成适配不同数据分布的多个模型，但如何识别信息丰富的聚类来对AP模型进行正确分组仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 提出了一种两步聚类的CFL工具：首先生成多个聚类方案，并根据最低聚类标准进行过滤；如果没有方案满足质量指标，则聚合所有AP模型生成全局模型；否则，选择使最小聚类的差分熵（信息量）最大化的聚类方案作为最终结果。

Result: 在Wi-Fi流量预测任务中，所提出的CFL工具在所有分布式策略中取得了最佳预测性能，同时在聚类策略中实现了最低的通信和能耗开销；仅在显著提升准确率的场景下，其成本才超过单模型联邦学习。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于两步聚类与差分熵最大化的CFL工具，能够在预测精度与通信/能耗成本之间实现良好的权衡，是一种适用于Wi-Fi网络性能优化的有效分布式AI方案。

Original Abstract: Centrally-managed Wi-Fi solutions are increasingly leveraging Distributed Artificial Intelligence (AI) to predict key operational statistics of Access Points (APs) and proactively optimize network performance. In this context, Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) represents a fitting methodology, enabling the generation of multiple AI models that account for diverse statistical properties of the APs data distribution. However, identifying informative clusters for grouping APs models remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we address this problem by proposing a novel CFL tool integrating a two step clustering procedure. Initially, multiple clustering solutions are generated and filtered based on a minimum set of desired clustering criteria. Subsequently, if no solutions meet sufficient quality metrics, a global model is produced by aggregating all AP models. Otherwise, the final clustering solution is selected as the one that maximizes the informativeness (quantified via differential entropy) for the smallest cluster. Our results, focusing on a Wi-Fi traffic prediction problem, demonstrate that the developed CFL tool achieves the best predictive performance among all evaluated distributed strategies and the lowest communication and energy footprint among the clustered ones, exceeding the cost of single-model FL only in the regimes where it markedly improves accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 集中式管理的Wi-Fi解决方案正越来越多地利用分布式人工智能（AI）来预测接入点（AP）的关键运行统计数据，并主动优化网络性能。在此背景下，聚类联邦学习（CFL）是一种合适的方法，能够生成多个AI模型以适应AP数据分布的不同统计特性。然而，如何识别用于AP模型分组的有效聚类仍是一个重大挑战。本文针对该问题，提出了一种集成两步聚类流程的新型CFL工具。首先，生成多个聚类方案，并根据一组最低聚类标准进行筛选。随后，如果没有方案满足足够的质量指标，则通过聚合所有AP模型生成一个全局模型；否则，最终选择的聚类方案是能使最小聚类的信息量（通过差分熵量化）最大化的方案。我们在Wi-Fi流量预测问题上的实验结果表明，所开发的CFL工具在所有评估的分布式策略中取得了最佳预测性能，同时在所有聚类策略中实现了最低的通信和能耗开销；仅在显著提升准确率的场景下，其成本才超过单模型联邦学习。

</details>


### [185] [A Dual-Mode FM/AM Modulator Based on a Time-Varying Inverting Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26738)
*Azalía G. Gil,Alfonso T. Muriel-Barrado,Mario Pérez-Escribano,Carlos Molero,Antonio Alex-Amor*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于时变调制变容二极管的双模频率/幅度调制器，利用变容二极管作为时变电容器，结合反相积分器和带通滤波器，高效生成FM信号并可通过简单修改生成AM信号。微带线原型PCB的实验结果与理论及仿真高度吻合，展示了时变电容器在电信和雷达应用中替代传统FM技术的潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索时变电容器（尤其是变容二极管）作为传统频率调制技术的替代方案，利用其固有的频率混合能力实现高效、双模（FM/AM）调制的电路设计，以满足电信和雷达应用的需求。

Method: 采用时变调制的变容二极管作为时变电容器，将其与配置为反相积分器和带通滤波器的运算放大器相结合，构建电路生成FM信号；通过简单修改电路结构亦可获得AM信号。使用微带线技术实现PCB原型，推导解析表达式预测波形特性，并在Keysight ADS中进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 推导了预测生成波形特性的解析表达式，数值仿真验证了其准确性；制造的微带线PCB原型实验测量结果与理论预测和数值仿真高度一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于时变电容器的双模调制方法在电信和雷达应用中具有替代传统FM技术的潜力，实验验证了其可行性与有效性。

Original Abstract: This paper presents the analysis, design, fabrication, and experimental validation of a dual-mode frequency/amplitude modulator based on a time-modulated varactor diode. By exploiting the varactor as a time-varying capacitor in combination with an operational amplifier configured as an inverting integrator and a passband filter, the proposed circuit generates frequency-modulated (FM) signals in an efficient manner. Amplitude-modulated (AM) signals can also be obtained with a simple modification. The implementation, realized in microstrip technology, leverages the unique properties of time-modulated electronic components, particularly their inherent frequency-mixing capability. Analytical expressions are derived to predict the characteristics of the generated waveforms, and their accuracy is verified through numerical simulations performed in Keysight ADS. A microstrip PCB prototype is then fabricated and experimentally characterized. The measured results show excellent agreement with both the theoretical predictions and the numerical simulations. The proposed approach demonstrates the potential of time-varying capacitors as an attractive alternative to conventional FM techniques for telecommunications and radar applications.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了基于时变调制变容二极管的双模频率/幅度调制器的分析、设计、制造和实验验证。通过利用变容二极管作为时变电容器，并结合配置为反相积分器和带通滤波器的运算放大器，所提出的电路能够高效生成调频信号。通过简单的修改，还可以获得调幅信号。该实现采用微带线技术，利用了时变电子元件的独特特性，特别是其固有的频率混合能力。推导出解析表达式以预测生成波形的特性，并通过Keysight ADS中的数值仿真验证了其准确性。随后制造了微带线PCB原型并进行了实验表征。测量结果与理论预测和数值仿真均显示出极好的一致性。所提出的方法展示了时变电容器作为电信和雷达应用中传统调频技术的有吸引力的替代方案的潜力。

</details>


### [186] [Efficient Channel Prediction based on Gram-Square-Root Factorization using GMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26959)
*Kathrin Klein,Amar Kasibovic,Michael Joham,Shachar Shayovitz,Wolfgang Utschick*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的框架，利用Gram-平方根分解对部分反馈下的MIMO-OFDM信道进行CSI预测，通过结构化协方差矩阵的参数缩减技术降低复杂度，在部分CSI条件下达到与全CSI预测相当的性能，优于ZOH、FOH、LMMSE及神经网络预测器等经典方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在下行多用户MIMO系统中，CSI的准确性对预编码性能至关重要，但反馈延迟和移动性会导致预编码性能下降。由于全CSI反馈的信令开销过高，实际系统只能依赖部分CSI，因此需要一种能够在部分反馈条件下高效预测CSI的方法。

Method: 提出基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的预测框架，结合Gram-平方根分解对MIMO-OFDM信道进行建模；引入基于结构化协方差矩阵的参数缩减技术，有效降低高维度的计算复杂度。

Result: 仿真表明，GMM预测精度最高，能正确捕获底层信道子空间；在部分CSI条件下，参数缩减的GMM性能与全CSI预测相当；该方法优于ZOH、FOH、LMMSE及基于神经网络的预测器。

Conclusion: GMM结合Gram-平方根分解与参数缩减技术，能够高效地在有限反馈条件下对信道结构进行建模，实现高精度CSI预测，为实际MIMO系统提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate channel state information (CSI) is critical for downlink (DL)-multi-user (MU)-multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems, where feedback delays and mobility can degrade precoding performance. To ensure reliable beamforming and interference mitigation, CSI prediction is required. In practical systems, full CSI feedback is often infeasible due to signaling overhead, so transmitters rely on partial CSI reported by the receivers. In this work, we propose a Gaussian mixture model (GMM)-based prediction framework for MIMO-orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) channels under partial feedback using Gram-square-root factorization. To address the high dimensionality, we introduce an efficient parameter reduction technique that exploits structured covariance matrices, significantly lowering complexity without noticeable performance degradation. This reduction is based on the Gram-square-root factorization and remains of interest even when full CSI is available. Simulation results demonstrate that GMMs achieve the highest prediction accuracy and correctly capture the underlying channel subspaces, which is essential for effective MU-precoding. The proposed method outperforms classical baselines such as zero-order hold (ZOH), first-order hold (FOH), and linear minimum mean squared error (LMMSE) predictors, and an advanced neural network (NN)-based predictor. Notably, the parameter-reduced partial CSI GMM achieves performance comparable to that of full CSI prediction, highlighting its ability to efficiently model the channel structure under limited feedback.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的信道状态信息（CSI）对下行多用户多输入多输出（DL-MU-MIMO）系统至关重要，反馈延迟和移动性会降低预编码性能。为确保可靠的波束成形和干扰抑制，需要进行CSI预测。在实际系统中，全CSI反馈因信令开销过大而难以实现，因此发射机依赖接收机报告的部分CSI。本文提出一种基于高斯混合模型（GMM）的预测框架，利用Gram-平方根分解对部分反馈下的MIMO-正交频分复用（OFDM）信道进行建模。为解决高维问题，我们引入一种高效参数缩减技术，利用结构化协方差矩阵显著降低复杂度而几乎不损失性能。该缩减基于Gram-平方根分解，即使在全CSI条件下也具有参考价值。仿真结果表明，GMM实现了最高的预测精度，并能正确捕获底层信道子空间，这对有效的多用户预编码至关重要。所提方法优于零阶保持（ZOH）、一阶保持（FOH）、线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）预测器以及先进的神经网络（NN）预测器。值得注意的是，参数缩减的部分CSI GMM达到了与全CSI预测相当的性能，突显了其在有限反馈条件下对信道结构进行高效建模的能力。

</details>


### [187] [Probabilistic Denoising-Enhanced ISAC for Stochastic Cluttered Mobile Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26994)
*Nghia Thinh Nguyen,Tri Nhu Do*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于概率去噪的感知与通信一体化框架（PDISAC），通过多比特时隙划分波形实现高速率数据嵌入，并利用轻量级概率去噪网络RDPDNet抑制数据引入的旁瓣干扰，在不修改匹配滤波接收机的前提下提升感知性能，同时理论分析了感知-通信的折中关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中，通过多比特时隙划分波形嵌入数据会引入数据依赖的非白色旁瓣，降低匹配滤波感知性能。现有方法通常修改接收机结构，作者希望在保持传统匹配滤波接收机不变的前提下，抑制数据引起的感知退化。

Method: 提出PDISAC框架：采用多比特时隙划分ISAC波形，将每个最大长度序列划分为交替的导频和数据时隙，通过符号级扩频嵌入多比特信息；开发轻量级概率去噪网络RDPDNet，插入在距离-多普勒热图形成与恒虚警率检测之间；采用对抗性频率混合机制训练网络，无需知晓嵌入符号即可抑制数据引入的旁瓣和热噪声。

Result: 实验结果表明：RDPDNet吸收了大部分数据嵌入带来的感知损失，在低信噪比下显著降低均方根误差；传统无数据链在高信噪比下达到去偏基准性能；增加时隙分配提高数据速率但带来更高的误码率，揭示了可调的感知-通信折中关系。

Conclusion: 通过概率去噪网络可以在不修改匹配滤波接收机的前提下有效抑制数据嵌入对ISAC感知性能的负面影响，时隙分配和序列长度共同决定了感知与通信之间的可调折中。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose Probabilistic Denoising ISAC (PDISAC), a framework built on a multi-bit slot-partitioned ISAC waveform: by partitioning each maximal-length sequence into alternating pilot and data slots, we embed multiple bits per sequence through symbol-level spreading, multiplying the data rate while every chip retains the deterministic radar code. The added throughput, however, injects data-dependent, non-white sidelobes into the range-Doppler (RD) heatmap that degrade matched-filter (MF) sensing. Rather than modifying the MF receiver, we develop RDPDNet, a lightweight probabilistic denoising network inserted between RD-map formation and constant-false-alarm-rate detection; training it with an adversarial frequency-mixup mechanism, we suppress the data-induced sidelobes and thermal noise without knowledge of the embedded symbols. We further characterize the statistics of the geometry-determined channel. The fundamental performance limits of the design are then analyzed through an analytical lower bound, a semi-analytical bit error rate (BER), and an average capacity that tie the slot allocation and sequence length to the sensing-communication trade-off. Through analytical and numerical results over a realistic urban geometry, we show that RDPDNet absorbs most of the data-embedding sensing penalty and markedly lowers the RMSE at low SNR, while the conventional data-free chain attains the bias-adjusted benchmark at high SNR. Moreover, increasing the slot allocation raises the data rate at the expense of a higher BER, exposing a tunable sensing--communication trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出概率去噪集成感知与通信（PDISAC）——一个基于多比特时隙划分ISAC波形的框架：通过将每个最大长度序列划分为交替的导频时隙和数据时隙，我们利用符号级扩频在每个序列中嵌入多个比特，从而在保持每个码片具有确定性雷达码字的同时倍增数据速率。然而，由此增加的数据吞吐量会向距离-多普勒热图中注入数据依赖的非白色旁瓣，从而降低匹配滤波感知性能。我们不修改匹配滤波接收机，而是开发了RDPDNet——一个轻量级概率去噪网络，插入在距离-多普勒图形成与恒虚警率检测之间；通过对抗性频率混合机制训练该网络，无需知道嵌入符号即可抑制数据引入的旁瓣和热噪声。我们进一步刻画了几何决定信道的统计特性。随后通过解析下界、半解析误码率和平均容量分析了该设计的基本性能极限，将时隙分配和序列长度与感知-通信折中联系起来。通过在真实城市几何场景上的解析和数值结果，我们表明RDPDNet吸收了大部分数据嵌入带来的感知损失，并在低信噪比下显著降低均方根误差，而传统无数据链在高信噪比下达到去偏基准性能。此外，增加时隙分配会提高数据速率但导致更高的误码率，揭示了一个可调的感知-通信折中关系。

</details>


### [188] [Two-Filter Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Smoothing for Nonlinear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27151)
*Benjamin Schneiderheinze,Andrea De Vittori,Keith A. LeGrand,Jill Bruer*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于非线性系统的递归贝叶斯平滑算法，通过信息形式的高斯混合近似未来测量信息，并结合分裂、合并和递归测量更新等非线性高斯混合滤波技术，显著提升了空间目标跟踪的估计精度和不确定性降低效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在空间目标跟踪中，非高斯分布、高度非线性动力学以及测量数据缺失会导致滤波估计高度不确定或不准确，而现有的平滑算法在非线性、非高斯场景下存在理论和计算上的挑战，亟需一种能够有效利用未来测量信息来改进估计的递归贝叶斯平滑方法。

Method: 提出一种双滤波平滑方法：首先通过前向自适应高斯混合滤波器生成高斯混合后验，然后利用信息形式的高斯混合近似未来测量信息，结合分裂、合并和递归测量更新等非线性高斯混合滤波技术进行后向平滑，实现递归贝叶斯平滑。

Result: 在Molniya轨道和地月光环轨道空间目标跟踪问题上的实验表明，所提出的平滑器相比前向滤波器显著降低了估计误差和不确定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的递归贝叶斯平滑算法能够有效利用未来测量信息，显著提升非线性、非高斯场景下空间目标跟踪的估计性能，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Original Abstract: Space object tracking poses challenging estimation problems due to the significantly non-Gaussian distributions that can arise, particularly under highly nonlinear dynamics or during periods of measurement unavailability. Adaptive Gaussian mixture filters can dynamically adjust their mixture resolution to systematically approximate these non-Gaussian distributions, but challenging estimation problems can still produce highly uncertain or inaccurate estimates, especially during prolonged observation gaps. Smoothing algorithms can significantly improve filtered estimates by incorporating future measurement information for applications where immediacy is not required, but smoothing in nonlinear, non-Gaussian settings poses additional theoretical and computational challenges. This work develops a new recursive Bayesian smoothing algorithm for nonlinear systems that refines Gaussian mixture posteriors produced by a forward adaptive Gaussian mixture filter. A two-filter smoothing approach approximates the future measurement information by an information-form Gaussian mixture in the state variable. Techniques from nonlinear Gaussian mixture filtering including splitting, merging, and recursive measurement updating are also incorporated to improve the accuracy and computational efficiency of this approximation. The proposed smoother's estimation capabilities are demonstrated on space object tracking problems for Molniya and Earth-Moon halo orbits and shown to significantly reduce estimation error and uncertainty compared to the forward filter.

中文摘要翻译: 空间目标跟踪由于可能出现显著的非高斯分布（尤其是在高度非线性动力学或测量不可用期间），带来了具有挑战性的估计问题。自适应高斯混合滤波器能够动态调整其混合分辨率以系统性地逼近这些非高斯分布，但在具有挑战性的估计问题中，尤其是在长期观测间隙期间，仍然可能产生高度不确定或不准确的估计。平滑算法通过纳入未来测量信息可以显著改进滤波估计，适用于不需要即时性的应用场景，但在非线性、非高斯环境下的平滑会带来额外的理论和计算挑战。本文针对非线性系统提出了一种新的递归贝叶斯平滑算法，该算法对由前向自适应高斯混合滤波器生成的高斯混合后验进行精细化处理。一种双滤波平滑方法通过状态变量的信息形式高斯混合来近似未来测量信息。为了提高该近似的精度和计算效率，还引入了非线性高斯混合滤波技术，包括分裂、合并和递归测量更新。所提出的平滑器在Molniya轨道和地月光环轨道的空间目标跟踪问题上展示了其估计能力，并表明与前向滤波器相比，显著降低了估计误差和不确定性。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Shape-Based Inductive Bias for Glioma Grading from Tumor Contours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26090)
*Puneet Velidi,Michelle F. Miranda,Farouk Nathoo,Ashery Mbilinyi,Cédric Beaulac*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于形状描述的神经胶质瘤分级方法：利用函数形状对齐框架将肿瘤轮廓对齐，分离全局变形与残差傅里叶形状，并将其组织为频序令牌。实验表明，紧凑型MLP在BraTS 2020肿瘤轮廓上实现了71.5%的平均平衡准确率，远超ResNet-18和ViT-Tiny，且参数量至少减少46倍。在无噪声模拟中，形状模型显著优于像素模型。该工作证明在表征层面融入基于形状的归纳偏置可提升可解释性和可扩展性，并实现大幅降维。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将基于肿瘤轮廓的胶质瘤分级问题视为像素问题，但信号本质上是形状信息，缺乏对形状归纳偏置的有效利用。

Method: 采用函数形状对齐框架对齐闭合轮廓，分离全局形变与残差傅里叶形状，并将这些量组织为频序令牌，输入紧凑型MLP进行分类。

Result: 在BraTS 2020的五折患者无关交叉验证中，紧凑MLP平均平衡准确率71.5%（优于ResNet-18的65.9%和ViT-Tiny的63.3%），低级别胶质瘤F1为54.9%，池化平衡准确率72.4%（95% CI: 66.4–77.8%）。MLP参数量仅2.9k–117.3k，至少比像素基线少46倍。无噪声模拟中形状模型平衡准确率56.3–71.5%，像素模型仅50.0–52.5%。

Conclusion: 在表征层面融入基于形状的归纳偏置能够提升模型的可解释性和可扩展性，同时实现显著的维度缩减。

Original Abstract: Glioma grading from tumor contours is often treated as a pixel problem even when the signal of interest is shape. We align closed contours with a functional shape-alignment framework, separate global deformation from residual Fourier shape, and organize these quantities as frequency-ordered tokens. In five-fold patient-disjoint cross-validation on BraTS~2020 tumor contours, with model selection performed using grouped inner validation, a compact multilayer perceptron (MLP) achieves the highest mean balanced accuracy at 71.5\%, compared with 65.9\% for ResNet-18 and 63.3\% for ViT-Tiny. It also gives the highest mean low-grade glioma F1 at 54.9\%. Its pooled out-of-fold balanced accuracy is 72.4\% (patient-bootstrap 95\% CI: 66.4--77.8\%). The selected MLPs use 2.9k--117.3k parameters across folds, at least 46 times fewer than the pixel baselines. In a controlled noise-free simulation, shape-based models reach 56.3--71.5\% balanced accuracy while the pixel models remain at 50.0--52.5\%. This work demonstrates how incorporating a shape-based inductive bias at the representation level can improve interpretability and scalability while enabling substantial dimensionality reduction.

中文摘要翻译: 基于肿瘤轮廓的神经胶质瘤分级通常被视为像素问题，即便感兴趣的信号本质上是形状信息。本文采用函数形状对齐框架对齐闭合轮廓，将全局形变与残差傅里叶形状分离，并将这些量组织为频序令牌。在BraTS 2020肿瘤轮廓上进行的五折患者无关交叉验证中，通过分组内部验证进行模型选择，紧凑型多层感知机（MLP）取得了最高的平均平衡准确率71.5%，相比之下ResNet-18为65.9%，ViT-Tiny为63.3%。该MLP在低级别胶质瘤F1分数上也达到最高值54.9%。其池化折外平衡准确率为72.4%（患者自助法95%置信区间：66.4–77.8%）。所选MLP在各折中使用的参数量为2.9k–117.3k，至少比像素基线模型少46倍。在受控无噪声模拟中，基于形状的模型平衡准确率达到56.3–71.5%，而像素模型仅维持在50.0–52.5%。本工作证明，在表征层面融入基于形状的归纳偏置能够提升模型的可解释性和可扩展性，同时实现显著的维度缩减。

</details>


### [190] [ScalablePromptus: Scalable and High-Fidelity Prompt-Based Video Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26106)
*Zehao Cao,Bowei Xu,Xun Cao,Zhan Ma,Hao Chen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出 ScalablePromptus，通过丢弃训练策略生成有序提示表示，使接收端能从任意截断的提示中重建高质量视频，解决基于提示的视频流在丢包场景下的质量崩溃问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 Promptus 框架在有损网络下脆弱，部分接收提示会导致视频质量灾难性下降，限制了基于提示的视频流在实际中的部署。

Method: 1) 语义和颜色感知的提示反转；2) 中间帧的球面线性插值；3) 丢弃训练策略，生成秩有序的提示表示。

Result: 稳定网络下略有质量提升；有损条件下相比基线将截断导致的性能下降降低 82%-95%。

Conclusion: ScalablePromptus 使基于提示的视频流在现实网络条件下具备足够鲁棒性，可用于实际部署。

Original Abstract: Prompt-based video streaming transmits compact semantic prompts instead of pixel-level content for generative reconstruction, enabling ultra-low-bitrate communication. However, the state-of-the-art Promptus framework is vulnerable to network fluctuation, where partially received prompts lead to catastrophic quality collapse. We propose ScalablePromptus, which enhances Promptus with semantic and color-aware prompt inversion, spherical linear interpolation for intermediate frames, and--most critically--a dropout training strategy that produces rank-ordered prompt representations. This allows the receiver to reconstruct meaningful video from arbitrarily truncated prompts without any adaptation. Under stable networks, ScalablePromptus achieves modest quality gains. Under lossy conditions, it reduces the performance degradation caused by truncation by 82%-95% compared to the baseline, making prompt-based streaming robust enough for real-world deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 基于提示的视频流传输将紧凑的语义提示替代像素级内容进行生成式重建，从而实现超低比特率通信。然而，最先进的 Promptus 框架容易受到网络波动的影响，部分接收到的提示会导致灾难性的质量崩溃。我们提出 ScalablePromptus，它通过语义和颜色感知的提示反转、中间帧的球面线性插值，以及最关键的一点——一种生成秩有序提示表示的丢弃训练策略，增强了 Promptus。这使得接收端能够在无需任何适配的情况下，从任意截断的提示中重建有意义的视频。在稳定网络下，ScalablePromptus 取得了适度的质量提升。在有损条件下，它相比基线将截断导致的性能下降降低了 82%-95%，使基于提示的视频流具备足够鲁棒性以支持实际部署。

</details>


### [191] [A Hierarchical Framework for Graph Structure Learning in Histopathology Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26153)
*Sudipta Paul,Amanda W. Lund,Bülent Yener*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 $G_2^*$-Net 的两层图学习框架，用于大规模组织病理学图像分类。该框架将WSI/大ROI分割为图像块，构建块内细胞级图，再将每个块作为节点学习可优化的图像级图结构，并通过二阶双层优化（含DARTS风格近似）联合学习图连接与分类器，在三个数据集上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于图的组织病理学分析方法通常依赖固定或启发式的图结构，无法准确表征组织真实的连通性，限制了模型对组织微环境空间组织的建模能力。

Method: 1) 将WSI/大ROI划分为图像块；2) 在每个块内构建细胞级图以捕获局部组织架构；3) 将每个块作为节点构建可学习的图像级图；4) 将图像级图结构学习建模为二阶双层优化问题，分离图连接学习与分类器优化，并通过验证驱动的反馈耦合两者；5) 采用DARTS启发的单步展开近似实现高效超梯度估计。

Result: 在三个不同的组织病理学数据集上进行了实验验证，结果表明所提方法有效。

Conclusion: $G_2^*$-Net通过可学习的双层图结构和高效的双层优化策略，有效提升了大规模组织病理学图像分类性能。

Original Abstract: The spatial organization of cells and tissues provides important diagnostic cues in histopathology images. Although graph-based approaches can model these relationships, many rely on fixed or heuristic graph structures that may not accurately represent tissue connectivity. In this work, we propose $G_2^*$-Net, an optimized two-level graph learning framework for classifying large-scale histopathology images, such as whole-slide images (WSIs) or large regions of interest (ROIs). Here, $G_2$ denotes the two-level hierarchical graph representation, and the superscript $*$ indicates the optimized image-level graph structure learned from the proposed framework. The method first divides each WSI or large ROI into image patches, constructs cell-level graphs within each patch to capture local tissue architecture, and then represents each patch as a node in a learnable image-level graph. $G_2^*$-Net formulates image-level graph structure learning as a second-order bilevel optimization problem, separating graph connectivity learning from classifier optimization while coupling them through validation-driven feedback. To make this formulation computationally practical, we adopt a DARTS-inspired one-step unrolled approximation for efficient hypergradient estimation. Experimental validation on three distinct histopathology datasets demonstrates the effectiveness of our proposed method.

中文摘要翻译: 细胞和组织的空间排布为组织病理学图像提供了重要的诊断线索。尽管基于图的方法可以建模这些关系，但许多方法依赖固定或启发式的图结构，可能无法准确表征组织连接性。在本文中，我们提出了 $G_2^*$-Net，一个用于大规模组织病理学图像（如全切片图像WSI或大感兴趣区域ROI）分类的优化双层图学习框架。其中，$G_2$ 表示双层层次化图表示，上标 $*$ 表示通过所提框架学习得到的优化图像级图结构。该方法首先将每个WSI或大ROI划分为图像块，在每个块内构建细胞级图以捕获局部组织架构，然后将每个块作为一个节点，构建可学习的图像级图。$G_2^*$-Net 将图像级图结构学习形式化为一个二阶双层优化问题，将图连接性学习与分类器优化相分离，并通过验证驱动的反馈耦合二者。为使该形式化计算可行，我们采用了一种受DARTS启发的单步展开近似来实现高效超梯度估计。在三个不同的组织病理学数据集上的实验验证表明了所提方法的有效性。

</details>


### [192] [Rethinking Clinical Relevance in Chest X-ray Machine Learning: How Evaluation References Define Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26333)
*Panagiotis Fytas,Ian Selby,Clemens Karner,Judith Babar,Simon Baker,Jake Beckford,Timothy J. Sadler,Shahab Shahipasand,Arthikkaa Thavakumar,John Li Chen,Alex Sawer,Michael Roberts,Jonathan Weir-McCall,J. H. F. Rudd,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Anna Korhonen,Anna Breger*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在胸部X光（CXR）机器学习中，不同的评估参考标准（如报告派生标签与专家标注、图像质量度量选择）如何显著影响病理分类和图像质量评估的模型性能与排名，并强调评估参考的选择应作为临床有效性的核心组成部分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 常用的基于报告派生的病理分类标签或通用图像质量指标可能无法可靠反映临床判断，导致模型评估结果不可靠，因此需要系统研究评估参考选择对模型性能与排名的影响。

Method: 收集了剑桥大学医院（CUH）临床队列的配对专家图像标注和报告派生标签，以及MIMIC-CXR数据集子集和专家诊断图像质量评分；对比了监督图像分类器（ResNet, DenseNet）与多种零样本/微调视觉语言模型（MedKLIP, GLoRIA, ConVIRT）在不同标签源下的表现，并评估了多种IQA度量与专家判断的一致性。

Result: 改变标签来源不仅导致性能估计的显著差异，还改变了模型排名；常用的IQA指标（如SSIM、PSNR）往往与专家对诊断可用性的评估不一致。

Conclusion: 评估参考的选择至关重要，决定了哪些模型和方法被视为最佳并被选作进一步开发或部署；应将其视为CXR机器学习中临床有效性的核心组成部分，并根据病理、成像任务和预期下游临床使用进行论证。

Original Abstract: Chest X-ray (CXR) machine learning relies heavily on automated evaluation using reference standards that aim to approximate clinical judgment. However, commonly used report-derived labels for pathology classification or generic image quality metrics for reconstruction may not reliably reflect clinical judgment. We systematically investigate how evaluation-reference choices affect model performance and ranking in both pathology classification and image quality assessment (IQA). To enable controlled comparison across evaluation references, we collected paired expert image- and report-derived labels for thoracic findings from a clinical cohort at Cambridge University Hospitals (CUH) and curated a subset of the public MIMIC-CXR dataset, along with expert ratings of diagnostic image quality. We show that for supervised image classifiers (ResNet, DenseNet), several zero-shot and fine-tuned vision-language models (e.g., MedKLIP, GLoRIA, and ConVIRT), changing the label source leads to substantial differences not only in performance estimates but also in model rankings. In parallel, alignment of IQA measures with expert judgment depends heavily on the choice of measure, and commonly used IQA metrics such as SSIM and PSNR often fail to align with expert assessments of diagnostic usability. Our results demonstrate that evaluation choices are crucial: they can determine which models and methods appear best and are therefore selected for further development or deployment. The selection of evaluation references should therefore be treated as a central component of clinical validity in CXR machine learning, and justified with respect to the pathology, imaging task, and intended downstream clinical use.

中文摘要翻译: 胸部X光（CXR）机器学习高度依赖自动化评估，其使用的参考标准旨在近似临床判断。然而，常用的基于报告派生的病理分类标签或通用图像质量度量指标可能无法可靠反映临床判断。我们系统研究了评估参考选择如何影响病理分类和图像质量评估（IQA）中的模型性能与排名。为实现跨评估参考的受控比较，我们收集了来自剑桥大学医院（CUH）临床队列的配对专家图像标注和报告派生标签，并整理了公开MIMIC-CXR数据集的子集及专家对诊断图像质量的评分。我们表明，对于监督图像分类器（ResNet, DenseNet）以及多种零样本和微调视觉语言模型（如MedKLIP, GLoRIA, ConVIRT），改变标签来源不仅导致性能估计的显著差异，还会改变模型排名。同时，IQA度量与专家判断的一致性高度依赖于度量选择，常用的IQA指标（如SSIM和PSNR）往往无法与专家对诊断可用性的评估对齐。我们的结果表明，评估选择至关重要：它们决定了哪些模型和方法被视为最优，从而被选作进一步开发或部署。因此，评估参考的选择应被视为CXR机器学习中临床有效性的核心组成部分，并需根据病理、成像任务及预期下游临床使用进行论证。

</details>


### [193] [Decoder-Guided Lossy Contour Coding Via Anchor Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26426)
*Ruoyu Yang,Yichi Zhang,Haohong Wang,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种粗到细的轮廓编码框架，利用解码器端已有的粗轮廓作为先验，对高质量轮廓进行有损压缩，实现率失真权衡，相比JBIG2等无损方法可节省最高5倍码率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有轮廓编码方法（如JBIG2、链编码）是无损、对称的，忽略了编码器-解码器之间的不对称性：解码器已免费获得粗轮廓，但编码器仍需传输精细轮廓，导致码率过高，且缺乏率失真控制。

Method: 提出粗到细轮廓编码框架：编码器沿精细轮廓提取有序锚点，在失真约束下进行自适应跳点；解码器利用粗轮廓先验指导锚点连接，重建精细轮廓，实现有损压缩与显式率失真权衡。

Result: 相比无解码器端指导的方法，码率降低54.5%-66.9%；相比JBIG2，码率节省最高5倍，同时保持高几何精度。

Conclusion: 该粗到细框架有效利用了解码器端的粗轮廓先验，在轮廓编码中实现了显著的码率节省和灵活的率失真控制。

Original Abstract: Object contours serve as compact structural priors for many receiver-side vision tasks such as image super-resolution, edge-conditioned generation, and machine vision. When such tasks are deployed over a bandwidth-limited channel, the sender transmits the high-quality object contour as structural side information to guide reconstruction at the receiver, while-to save bandwidth-only a low-quality reference such as a downsampled image or base-layer reconstruction is delivered. As a result, the decoder can already extract a coarse contour from this reference at no transmission cost, creating an encoder-decoder asymmetry: the fine contour must be coded and sent, yet a free coarse version is available at the decoder. This asymmetry is ignored by existing contour codecs such as JBIG2 and chain coding, which are lossless, symmetric, and offer no rate-distortion control, leading to high bitrates. In this paper, we propose a coarse-to-fine contour coding framework that models a high-quality contour as a structured geometric refinement of the decoder-available coarse contour. The encoder extracts ordered anchors along the fine contour and performs adaptive anchor skipping under a distortion constraint. The decoder then reconstructs the contour by using the coarse prior to guide anchor connectivity. This formulation enables lossy contour compression with an explicit rate-distortion trade-off. Experiments show 54.5%-66.9% bitrate reduction over methods without decoder-side guidance, and up to 5 times savings over JBIG2, while preserving high geometric accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 物体轮廓作为紧凑的结构先验，可服务于许多接收端视觉任务，如图像超分辨率、边缘条件生成和机器视觉。当此类任务部署在带宽受限的信道上时，发送端传输高质量物体轮廓作为结构性边信息以指导接收端重建，而为了节省带宽，仅传输低质量参考（如下采样图像或基层重建）。因此，解码器可以无传输成本地从该参考中提取粗轮廓，这就形成了编码器-解码器的不对称性：精细轮廓必须被编码和传输，而解码器端已有免费的粗版本。现有轮廓编解码器（如JBIG2和链编码）忽略了这种不对称性，它们是无损、对称的，且缺乏率失真控制，导致高码率。本文提出一种粗到细的轮廓编码框架，将高质量轮廓建模为解码器端可用粗轮廓的结构化几何细化。编码器沿精细轮廓提取有序锚点，并在失真约束下进行自适应跳点。解码器则利用粗先验指导锚点连接来重建轮廓。该公式实现了有损轮廓压缩与显式率失真权衡。实验表明，相比无解码器端指导的方法，码率降低54.5%-66.9%，相比JBIG2节省高达5倍，同时保持高几何精度。

</details>


### [194] [Multi-scale radiomics in pelvic MRI for endometriosis subtyping: highlighting data heterogeneity constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26692)
*Eliot Leguy,Chloe Mallet,Nicolas Normand,Elodie Germani*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种基于影像组学的流程，用于利用公开的UT-EndoMRI数据集进行患者级别的子宫内膜异位症亚型分类。最佳监督性能（AUC 0.80）来自原始小波特征与梯度提升分类器，但特异性较低；无监督聚类分析发现了可重复但分离度较差的亚组，且与采集变量相关。结果表明盆腔MRI影像组学包含子宫内膜异位症亚型分类的初步信号，但在小样本多中心数据集中存在稳定性问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 女性盆腔MRI分析具有挑战性，尤其对于子宫内膜异位症的评估，视觉特征受解剖复杂性、技术变异和阅片者间变异等多因素影响。因此，需要探索更客观、可重复的自动化分析方法（如影像组学）来辅助子宫内膜异位症的亚型分类。

Method: 从UT-EndoMRI数据集中手工分割的子宫和卵巢区域提取影像组学特征，比较多种多尺度特征表示和特征选择策略；使用监督分类器区分至少有一个子宫内膜瘤的患者与无此特征的患者；进行无监督扰动分析评估影像组学谱是否揭示可重复的患者亚组；采用ComBat协调方法尝试消除多中心采集变异。

Result: 最佳监督性能：原始小波特征+梯度提升分类器，AUC为0.80，但假阳性较多导致特异性低；ComBat协调未持续改善性能，表明在患者数量少的多中心队列中事后协调不足；无监督聚类发现可重复但分离度差的亚组，且与采集变量相关。

Conclusion: 盆腔MRI影像组学包含子宫内膜异位症亚型分类的初步信号，但研究结果突显了在小样本多中心数据集中影像组学亚型分类的脆弱性，事后协调方法不足以克服采集变异带来的影响。

Original Abstract: Analyzing female pelvic MRIs is challenging, especially for evaluating endometriosis, where visual features are influenced by several factors, including anatomical complexity, technical variability, and inter-reader variability. Here, we evaluate a radiomics-based pipeline for patient-level endometriosis subtyping using the publicly available UT-EndoMRI dataset. We extract radiomics features from manually segmented uterine and ovarian regions and compare several multi-scale feature representations and feature-selection strategies. We train supervised classifiers to distinguish patients with at least one endometrioma from those without, and perform an unsupervised perturbation analysis to assess whether radiomics profiles reveal reproducible patient subgroups. The best supervised performance is achieved using raw Wavelet-derived features and a Gradient Boosting classifier, yielding an AUC of 0.80. However, this model produces several false positives, resulting in low specificity. ComBat harmonization does not consistently improve performance, suggesting that post hoc harmonization is insufficient in a small, multi-site cohort in which acquisition groups contained very few patients. Using an unsupervised clustering analysis, we identify reproducible but poorly separated partitions that remain associated with acquisition variables. Overall, these results suggest that pelvic MRI radiomics contain a preliminary signal for endometriosis subtyping, while highlighting the fragility of radiomics-based subtyping in small, multi-site datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 分析女性盆腔磁共振成像具有挑战性，尤其是在评估子宫内膜异位症时，其视觉特征受到多种因素的影响，包括解剖复杂性、技术变异和阅片者间变异。本文利用公开的UT-EndoMRI数据集，评估了一种基于影像组学的患者级别子宫内膜异位症亚型分类流程。我们从手工分割的子宫和卵巢区域提取影像组学特征，并比较了多种多尺度特征表示和特征选择策略。我们训练监督分类器以区分至少有一个子宫内膜瘤的患者与无此特征的患者，并执行无监督扰动分析以评估影像组学谱是否揭示可重复的患者亚组。最佳监督性能来自原始小波特征与梯度提升分类器的组合，AUC达到0.80。然而，该模型产生较多假阳性，导致特异性较低。ComBat协调方法未能持续改善性能，表明在小样本多中心队列中，由于各采集组患者数量极少，事后协调方法不足以解决问题。通过无监督聚类分析，我们识别出可重复但分离度较差的亚组，且这些亚组与采集变量相关。总体而言，这些结果表明盆腔MRI影像组学包含子宫内膜异位症亚型分类的初步信号，同时突显了在小样本多中心数据集中影像组学亚型分类的脆弱性。

</details>


### [195] [An Attention-Based Framework for Alzheimers Disease Classification Using Resting-State fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26746)
*Harshiddhi Pathak,Gowtham Reddy N,Mrinal Acharya,Manjunatha Mahadevappa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力机制的深度学习框架，将脑区视为token，利用Transformer风格的自注意力机制直接建模rs-fMRI功能连接矩阵，用于阿尔茨海默病分类，在ADNI数据集上取得了88.95%的准确率和0.90的ROC-AUC。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于手工设计的功能连接特征或传统机器学习模型在利用rs-fMRI进行AD分类时，受限于功能脑连接的高维度、噪声和复杂的跨区域依赖关系，效果不佳。因此需要一种能够自动学习判别性功能表征且能建模全局依赖关系的深度学习方法。

Method: 提出基于注意力机制的深度学习框架，将脑区视为"token"，采用类似Transformer的自注意力机制直接处理rs-fMRI功能连接矩阵，建模跨脑网络的长距离和全局功能依赖关系，无需手动特征工程。采用受试者级别的评估协议防止跨访视信息泄露，并引入类别加权优化应对轻度类别不平衡。

Result: 在ADNI纵向队列的二元AD与认知正常分类任务中，该方法达到88.95%的准确率、0.90的ROC-AUC，且精确率-召回率平衡良好。

Conclusion: 自注意力驱动的功能连接建模是一种鲁棒且可解释的AD检测方法，证明了基于注意力的深度学习在rs-fMRI分析中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Accurate identification of Alzheimers disease (AD) using resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) remains challenging due to the high dimensionality, noise, and complex inter-regional dependencies inherent in functional brain connectivity, which limit the effectiveness of traditional approaches based on handcrafted connectivity features or conventional machine learning models. In this work, we present an attention-based deep learning framework for Alzheimers disease classification that operates directly on rs-fMRI functional connectivity matrices by treating brain regions as tokens and employing a Transformer-inspired self-attention mechanism to model long-range and global functional dependencies across distributed brain networks. The proposed framework learns discriminative functional representations without reliance on manual feature engineering and is evaluated on a longitudinal cohort from the Alzheimers Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) comprising cognitively normal and Alzheimers disease subjects with multiple visits. A subject-wise evaluation protocol is adopted to prevent information leakage across visits, and class-weighted optimization is incorporated to address mild class imbalance. Experimental results for binary AD versus cognitively normal classification demonstrate that the proposed attention- based rs-fMRI model achieves an accuracy of 88.95% and a ROC-AUC of 0.90, along with a favorable precision-recall balance, highlighting the effectiveness of self-attention-driven functional connectivity modeling as a robust and interpretable approach for Alzheimers disease detection using resting-state fMRI.

中文摘要翻译: 由于功能脑连接固有的高维度、噪声和复杂的跨区域依赖性，基于手工设计的功能连接特征或传统机器学习模型的方法效果有限，使得利用静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）对阿尔茨海默病（AD）进行准确识别仍然具有挑战性。本文提出了一种基于注意力的深度学习框架用于阿尔茨海默病分类，该框架将脑区视为token，并采用Transformer启发的自注意力机制，直接基于rs-fMRI功能连接矩阵建模跨分布式脑网络的长距离和全局功能依赖关系。所提框架无需依赖手动特征工程即可学习判别性功能表征，并在来自阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）的纵向队列上进行了评估，该队列包含多次访视的认知正常和阿尔茨海默病受试者。采用受试者级别的评估协议以防止跨访视信息泄露，并引入类别加权优化以应对轻度类别不平衡。二元AD与认知正常分类的实验结果表明，所提出的基于注意力的rs-fMRI模型达到了88.95%的准确率和0.90的ROC-AUC，并取得了良好的精确率-召回率平衡，突显了自注意力驱动的功能连接建模作为一种利用静息态fMRI进行阿尔茨海默病检测的鲁棒且可解释方法的有效性。

</details>
